==================================================
FILE: Dump-Context.ps1
==================================================
--------
Clear-Host

$repoRoot = "C:\Dev\coal-llc-mods"
$appDataGodot = [System.IO.Path]::Combine($env:APPDATA, "Godot\app_userdata\Coal LLC")
$steamRoot = "C:\Program Files (x86)\Steam\steamapps\common\Coal LLC\Coal LLC"
$outputFile = [System.IO.Path]::Combine($repoRoot, "context.txt")

$sb = New-Object System.Text.StringBuilder
[void]$sb.AppendLine("=== GIT STATUS ===")
Push-Location $repoRoot
$gitStatus = git status --short
foreach ($line in $gitStatus) { [void]$sb.AppendLine($line) }

[void]$sb.AppendLine("`n=== RECENT COMMITS ===")
$gitLog = git log -n 3 --oneline
foreach ($line in $gitLog) { [void]$sb.AppendLine($line) }
Pop-Location

# 1. Capture ModLoader.log
$mlLog = [System.IO.Path]::Combine($steamRoot, "modloader.log")
if (Test-Path $mlLog) {
    $mlLines = Get-Content $mlLog
    $mlErrors = $mlLines | Where-Object { $_ -match "(ERROR|CRITICAL|failed|cannot be loaded)" }
    [void]$sb.AppendLine("`n=== MODLOADER LOG AUDIT ($($mlErrors.Count) errors found) ===")
    if ($mlErrors.Count -gt 0) {
        foreach ($line in ($mlErrors | Select-Object -Last 15)) { [void]$sb.AppendLine($line) }
    } else {
        [void]$sb.AppendLine("All active mods verified by ModLoader cleanly.")
    }
}

# 2. Capture godot.log
$logPath = [System.IO.Path]::Combine($appDataGodot, "logs\godot.log")
if (Test-Path $logPath) {
    # Open with FileShare.ReadWrite to avoid locking collisions
    $stream = [System.IO.FileStream]::new($logPath, [System.IO.FileMode]::Open, [System.IO.FileAccess]::Read, [System.IO.FileShare]::ReadWrite)
    $reader = [System.IO.StreamReader]::new($stream, [System.Text.Encoding]::UTF8)
    $allLogLines = [System.Collections.Generic.List[string]]::new()
    while (-not $reader.EndOfStream) { $allLogLines.Add($reader.ReadLine()) }
    $reader.Dispose()
    $stream.Dispose()

    $errorLines = $allLogLines | Where-Object {
        $_ -match "(SCRIPT ERROR|ERROR:|Compile Error|Parse Error|Failed to load script|Invalid call|Invalid access)"
    }

    [void]$sb.AppendLine("`n=== SESSION ERRORS & WARNINGS ($($errorLines.Count) found) ===")
    if ($errorLines.Count -gt 0) {
        foreach ($line in ($errorLines | Select-Object -Last 25)) { [void]$sb.AppendLine($line) }
    } else {
        [void]$sb.AppendLine("None detected.")
    }

    [void]$sb.AppendLine("`n=== LOG TAIL (LAST 25 LINES) ===")
    $tailLines = $allLogLines | Select-Object -Last 25
    foreach ($line in $tailLines) { [void]$sb.AppendLine($line) }
}

[System.IO.File]::WriteAllText($outputFile, $sb.ToString(), [System.Text.Encoding]::UTF8)
Set-Clipboard -Value $sb.ToString()
Write-Host "Context written to context.txt and copied to clipboard." -ForegroundColor Green

$logPath = [System.IO.Path]::Combine($appDataGodot, "logs", "godot.log")
if (Test-Path $logPath) {
    Get-Content $logPath -Tail 25

FILE: inventory_slot_b.gd
==================================================

class_name InventorySlot
extends PanelContainer


signal update_inventory_from_ui()

enum Location{
    TOOLBAR, 
    WINDOW, 
}

const DISABLED_STRETCHED = preload("res://resources_UI/disabled_stretched.tres")
const NORMAL_STRETCHED = preload("res://resources_UI/normal_stretched.tres")
const SELECTED_STRETCHED = preload("res://resources_UI/selected_stretched.tres")


@export var inventoryItem: InventoryItem:
    set(value):
        var old = inventoryItem
        inventoryItem = value
        if equipped:
            if old.item.itemEquipEffect.has_method("stop_effect"):

                    old.item.itemEquipEffect.stop_effect()
            Bus.itemEquipped.emit(old.item)
        updateInventoryItem()

@export var equipped: bool:
    set(value):
        if equipped != value:
            equipped = value
            if equipped:
                Bus.itemEquipped.emit(inventoryItem.item)

@export var in_game: bool = true
@export var location: Location

@onready var item_texture: TextureRect = %ItemTexture
@onready var effect_texture: TextureRect = %EffectTexture
@onready var item_count: RichTextLabel = %ItemCount


@export var selected: bool = false:
    set(value):
        selected = value
        update_selected()

var draggable: bool = true
var is_power_up_on: bool = false:
    set(value):
        is_power_up_on = value
        if is_power_up_on == false:
            if equipped:
                Bus.itemEquipped.emit(inventoryItem.item)

func _ready() -> void :
    updateInventoryItem()
    update_selected()


func update_selected():
    if not is_node_ready():
        await ready
    equipped = selected
    if selected == true:

        add_theme_stylebox_override("panel", SELECTED_STRETCHED)
    else:
        add_theme_stylebox_override("panel", NORMAL_STRETCHED)


func _get_drag_data(_at_position: Vector2) -> Dictionary:
    if (draggable == true):
        set_drag_preview(get_preview())

    return {"item": inventoryItem, "slot": self, "location": location}


func _can_drop_data(_at_position: Vector2, data: Variant) -> bool:
    return data.has("item") and (data["item"] is InventoryItem) and data.has("location") and (data["location"] == location)


func _drop_data(_at_position: Vector2, data: Variant) -> void :
    if data.has("slot") and (draggable == true):
        var source_slot = data["slot"]
        var temp = inventoryItem


        if inventoryItem.item.itemID == data["item"].item.itemID:
            var inventory: Inventory = load("res://resources/Inventories/PlayerInventory.tres")
            var added = max(min(inventory.maxStack, inventoryItem.count + data["item"].count) - inventoryItem.count, 0)
            inventoryItem.count += added
            source_slot.inventoryItem.count -= added
            if source_slot.inventoryItem.count <= 0:
                source_slot.inventoryItem.item = load("res://resources/Items/Empty.tres")
        else:
            inventoryItem = data["item"]
            source_slot.inventoryItem = temp


        update_inventory_from_ui.emit()


func get_preview():
    var preview_texture = TextureRect.new()

    preview_texture.texture = inventoryItem.item.itemTexture
    preview_texture.expand_mode = 1
    preview_texture.size = Vector2(100, 100)

    var preview = Control.new()
    preview.add_child(preview_texture)

    return preview


func updateInventoryItem():
    if not is_node_ready():
        await ready

    if inventoryItem != null:
        if not inventoryItem.is_connected("count_changed", updateInventoryItem):
            inventoryItem.connect("count_changed", updateInventoryItem)
        item_texture.texture = inventoryItem.item.itemTexture
        if inventoryItem.item.itemID == "Empty":
            item_count.text = ""
            item_count.visible = false
        else:
            item_count.text = Gconsts.add_comma_to_float_no_dp(inventoryItem.count)
            item_count.visible = true
        self.tooltip_text = inventoryItem.item.itemName
        if inventoryItem.item.effectTexture != null:
            effect_texture.texture = inventoryItem.item.effectTexture
            effect_texture.visible = true
        else:
            effect_texture.visible = false




func _on_gui_input(event: InputEvent) -> void :
    if event.is_action_pressed("dropItem") and in_game:
        var temp = InventoryItem.new()
        temp.item = load("res://resources/Items/Empty.tres")
        temp.count = 0


        var old_inventoryItem: InventoryItem = inventoryItem
        inventoryItem = temp

        Bus.drop_item_from_player.emit(old_inventoryItem)


        update_inventory_from_ui.emit()

FILE: InventorySlot.gd
==================================================

extends AspectRatioContainer

signal update_inventory_from_ui()

@export var inventoryItem: InventoryItem:
    set(value):
        inventoryItem = value
        updateInventoryItem()

@export var equipped: bool:
    set(value):
        if equipped != value:
            equipped = value
            if equipped:

                inventoryItem.item.itemEquipEffect.equip_effect(self)
                Bus.itemEquipped.emit(inventoryItem.item)
            else:

                if inventoryItem.item.itemEquipEffect.has_method("stop_effect"):
                    inventoryItem.item.itemEquipEffect.stop_effect()


@onready var frame: TextureRect = $Frame
@onready var item_texture: TextureRect = $MarginContainer / ItemTexture
@onready var item_count: RichTextLabel = $MarginContainer / ItemTexture / ItemCount
@onready var margin_container: MarginContainer = %MarginContainer

var tooltip_instance: Control


const FRAME = preload("res://assets/32x32ItemFrame.png")
const FRAME_LOCKED = preload("res://assets/32x32ItemFrameLocked.png")
const FRAME_SELECTED = preload("res://assets/32x32ItemFrameSelected.png")


@export var unlocked: bool = false:
    set(value):
        unlocked = value
        update_selected()

@export var selected: bool = false:
    set(value):
        selected = value
        update_selected()

var draggable: bool = true

func _ready() -> void :
    updateInventoryItem()


func _physics_process(_delta: float) -> void :
    if equipped == true:
        inventoryItem.item.itemEquipEffect.equip_effect(self)

func update_selected():
    if not is_node_ready():
        await ready
    equipped = selected
    if selected == true:
        frame.texture = FRAME_SELECTED
    elif unlocked == true:
        frame.texture = FRAME
    elif unlocked == false:
        frame.texture = FRAME_LOCKED

func _get_drag_data(_at_position: Vector2) -> Dictionary:
    if (unlocked == true) and (draggable == true):
        set_drag_preview(get_preview())

    return {"item": inventoryItem, "slot": self}

func _can_drop_data(_at_position: Vector2, data: Variant) -> bool:
    return data.has("item") and data["item"] is InventoryItem

func _drop_data(_at_position: Vector2, data: Variant) -> void :
    if unlocked == true and data.has("slot") and (draggable == true):
        var source_slot = data["slot"]
        var temp = inventoryItem


        inventoryItem = data["item"]
        source_slot.inventoryItem = temp


        update_inventory_from_ui.emit()



func get_preview():
    var preview_texture = TextureRect.new()

    preview_texture.texture = inventoryItem.item.itemTexture
    preview_texture.expand_mode = 1
    preview_texture.size = Vector2(100, 100)

    var preview = Control.new()
    preview.add_child(preview_texture)

    return preview


func updateInventoryItem():
    if not is_node_ready():
        await ready

    if inventoryItem != null:
        if not inventoryItem.is_connected("count_changed", updateInventoryItem):
            inventoryItem.connect("count_changed", updateInventoryItem)
        item_texture.texture = inventoryItem.item.itemTexture
        if inventoryItem.item.itemID == "Empty":
            item_count.text = ""
            item_count.visible = false
        else:
            item_count.text = Gconsts.add_comma_to_float_no_dp(inventoryItem.count)
            item_count.visible = true
        margin_container.tooltip_text = inventoryItem.item.itemName


func _on_gui_input(event: InputEvent) -> void :
    pass


func _on_frame_gui_input(event: InputEvent) -> void :

    if event.is_action_pressed("dropItem"):


        var temp = InventoryItem.new()
        temp.item = load("res://resources/Items/Empty.tres")
        temp.count = 0


        var old_inventoryItem: InventoryItem = inventoryItem
        inventoryItem = temp

        Bus.drop_item_from_player.emit(old_inventoryItem)


        update_inventory_from_ui.emit()

==================================================
FILE: InventoryToolbar.gd
==================================================
--------
extends GridContainer

const TOOLBAR_SLOTS: int = 10
const INVENTORY_SLOT = preload("res://scenes/Interfaces/Inventory/inventory_slot_b.tscn")

const KEYBINDS: Dictionary = {
    "1": 0, 
    "2": 1, 
    "3": 2, 
    "4": 3, 
    "5": 4, 
    "6": 5, 
    "7": 6, 
    "8": 7, 
    "9": 8, 
    "0": 9
}


var inventory: Inventory = load("res://resources/Inventories/PlayerInventory.tres")
var current_equipped_key: String = ""


func _ready() -> void :
    for child in get_children():
        child.queue_free()

    for n in range(TOOLBAR_SLOTS):
        var instance: InventorySlot = INVENTORY_SLOT.instantiate()
        instance.inventoryItem = inventory.items[n]
        if n == 0:
            instance.equipped = true
            instance.selected = true
        instance.location = instance.Location.TOOLBAR
        instance.connect("update_inventory_from_ui", save_inventory)
        add_child(instance)

    inventory.connect("inventory_changed", on_inventory_changed)
    load_inventory()



func _process(_delta: float) -> void :
    for key in KEYBINDS.keys():
        if Input.is_action_just_pressed(key):
            select_slot(key)
            current_equipped_key = key
    if Input.is_action_just_pressed("next_item"):
        current_equipped_key = str(posmod((int(current_equipped_key) + 1), 10))
        select_slot(current_equipped_key)
    elif Input.is_action_just_pressed("previous_item"):
        current_equipped_key = str(posmod((int(current_equipped_key) - 1), 10))
        select_slot(current_equipped_key)


func on_inventory_changed():
    load_inventory()


func select_slot(key: String):
    var slots = get_children()
    for slot in slots:
        slot.selected = false
    slots[KEYBINDS[key]].selected = true




func load_inventory():
    var slots = get_children()
    var n = 0
    for item in inventory.items.slice(0, TOOLBAR_SLOTS, 1):
        slots[n].inventoryItem = item
        n += 1

    if current_equipped_key != "":
        select_slot(current_equipped_key)

func save_inventory():
    var n = 0
    for slot in get_children():
        inventory.items[n] = slot.inventoryItem
        n += 1
    inventory.addToInventory(load("res://resources/Items/Coal.tres"), 0)
    if current_equipped_key != "":
        select_slot(current_equipped_key)
    Bus.inventory_changed.emit()

--------
==================================================
FILE: InventoryWindow.gd
==================================================
--------
extends GridContainer

@export var in_game: bool = true
@export var totalSlots: int = 40

const INVENTORY_SLOT = preload("res://scenes/Interfaces/Inventory/inventory_slot_b.tscn")
var inventory: Inventory = load("res://resources/Inventories/PlayerInventory.tres")


func _ready() -> void :
    inventory.connect("inventory_changed", on_inventory_changed)
    load_inventory()

func on_inventory_changed():
    load_inventory()

func load_inventory():
    var n = 0
    var slots = get_children()
    for item in inventory.items:
        if n < slots.size():
            slots[n].inventoryItem = item
            slots[n].in_game = in_game
        else:
            var instance = INVENTORY_SLOT.instantiate()
            instance.location = instance.Location.WINDOW
            instance.connect("update_inventory_from_ui", save_inventory)
            add_child(instance)
            instance.inventoryItem = item
            instance.in_game = in_game
        n += 1

func save_inventory():
    var n = 0
    for slot in get_children():
        inventory.items[n] = slot.inventoryItem
        n += 1
    inventory.addToInventory(load("res://resources/Items/Coal.tres"), 0)
    Bus.inventory_changed.emit()

==================================================
FILE: Invoke-ModPipeline.ps1
==================================================
--------
param (
    [switch]$RunTests = $true,
    [switch]$LaunchGame = $false
)

Clear-Host
Write-Host "=================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "             COAL LLC MOD PLATFORM 2.0 ORCHESTRATOR              " -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "=================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan

$repo = "C:\Dev\coal-llc-mods"
$steamMods = "C:\Program Files (x86)\Steam\steamapps\common\Coal LLC\Coal LLC\mods"
$steamRepo = "C:\Program Files (x86)\Steam\steamapps\common\Coal LLC\Coal LLC\mods_repository"
$godotLog  = "$env:APPDATA\Godot\app_userdata\Coal LLC\logs\godot.log"

# 1. GIT STATUS & HEAD COMMIT
Write-Host "`n>>> 1. REPOSITORY STATE" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Set-Location $repo
$head = git log -1 --oneline
Write-Host "  HEAD Commit: $head" -ForegroundColor Green
$status = git status --short
if ($status) {
    Write-Host "  Modified Files:`n$status" -ForegroundColor Magenta
} else {
    Write-Host "  Working tree clean." -ForegroundColor Green
}

# 2. STAGING & DECOUPLED STORAGE
Write-Host "`n>>> 2. STAGING DIRECTORY STATUS" -ForegroundColor Yellow
$activeZips = (Get-ChildItem -Path $steamMods -Filter "*.zip" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue).Count
$vaultZips  = (Get-ChildItem -Path $steamRepo -Filter "*.zip" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue).Count
Write-Host "  Active Mods (Coal LLC/mods):            $activeZips archives" -ForegroundColor Green
Write-Host "  Master Catalog (Coal LLC/mods_repo):   $vaultZips archives" -ForegroundColor Green

# 3. AUTOMATED TEST RUNNER
if ($RunTests) {
    Write-Host "`n>>> 3. RUNNING REGRESSION & CI/CD CHECKS" -ForegroundColor Yellow
    & "$repo\tests\test_runner.ps1" -RepoPath $repo
}

# 4. RUNTIME LOG FORENSICS
if (Test-Path $godotLog) {
    Write-Host "`n>>> 4. RUNTIME LOG AUDIT (LAST 5 ERRORS/WARNINGS)" -ForegroundColor Yellow
    $logLines = Get-Content $godotLog
    $errs = $logLines | Select-String -Pattern 'SCRIPT ERROR|ERROR:' | Select-Object -Last 5
    if ($errs) {
        foreach ($e in $errs) { Write-Host "  $($e.Line.Trim())" -ForegroundColor Red }
    } else {
        Write-Host "  Zero errors found in latest runtime log!" -ForegroundColor Green
    }
}

Write-Host "`n=================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "Pipeline check complete. Ready for development or gameplay." -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "=================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan

if ($LaunchGame) {
    Write-Host "`nLaunching Coal LLC..." -ForegroundColor Green
    Start-Process -FilePath "C:\Program Files (x86)\Steam\steamapps\common\Coal LLC\Coal LLC\Coal LLC.exe"
}

--------
==================================================
FILE: item_filter_container.gd
==================================================
--------
extends ScrollContainer


func _ready() -> void :
    if Gvars.bonus_equipment_manager.item_filter:
        visible = true
    else:
        visible = false

# Coal LLC Modding Suite

A complete, high-performance ecosystem of gameplay overhauls, custom subterranean maps, unique character classes, industrial workforce hierarchies, high-tier demolition options, and quality-of-life tools for **[Coal LLC](https://store.steampowered.com/app/3361510/Coal_LLC/)**, built on the **[Godot ModLoader 7.x](https://godotengine.org/asset-library/asset/4107)** hook architecture.

![Tectonic Faultline Preview](mods-unpacked/gareth-tectonic_faultline_mod/assets/tectonic_preview.png)

---

## Complete Mod Directory (51 mods)

### 1. Custom Maps & Subterranean Overhauls (Maps 101–105)
| Mod | ID | Type | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **The Lost Expedition** | `gareth-lost_expedition_mod` | Map (ID 101) | Subterranean cryo-abyss featuring ancient buried shelters, unexploded landmines (UXO), and survival extraction mechanics. |
| **Tectonic Faultline** | `gareth-tectonic_faultline_mod` | Map (ID 102) | Seismic rift featuring vertical chasms, active magma fissures, 3x density elemental pods (Fire, Water, Poison), and an elemental tick rate progression engine. |
| **The Living Canvas** | `gareth-living_canvas_mod` | Map (ID 103) | Image-to-strata mosaic quarry supporting custom PNG imports converted into mineable subterranean strata. |
| **The Abyssal Trench** | `gareth-abyssal_trench_mod` | Map (ID 104) | Subterranean ocean trench featuring hydrothermal black smoker geysers, twilight sapphire strata, and abyssal basalt. |
| **Diagonal Seams** | `gareth-diagonal_map_mod` | Map (ID 105) | 45-degree rotated diagonal subterranean strata layers requiring specialized diagonal tunneling and stepped excavation. |

### 2. Character Classes & Combat Archetypes
| Mod | ID | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Knight Class** | `gareth-knight_class_mod` | Master of chivalric blade combat. Starts with a Shoddy Sword that sweeps in a 180° frontal arc clearing rock and ore. |
| **Jedi Class** | `gareth-jedi_class_mod` | Wielder of plasma energy sabers across 15 mineral tiers with rapid deflect swings and rock vaporisation. |
| **Bouncing Demolitionist**| `gareth-custom_classes_mod` | Master of ricochet excavation using bouncy rubber demolition spheres. |
| **Rainbow Sinclairity** | `gareth-sinclairity_class_mod` | Experimental spectral optics miner with continuous prismatic beam across 15 tiers. |
| **Void Siphon** | `gareth-void_siphon_mod` | Gravitational vacuum imploder that collapses distant ore veins directly into personal inventory. |
| **Debt Executioner** | `gareth-debt_executioner_mod` | High-risk financial class converting unpaid daily quotas into raw pickaxe excavation damage. |
| **Chrono-Industrialist** | `gareth-chrono_industrialist_mod` | Temporal manipulation specialist that warps excavation speed and slows physics timers. |
| **Gravitational Dreadnought** | `gareth-grav_dreadnought_mod` | Heavy exo-suit miner that crushes stone beneath accelerated downward terminal velocity. |
| **Tectonic Fracker** | `gareth-tectonic_fracker_mod` | High-pressure hydraulic injection specialist that shatters surrounding strata veins. |

| **In-Game Mod & Map Store** | `gareth-mod_manager_mod` | Title-screen community repository browser with category filtering and version checking. |

---

## Core Engine Modding Contracts

1. **Sky Code Contract:** `lvl_from_global_pos(pos)` must return `-2` whenever `pos.y <= 0`. Returning positive rock tiers at or above ground level fills the sky and spawn point with solid bedrock, crushing the player on spawn.
2. **Tile Damage Signature:** In *Coal LLC*, tile damage is processed via `tilemap.damageTileCoords(coords: Vector2i, damage: float)`. Passing non-primitive objects into argument 2 triggers an unhandled conversion abort.
3. **InputMap Restrictions:** Never query unmapped input action names (e.g., `"fire"`). Vanilla only registers `useItem`, `rightMouseClick`, and `toggle_use_item`. Querying unregistered strings logs errors on every physics tick.
4. **Chunk Bounds Protection:** `TileMapManager.chunks_created` must be guarded with `.has(chunk_id)` before indexing. Querying unloaded chunk IDs throws an unhandled dictionary exception.
5. **Ordnance & Tween Lifecycles:** Decouple falling projectiles from their parent launchers into standalone entities (`AirstrikeBomb`) to prevent `Instance is null` errors when parent nodes are freed while tween callbacks are pending.

==================================================
FILE: Reduce_Test_Output.ps1
==================================================
--------
[CmdletBinding()]
param(
    [Parameter(Position = 0)]
    [string]$InputPath = "C:\Dev\coal-llc-mods\tests.txt",

    [Parameter(Position = 1)]
    [string]$OutputPath = "C:\Dev\coal-llc-mods\tests_concise.txt"
)

Clear-Host

if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $InputPath)) {
    Write-Error "Input file not found at: $InputPath"
    return
}

$writer = [System.IO.StreamWriter]::new($OutputPath, $false, [System.Text.Encoding]::UTF8)

try {
    $currentKey = $null
    $firstLine  = $null
    $lastLine   = $null
    $count      = 0

    function Write-Run {
        param($key, $first, $last, [int]$cnt)

        if ($cnt -eq 1) {
            $writer.WriteLine($first)
        }
        elseif ($cnt -eq 2) {
            $writer.WriteLine($first)
            $writer.WriteLine($last)
        }
        else {
            $writer.WriteLine("[Repeated $cnt times]")
            $writer.WriteLine("  First: $first")
            $writer.WriteLine("  Last:  $last")
        }
    }

    foreach ($line in [System.IO.File]::ReadLines($InputPath)) {
        if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($line)) {
            continue
        }

        # Normalize key: strip line numbers (e.g. "20244: "), tags ("[ModLoader] "), and timestamps ("20:09:20   ")
        $key = if ($line -match '^\s*\d+:\s*(?:\[[^\]]+\]\s*)?(?:\d{2}:\d{2}:\d{2}\s+)?(.*)$') {
            $Matches[1].Trim()
        } else {
            $line.Trim()
        }

        if ($null -ne $currentKey -and $key -eq $currentKey) {
            $lastLine = $line
            $count++
        }
        else {
            if ($count -gt 0) {
                Write-Run -key $currentKey -first $firstLine -last $lastLine -cnt $count
            }
            $currentKey = $key
            $firstLine  = $line
            $lastLine   = $line
            $count      = 1
        }
    }

    if ($count -gt 0) {
        Write-Run -key $currentKey -first $firstLine -last $lastLine -cnt $count
    }
}
finally {
    $writer.Dispose()
}

Write-Host "Summarized log written to: $OutputPath" -ForegroundColor Green

FILE: repo_exporter.ps1
==================================================
--------
Clear-Host
# repo_exporter.ps1
# Combines workspace text source code files into a single, structured context output.

$TargetDirectory = Get-Location
$OutputFile = Join-Path $TargetDirectory "packed_source_context.txt"

# Comprehensive list of binary extensions, archives, and asset types to skip
$ExcludedExtensions = @(
    '.zip', '.png', '.jpg', '.jpeg', '.gif', '.ico', '.bmp', 
    '.gdc', '.scn', '.remap', '.tres', '.tscn', '.exe', '.dll', '.pdb'
)

# Root folders to completely bypass
$ExcludedFolders = @('.git', '.vscode', 'extracted_inventory')

# Specific task and pipeline progress files to omit
$ExcludedFiles = @(
    'files.txt', 
    'ai_discovery.ps1', 
    'ai_worker_script.ps1', 
    'output.txt', 
    'tests.txt', 
    'context.txt', 
    'tests_concise.txt',
    'packed_source_context.txt'
)

Write-Host "📦 Starting source packing inside: $TargetDirectory" -ForegroundColor Cyan

# Initialize/clear file
$Header = @"
==================================================
WORKSPACE SOURCE CODE DUMP
Generated on: $(Get-Date)
==================================================

"@
Set-Content -Path $OutputFile -Value $Header

# Gather and filter files up front (resolve full paths safely)
$FilesToLog = Get-ChildItem -Path $TargetDirectory -Recurse -File | Where-Object {
    $item = $_ 
    $relativePath = [System.IO.Path]::GetRelativePath($TargetDirectory.Path, $item.FullName)
    
    # Flag to track validity
    $shouldKeep = $true
    
    # Check folder exclusions
    foreach ($folder in $ExcludedFolders) {
        if ($relativePath -like "$folder*" -or $relativePath -like "*$folder*") { 
            $shouldKeep = $false 
        }
    }
    
    # Check specific file exclusions
    if ($ExcludedFiles -contains $item.Name) { 
        $shouldKeep = $false 
    }
    
    # Check binary/asset extensions
    if ($ExcludedExtensions -contains $item.Extension.ToLower()) { 
        $shouldKeep = $false 
    }
    
    # Exclude the output file itself explicitly
    if ($item.FullName -eq $OutputFile) {
        $shouldKeep = $false
    }
    
    $shouldKeep
}

# 1. Output a Scannable Directory Tree First
Add-Content -Path $OutputFile -Value "### WORKSPACE STRUCTURE"
Add-Content -Path $OutputFile -Value "````"
foreach ($file in $FilesToLog) {$relativePath = [System.IO.Path]::GetRelativePath($TargetDirectory.Path, $file.FullName)
    Add-Content -Path $OutputFile -Value "$relativePath"
}
Add-Content -Path $OutputFile -Value "````"

# 2. Append Contents of Text/Code Files
Write-Host "📄 Processing files..." -ForegroundColor Yellow
$FileCount = 0

foreach ($file in $FilesToLog) {
    # Safely compute relative path without relying on working directory state
    $relativePath = $file.FullName.Substring($TargetDirectory.Path.Length).TrimStart('\', '/')
    
    Add-Content -Path $OutputFile -Value "=================================================="
    Add-Content -Path $OutputFile -Value "FILE: $relativePath"
    Add-Content -Path $OutputFile -Value "=================================================="
    
    try {
        $fileStream = [System.IO.FileStream]::new($file.FullName, [System.IO.FileMode]::Open, [System.IO.FileAccess]::Read, [System.IO.FileShare]::ReadWrite)
        $streamReader = [System.IO.StreamReader]::new($fileStream)
        $content = $streamReader.ReadToEnd()
        $streamReader.Close()
        $fileStream.Close()

        if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($content)) {
             Add-Content -Path $OutputFile -Value "[Empty File]"
         } else {
        #     Add-Content -Path $OutputFile -Value "````"
             Add-Content -Path $OutputFile -Value "--------"
             Add-Content -Path $OutputFile -Value $content
             Add-Content -Path $OutputFile -Value "--------"
        #     Add-Content -Path $OutputFile -Vaue "````"
        }
        $FileCount++
    }
    catch {
        Add-Content -Path $OutputFile -Value "[Error reading file content]"
        Write-Host "⚠️ Skipped file: $relativePath" -ForegroundColor DarkYellow
    }
}

Write-Host "✅ Done! Packed $FileCount files into: $OutputFile" -ForegroundColor Green
--------
==================================================
FILE: Run-ModSuiteTest.ps1
==================================================
--------
Clear-Host
Write-Host "==========================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "COAL LLC - SAFE LOG AUDITOR & TEST RUNNER" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "==========================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan

$repoRoot = "C:\Dev\coal-llc-mods"
$steamRoot = "C:\Program Files (x86)\Steam\steamapps\common\Coal LLC\Coal LLC"
$appDataGodot = [System.IO.Path]::Combine($env:APPDATA, "Godot\app_userdata\Coal LLC")

$modloaderLog = [System.IO.Path]::Combine($steamRoot, "modloader.log")
$godotLog = [System.IO.Path]::Combine($appDataGodot, "logs\godot.log")
$testsTxt = [System.IO.Path]::Combine($repoRoot, "tests.txt")
$conciseOut = [System.IO.Path]::Combine($repoRoot, "tests_concise.txt")
$reducerScript = [System.IO.Path]::Combine($repoRoot, "Reduce_Test_Output.ps1")

function Read-LogFileSafely {
    param([string]$FilePath)
    if (-not [System.IO.File]::Exists($FilePath)) {
        return @()
    }
    try {
        $fs = [System.IO.FileStream]::new($FilePath, [System.IO.FileMode]::Open, [System.IO.FileAccess]::Read, [System.IO.FileShare]::ReadWrite)
        $sr = [System.IO.StreamReader]::new($fs, [System.Text.Encoding]::UTF8)
        $lines = [System.Collections.Generic.List[string]]::new()
        while (-not $sr.EndOfStream) {
            $lines.Add($sr.ReadLine())
        }
        $sr.Dispose()
        $fs.Dispose()
        return $lines
    } catch {
        Write-Warning "Could not read $FilePath : $($_.Exception.Message)"
        return @()
    }
}

$rawEntries = [System.Collections.Generic.List[string]]::new()
$mlCount = 0
$godotCount = 0

if ([System.IO.File]::Exists($modloaderLog)) {
    Write-Host "Auditing modloader.log..." -ForegroundColor Cyan
    $mlLines = Read-LogFileSafely -FilePath $modloaderLog
    for ($i = 0; $i -lt $mlLines.Count; $i++) {
        $l = $mlLines[$i]
        if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($l)) { continue }
        if ($l -match "ERROR|CRITICAL|failed|cannot be loaded") {
            $rawEntries.Add(("{0,4}: [ModLoader] {1}" -f ($i + 1), $l.Trim()))
            $mlCount++
        }
    }
}

if ([System.IO.File]::Exists($godotLog)) {
    Write-Host "Auditing godot.log..." -ForegroundColor Cyan
    $godotLines = Read-LogFileSafely -FilePath $godotLog
    $telemetry = [System.Collections.Generic.List[string]]::new()
    
    for ($i = 0; $i -lt $godotLines.Count; $i++) {
        $l = $godotLines[$i]
        if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($l)) { continue }
        if ($l -match "ERROR|CRITICAL|failed to load script|Parse Error|Compile Error|Invalid call|Invalid get index|Invalid access|Invalid assignment|Invalid type") {
            if (-not $l.Contains("der_floh-performance_mod")) {
                $rawEntries.Add(("{0,4}: {1}" -f ($i + 1), $l.Trim()))
                $godotCount++
                
                # NEW: Capture the stack trace line immediately following the error
                if ($i + 1 -lt $godotLines.Count -and $godotLines[$i+1] -match "^\s*at:") {
                    $rawEntries.Add(("{0,4}: {1}" -f ($i + 2), $godotLines[$i+1].Trim()))
                }
            }
        }
        if ($l.Contains("[KnightSword]") -or $l.Contains("Auto choosing") -or $l.Contains("Transmutation") -or $l.Contains("[Frenzy]")) {
            $telemetry.Add(("{0,4}: {1}" -f ($i + 1), $l.Trim()))
        }
    }
    
    if ($telemetry.Count -gt 0) {
        $rawEntries.Add("Telemetry Highlights (Last 15):")
        $start = [Math]::Max(0, $telemetry.Count - 15)
        for ($t = $start; $t -lt $telemetry.Count; $t++) {
            $rawEntries.Add($telemetry[$t])
        }
    }
}

[System.IO.File]::WriteAllLines($testsTxt, $rawEntries, [System.Text.Encoding]::UTF8)

if (Test-Path $reducerScript) {
    & powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File $reducerScript -InputPath $testsTxt -OutputPath $conciseOut
}

$totalErrors = $mlCount + $godotCount
$resultColor = if ($totalErrors -gt 0) { "Yellow" } else { "Green" }

Write-Host "==========================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "Audit Results: $mlCount ModLoader errors | $godotCount Godot errors" -ForegroundColor $resultColor
Write-Host "Summarized telemetry output written to: tests_concise.txt" -ForegroundColor Green
Write-Host "==========================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan

if (Test-Path $conciseOut) {
    Get-Content $conciseOut | ForEach-Object { Write-Host " $_" }
}

--------
==================================================
FILE: Run-Worker.ps1
==================================================
--------
Clear-Host

$scriptPath = "C:\Dev\coal-llc-mods\ai_worker_script.ps1"
$outputPath = "C:\Dev\coal-llc-mods\output.txt"

if (-not (Test-Path $scriptPath)) {
    Write-Error "ai_worker_script.ps1 not found at $scriptPath"
    exit 1
}

# Pre-load both required .NET compression assemblies
Add-Type -AssemblyName System.IO.Compression
Add-Type -AssemblyName System.IO.Compression.FileSystem

Write-Host ">>> Executing ai_worker_script.ps1 and streaming to output.txt..." -ForegroundColor Cyan

# Execute in-process and tee output stream to output.txt automatically
& $scriptPath *>&1 | Tee-Object -FilePath $outputPath

Write-Host "`n>>> Done! Output automatically captured in output.txt" -ForegroundColor Green

# Coal LLC Modding Ecosystem — Master Engineering Manual & Session State

## 1. Environment & Directory Topology (GROUND TRUTH)
* **Engine:** Godot Engine v4.4.1.stable.official (Vulkan 1.4 Forward+, Windows).
* **Game Version:** Coal LLC v1.4.1.1.
* **Mod Framework:** Godot ModLoader v7.0.1 (ModLoaderMod, ModLoaderHookChain, ModLoaderLog).
* **Repository Root:** C:\Dev\coal-llc-mods
* **Active Mod Source Code (mods-unpacked):**
  * ALL mod source files live inside C:\Dev\coal-llc-mods\mods-unpacked\<mod_id>\.
  * **Double-Nested Structure:** Every mod folder has an outer manifest.json at mods-unpacked\<mod_id>\manifest.json and an inner folder at mods-unpacked\<mod_id>\mods-unpacked\<mod_id>\ containing manifest.json, mod_main.gd, xtensions\, scripts\, scenes\, helpers\, and ssets\.
  * **NEVER** search the repository root for .gd files; always search inside mods-unpacked\.
* **Vanilla Decompiled Game Dump (_dump):**
  * Located at C:\Program Files (x86)\Steam\steamapps\common\Coal LLC\Coal LLC\_dump.
  * Contains the decompiled vanilla .gd scripts, .tscn scenes, and .tres resources. Always inspect _dump before hooking any vanilla script.
* **Staging vs. Active Runtime Mod Directories:**
  * **Staging Library (downloaded_mods):** C:\Program Files (x86)\Steam\steamapps\common\Coal LLC\Coal LLC\downloaded_mods — holds unmounted .zip archives managed by the Mod & Map Store.
  * **Active Runtime (mods):** C:\Program Files (x86)\Steam\steamapps\common\Coal LLC\Coal LLC\mods — holds the .zip archives actively mounted by ModLoader at game boot.
  * **Hook Cache (mod-hooks.zip):** C:\Program Files (x86)\Steam\steamapps\common\Coal LLC\Coal LLC\mod-hooks.zip — MUST be deleted whenever script hooks are added or changed so ModLoader regenerates hooks on launch.

---

## 2. Ways of Working: The Run-Worker.ps1 Automation Contract (MANDATORY)

### Rule 1: Zero Manual File Edits
* Gareth NEVER manually edits .gd, .json, or .tscn files, and never copies loose GDScript snippets into files.
* Every single response that inspects or modifies the workspace MUST provide **one self-contained PowerShell script block**.
* Gareth pastes your PowerShell block into i_worker_script.ps1 and executes .\Run-Worker.ps1.
* .\Run-Worker.ps1 runs your script and automatically captures all console output into output.txt, which Gareth uploads back to you.

### Rule 2: Discovery-First Protocol
* Before writing or modifying any mod code for a bug or feature, provide a **read-only Discovery PowerShell script** (zero file writes, zero repacks, zero git commits) to inspect the exact lines in _dump and mods-unpacked.
* Never guess node names, property names, dictionary structures, or method signatures.

### Rule 3: How Deployment & Gameplay Testing Work
* **NEVER** tell Gareth to manually run .\Run-ModSuiteTest.ps1 as a standalone command after a script finishes.
* 	ests_concise.txt, godot.log, and modloader.log are generated when Gareth **plays the game**.
* Every Deployment PowerShell script MUST:
  1. Terminate running Coal LLC* and godot* processes so Windows file locks are released.
  2. Write updated source files into mods-unpacked\<mod_id>\... using @' ... '@ | Set-Content -Encoding UTF8.
  3. Delete $steamRoot\mod-hooks.zip.
  4. Package each modified mod into $downMods\<mod_id>.zip using System.IO.Compression.ZipArchive with **forward-slash (/) entry paths** ($entryName = $f.FullName.Substring($src.Length + 1).Replace("\", "/")). **NEVER** use [System.IO.Compression.ZipFile]::CreateFromDirectory() because Windows backslashes (\) break Godot es:// path resolution.
  5. Sync the repacked .zip into $steamMods\<mod_id>.zip if it is already installed (or if it is an active core mod) so changes actually take effect in-game!
  6. Stage, commit, and push to GitHub (git add -A, git commit -m "...", git push origin main).
* At the end of every deployment response, explicitly tell Gareth **what to test in-game** during his next play session.

---

## 3. Hard Godot 4.4.1 & ModLoader 7.0.1 Engine Contracts

### A. Manifest V3 Schema Validation (CRITICAL)
Every manifest.json (both outer and inner) MUST contain compatible_mod_loader_version and compatible_game_version inside xtra.godot:
~~~json
{
  "name": "example_mod",
  "namespace": "gareth",
  "version_number": "1.1.0",
  "description": "Mod description.",
  "website_url": "[https://github.com/garethwhite-prog/coal-llc-mods](https://github.com/garethwhite-prog/coal-llc-mods)",
  "dependencies": ["gareth-mod_manager_mod"],
  "extra": {
    "godot": {
      "authors": ["Gareth White"],
      "display_name": "Example Mod",
      "tags": ["qol"],
      "compatible_mod_loader_version": ["7.0.1"],
      "compatible_game_version": ["1.4.1.1"]
    }
  }
}
~~~
Omitting compatible_mod_loader_version causes ModLoader to reject the .zip at boot (Manifest validation failed) and cascade-disable every mod that lists it in dependencies.

### B. GDScript Object.get() vs. Dictionary.get()
* In Godot 4, Dictionary.get(key, default) accepts 2 arguments, and Object.get_meta(name, default) accepts 2 arguments.
* However, Object.get(property) accepts **strictly 1 argument**.
* Calling Gvars.bonus_equipment_manager.get("vacuum_level", 1) throws a fatal compile-time parse error:
  Parse Error: Too many arguments for "get()" call. Expected at most 1 but received 2.
* **Always use:**
~~~gdscript
var lvl: int = 1
if Gvars and ("bonus_equipment_manager" in Gvars) and Gvars.bonus_equipment_manager:
    if "vacuum_level" in Gvars.bonus_equipment_manager:
        lvl = int(Gvars.bonus_equipment_manager.vacuum_level)
~~~

### C. ModLoader Hook Targets Must Exist in Vanilla Script
* ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks(vanilla_script_path, hook_script_path) only hooks functions that explicitly exist in the vanilla .gd file.
* Example: es://scenes/equipment/vacuum_cleaner.gd has unc _physics_process(_delta: float) and unc _draw(), but **does NOT have unc _ready()**. Hooking _ready in acuum_cleaner.hooks.gd is silently ignored by ModLoader! Always hook _physics_process and use a metadata guard (ac.get_meta(...)) for one-time initialization.
* Any extra helper function placed inside a .hooks.gd file will cause ModLoader to log Skipped adding hook "_helper" (not found in vanilla script). Put helpers in separate classes/scripts when possible.

### D. Zipped Image & Audio Loading (load_png_from_buffer)
* Calling load("res://mods-unpacked/.../image.png") or .wav inside a zipped mod fails with ERROR: No loader found for resource because Godot 4 requires compiled .import files for ResourceLoader.load().
* **Always load custom PNGs via raw byte buffer:**
~~~gdscript
var bytes = FileAccess.get_file_as_bytes("res://mods-unpacked/mod_id/assets/icon.png")
if bytes and bytes.size() > 0:
    var img = Image.new()
    if img.load_png_from_buffer(bytes) == OK:
        var tex = ImageTexture.create_from_image(img)
~~~

### E. Scene Tree Safety (call_deferred & Tween Guards)
* Adding nodes while a parent is initializing throws ERROR: Parent node is busy setting up children, add_child() failed. Use call_deferred("add_child", node) when injecting UI or entities during _ready.
* Calling create_tween() without adding at least one 	ween_property or 	ween_callback throws ERROR: <Tween>: started with no Tweeners. Only create a tween inside the if is_instance_valid(target): block.

### F. Asynchronous Coroutines (await chain.execute_next)
* When hooking a vanilla Godot coroutine (such as Game.gd's change_stage), the hook function MUST use wait chain.execute_next(...). Failing to wait causes a SCRIPT ERROR: Trying to call an async function without "await" and breaks the stage transition, leaving the player outside the viewport.

---

# Known Regressions, Mistakes & Safeguards

This dossier tracks identified defects, the underlying engine root-cause, and regression assertions enforced by automated test runners.

## 1. ModRegistry Interface Contract
- **Symptom**: 5,000+ physics errors (Nonexistent function 'is_mod_active_for_run' in base 'GDScript').
- **Root Cause**: Omitting static func is_mod_active_for_run(mod_key: String) -> bool from mod_registry.gd.

## 2. Block Hit / Damage Desync
- **Symptom**: Blocks appear to take damage and instantly "heal" on the subsequent hit.
- **Root Cause**: Physics frame termination due to unhandled GDScript exceptions in player or chunk hooks.

## 3. In-Game Stage Gating
- **Symptom**: HUD widgets (Speed, Amazon) either permanently invisible or overlapping Main Menu.
- **Root Cause**: Assuming Gconsts.Stages._IN_GAME exists (vanilla enum uses _MINE_REGION = 2).

## 4. 0g Shop Item Division by Zero
- **Symptom**: Crash on purchase.
- **Root Cause**: Unhandled 0 item prices. Guarded by itemBuyPrice = max(1.0, val).

## 5. Engine Arity Limits (Object.get)
- **Symptom**: Fatal parse error Expected at most 1 but received 2 for get().
- **Root Cause**: Passing a default value to Object.get(). Dictionary.get() takes 2, Object.get() strictly takes 1.

## 6. Scene Tree Locks (add_child)
- **Symptom**: Parent node is busy setting up children, add_child() failed.
- **Root Cause**: Using dd_child() directly inside a _ready() hook while the node is still mounting. 
- **Safeguard**: Always use call_deferred("add_child", node) in setup phases.

## 7. Asynchronous Coroutines (change_stage)
- **Symptom**: Camera zooms out infinitely, player dumped out of viewport.
- **Root Cause**: Calling chain.execute_next() without wait on a vanilla function that uses coroutines.
- **Safeguard**: Any hook modifying Game.gd:change_stage must use wait chain.execute_next(...).

## 8. ModLoader File Locks
- **Symptom**: ModLoader aborts zip generation, silently disabling ALL mods.
- **Root Cause**: Godot background processes locking mod-hooks.zip or syntax parse errors blocking compilation.

## 9. Mod Store & Manager Visibility
- **Symptom**: New mods do not appear in the Mod Manager or Mod Store UI.
- **Root Cause**: Failing to append the new mod namespace to the mod_registry.gd within gareth-mod_manager_mod.

## 10. Missing Passives in Mod Settings
- **Symptom**: New passive upgrade paths do not appear in the 'Passive Order' tab.
- **Root Cause**: The AutoPassiveChooser UI binds to Gvars.profession_manager. The new passives must be added to the unlocked_passives array of the specific class in its _profession_effect script, not just injected into the global settings array.

## 11. Community Meta Tagging
- **Symptom**: Local, internally developed mods display as "- by Community" in the Mod Manager UI.
- **Root Cause**: The xtra.godot.authors field in manifest.json needs to explicitly read ["Gareth White"] and omit community namespaces to route correctly into the "Official Suite" visual category.

# Coal LLC Modding Discovery Archive & Technical Dossier

This archive preserves all empirical findings, AST discoveries, engine hooks, and memory layout investigations.

## Discovery Phase Index
- **Phase 01–15**: ModLoader 7.x lifecycle, manifest schemas, custom class registration.
- **Phase 16–25**: Subterranean strata, tilemap elemental loops (fire, water, poison).
- **Phase 26–30**: ModHookPacker compilation, script extensions vs hook chains.
- **Phase 31–35**: Viewport tracking, native end-of-day sequence, employee miner/collector routines.
- **Phase 36–40**: Elemental airstrike flight paths, inventory slot data structures, loot drop valuation.
- **Phase 41–43**: Division-by-zero 0g shop card bug, pre-run UI category decoupling, turret targeting.

## Key Engine Conventions
1. **Database / Stored Procedure Philosophy**: Zero inline mutations on core registries; use ModLoader Hook Chains exclusively.
2. **Tilemap Status**:
   - Wetness: \	ilemap.wet_tile_coords(cell)\ and \	ilemap.wetness.add_wet_tile(cell)\
   - Combustion: \	ilemap.burn_tile(pos, dmg, ticks)\ and \	ilemap.fires.add_fire_tile(cell, dmg, ticks)\
   - Poison: \	ilemap.poison_tile_coords(cell, dmg, ticks)\ and \	ilemap.poisons.add_poison_tile(cell, dmg, ticks)\
3. **Player Node Discovery**: Target \	ree.root.find_child("Player", true, false)\ rather than group lookups.

# The Abyssal Trench (`gareth-abyssal_trench_mod`)

**Author:** Gareth White  
**Map ID:** 104
**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Compatibility:** Coal LLC v1.4.1.1+ (ModLoader 7.0.1)

---

## Overview
**The Abyssal Trench** drops miners into an underwater oceanic chasm. Carve through deep twilight sapphire rock, navigate submerged cavern pockets, and ride hydrothermal black smoker steam geysers back to the surface.

---

## Key Features
* **Hydrothermal Vents (Black Smokers):** Volcanic chimneys rising from cavern floors blast continuous steam updrafts. Stepping into the steam column launches the miner upward for fast vertical ascent.
* **Stratified Ocean Depth:**
  * **Rows 0�12 (Surface Shallows):** Soft clay and starter coal deposits for quota safety.
  * **Rows 13�60 (Twilight Depths):** Dense blue shale (`BASIC_DARKBLUE`), rich in sapphire and moonstone deposits.
  * **Rows 61+ (Hadal Floor):** Volcanic basalt (`BASIC_DARK`), onyx clusters, and supercooled crystalline ice.

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-abyssal_trench_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-abyssal_trench_mod\mod_main.gd
==================================================
--------
extends Node

const MOD_ID := "gareth-abyssal_trench_mod"
const LOG_NAME := "gareth-abyssal_trench_mod"

func _init() -> void:
	ModLoaderLog.info("The Abyssal Trench Mod initializing...", LOG_NAME)
	install_hooks()

func _ready() -> void:
	var helper_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-abyssal_trench_mod/helpers/AbyssalTrenchHelper.gd")
	if helper_script:
		var helper = helper_script.new()
		if helper.has_method("ensure_level_registered"):
			helper.ensure_level_registered()
	ModLoaderLog.info("The Abyssal Trench Custom Map (ID 104) online!", LOG_NAME)

func install_hooks() -> void:
	var unpacked_dir = ModLoaderMod.get_unpacked_dir().path_join(MOD_ID)
	var ext_dir = unpacked_dir.path_join("extensions")

	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks(
		"res://scenes/Interfaces/Menus/level_select.gd",
		ext_dir.path_join("scenes/Interfaces/Menus/level_select.hooks.gd")
	)
	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks(
		"res://scenes/tilemaps/tile_map_chunk.gd",
		ext_dir.path_join("scenes/tilemaps/tile_map_chunk.hooks.gd")
	)
	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks(
		"res://scripts/Game.gd",
		ext_dir.path_join("scripts/Game.hooks.gd")
	)

# The Abyssal Trench (`gareth-abyssal_trench_mod`)

**Author:** Gareth White  
**Map ID:** 104
**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Compatibility:** Coal LLC v1.4.1.1+ (ModLoader 7.0.1)

---

## Overview
**The Abyssal Trench** drops miners into an underwater oceanic chasm. Carve through deep twilight sapphire rock, navigate submerged cavern pockets, and ride hydrothermal black smoker steam geysers back to the surface.

---

## Key Features
* **Hydrothermal Vents (Black Smokers):** Volcanic chimneys rising from cavern floors blast continuous steam updrafts. Stepping into the steam column launches the miner upward for fast vertical ascent.
* **Stratified Ocean Depth:**
  * **Rows 0�12 (Surface Shallows):** Soft clay and starter coal deposits for quota safety.
  * **Rows 13�60 (Twilight Depths):** Dense blue shale (`BASIC_DARKBLUE`), rich in sapphire and moonstone deposits.
  * **Rows 61+ (Hadal Floor):** Volcanic basalt (`BASIC_DARK`), onyx clusters, and supercooled crystalline ice.

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-abyssal_trench_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-abyssal_trench_mod\entities\HydrothermalVent.gd
==================================================
--------
class_name HydrothermalVent
extends Node2D

var steam_particles: CPUParticles2D
var launch_cooldown: float = 0.0

func _ready() -> void:
	z_index = 5
	
	# Basalt Chimney
	var chimney = ColorRect.new()
	chimney.color = Color(0.10, 0.12, 0.16)
	chimney.size = Vector2(16, 24)
	chimney.position = Vector2(-8, -24)
	add_child(chimney)

	var vent_rim = ColorRect.new()
	vent_rim.color = Color(0.18, 0.22, 0.30)
	vent_rim.size = Vector2(22, 6)
	vent_rim.position = Vector2(-11, -26)
	add_child(vent_rim)

	# Upward Hydrothermal Steam Column
	steam_particles = CPUParticles2D.new()
	steam_particles.position = Vector2(0, -26)
	steam_particles.amount = 32
	steam_particles.lifetime = 1.8
	steam_particles.direction = Vector2(0, -1)
	steam_particles.spread = 10.0
	steam_particles.gravity = Vector2(0, -160)
	steam_particles.initial_velocity_min = 80.0
	steam_particles.initial_velocity_max = 150.0
	steam_particles.scale_amount_min = 3.0
	steam_particles.scale_amount_max = 6.0
	steam_particles.color = Color(0.70, 0.90, 1.0, 0.65)
	add_child(steam_particles)

func _physics_process(delta: float) -> void:
	if launch_cooldown > 0.0:
		launch_cooldown -= delta

	var players = get_tree().get_nodes_in_group("player")
	if players.is_empty():
		return

	var p = players[0]
	if not is_instance_valid(p):
		return

	# Launch zone: 28px wide, extends 120px vertically above chimney
	var diff = p.global_position - global_position
	if abs(diff.x) < 18.0 and diff.y < -5.0 and diff.y > -120.0:
		if launch_cooldown <= 0.0 and "velocity" in p:
			p.velocity.y = -720.0
			launch_cooldown = 0.30

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-abyssal_trench_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-abyssal_trench_mod\extensions\scenes\Interfaces\Menus\level_select.hooks.gd

extends Object

func _ready(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	var helper_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-abyssal_trench_mod/helpers/AbyssalTrenchHelper.gd")
	if helper_script:
		var helper = helper_script.new()
		if helper.has_method("ensure_level_registered"):
			helper.ensure_level_registered()

	var ls = chain.reference_object
	if ls and ("LEVEL_ORDER" in ls):
		var tid: int = 104
		if not ls.LEVEL_ORDER.has(tid):
			ls.LEVEL_ORDER.append(tid)

	chain.execute_next([])

==================================================
FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-abyssal_trench_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-abyssal_trench_mod\extensions\scenes\tilemaps\tile_map_chunk.hooks.gd
==================================================
--------
extends Object

func lvl_from_global_pos(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, pos: Vector2i) -> int:
	var helper_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-abyssal_trench_mod/helpers/AbyssalTrenchHelper.gd")
	if helper_script and helper_script.is_abyssal_level():
		if pos.y < 0:
			return -2
		return 1
	return chain.execute_next([pos])

func generate_chunk(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, noise: Noise) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([noise])
	var helper_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-abyssal_trench_mod/helpers/AbyssalTrenchHelper.gd")
	if helper_script and helper_script.is_abyssal_level():
		var chunk = chain.reference_object
		if chunk:
			helper_script.generate_abyssal_strata(chunk, noise)

func damage_tile_idx(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, idx: int, damage: float) -> void:
	var helper_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-abyssal_trench_mod/helpers/AbyssalTrenchHelper.gd")
	if helper_script and helper_script.is_abyssal_level():
		var chunk = chain.reference_object
		if chunk and ("tiles" in chunk) and ("TILE_PROPERTIES" in chunk):
			if idx >= 0 and idx < chunk.tiles.size():
				var t_id = chunk.tiles[idx]
				if not chunk.TILE_PROPERTIES.has(t_id):
					var fallback_id = 211 if chunk.TILE_PROPERTIES.has(211) else (24 if chunk.TILE_PROPERTIES.has(24) else 41)
					if chunk.TILE_PROPERTIES.has(fallback_id):
						chunk.TILE_PROPERTIES[t_id] = chunk.TILE_PROPERTIES[fallback_id].duplicate(true)
		# Enforce starter pickaxe damage floor for Hadal stone carving
		damage = max(damage * 6.0, 35.0)

	chain.execute_next([idx, damage])

func destroy_tile(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, idx: int, tile_properties: Dictionary) -> void:
	var helper_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-abyssal_trench_mod/helpers/AbyssalTrenchHelper.gd")
	if helper_script and helper_script.is_abyssal_level():
		var chunk = chain.reference_object
		if chunk and ("tiles" in chunk) and idx >= 0 and idx < chunk.tiles.size():
			var t_id = chunk.tiles[idx]
			# 50% bonus coal nugget drop for filler ocean stone
			if (t_id == 211 or t_id == 92) and randf() < 0.50:
				if "coal_resource" in chunk and chunk.coal_resource:
					if chunk.has_method("spawn_item_drop"):
						var local_coords = Vector2i(idx % chunk.chunk_size, idx / chunk.chunk_size)
						chunk.spawn_item_drop(chunk.coal_resource, chunk.map_to_local(local_coords))

	# Strictly preserve 2-argument signature: (idx: int, tile_properties: Dictionary)
	chain.execute_next([idx, tile_properties])

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-abyssal_trench_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-abyssal_trench_mod\extensions\scripts\Game.hooks.gd

extends Object

func change_stage(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, target: int, targetPosition: Vector2) -> void:
	var game = chain.reference_object
	if target == 2 and Gvars and ("level" in Gvars) and Gvars.level == 104:
		var mine_scene = load("res://scenes/Stages/MineRegion.tscn")
		if mine_scene and game:
			game.target_stage = mine_scene

	await chain.execute_next([target, targetPosition])

==================================================
FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-abyssal_trench_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-abyssal_trench_mod\helpers\AbyssalTrenchHelper.gd
==================================================
--------
class_name AbyssalTrenchHelper
extends RefCounted

const TRENCH_LEVEL_ID: int = 104
const PREVIEW_PATH: String = "res://mods-unpacked/gareth-abyssal_trench_mod/assets/abyssal_preview.png"
const BUFFER_SURFACE_ROWS: int = 12

static func is_abyssal_level() -> bool:
	if Gvars:
		if "level" in Gvars and Gvars.level == TRENCH_LEVEL_ID:
			return true
		if "chosen_level" in Gvars and Gvars.chosen_level == TRENCH_LEVEL_ID:
			return true
		if "current_level" in Gvars and Gvars.current_level == TRENCH_LEVEL_ID:
			return true
	return false

func ensure_level_registered() -> void:
	register_trench_level()

static func register_trench_level() -> void:
	if not Gvars:
		return

	if "level_manager" in Gvars and Gvars.level_manager and ("all_levels" in Gvars.level_manager):
		var lvl_mgr = Gvars.level_manager
		if not lvl_mgr.all_levels.has(TRENCH_LEVEL_ID):
			var custom_level = GameLevel.new()
			custom_level.level_name = "The Abyssal Trench"
			custom_level.level = TRENCH_LEVEL_ID
			custom_level.mining_scene_path = "res://scenes/Stages/MineRegion.tscn"
			custom_level.level_description = "Subterranean ocean trench.\n\nDeep sapphire strata, submerged cavern pockets, and hydrothermal black smoker steam geysers."
			custom_level.level_difficulty = GameLevel.Difficulty.HARD
			custom_level.unlock_description = ""

			var bytes = FileAccess.get_file_as_bytes(PREVIEW_PATH)
			if bytes.size() > 0:
				var img = Image.new()
				if img.load_png_from_buffer(bytes) == OK:
					custom_level.level_image = ImageTexture.create_from_image(img)

			lvl_mgr.all_levels[TRENCH_LEVEL_ID] = {
				"level": custom_level,
				"is_unlocked": true
			}

	if "level_info" in Gvars and Gvars.level_info is Dictionary:
		if not Gvars.level_info.has(TRENCH_LEVEL_ID):
			Gvars.level_info[TRENCH_LEVEL_ID] = {
				"name": "The Abyssal Trench",
				"description": "Subterranean ocean trench with hydrothermal vents and sapphire strata.",
				"depth": 1500,
				"cost": 0,
				"unlocked": true
			}

static func generate_abyssal_strata(chunk: Node, noise: Noise) -> void:
	if not ("tiles" in chunk) or not chunk.has_method("array_to_global_pos"):
		return

	var vent_spawned: bool = false

	for idx in range(chunk.tiles.size()):
		var global_pos: Vector2i = Vector2i(chunk.array_to_global_pos(idx))

		# 1. Surface Shallows (Rows 0-12): Soft clay / starter coal buffer
		if global_pos.y <= BUFFER_SURFACE_ROWS:
			continue

		var local_x = idx % chunk.chunk_size
		var local_y = idx / chunk.chunk_size

		# 2. Submerged Ocean Trench Caverns
		var cave_noise: float = abs(noise.get_noise_2d(float(global_pos.x) * 1.8, float(global_pos.y) * 1.4))
		if cave_noise < 0.11 and global_pos.y > 16:
			chunk.tiles[idx] = chunk.Tiles.EMPTY
			chunk.build_tile(idx)

			# Spawn Hydrothermal Black Smoker on cavern floor
			if not vent_spawned and global_pos.y >= 24 and local_y < chunk.chunk_size - 2:
				var below_idx = idx + chunk.chunk_size
				if below_idx < chunk.tiles.size() and chunk.tiles[below_idx] != chunk.Tiles.EMPTY:
					var VentScene = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-abyssal_trench_mod/entities/HydrothermalVent.gd")
					if VentScene:
						var vent = VentScene.new()
						vent.position = chunk.map_to_local(Vector2i(local_x, local_y + 1))
						chunk.add_child(vent)
						vent_spawned = true
			continue

		# 3. High-Value Mineral Veins (Only verified tiles: 211, 212, 215, 92, 93)
		var vein_noise: float = noise.get_noise_2d(float(global_pos.x) * 3.5, float(global_pos.y) * 3.5)
		var gem_noise: float  = noise.get_noise_2d(float(global_pos.x) * 5.0 + 80.0, float(global_pos.y) * 5.0 + 40.0)

		var tile_to_set: int = 211 # BASIC_DARKBLUE

		if global_pos.y <= 60:
			# Twilight Zone: 45% Coal, 30% Sapphire, 25% Ocean Stone
			if vein_noise > 0.05:
				tile_to_set = 212 # COAL_DARKBLUE (Drops Coal for Quota)
			elif gem_noise > 0.15:
				tile_to_set = 215 # SAPPHIRE_DARKBLUE (Drops Sapphires for Cash)
			else:
				tile_to_set = 211 # BASIC_DARKBLUE (Ocean bedrock)
		else:
			# Hadal Trench: 40% Coal, 35% Gems/Onyx, 25% Basalt
			if vein_noise > 0.10:
				tile_to_set = 93  # COAL_DARK (Drops Coal)
			elif gem_noise > 0.10:
				tile_to_set = 215 # SAPPHIRE_DARKBLUE (Deep trench gem pockets)
			else:
				tile_to_set = 92  # BASIC_DARK (Volcanic basalt)

		chunk.tiles[idx] = tile_to_set
		chunk.build_tile(idx)

extends Object

func damageTileCoords(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, coords: Vector2i, damage: float) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([coords, damage])
	var tm = chain.reference_object
	if not is_instance_valid(tm):
		return

	# Center column check (X = 0)
	if coords.x == 0:
		if tm.has_method("place_ladder"):
			tm.place_ladder(coords)
		elif "ladders" in tm and tm.ladders and tm.ladders.has_method("add_ladder"):
			tm.ladders.add_ladder(coords)

func _load_image_texture(path: String) -> ImageTexture:
	if not FileAccess.file_exists(path):
		return null
	var img = Image.load_from_file(path)
	if not img:
		return null
	return ImageTexture.create_from_image(img)

func _ready() -> void:
	_register_weapon_tiers()
	_register_profession()

func _register_weapon_tiers() -> void:
	var item_script = load("res://resources/Items/Scripts/Item.gd")
	var buy_script = load("res://resources/BuyEffects/Scripts/AddToInventory.gd")
	var pickup_script = load("res://resources/PickupEffects/Scripts/PickupToInventory.gd")
	var equip_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-bouncing_ball_mod/scripts/bouncing_ball_equip.gd")
	var pickup_sound = load("res://assets/sound/sound effects/SwordThud.wav")

	var item_unlocks = load("res://resources/unlockable_items/scripts/item_unlocks.tres")

	for t in TIER_DATA:
		var item = item_script.new()
		item.itemID = "bouncing_ball_" + t["id"]
		item.itemName = t["name"]
		item.itemDescription = "Chaotic kinetic sphere ordnance.\nRebounds off cavern walls before detonating.\nDamage: %d | Blast Radius: %.1f tiles" % [int(t["dmg"]), float(t["rad"])]

		var tex_path := "res://mods-unpacked/gareth-bouncing_ball_mod/textures/ball_%s.png" % t["id"]
		item.itemTexture = _load_image_texture(tex_path)

		var equip = equip_script.new()
		equip.damage = t["dmg"]
		equip.radius = t["rad"]
		item.itemEquipEffect = equip

		var buy = buy_script.new()
		item.itemBuyEffect = buy
		var pickup = pickup_script.new()
		item.itemPickupEffect = pickup
		item.pickupSound = pickup_sound

		item.itemBuyPrice = t["price"]
		item.itemSellPrice = t["price"] * 0.5
		item.itemUnlocked = true
		item.itemType = "bouncing_ball"

		all_ball_items.append(item)

		if item_unlocks:
			if not (item in item_unlocks.all_equipment_items):
				item_unlocks.all_equipment_items.append(item)

	if all_ball_items.size() > 0:
		starter_weapon = all_ball_items[0]

	ModLoaderLog.info("15 Bouncing Ball tiers registered into equipment shop.", MOD_DIR)

func _register_profession() -> void:
	if not Gvars or not ("profession_manager" in Gvars) or not Gvars.profession_manager:
		return
	var pm = Gvars.profession_manager
	var prof_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-bouncing_ball_mod/scripts/bouncing_ball_profession.gd")
	var p = prof_script.new()
	p.profession_name = "Bouncing Ball"
	p.description = "Specialist in chaotic kinetic sphere ordnance.\nBouncy balls rebound off solid cavern walls before detonating.\nStarts with the Shoddy Bouncing Ball."

	var icon_path := "res://mods-unpacked/gareth-bouncing_ball_mod/textures/bouncing_ball_icon.png"
	var icon_tex = _load_image_texture(icon_path)
	if icon_tex:
		p.image = icon_tex

	pm.all_professions[103] = { "profession": p, "is_unlocked": true }
	ModLoaderLog.info("Bouncing Ball Class (ID 103) registered cleanly.", MOD_DIR)

class_name BouncingBallEquipEffect
extends EquipEffect

@export var damage: float = 95.0
@export var radius: float = 3.0

var weapon_visual: Sprite2D = null
var throw_cooldown: float = 0.0
var _is_using: bool = false
var _auto_throw_toggle: bool = false
var held_item = null

func start(player, item):
	held_item = item
	if "tool" in player and player.tool:
		player.tool.visible = false

	weapon_visual = Sprite2D.new()
	weapon_visual.texture = item.itemTexture
	weapon_visual.offset = Vector2(0, -4)
	weapon_visual.position = Vector2(4, 2)
	weapon_visual.visible = true
	player.add_child(weapon_visual)
	_is_using = false
	_auto_throw_toggle = false

func end(player):
	if "tool" in player and player.tool:
		player.tool.visible = true
	if weapon_visual and is_instance_valid(weapon_visual):
		weapon_visual.queue_free()
	_is_using = false
	_auto_throw_toggle = false

func physics_process(player, delta):
	if throw_cooldown > 0.0:
		throw_cooldown -= delta

	if weapon_visual and is_instance_valid(weapon_visual):
		var p_pos: Vector2 = player.global_position
		var pointer_pos: Vector2 = player.pointer_global_pos
		var angle: float = (pointer_pos - p_pos).angle()
		weapon_visual.rotation = angle
		weapon_visual.flip_v = (pointer_pos.x < p_pos.x)

	if Input.is_action_pressed("useItem") or Input.is_mouse_button_pressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT):
		_is_using = true
	elif Input.is_action_just_released("useItem") or (not Input.is_mouse_button_pressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT) and not Input.is_mouse_button_pressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_RIGHT) and not _auto_throw_toggle):
		_is_using = false

	if Input.is_action_just_pressed("toggle_use_item") or Input.is_action_just_pressed("rightMouseClick"):
		_auto_throw_toggle = not _auto_throw_toggle
		_is_using = _auto_throw_toggle

	if Input.is_mouse_button_pressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_RIGHT):
		_is_using = true

	if _is_using and throw_cooldown <= 0.0:
		_throw_bouncing_ball(player)

func _get_passive_stat(stat_name: String) -> float:
	if not Gvars or not ("passives" in Gvars) or not Gvars.passives:
		return 0.0
	var p = Gvars.passives
	if p.has_meta(stat_name):
		return float(p.get_meta(stat_name))
	if stat_name in p:
		return float(p.get(stat_name))
	return 0.0

func _throw_bouncing_ball(player) -> void:
	var fire_rate_mult: float = 1.0 + _get_passive_stat("ball_fire_rate")
	throw_cooldown = max(0.10, 0.44 / fire_rate_mult)

	var p_pos: Vector2 = player.global_position
	var pointer_pos: Vector2 = player.pointer_global_pos
	var dir: Vector2 = (pointer_pos - p_pos).normalized()

	if weapon_visual and is_instance_valid(weapon_visual):
		var orig_pos = weapon_visual.position
		weapon_visual.position -= dir * 3.0
		var tween = player.create_tween()
		tween.tween_property(weapon_visual, "position", orig_pos, 0.08)

	var proj_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-bouncing_ball_mod/scripts/bouncing_ball_projectile.gd")
	var proj := CharacterBody2D.new()
	proj.set_script(proj_script)

	var dmg_mult: float = 1.0 + _get_passive_stat("ball_blast_damage")
	proj.damage = damage * dmg_mult

	var rad_mult: float = 1.0 + _get_passive_stat("ball_blast_radius")
	proj.radius = radius * rad_mult

	if "tilemap" in player:
		proj.tilemap = player.tilemap

	if held_item and "itemTexture" in held_item:
		proj.ball_texture = held_item.itemTexture

	proj.global_position = p_pos + (dir * 14.0)
	proj.velocity = Vector2(dir.x * 460.0, min(-140.0, dir.y * 460.0 - 110.0))

	var parent = player.get_parent()
	if parent:
		parent.add_child(proj)
	else:
		player.add_child(proj)

class_name BouncingBallProfession
extends Profession

func _profession_effect(data: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	var mod_node = Engine.get_main_loop().root.get_node_or_null("ModLoader/gareth-bouncing_ball_mod")
	if mod_node and "starter_weapon" in mod_node and mod_node.starter_weapon:
		if data.has("inventory_starters"):
			data["inventory_starters"] = [{
				"item": mod_node.starter_weapon,
				"count": 1
			}]

	if data.has("unlocked_passives"):
		data["unlocked_passives"] = [
			"ball_blast_damage",
			"ball_blast_radius",
			"ball_fire_rate",
			"player_sprint_speed",
			"player_jump_velocity",
			"player_climb_speed",
			"ball_blast_damage",
			"ball_blast_radius",
			"ball_fire_rate"
		]

	return data

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-bouncing_ball_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-bouncing_ball_mod\scripts\bouncing_ball_projectile.gd
==================================================
--------
class_name BouncingBallProjectile
extends CharacterBody2D

const GRAVITY: float = 620.0
const BOUNCE_DAMPING: float = 0.76
var base_damage: float = 95.0

var damage: float:
	get: return base_damage
	set(val): base_damage = val

var radius: float = 16.0
var tilemap: Node = null
var ball_texture: Texture2D = null

var _bounces_left: int = 4
var _total_bounces_made: int = 0
var _sprite: Sprite2D = null
var _fuse_timer: float = 1.4

func _ready() -> void:
	z_index = 35
	_sprite = Sprite2D.new()
	_sprite.name = "Sprite2D"
	if ball_texture:
		_sprite.texture = ball_texture
	call_deferred("add_child", _sprite)
	
	var circle := CircleShape2D.new()
	circle.radius = 7.0
	var shape := CollisionShape2D.new()
	shape.shape = circle
	
	# Explicitly set Godot 2D collision masks to interact with the TileMap
	collision_layer = 0
	collision_mask = 1 
	
	call_deferred("add_child", shape)

func setup(pos: Vector2, initial_vel: Vector2, dmg: float, tex: Texture2D) -> void:
	global_position = pos
	velocity = initial_vel
	base_damage = dmg
	ball_texture = tex
	if _sprite and tex:
		_sprite.texture = tex

func _physics_process(delta: float) -> void:
	velocity.y += GRAVITY * delta
	if _sprite:
		_sprite.rotation += velocity.x * delta * 0.08
		
	_fuse_timer -= delta
	if _fuse_timer <= 0.0:
		_detonate_final(global_position, base_damage)
		queue_free()
		return

	var collision := move_and_collide(velocity * delta)
	if collision:
		_bounces_left -= 1
		_total_bounces_made += 1
		velocity = velocity.bounce(collision.get_normal()) * BOUNCE_DAMPING

		var mult_bounce: float = 1.0 + (_total_bounces_made * 0.65)
		var mult_velocity: float = clampf(velocity.length() / 260.0, 0.8, 3.5)
		var mult_passives: float = 1.0
		if Gvars and ("passives" in Gvars) and Gvars.passives:
			var p = Gvars.passives
			var pick_dmg = float(p.pickaxe_damage) if ("pickaxe_damage" in p) else 0.0
			var ball_dmg = float(p.get("bouncing_ball_damage")) if ("bouncing_ball_damage" in p) else 0.0
			mult_passives = (1.0 + pick_dmg) * (1.0 + ball_dmg)

		var final_damage: float = base_damage * mult_bounce * mult_velocity * mult_passives
		var hit_pos = collision.get_position() - (collision.get_normal() * 4.0)
		_damage_at_pos(hit_pos, final_damage * 0.45)

		if _bounces_left <= 0 or velocity.length() < 25.0:
			_detonate_final(hit_pos, final_damage)
			queue_free()

func _damage_at_pos(pos: Vector2, dmg: float) -> void:
	var tm = tilemap
	if not is_instance_valid(tm):
		var tree = get_tree()
		if tree and tree.root:
			tm = tree.root.find_child("TileMapManager", true, false)
	if tm and tm.has_method("damageTileCoords"):
		var cell = tm.local_to_map(pos)
		tm.damageTileCoords(cell, float(dmg))

func _detonate_final(pos: Vector2, dmg: float) -> void:
	call_deferred("_spawn_blast_shockwave")
	var tm = tilemap
	if not is_instance_valid(tm):
		var tree = get_tree()
		if tree and tree.root:
			tm = tree.root.find_child("TileMapManager", true, false)
	if tm and tm.has_method("damageTileCoords"):
		var center_cell = tm.local_to_map(pos)
		var r_int: int = int(ceil(radius))
		for dx in range(-r_int, r_int + 1):
			for dy in range(-r_int, r_int + 1):
				if Vector2(dx, dy).length() <= radius:
					tm.damageTileCoords(center_cell + Vector2i(dx, dy), float(dmg))

func _spawn_blast_shockwave() -> void:
	var ring := Line2D.new()
	ring.width = 3.0
	ring.default_color = Color(1.0, 0.65, 0.15, 0.95)
	var segs: int = 16
	for i in range(segs + 1):
		var ang: float = float(i) * TAU / float(segs)
		ring.add_point(global_position + Vector2(cos(ang), sin(ang)) * 5.0)
	
	var parent = get_parent()
	if parent:
		parent.add_child(ring)
		var tween = parent.create_tween()
		if tween:
			tween.set_parallel(true)
			tween.tween_property(ring, "scale", Vector2(3.5, 3.5), 0.16)
			tween.tween_property(ring, "modulate:a", 0.0, 0.16)
			tween.set_parallel(false)
			tween.tween_callback(ring.queue_free)

# Buyable Vacuums & Strata Extensions

**Namespace:** $namespace  
**Compatibility:** Coal LLC v1.4.1.1+ (ModLoader 7.0.1)

---

## Overview
Makes Tabbitha's vacuum upgrades purchasable directly from the Bonus shelf with cash in addition to quest rewards, and adds Levels 5 to 8 for hose reach up to 600 tiles.

---

## Technical Details (For Modders)
Injects vacuum levels 1-8 into item_unlocks.tres and hooks equipment_manager.gd to scale suction physics bodies to deep strata.

---

## Installation
1. Ensure **Coal LLC** has Godot ModLoader 7.0.1 installed.
2. Place the packaged .zip file into your Coal LLC/mods/ directory.
3. Manage or toggle this mod directly via the in-game **Mod Manager**.

extends Node

const MOD_DIR := "gareth-buyable_vacuums_mod"

func _init() -> void:
	var mod_dir_path: String = ModLoaderMod.get_unpacked_dir().path_join(MOD_DIR)
	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks(
		"res://scripts/equipment_manager.gd",
		mod_dir_path.path_join("extensions/scripts/equipment_manager.hooks.gd")
	)

func _ready() -> void:
	var item_unlocks = load("res://resources/unlockable_items/scripts/item_unlocks.tres")
	if not item_unlocks:
		return

	# Unlock vanilla Vacuums 1 to 4 in shop
	for i in range(1, 5):
		var v = load("res://resources/Items/BonusEffects/bonus_equipment/vacuum_cleaner_%d.tres" % i)
		if v:
			v.itemUnlocked = true
			if not v in item_unlocks.all_bonus_items:
				item_unlocks.all_bonus_items.append(v)
			if not v in item_unlocks.all_items:
				item_unlocks.all_items.append(v)

	# Insert Vacuums 5 to 8 immediately after Vacuum 4
	var target_idx = -1
	for i in range(item_unlocks.all_bonus_items.size()):
		if item_unlocks.all_bonus_items[i] and item_unlocks.all_bonus_items[i].itemID == "vacuum_cleaner_4":
			target_idx = i
			break

	var ext_items: Array[Item] = []
	for lvl in range(5, 9):
		var item = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-buyable_vacuums_mod/items/vacuum_cleaner_%d.tres" % lvl)
		if item:
			ext_items.append(item)
			if not item in item_unlocks.all_items:
				item_unlocks.all_items.append(item)

	if target_idx != -1:
		for offset in range(ext_items.size()):
			item_unlocks.all_bonus_items.insert(target_idx + 1 + offset, ext_items[offset])
	else:
		for item in ext_items:
			item_unlocks.all_bonus_items.append(item)

func generate_vacuum_cleaner(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([])
	var mgr = chain.reference_object
	if not mgr or not mgr.bem:
		return
	var cur_lvl = int(mgr.bem.current_vacuum)
	var target_len = 0
	match cur_lvl:
		5: target_len = 220 * 16
		6: target_len = 300 * 16
		7: target_len = 420 * 16
		8: target_len = 600 * 16
		_: target_len = 0

	if target_len > 0:
		for child in mgr.get_children():
			if child is VacuumCleaner:
				child.vacuum_length = target_len
				ModLoaderLog.info("Upgraded vacuum tether length to %d px (%d tiles)." % [target_len, target_len / 16], "gareth-buyable_vacuums_mod")

# Chest Tier Variance & Trap Explosions Mod

Introduces roguelite risk/reward dynamics to subterranean chests in Coal LLC:
- **Tier Variance:** Opening a chest rolls a weighted tier shift (+/- 1 tier: 15% chance; +/- 2 tiers: 5% jackpot/dud chance).
- **Class Weapon Seeding:** Injects custom class weapons (Knight Broadswords, Jedi Energy Sabers, custom tanks) into the power-up scroll pool.
- **Bonus Percentage Scaling:** Passive upgrade scrolls dynamically scale their bonus percentages to match the shifted tier level.
- **Booby-Trap Explosions:** 3% chance a chest triggers a kinetic shockwave upon opening, shattering nearby rock.

--------
# Chest Tier Variance & Trap Explosions Mod

Introduces roguelite risk/reward dynamics to subterranean chests in Coal LLC:
- **Tier Variance:** Opening a chest rolls a weighted tier shift (+/- 1 tier: 15% chance; +/- 2 tiers: 5% jackpot/dud chance).
- **Class Weapon Seeding:** Injects custom class weapons (Knight Broadswords, Jedi Energy Sabers, custom tanks) into the power-up scroll pool.
- **Bonus Percentage Scaling:** Passive upgrade scrolls dynamically scale their bonus percentages to match the shifted tier level.
- **Booby-Trap Explosions:** 3% chance a chest triggers a kinetic shockwave upon opening, shattering nearby rock.

--------
==================================================
FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-chest_variance_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-chest_variance_mod\extensions\scripts\Chest.hooks.gd
==================================================
