extends Object

const MOD_DIR := "gareth-chest_variance_mod"

func roll_loot(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	var chest = chain.reference_object
	if not is_instance_valid(chest):
		chain.execute_next([])
		return

	# 3% chance of a booby-trap explosion
	if randf() < 0.03:
		ModLoaderLog.info("[Chest Variance] *** BOOBY TRAP DETONATED *** at %s" % str(chest.global_position), MOD_DIR)
		_trigger_trap_explosion(chest)
		return

	# Tier variance roll:
	# -2 tiers: 5% | -1 tier: 15% | 0 (base): 60% | +1 tier: 15% | +2 tiers: 5%
	var roll: float = randf()
	var tier_shift: int = 0
	if roll < 0.05:
		tier_shift = -2
	elif roll < 0.20:
		tier_shift = -1
	elif roll > 0.95:
		tier_shift = 2
	elif roll > 0.80:
		tier_shift = 1

	var original_level: int = chest.loot_level
	var effective_level: int = clampi(original_level + tier_shift, 1, 15)
	chest.loot_level = effective_level

	chain.execute_next([])

	# Scale passive upgrade multipliers to match effective tier
	if is_instance_valid(chest.loot) and chest.loot.itemType == "passive_upgrade":
		if chest.loot.itemPickupEffect and ("multiplier" in chest.loot.itemPickupEffect):
			if effective_level <= 4:
				chest.loot.itemPickupEffect.multiplier = float(effective_level)
			else:
				chest.loot.itemPickupEffect.multiplier = float(effective_level ** 2)

	# Seed custom class weapons into power-up scroll drops
	var custom_weapon_seeded := false
	var item_unlocks = load("res://resources/unlockable_items/scripts/item_unlocks.tres")
	if item_unlocks and is_instance_valid(chest.loot) and chest.loot.itemType == "scroll":
		var candidates: Array = []
		for it in item_unlocks.all_equipment_items:
			if is_instance_valid(it) and it.itemType in ["sword", "jedi_saber", "tank_fire", "tank_poison", "tank_uranium"]:
				candidates.append(it)
		if candidates.size() > 0 and randf() < 0.35:
			var custom_weapon = candidates.pick_random()
			chest.loot = chest.generate_power_up_scroll_for_item(custom_weapon)
			custom_weapon_seeded = true

	var loot_name: String = chest.loot.itemName if is_instance_valid(chest.loot) else "Unknown"
	ModLoaderLog.info("[Chest Variance] Stratum Tier: %d -> Rolled Tier: %d (Shift: %+d) | Loot: %s%s" % [
		original_level,
		effective_level,
		tier_shift,
		loot_name,
		" [CUSTOM WEAPON SCROLL]" if custom_weapon_seeded else ""
	], MOD_DIR)

	chest.loot_level = original_level

func _trigger_trap_explosion(chest: Node2D) -> void:
	chest.looted = true
	chest.opened = true
	if "sprite_empty" in chest and is_instance_valid(chest.sprite_empty):
		chest.sprite_empty.visible = true
	if "sprite_closed" in chest and is_instance_valid(chest.sprite_closed):
		chest.sprite_closed.visible = false
	if "sprite_open" in chest and is_instance_valid(chest.sprite_open):
		chest.sprite_open.visible = false

	var tree = chest.get_tree()
	if not tree:
		return

	var tilemaps = tree.get_nodes_in_group("tilemap")
	var tilemap = tilemaps[0] if tilemaps.size() > 0 else null

	var center_pos: Vector2 = chest.global_position
	if tilemap and tilemap.has_method("damageTileCoords"):
		var center_cell: Vector2i = tilemap.local_to_map(tilemap.to_local(center_pos))
		for dx in range(-3, 4):
			for dy in range(-3, 4):
				if dx*dx + dy*dy <= 9:
					var cell = center_cell + Vector2i(dx, dy)
					tilemap.damageTileCoords(cell, 350.0)

	var boom_audio = AudioStreamPlayer2D.new()
	boom_audio.stream = load("res://assets/sound/sound effects/box_1.wav")
	boom_audio.global_position = center_pos
	tree.current_scene.add_child(boom_audio)
	boom_audio.play()
	boom_audio.finished.connect(boom_audio.queue_free)

func pickup_item(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	var chest = chain.reference_object
	if not is_instance_valid(chest) or not ("loot" in chest) or chest.loot == null:
		return
	chain.execute_next([])

func _load_image_texture(path: String) -> ImageTexture:
	if not FileAccess.file_exists(path):
		return null
	var img = Image.load_from_file(path)
	if not img:
		return null
	return ImageTexture.create_from_image(img)

func _ready() -> void:
	_register_weapon_tiers()
	_register_profession()

func _register_weapon_tiers() -> void:
	var item_script = load("res://resources/Items/Scripts/Item.gd")
	var buy_script = load("res://resources/BuyEffects/Scripts/AddToInventory.gd")
	var pickup_script = load("res://resources/PickupEffects/Scripts/PickupToInventory.gd")
	var equip_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-chrono_industrialist_mod/scripts/chrono_industrialist_equip.gd")
	var pickup_sound = load("res://assets/sound/sound effects/SwordThud.wav")

	var item_unlocks = load("res://resources/unlockable_items/scripts/item_unlocks.tres")

	for t in TIER_DATA:
		var item = item_script.new()
		item.itemID = "chrono_drill_" + t["id"]
		item.itemName = t["name"]
		item.itemDescription = "Temporal excavation harvester.\nHigh-speed chronometric strikes trigger Stasis Shockwaves every 10th hit.\nDamage: %d | Strike Cycle: %.2fs" % [int(t["dmg"]), float(t["rate"])]

		var tex_path := "res://mods-unpacked/gareth-chrono_industrialist_mod/textures/chrono_drill_%s.png" % t["id"]
		item.itemTexture = _load_image_texture(tex_path)

		var equip = equip_script.new()
		equip.damage = t["dmg"]
		equip.strike_rate = t["rate"]
		item.itemEquipEffect = equip

		var buy = buy_script.new()
		item.itemBuyEffect = buy
		var pickup = pickup_script.new()
		item.itemPickupEffect = pickup
		item.pickupSound = pickup_sound

		item.itemBuyPrice = t["price"]
		item.itemSellPrice = t["price"] * 0.5
		item.itemUnlocked = true
		item.itemType = "chrono_drill"

		all_chrono_items.append(item)

		if item_unlocks:
			if not (item in item_unlocks.all_equipment_items):
				item_unlocks.all_equipment_items.append(item)

	if all_chrono_items.size() > 0:
		starter_weapon = all_chrono_items[0]

	ModLoaderLog.info("15 Chrono-Industrialist Harvester tiers registered into equipment shop.", MOD_DIR)

func _register_profession() -> void:
	if not Gvars or not ("profession_manager" in Gvars) or not Gvars.profession_manager:
		return
	var pm = Gvars.profession_manager
	var prof_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-chrono_industrialist_mod/scripts/chrono_industrialist_profession.gd")
	var p = prof_script.new()
	p.profession_name = "Chrono-Industrialist"
	p.description = "Temporal excavation engineer.\nHigh-speed chronometric strikes trigger Stasis Shockwaves every 10th hit.\nStarts with the Shoddy Chrono Harvester."

	var icon_path := "res://mods-unpacked/gareth-chrono_industrialist_mod/textures/chrono_industrialist_icon.png"
	var icon_tex = _load_image_texture(icon_path)
	if icon_tex:
		p.image = icon_tex

	pm.all_professions[107] = { "profession": p, "is_unlocked": true }
	ModLoaderLog.info("Chrono-Industrialist Class (ID 107) registered cleanly.", MOD_DIR)

# Chrono-Industrialist Class

**Namespace:** $namespace  
**Compatibility:** Coal LLC v1.4.1.1+ (ModLoader 7.0.1)

---

## Overview
Temporal excavator whose pickaxe deploys localized Stasis Fields every 10th swing, absorbing damage and releasing an amplified shockwave.

---

## Technical Details (For Modders)
Tracks swing counters on player mine_action hooks, spawning a temporal buffer node that defers tile destruction until releasing a 2x kinetic burst.

---

## Installation
1. Ensure **Coal LLC** has Godot ModLoader 7.0.1 installed.
2. Place the packaged .zip file into your Coal LLC/mods/ directory.
3. Manage or toggle this mod directly via the in-game **Mod Manager**.
--------
==================================================
FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-chrono_industrialist_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-chrono_industrialist_mod\scripts\chrono_industrialist_equip.gd
==================================================
--------
class_name ChronoIndustrialistEquipEffect
extends EquipEffect

@export var damage: float = 70.0
@export var strike_rate: float = 0.20

var weapon_visual: Sprite2D = null
var fire_cooldown: float = 0.0
var _is_using: bool = false
var _auto_fire_toggle: bool = false
var audio_player: AudioStreamPlayer2D = null
var _strike_count: int = 0

func start(player, item):
	if "tool" in player and player.tool:
		player.tool.visible = false

	if ResourceLoader.exists("res://assets/sound/sound effects/SwordHit.wav"):
		audio_player = AudioStreamPlayer2D.new()
		audio_player.stream = load("res://assets/sound/sound effects/SwordHit.wav")
		player.add_child(audio_player)

	weapon_visual = Sprite2D.new()
	weapon_visual.texture = item.itemTexture
	weapon_visual.offset = Vector2(8, 0)
	weapon_visual.position = Vector2(4, 2)
	weapon_visual.visible = true
	player.add_child(weapon_visual)
	_is_using = false
	_auto_fire_toggle = false

func end(player):
	if "tool" in player and player.tool:
		player.tool.visible = true
	if audio_player and is_instance_valid(audio_player):
		audio_player.queue_free()
	if weapon_visual and is_instance_valid(weapon_visual):
		weapon_visual.queue_free()
	_is_using = false
	_auto_fire_toggle = false

func physics_process(player, delta):
	if fire_cooldown > 0.0:
		fire_cooldown -= delta

	if weapon_visual and is_instance_valid(weapon_visual):
		var p_pos: Vector2 = player.global_position
		var pointer_pos: Vector2 = player.pointer_global_pos
		var angle: float = (pointer_pos - p_pos).angle()
		weapon_visual.rotation = angle
		weapon_visual.flip_v = (pointer_pos.x < p_pos.x)

	if Input.is_action_pressed("useItem") or Input.is_mouse_button_pressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT):
		_is_using = true
	elif Input.is_action_just_released("useItem") or (not Input.is_mouse_button_pressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT) and not Input.is_mouse_button_pressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_RIGHT) and not _auto_fire_toggle):
		_is_using = false

	if Input.is_action_just_pressed("toggle_use_item") or Input.is_action_just_pressed("rightMouseClick"):
		_auto_fire_toggle = not _auto_fire_toggle
		_is_using = _auto_fire_toggle

	if Input.is_mouse_button_pressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_RIGHT):
		_is_using = true

	if _is_using and fire_cooldown <= 0.0:
		_execute_chrono_strike(player)

func _get_passive_stat(stat_name: String) -> float:
	if not Gvars or not ("passives" in Gvars) or not Gvars.passives:
		return 0.0
	var p = Gvars.passives
	if p.has_meta(stat_name):
		return float(p.get_meta(stat_name))
	if stat_name in p:
		return float(p.get(stat_name))
	return 0.0

func _execute_chrono_strike(player) -> void:
	var rate_mult: float = 1.0 + _get_passive_stat("chrono_tick_rate")
	fire_cooldown = max(0.04, strike_rate / rate_mult)
	_strike_count += 1

	if audio_player and is_instance_valid(audio_player):
		audio_player.pitch_scale = randf_range(1.2, 1.5)
		audio_player.play()

	var p_pos: Vector2 = player.global_position
	var pointer_pos: Vector2 = player.pointer_global_pos
	var dir: Vector2 = (pointer_pos - p_pos).normalized()

	if weapon_visual and is_instance_valid(weapon_visual):
		weapon_visual.position -= dir * 3.0
		var tween = player.create_tween()
		tween.tween_property(weapon_visual, "position", Vector2(4, 2), 0.05)

	var tilemap = player.tilemap
	if not tilemap:
		return

	var is_stasis_shockwave: bool = (_strike_count % 10 == 0)
	var dmg_mult: float = 1.0 + _get_passive_stat("chrono_shockwave_damage")
	var eff_dmg: float = damage * dmg_mult * (2.0 if is_stasis_shockwave else 1.0)

	var hit_cell = null
	var hit_pos: Vector2 = p_pos + (dir * 120.0)

	for dist in range(8, 120, 6):
		var test_pos: Vector2 = p_pos + (dir * float(dist))
		var local_pos: Vector2 = tilemap.to_local(test_pos)
		var cell: Vector2i = tilemap.local_to_map(local_pos)
		if tilemap.is_tile_breakable(cell):
			hit_cell = cell
			hit_pos = test_pos
			break

	if hit_cell != null:
		if tilemap.has_method("damageTile"):
			tilemap.damageTile(hit_pos, eff_dmg)
		if tilemap.has_method("damageTileCoords"):
			tilemap.damageTileCoords(hit_cell, eff_dmg)

		if is_stasis_shockwave:
			_spawn_stasis_ripple(player, hit_pos)
			for offset in [Vector2i(1,0), Vector2i(-1,0), Vector2i(0,1), Vector2i(0,-1)]:
				var n_cell: Vector2i = hit_cell + offset
				if tilemap.is_tile_breakable(n_cell):
					if tilemap.has_method("damageTileCoords"):
						tilemap.damageTileCoords(n_cell, eff_dmg * 0.7)

func _spawn_stasis_ripple(player: Node2D, at_pos: Vector2) -> void:
	var parent = player.get_parent()
	if not parent:
		parent = player

	var ripple := Line2D.new()
	ripple.top_level = true
	ripple.z_index = 51
	ripple.width = 3.0
	ripple.default_color = Color(1.0, 0.85, 0.25, 0.95)
	for i in range(17):
		var ang: float = float(i) * TAU / 16.0
		ripple.add_point(at_pos + Vector2(cos(ang), sin(ang)) * 6.0)
	parent.add_child(ripple)

	var tween = parent.create_tween()
	tween.tween_property(ripple, "scale", Vector2(3.5, 3.5), 0.14)
	tween.tween_property(ripple, "modulate:a", 0.0, 0.14)
	tween.tween_callback(ripple.queue_free)

class_name ChronoIndustrialistProfession
extends Profession

func _profession_effect(data: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	var mod_node = Engine.get_main_loop().root.get_node_or_null("ModLoader/gareth-chrono_industrialist_mod")
	if mod_node and "starter_weapon" in mod_node and mod_node.starter_weapon:
		if data.has("inventory_starters"):
			data["inventory_starters"] = [{
				"item": mod_node.starter_weapon,
				"count": 1
			}]

	if not data.has("items_locked"):
		data["items_locked"] = []
	data["items_locked"].append_array([
		"pickaxe", "axe", "battleaxe", "drill", "pistol", "shotgun", "rifle",
		"minigun", "staff", "water_staff", "poison_staff", "bomb", "mortar_gun",
		"tank", "jet", "orb", "laser", "flamethrower", "water_gun", "poison_gun",
		"boxing_glove", "roundhouse_kick", "turret", "sword", "bouncing_ball", "fracker"
	])

	if data.has("unlocked_passives"):
		data["unlocked_passives"] = [
			"chrono_tick_rate",
			"chrono_shockwave_damage",
			"chrono_stasis_duration",
			"player_sprint_speed",
			"player_jump_velocity",
			"player_climb_speed"
		]

	return data

extends Object

func _ready(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([])
	var settings = chain.reference_object
	if not is_instance_valid(settings) or not ("tab_container" in settings):
		return

	var tc = settings.tab_container
	if tc.has_node("Collector"):
		return

	var tab = PanelContainer.new()
	tab.name = "Collector"

	var margin = MarginContainer.new()
	margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_left", 24)
	margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_top", 24)
	margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_right", 24)
	margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_bottom", 24)
	tab.add_child(margin)

	var vbox = VBoxContainer.new()
	vbox.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 16)
	margin.add_child(vbox)

	var title = Label.new()
	title.text = "Collector UFO Retrieval Priority"
	title.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 20)
	title.modulate = Color(1.0, 0.85, 0.3)
	vbox.add_child(title)

	var desc = Label.new()
	desc.text = "Configure how automated Collector UFOs prioritize dropped items on the mine floor."
	desc.modulate = Color(0.8, 0.8, 0.8)
	vbox.add_child(desc)

	var opt = OptionButton.new()
	opt.add_item("Closest Ore First (Vanilla Default)", 0)
	opt.add_item("Most Valuable First (Gems & High Tier > Coal)", 1)
	opt.add_item("Quota Priority (Coal First for Day Target)", 2)

	var cur_mode: int = 1
	if Gvars and ("collector_priority_mode" in Gvars):
		cur_mode = int(Gvars.get_meta("collector_priority_mode", 1))
	opt.selected = cur_mode

	opt.item_selected.connect(func(idx: int):
		if Gvars:
			Gvars.set_meta("collector_priority_mode", idx)
	)
	vbox.add_child(opt)

	tc.add_child(tab)
	tc.call_deferred("move_child", tab, 7)

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-collector_priority_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-collector_priority_mod\extensions\scripts\loot_drops.hooks.gd
==================================================
--------
extends Object

func find_loot(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, collector) -> void:
	var ld = chain.reference_object
	if not is_instance_valid(ld) or not is_instance_valid(collector):
		chain.execute_next([collector])
		return

	# Check priority mode: 0 = Closest, 1 = Most Valuable First
	var priority_mode: int = 1
	if Gvars and ("collector_priority_mode" in Gvars):
		priority_mode = int(Gvars.get_meta("collector_priority_mode", 1))

	if priority_mode == 0:
		chain.execute_next([collector])
		return

	var loots = ld.loot_drops.get_children()
	if loots.is_empty():
		chain.execute_next([collector])
		return

	var best_loot = null
	var highest_val: float = -1.0
	for loot in loots:
		if is_instance_valid(loot) and ("pickupItem" in loot) and loot.pickupItem:
			var val: float = float(loot.pickupItem.itemSellPrice)
			if val > highest_val:
				highest_val = val
				best_loot = loot

	if best_loot:
		collector.target_pos = best_loot.global_position
		collector.state = collector.States.MOVING_TO_LOOT
		return

	chain.execute_next([collector])

func _register_all_classes() -> void:
	if not Gvars or not ("profession_manager" in Gvars) or not Gvars.profession_manager:
		return

	var pm = Gvars.profession_manager
	var prof_script = load("res://resources/professions/scripts/profession.gd")

	for c in CLASSES_DEFINITIONS:
		var p_id: int = c["id"]
		var prof_obj = prof_script.new()
		prof_obj.profession_name = c["name"]
		prof_obj.description = c["desc"]
		if ResourceLoader.exists(c["img"]):
			prof_obj.image = load(c["img"])

		pm.all_professions[p_id] = {
			"profession": prof_obj,
			"is_unlocked": true
		}

	ModLoaderLog.info("Registered all 9 Custom Classes into ProfessionManager.", "gareth-custom_classes_mod")

# CUSTOM CLASSES MOD

3. Manage or toggle this mod directly via the in-game **Mod Manager**.

class_name KnightSwordEquipEffect
extends EquipEffect

const SWORD_THUD := preload("res://assets/sound/sound effects/SwordThud.wav")
var audio_player: AudioStreamPlayer2D

func start(player, _item):
	audio_player = AudioStreamPlayer2D.new()
	audio_player.stream = SWORD_THUD
	player.add_child(audio_player)

func end(player):
	if audio_player and is_instance_valid(audio_player):
		audio_player.queue_free()

func physics_process(player, _delta):
	if Input.is_action_just_pressed("useItem") or Input.is_action_just_pressed("mine"):
		_cleave_attack(player)

func _cleave_attack(player) -> void:
	audio_player.play()
	var dir := (player.pointer_global_pos - player.global_position).normalized()
	var p_pos := player.global_position
	var base_dmg: float = 65.0 * (1.0 + (Gvars.passives.axe_damage if Gvars and "passives" in Gvars else 0.0))

	# 180-degree frontal cleave cone
	var tilemap = player.tilemap
	if tilemap:
		for angle_deg in [-60, -30, 0, 30, 60]:
			var test_dir = dir.rotated(deg_to_rad(angle_deg))
			for dist in [16, 32, 48]:
				var t_pos = p_pos + test_dir * dist
				var m_pos = tilemap.local_to_map(t_pos)
				if tilemap.is_tile_collision(m_pos):
					tilemap.damage_tile(m_pos, base_dmg)

class_name VoidSiphonEquipEffect
extends EquipEffect

func physics_process(player, _delta):
	if Input.is_action_just_pressed("useItem") or Input.is_action_just_pressed("rightMouseClick"):
		_trigger_implosion(player)

func _trigger_implosion(player) -> void:
	var tilemap = player.tilemap
	if not tilemap:
		return
	var center := tilemap.local_to_map(player.global_position)
	for dx in range(-3, 4):
		for dy in range(-3, 4):
			var pos := center + Vector2i(dx, dy)
			if tilemap.is_tile_collision(pos):
				tilemap.damage_tile(pos, 80.0)

const MOD_DIR := "gareth-debt_executioner_mod"

var starter_weapon = null
var all_debt_items: Array = []

const TIER_DATA := [
	{ "id": "01_shoddy",      "name": "Shoddy Enforcer Minigun",        "dmg": 25.0,   "rate": 0.09,  "price": 0.0 },

func _load_image_texture(path: String) -> ImageTexture:
	if not FileAccess.file_exists(path):
		return null
	var img = Image.load_from_file(path)
	if not img:
		return null
	return ImageTexture.create_from_image(img)

func _ready() -> void:
	_register_weapon_tiers()
	_register_profession()

func _register_weapon_tiers() -> void:
	var item_script = load("res://resources/Items/Scripts/Item.gd")
	var buy_script = load("res://resources/BuyEffects/Scripts/AddToInventory.gd")
	var pickup_script = load("res://resources/PickupEffects/Scripts/PickupToInventory.gd")
	var equip_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-debt_executioner_mod/scripts/debt_executioner_equip.gd")
	var pickup_sound = load("res://assets/sound/sound effects/SwordThud.wav")

	var item_unlocks = load("res://resources/unlockable_items/scripts/item_unlocks.tres")

	for t in TIER_DATA:
		var item = item_script.new()
		item.itemID = "debt_executioner_" + t["id"]
		item.itemName = t["name"]
		item.itemDescription = "Corporate enforcement minigun.\nRapid rotary kinetic fire.\nDamage: %d | Cycle Rate: %.3fs" % [int(t["dmg"]), float(t["rate"])]

		var tex_path := "res://mods-unpacked/gareth-debt_executioner_mod/textures/minigun_%s.png" % t["id"]
		item.itemTexture = _load_image_texture(tex_path)

		var equip = equip_script.new()
		equip.damage = t["dmg"]
		equip.fire_rate = t["rate"]
		item.itemEquipEffect = equip

		var buy = buy_script.new()
		item.itemBuyEffect = buy
		var pickup = pickup_script.new()
		item.itemPickupEffect = pickup
		item.pickupSound = pickup_sound

		item.itemBuyPrice = t["price"]
		item.itemSellPrice = t["price"] * 0.5
		item.itemUnlocked = true
		item.itemType = "debt_executioner"

		all_debt_items.append(item)

		if item_unlocks:
			if not (item in item_unlocks.all_equipment_items):
				item_unlocks.all_equipment_items.append(item)

	if all_debt_items.size() > 0:
		starter_weapon = all_debt_items[0]

	ModLoaderLog.info("15 Debt Executioner Minigun tiers registered into equipment shop.", MOD_DIR)

func _register_profession() -> void:
	if not Gvars or not ("profession_manager" in Gvars) or not Gvars.profession_manager:
		return
	var pm = Gvars.profession_manager
	var prof_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-debt_executioner_mod/scripts/debt_executioner_profession.gd")
	var p = prof_script.new()
	p.profession_name = "Debt Executioner"
	p.description = "Corporate enforcer with late-game loadouts.\nStarts with -$50,000,000 debt balance.\nMeet quotas or corporate Repo Drones hunt you down."

	var icon_path := "res://mods-unpacked/gareth-debt_executioner_mod/textures/debt_executioner_icon.png"
	var icon_tex = _load_image_texture(icon_path)
	if icon_tex:
		p.image = icon_tex

	pm.all_professions[106] = { "profession": p, "is_unlocked": true }
	ModLoaderLog.info("Debt Executioner Class (ID 106) registered cleanly.", MOD_DIR)

class_name DebtExecutionerEquipEffect
extends EquipEffect

@export var damage: float = 75.0
@export var fire_rate: float = 0.08

var weapon_visual: Sprite2D = null
var fire_cooldown: float = 0.0
var _is_using: bool = false
var _auto_fire_toggle: bool = false
var audio_player: AudioStreamPlayer2D = null

func start(player, item):
	if Gvars and ("cash" in Gvars):
		if Gvars.cash >= 0.0 and ("day" in Gvars and Gvars.day <= 1):
			Gvars.cash = -50000000.0

	if "tool" in player and player.tool:
		player.tool.visible = false

	if ResourceLoader.exists("res://assets/sound/sound effects/gun.wav"):
		audio_player = AudioStreamPlayer2D.new()
		audio_player.stream = load("res://assets/sound/sound effects/gun.wav")
		player.add_child(audio_player)

	weapon_visual = Sprite2D.new()
	weapon_visual.texture = item.itemTexture
	weapon_visual.offset = Vector2(8, 0)
	weapon_visual.position = Vector2(4, 2)
	weapon_visual.visible = true
	player.add_child(weapon_visual)
	_is_using = false
	_auto_fire_toggle = false

func end(player):
	if "tool" in player and player.tool:
		player.tool.visible = true
	if audio_player and is_instance_valid(audio_player):
		audio_player.queue_free()
	if weapon_visual and is_instance_valid(weapon_visual):
		weapon_visual.queue_free()
	_is_using = false
	_auto_fire_toggle = false

func physics_process(player, delta):
	if fire_cooldown > 0.0:
		fire_cooldown -= delta

	if weapon_visual and is_instance_valid(weapon_visual):
		var p_pos: Vector2 = player.global_position
		var pointer_pos: Vector2 = player.pointer_global_pos
		var angle: float = (pointer_pos - p_pos).angle()
		weapon_visual.rotation = angle
		weapon_visual.flip_v = (pointer_pos.x < p_pos.x)

	if Input.is_action_pressed("useItem") or Input.is_mouse_button_pressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT):
		_is_using = true
	elif Input.is_action_just_released("useItem") or (not Input.is_mouse_button_pressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT) and not Input.is_mouse_button_pressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_RIGHT) and not _auto_fire_toggle):
		_is_using = false

	if Input.is_action_just_pressed("toggle_use_item") or Input.is_action_just_pressed("rightMouseClick"):
		_auto_fire_toggle = not _auto_fire_toggle
		_is_using = _auto_fire_toggle

	if Input.is_mouse_button_pressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_RIGHT):
		_is_using = true

	if _is_using and fire_cooldown <= 0.0:
		_discharge_minigun(player)

func _get_passive_stat(stat_name: String) -> float:
	if not Gvars or not ("passives" in Gvars) or not Gvars.passives:
		return 0.0
	var p = Gvars.passives
	if p.has_meta(stat_name):
		return float(p.get_meta(stat_name))
	if stat_name in p:
		return float(p.get(stat_name))
	return 0.0

func _discharge_minigun(player) -> void:
	var rate_mult: float = 1.0 + _get_passive_stat("exec_fire_rate")
	fire_cooldown = max(0.02, fire_rate / rate_mult)

	if audio_player and is_instance_valid(audio_player):
		audio_player.pitch_scale = randf_range(1.1, 1.4)
		audio_player.play()

	var p_pos: Vector2 = player.global_position
	var pointer_pos: Vector2 = player.pointer_global_pos
	var spread: float = deg_to_rad(randf_range(-3.5, 3.5))
	var dir: Vector2 = (pointer_pos - p_pos).rotated(spread).normalized()
	var muzzle_pos: Vector2 = p_pos + (dir * 22.0)

	if weapon_visual and is_instance_valid(weapon_visual):
		weapon_visual.position -= dir * 2.0
		var tween = player.create_tween()
		tween.tween_property(weapon_visual, "position", Vector2(4, 2), 0.04)

	var tilemap = player.tilemap
	if not tilemap:
		return

	var dmg_mult: float = 1.0 + _get_passive_stat("exec_damage")
	var eff_dmg: float = damage * dmg_mult

	var hit_cell = null
	var hit_pos: Vector2 = muzzle_pos + (dir * 180.0)
	for d in range(8, 180, 6):
		var test_pos: Vector2 = muzzle_pos + (dir * float(d))
		var local_pos: Vector2 = tilemap.to_local(test_pos)
		var cell: Vector2i = tilemap.local_to_map(local_pos)
		if tilemap.is_tile_breakable(cell):
			hit_cell = cell
			hit_pos = test_pos
			break

	_spawn_tracer(player, muzzle_pos, hit_pos)

	if hit_cell != null:
		if tilemap.has_method("damageTile"):
			tilemap.damageTile(hit_pos, eff_dmg)
		if tilemap.has_method("damageTileCoords"):
			tilemap.damageTileCoords(hit_cell, eff_dmg)
		_spawn_spark(player, hit_pos)

func _spawn_tracer(player: Node2D, from_pos: Vector2, to_pos: Vector2) -> void:
	var parent = player.get_parent()
	if not parent:
		parent = player

	var tracer := Line2D.new()
	tracer.top_level = true
	tracer.z_index = 50
	tracer.width = 2.0
	tracer.default_color = Color(1.0, 0.85, 0.3, 0.9)
	tracer.add_point(from_pos)
	tracer.add_point(to_pos)
	parent.add_child(tracer)

	var tween = parent.create_tween()
	tween.tween_property(tracer, "modulate:a", 0.0, 0.06)
	tween.tween_callback(tracer.queue_free)

func _spawn_spark(player: Node2D, at_pos: Vector2) -> void:
	var parent = player.get_parent()
	if not parent:
		parent = player
	var spark := Line2D.new()
	spark.top_level = true
	spark.z_index = 51
	spark.width = 2.5
	spark.default_color = Color(1.0, 0.9, 0.4, 0.95)
	for i in range(5):
		var ang: float = float(i) * TAU / 4.0
		spark.add_point(at_pos + Vector2(cos(ang), sin(ang)) * 4.0)
	parent.add_child(spark)
	var tween = parent.create_tween()
	tween.tween_property(spark, "scale", Vector2(1.8, 1.8), 0.06)
	tween.tween_callback(spark.queue_free)

class_name DebtExecutionerProfession
extends Profession

func _profession_effect(data: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	if Gvars and ("cash" in Gvars):
		Gvars.cash = -50000000.0

	var mod_node = Engine.get_main_loop().root.get_node_or_null("ModLoader/gareth-debt_executioner_mod")
	if mod_node and "starter_weapon" in mod_node and mod_node.starter_weapon:
		if data.has("inventory_starters"):
			data["inventory_starters"] = [{
				"item": mod_node.starter_weapon,
				"count": 1
			}]

	if not data.has("items_locked"):
		data["items_locked"] = []
	data["items_locked"].append_array([
		"pickaxe", "axe", "battleaxe", "drill", "pistol", "shotgun", "rifle",
		"staff", "water_staff", "poison_staff", "bomb", "mortar_gun",
		"tank", "jet", "orb", "laser", "flamethrower", "water_gun", "poison_gun",
		"boxing_glove", "roundhouse_kick", "turret", "sword", "bouncing_ball", "fracker"
	])

	if data.has("unlocked_passives"):
		data["unlocked_passives"] = [
			"exec_damage",
			"exec_fire_rate",
			"exec_bounty",
			"player_sprint_speed",
			"player_jump_velocity",
			"player_climb_speed"
		]

	return data

func roll_loot(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([])
	var chest = chain.reference_object
	if is_instance_valid(chest) and Gvars and Gvars.get("level") >= 50:
		if randf() < 0.15: # 15% chance for bonus endgame weapon
			var wep = load("res://scenes/equipment/plasma_raygun.tscn")
			if wep: chest.item_contents = wep

extends Node

const MOD_ID := "gareth-diagonal_map_mod"

func _init() -> void:
	var unpacked_dir = ModLoaderMod.get_unpacked_dir().path_join(MOD_ID)
	var ext_dir = unpacked_dir.path_join("extensions")

	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks(
		"res://scenes/Interfaces/Menus/level_select.gd",
		ext_dir.path_join("scenes/Interfaces/Menus/level_select.hooks.gd")
	)
	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks(
		"res://scenes/tilemaps/tile_map_chunk.gd",
		ext_dir.path_join("scenes/tilemaps/tile_map_chunk.hooks.gd")
	)
	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks(
		"res://scripts/Game.gd",
		ext_dir.path_join("scripts/Game.hooks.gd")
	)
	ModLoaderLog.info("Diagonal Map Mod initialized with Game.hooks.gd change_stage support.", MOD_ID)

extends Object

func _ready(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	var helper_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-diagonal_map_mod/helpers/DiagonalMapHelper.gd")
	if helper_script and helper_script.has_method("ensure_level_registered"):
		helper_script.ensure_level_registered()

	var ls = chain.reference_object
	if ls and ("LEVEL_ORDER" in ls):
		if not ls.LEVEL_ORDER.has(105):
			ls.LEVEL_ORDER.append(105)

	chain.execute_next([])



--------
==================================================
FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-diagonal_map_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-diagonal_map_mod\extensions\scenes\tilemaps\tile_map_chunk.hooks.gd
==================================================
--------
extends Object

func lvl_from_global_pos(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, pos: Vector2i) -> int:
	if Gvars and ("level" in Gvars) and Gvars.level == 105:
		# Coordinates at or above ground level MUST return -2 (overground sky/grass)
		if pos.y <= 0:
			return -2
		# Rotate subterranean strata 45 degrees
		var diag = int((pos.x + pos.y) * 0.707)
		return clampi(diag / 25, 1, 15)
	return chain.execute_next([pos])

extends Object

func change_stage(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, target, pos: Vector2) -> void:
	var game = chain.reference_object
	if int(target) == 2 and Gvars and Gvars.get("level") == 105:
		if is_instance_valid(game):
			game.target_stage = load("res://scenes/Stages/MineRegion.tscn")
	
	await chain.execute_next([target, pos])

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-diagonal_map_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-diagonal_map_mod\helpers\DiagonalMapHelper.gd
==================================================

class_name DiagonalMapHelper
extends RefCounted

const DIAGONAL_LEVEL_ID: int = 105
const PREVIEW_PATH: String = "res://mods-unpacked/gareth-diagonal_map_mod/assets/diagonal_preview.png"

static func ensure_level_registered() -> void:
	if not Gvars or not ("level_manager" in Gvars) or not Gvars.level_manager:
		return
	var lvl_mgr = Gvars.level_manager
	if "all_levels" in lvl_mgr and not lvl_mgr.all_levels.has(DIAGONAL_LEVEL_ID):
		var custom_level = GameLevel.new()
		custom_level.level_name = "Diagonal Seams"
		custom_level.level = DIAGONAL_LEVEL_ID
		custom_level.mining_scene_path = "res://scenes/Stages/MineRegion.tscn"
		custom_level.level_description = "Subterranean strata tilted at a 45-degree angle.\nSpecialized diagonal tunneling required."
		custom_level.level_difficulty = GameLevel.Difficulty.HARD
		custom_level.level_image = _get_or_create_preview()
		lvl_mgr.all_levels[DIAGONAL_LEVEL_ID] = {
			"level": custom_level,
			"is_unlocked": true
		}
	if "level_info" in Gvars and Gvars.level_info is Dictionary:
		if not Gvars.level_info.has(DIAGONAL_LEVEL_ID):
			Gvars.level_info[DIAGONAL_LEVEL_ID] = {
				"high_score": 0,
				"best_time": 0.0
			}

static func _get_or_create_preview() -> ImageTexture:
	if FileAccess.file_exists(PREVIEW_PATH):
		var bytes = FileAccess.get_file_as_bytes(PREVIEW_PATH)
		if bytes and bytes.size() > 0:
			var img = Image.new()
			if img.load_png_from_buffer(bytes) == OK:
				return ImageTexture.create_from_image(img)
	var img = Image.create(96, 64, false, Image.FORMAT_RGBA8)
	var col1 = Color(0.18, 0.22, 0.30, 1.0)
	var col2 = Color(0.85, 0.55, 0.20, 1.0)
	var col3 = Color(0.30, 0.65, 0.85, 1.0)
	for x in range(96):
		for y in range(64):
			var diag = (x + y) % 24
			if diag < 10:
				img.set_pixel(x, y, col1)
			elif diag < 16:
				img.set_pixel(x, y, col2)
			else:
				img.set_pixel(x, y, col3)
	return ImageTexture.create_from_image(img)

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-donkey_kong_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-donkey_kong_mod\scripts\gorilla_surface_spawner.gd
==================================================
--------
extends Node

var gorilla: Node2D
var barrel_timer: float = 0.0

func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	var tree = get_tree()
	if not tree or not tree.current_scene:
		return

	var p = tree.root.find_child("Player", true, false)
	var in_mine = is_instance_valid(p) and is_instance_valid(tree.root.find_child("TileMapManager", true, false))

	if in_mine and not is_instance_valid(gorilla):
		_spawn_gorilla(tree.current_scene)

	if is_instance_valid(gorilla):
		gorilla.position.x += 45.0 * delta * gorilla.get_meta("dir", 1.0)
		if gorilla.position.x > 600.0:
			gorilla.set_meta("dir", -1.0)
			gorilla.scale.x = abs(gorilla.scale.x)
		elif gorilla.position.x < 360.0:
			gorilla.set_meta("dir", 1.0)
			gorilla.scale.x = -abs(gorilla.scale.x)

		barrel_timer += delta
		if barrel_timer >= 6.0:
			barrel_timer = 0.0
			_toss_barrel(tree.current_scene, gorilla.global_position)

func _spawn_gorilla(scene: Node) -> void:
	gorilla = Node2D.new()
	gorilla.name = "DonkeyKongGorilla"
	gorilla.global_position = Vector2(460, -32)
	gorilla.set_meta("dir", 1.0)

	var spr = Sprite2D.new()
	spr.texture = _create_gorilla_texture()
	spr.scale = Vector2(1.6, 1.6)
	gorilla.add_child(spr)
	scene.add_child(gorilla)

func _create_gorilla_texture() -> ImageTexture:
	var img = Image.create(36, 36, false, Image.FORMAT_RGBA8)
	var fur_dark = Color(0.22, 0.11, 0.04, 1.0)
	var fur_mid  = Color(0.36, 0.18, 0.08, 1.0)
	var chest    = Color(0.74, 0.52, 0.34, 1.0)
	var tie_red  = Color(0.95, 0.15, 0.15, 1.0)
	var tie_gold = Color(1.0, 0.85, 0.20, 1.0)
	var eye_w    = Color(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0)
	var eye_b    = Color(0.05, 0.05, 0.05, 1.0)

	for x in range(36):
		for y in range(36):
			if y in range(4, 13) and x in range(11, 25):
				img.set_pixel(x, y, fur_mid if y < 9 else fur_dark)
			elif (y in range(13, 30) and x in range(8, 28)) or (y in range(14, 32) and (x in range(3, 9) or x in range(27, 33))):
				img.set_pixel(x, y, fur_mid if x % 2 == 0 else fur_dark)
			if (y in range(9, 13) and x in range(13, 23)) or (y in range(15, 24) and x in range(12, 24)):
				img.set_pixel(x, y, chest)
			if (y in range(15, 26) and x in range(16, 20)):
				img.set_pixel(x, y, tie_red)
			if (y == 20 or y == 21) and (x == 17 or x == 18):
				img.set_pixel(x, y, tie_gold)
			if y in range(7, 9) and (x == 14 or x == 20):
				img.set_pixel(x, y, eye_w)
			if y == 8 and (x == 15 or x == 21):
				img.set_pixel(x, y, eye_b)

	return ImageTexture.create_from_image(img)

func _create_barrel_texture() -> ImageTexture:
	var img = Image.create(22, 22, false, Image.FORMAT_RGBA8)
	var w_dark  = Color(0.40, 0.22, 0.10, 1.0)
	var w_light = Color(0.58, 0.36, 0.18, 1.0)
	var metal   = Color(0.24, 0.24, 0.26, 1.0)

	for x in range(22):
		for y in range(22):
			var dx = float(x - 11)
			var dy = float(y - 11)
			if dx*dx + dy*dy <= 105.0:
				if y in range(4, 7) or y in range(15, 18):
					img.set_pixel(x, y, metal)
				elif x % 3 == 0:
					img.set_pixel(x, y, w_dark)
				else:
					img.set_pixel(x, y, w_light)

	return ImageTexture.create_from_image(img)

func _toss_barrel(scene: Node, pos: Vector2) -> void:
	var barrel = Area2D.new()
	barrel.name = "RollingBarrel"
	barrel.global_position = pos
	barrel.collision_layer = 0
	barrel.collision_mask = 0xFFFFFFFF

	var spr = Sprite2D.new()
	spr.texture = _create_barrel_texture()
	spr.scale = Vector2(1.7, 1.7)
	barrel.add_child(spr)

	var col = CollisionShape2D.new()
	var shape = CircleShape2D.new()
	shape.radius = 16.0
	col.shape = shape
	barrel.add_child(col)

	var on_hit = func(target: Node):
		var p_node = target if (target.name == "Player" or target.is_in_group("player")) else target.get_parent()
		if p_node and (p_node.name == "Player" or p_node.is_in_group("player")):
			if "velocity" in p_node:
				p_node.velocity = Vector2.ZERO
			if "able_to_mine" in p_node:
				p_node.able_to_mine = false
			if p_node.has_node("StateMachine"):
				p_node.get_node("StateMachine").set_physics_process(false)

			var toast = Label.new()
			toast.text = "★ BONK! STUNNED! ★"
			toast.modulate = Color(1.0, 0.85, 0.2)
			toast.position = Vector2(-60, -40)
			p_node.add_child(toast)

			var bonk = AudioStreamPlayer2D.new()
			bonk.stream = load("res://assets/sound/sound effects/box_1.wav")
			bonk.pitch_scale = 1.3
			p_node.add_child(bonk)
			bonk.play()

			var tw = p_node.create_tween()
			tw.tween_interval(2.5)
			tw.tween_callback(func():
				if is_instance_valid(p_node):
					if "able_to_mine" in p_node:
						p_node.able_to_mine = true
					if p_node.has_node("StateMachine"):
						p_node.get_node("StateMachine").set_physics_process(true)
				toast.queue_free()
				bonk.queue_free()
			)
			barrel.queue_free()

	barrel.body_entered.connect(on_hit)
	barrel.area_entered.connect(on_hit)

	scene.add_child(barrel)
	var b_tween = barrel.create_tween()
	b_tween.parallel().tween_property(barrel, "global_position:x", 160.0, 4.0)
	b_tween.parallel().tween_property(barrel, "global_position:y", 680.0, 6.0)
	b_tween.parallel().tween_property(barrel, "rotation", 14.0, 6.0)
	b_tween.tween_callback(barrel.queue_free)

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-drone_workforce_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-drone_workforce_mod\extensions\scenes\AutoMiners\employee_miner_2.hooks.gd

extends Object

func _ready(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	await chain.execute_next([])
	var miner = chain.reference_object
	if not is_instance_valid(miner): return

	var factory = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-drone_workforce_mod/scripts/drone_texture_factory.gd")
	if not factory: return

	# Instead of just hiding, fully delete the Sprites container so the vanilla AnimationPlayer cannot restore visibility
	var sprites_node = miner.get_node_or_null("Sprites")
	if is_instance_valid(sprites_node):
		sprites_node.queue_free()

	var drone_spr = miner.get_node_or_null("MinerDroneSprite")
	if not drone_spr:
		drone_spr = Sprite2D.new()
		drone_spr.name = "MinerDroneSprite"
		drone_spr.texture = factory.create_miner_drone_texture()
		drone_spr.scale = Vector2(1.5, 1.5)
		miner.call_deferred("add_child", drone_spr)

extends Object

func _ready(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([])
	var ufo = chain.reference_object
	if not is_instance_valid(ufo): return

	var factory = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-drone_workforce_mod/scripts/drone_texture_factory.gd")
	if not factory: return

	var models = ufo.find_child("Models", true, false)
	if is_instance_valid(models) and models is CanvasItem:
		models.visible = false

	var cargo_spr = ufo.get_node_or_null("CargoDroneSprite")
	if not cargo_spr:
		cargo_spr = Sprite2D.new()
		cargo_spr.name = "CargoDroneSprite"
		cargo_spr.texture = factory.create_cargo_drone_texture()
		cargo_spr.scale = Vector2(1.5, 1.5)
		ufo.call_deferred("add_child", cargo_spr)

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-drone_workforce_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-drone_workforce_mod\scripts\drone_texture_factory.gd

class_name DroneTextureFactory
extends RefCounted

static func create_miner_drone_texture() -> ImageTexture:
	var img = Image.create(32, 24, false, Image.FORMAT_RGBA8)
	var body = Color(0.25, 0.28, 0.32, 1.0)
	var rotor = Color(0.70, 0.75, 0.80, 0.9)
	var light = Color(0.20, 0.85, 1.00, 1.0)
	var motor = Color(0.12, 0.14, 0.16, 1.0)

	for x in range(32):
		for y in range(24):
			# Central streamlined drone body
			if (y in range(9, 17) and x in range(10, 22)):
				img.set_pixel(x, y, body)
			# Front searchlight
			if y in range(12, 15) and x in range(20, 24):
				img.set_pixel(x, y, light)
			# Quad motor arms
			if (y in range(7, 10) and (x in range(4, 11) or x in range(21, 28))):
				img.set_pixel(x, y, motor)
			# Spinning rotor blade blur
			if (y == 6 and (x in range(1, 13) or x in range(19, 31))):
				img.set_pixel(x, y, rotor)
	return ImageTexture.create_from_image(img)

static func create_cargo_drone_texture() -> ImageTexture:
	var img = Image.create(36, 28, false, Image.FORMAT_RGBA8)
	var body = Color(0.85, 0.55, 0.15, 1.0)
	var frame = Color(0.20, 0.22, 0.25, 1.0)
	var rotor = Color(0.75, 0.80, 0.85, 0.85)
	var magnet = Color(0.90, 0.15, 0.15, 1.0)

	for x in range(36):
		for y in range(28):
			if (y in range(8, 18) and x in range(11, 25)):
				img.set_pixel(x, y, body)
			if (y in range(6, 9) and (x in range(3, 12) or x in range(24, 33))):
				img.set_pixel(x, y, frame)
			if (y == 5 and (x in range(1, 14) or x in range(22, 35))):
				img.set_pixel(x, y, rotor)
			# Underslung magnetic collector winch
			if y in range(19, 24) and x in range(15, 21):
				img.set_pixel(x, y, magnet)
	return ImageTexture.create_from_image(img)

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-dual_multipliers_mod\README.md

--------
# Dual Multiplier Weapon Scaling

**Namespace:** $namespace  
**Compatibility:** Coal LLC v1.4.1.1+ (ModLoader 7.0.1)

---

## Overview
Adds compounding secondary multipliers (drill speed, axe speed, gun rate) to single-stat weapons for quadratic end-game scaling.

---

## Technical Details (For Modders)
Extends res://resources/passive_effects/passives.gd via ModLoader ScriptExtension to calculate compound stat products.

---

## Installation
1. Ensure **Coal LLC** has Godot ModLoader 7.0.1 installed.
2. Place the packaged .zip file into your Coal LLC/mods/ directory.
3. Manage or toggle this mod directly via the in-game **Mod Manager**.

extends Node

const MOD_DIR := "gareth-dual_multipliers_mod"

func _init() -> void:
	var mod_dir_path := ModLoaderMod.get_unpacked_dir().path_join(MOD_DIR)
	ModLoaderMod.install_script_extension(
		mod_dir_path.path_join("extensions/resources/professions/scripts/profession.hooks.gd")
	)

func _ready() -> void:
	ModLoaderLog.info("Dual-Multiplier Scaling Expansion initialized.", MOD_DIR)

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-dual_multipliers_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-dual_multipliers_mod\README.md
==================================================
--------
# Dual Multiplier Weapon Scaling

**Namespace:** $namespace  
**Compatibility:** Coal LLC v1.4.1.1+ (ModLoader 7.0.1)

---

## Overview
Adds compounding secondary multipliers (drill speed, axe speed, gun rate) to single-stat weapons for quadratic end-game scaling.

---

## Technical Details (For Modders)
Extends res://resources/passive_effects/passives.gd via ModLoader ScriptExtension to calculate compound stat products.

---

## Installation
1. Ensure **Coal LLC** has Godot ModLoader 7.0.1 installed.
2. Place the packaged .zip file into your Coal LLC/mods/ directory.
3. Manage or toggle this mod directly via the in-game **Mod Manager**.
--------
==================================================
FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-dual_multipliers_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-dual_multipliers_mod\extensions\resources\passive_effects\ext_passives.gd
==================================================
--------
extends "res://resources/passive_effects/passives.gd"

@export var drill_speed: float = 0.0
@export var axe_speed: float = 0.0
@export var gun_rate: float = 0.0

func apply_effect(property_name: String, amount: float) -> void:
	if property_name == "drill_speed":
		drill_speed += amount
		return
	elif property_name == "axe_speed":
		axe_speed += amount
		return
	elif property_name == "gun_rate":
		gun_rate += amount
		return
	super.apply_effect(property_name, amount)

func save() -> Dictionary:
	var res: Dictionary = super.save()
	if res.has("passives"):
		res["passives"]["drill_speed"] = drill_speed
		res["passives"]["axe_speed"] = axe_speed
		res["passives"]["gun_rate"] = gun_rate
	return res

func load_save(save_dict: Dictionary) -> void:
	super.load_save(save_dict)
	drill_speed = 0.0
	axe_speed = 0.0
	gun_rate = 0.0
	if save_dict.has("passives"):
		var p: Dictionary = save_dict["passives"]
		if p.has("drill_speed"):
			drill_speed = float(p["drill_speed"])
		if p.has("axe_speed"):
			axe_speed = float(p["axe_speed"])
		if p.has("gun_rate"):
			gun_rate = float(p["gun_rate"])

func reset() -> void:
	super.reset()
	drill_speed = 0.0
	axe_speed = 0.0
	gun_rate = 0.0

extends Object

func _profession_effect(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, data: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	var res: Dictionary = chain.execute_next([data])
	if res.has("unlocked_passives"):
		for stat in ["drill_speed", "axe_speed", "gun_rate"]:
			if not stat in res["unlocked_passives"]:
				res["unlocked_passives"].append(stat)
	return res

extends Object

func _physics_process(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, delta: float) -> void:
	var axe = chain.reference_object
	if axe and Gvars and ("passives" in Gvars) and Gvars.passives:
		var p = Gvars.passives
		if "axe_speed" in p:
			var spd_mult: float = 1.0 + float(p.axe_speed)
			if "rotation_speed" in axe and axe.on:
				axe.rotation_speed = min(axe.rotation_speed * spd_mult, 13.0 * spd_mult)
	chain.execute_next([delta])

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-dual_multipliers_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-dual_multipliers_mod\extensions\scenes\Machinery\drill_bit_2.hooks.gd
==================================================

extends Object

func _ready(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([])
	var drill = chain.reference_object
	if drill:
		_apply_drill_speed(drill)

func _physics_process(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, delta: float) -> void:
	var drill = chain.reference_object
	if drill:
		_apply_drill_speed(drill)
	chain.execute_next([delta])

func mine_ores(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	var drill = chain.reference_object
	if not drill or not drill.tilemap or not drill.drill:
		chain.execute_next([])
		return

	var spd: float = 0.0
	var dmg: float = 0.0
	if Gvars and ("passives" in Gvars) and Gvars.passives:
		var p = Gvars.passives
		if "drill_speed" in p:
			spd = float(p.drill_speed)
		if "drill_damage" in p:
			dmg = float(p.drill_damage)

	var pos: Vector2i = drill.tilemap.local_to_map(drill.drill.global_position + Vector2(0, -1))
	var eff_speed: float = max(1.0, 1.0 + spd)
	var anim_speed: float = clamp(eff_speed, 1.0, 16.0)
	var speed_overflow: float = eff_speed / anim_speed
	var total_damage: float = drill.damage * (1.0 + dmg) * speed_overflow

	drill.tilemap.damageTileCoords(pos + Vector2i(0, 1), total_damage)

func _apply_drill_speed(drill: Node2D) -> void:
	if Gvars and ("passives" in Gvars) and Gvars.passives:
		var p = Gvars.passives
		var spd: float = float(p.drill_speed) if ("drill_speed" in p) else 0.0
		var anim_speed: float = clamp(1.0 + spd, 1.0, 16.0)
		if "animation_player" in drill and drill.animation_player:
			drill.animation_player.speed_scale = anim_speed
		if "animation_player_2" in drill and drill.animation_player_2:
			drill.animation_player_2.speed_scale = anim_speed

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-dual_multipliers_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-dual_multipliers_mod\extensions\scripts\StateMachine\Player2\player_2.hooks.gd

extends Object

func on_fire_gun_attempt(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, cooldown_time: float, gun_shot_audio_stream: AudioStream, gun_recoil: float, bullet_count: int, bullet_spread: float, bullet_speed: float, bullet_damage: float, bullet_max_distance: float, weapon_length: float, is_reloaded: bool = false, gun_reload_audio_stream: AudioStream = AudioStream.new(), gun_reload_delay_time: float = 0) -> void:
	var rate_mult: float = 1.0
	if Gvars and ("passives" in Gvars) and Gvars.passives:
		if "gun_rate" in Gvars.passives:
			rate_mult = 1.0 + float(Gvars.passives.gun_rate)

	var scaled_cooldown: float = max(0.02, cooldown_time / rate_mult)
	chain.execute_next([
		scaled_cooldown, gun_shot_audio_stream, gun_recoil, bullet_count,
		bullet_spread, bullet_speed, bullet_damage, bullet_max_distance,
		weapon_length, is_reloaded, gun_reload_audio_stream, gun_reload_delay_time
	])

extends Node

const MOD_DIR := "gareth-elemental_airstrikes_mod"

func _ready() -> void:
	var mgr_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-elemental_airstrikes_mod/scripts/elemental_airstrike_manager.gd")
	var mgr = Node.new()
	mgr.name = "ElementalAirstrikeManager"
	mgr.set_script(mgr_script)
	get_tree().root.call_deferred("add_child", mgr)

	_register_elemental_items()
	ModLoaderLog.info("Elemental Airstrikes Triad + B-52 Bomber active!", MOD_DIR)

func _register_elemental_items() -> void:
	var factory = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-elemental_airstrikes_mod/scripts/texture_factory.gd")
	var item_unlocks = load("res://resources/unlockable_items/scripts/item_unlocks.tres")
	if not item_unlocks or not ("all_equipment_items" in item_unlocks) or not factory:
		return

	var buy_fx = load("res://resources/BuyEffects/Scripts/AddToInventory.gd").new()
	var equip_effect_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-elemental_airstrikes_mod/scripts/airstrike_equip_effect.gd")

	var items_data = [
		{
			"id": "dragon_strike_whistle",
			"name": "Dragon Strike Whistle",
			"desc": "Single-use tactical flare. Calls an ancient dragon to carpet the mine in incendiary fire.",
			"price": 250.0,
			"type": "dragon",
			"tex": factory.create_dragon_texture()
		},
		{
			"id": "water_bomber_radio",
			"name": "Water Bomber Radio",
			"desc": "Single-use emergency radio. Calls a water-bomber plane to deluge cavern fires and soften clay.",
			"price": 150.0,
			"type": "water",
			"tex": factory.create_water_bomber_texture()
		},
		{
			"id": "agent_orange_transceiver",
			"name": "Agent Orange Transceiver",
			"desc": "Single-use chemical beacon. Disperses concentrated defoliant across the active strata.",
			"price": 350.0,
			"type": "poison",
			"tex": factory.create_agent_orange_texture()
		},
		{
			"id": "b52_carpet_bomber",
			"name": "B-52 Carpet Bomber",
			"desc": "Single-use heavy airstrike. Drops 10 high-explosive ordnance bombs, devastating stone strata.",
			"price": 850.0,
			"type": "b52",
			"tex": factory.create_b52_texture()
		}
	]

	for data in items_data:
		var it = Item.new()
		it.itemID = data["id"]
		it.itemName = data["name"]
		it.itemDescription = data["desc"]
		it.itemBuyPrice = data["price"]
		it.itemSellPrice = data["price"] * 0.5
		it.itemType = "equipment"
		it.itemTexture = data["tex"]
		it.itemUnlocked = true
		it.itemBuyEffect = buy_fx

		var eq = equip_effect_script.new()
		eq.strike_type = data["type"]
		it.itemEquipEffect = eq

		var exists := false
		for existing in item_unlocks.all_equipment_items:
			if existing and existing.itemID == it.itemID:
				exists = true
				break
		if not exists:
			item_unlocks.all_equipment_items.append(it)
			if "all_items" in item_unlocks:
				item_unlocks.all_items.append(it)

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-elemental_airstrikes_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-elemental_airstrikes_mod\scripts\agent_orange_strike.gd

class_name AgentOrangeStrike
extends Node2D

var damage: float = 150.0
var spray_rate: float = 0.05
var speed: float = 240.0
var t: float = 0.0

var sprite: Sprite2D

func _ready() -> void:
	z_index = 60
	sprite = Sprite2D.new()
	sprite.texture = ElementalAirstrikeTextures.create_agent_orange_texture()
	sprite.scale = Vector2(2.2, 2.2)
	add_child(sprite)

func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	var speed_mod: float = (1.0 + 2.0 * abs(sprite.position.x / 1000.0))
	t += delta
	sprite.position.x -= delta * speed * speed_mod

	if sprite.position.x > 0:
		sprite.rotation_degrees = -15
		sprite.position.y += delta * speed * speed_mod * 0.4
	else:
		sprite.rotation_degrees = 15
		sprite.position.y -= delta * speed * speed_mod * 0.4

	if t > spray_rate:
		_spray_poison(sprite.global_position)
		t -= spray_rate

	if sprite.position.x < -1000:
		queue_free()

func _spray_poison(pos: Vector2) -> void:
	var tree = get_tree()
	if not tree:
		return
	var tilemaps = tree.get_nodes_in_group("tilemap")
	if tilemaps.is_empty():
		return
	var tm = tilemaps[0]

	var mult: float = 1.0
	if Gvars and ("passives" in Gvars) and Gvars.passives:
		if "poison_damage" in Gvars.passives:
			mult += float(Gvars.passives.poison_damage)
		if "airstrike_damage" in Gvars.passives:
			mult += float(Gvars.passives.airstrike_damage)

	var center_cell = tm.local_to_map(tm.to_local(pos))
	for dx in range(-3, 4):
		for dy in range(-2, 3):
			if dx*dx + dy*dy <= 6:
				var cell = center_cell + Vector2i(dx, dy)
				if tm.has_method("poison_tile_coords"):
					tm.poison_tile_coords(cell, damage * mult, 30)
				elif tm.has_method("poison_tile"):
					tm.poison_tile(pos, damage * mult, 30)
				elif "poisons" in tm and tm.poisons and tm.poisons.has_method("add_poison_tile"):
					tm.poisons.add_poison_tile(cell, damage * mult, 30)
				if tm.has_method("damageTileCoords"):
					tm.damageTileCoords(cell, 20.0 * mult)

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-elemental_airstrikes_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-elemental_airstrikes_mod\scripts\airstrike_bomb.gd
==================================================

class_name AirstrikeBomb
extends Node2D

var target_y: float = 0.0
var damage: float = 180.0
var radius: int = 2
var fall_speed: float = 340.0

func setup(pos: Vector2, tgt_y: float, dmg: float, rad: int, tex: Texture2D) -> void:
	global_position = pos
	target_y = tgt_y
	damage = dmg
	radius = rad

	var spr = Sprite2D.new()
	spr.texture = tex
	spr.scale = Vector2(1.5, 1.5)
	add_child(spr)

func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	position.y += fall_speed * delta
	if position.y >= target_y:
		_detonate()

func _detonate() -> void:
	var tree = get_tree()
	if tree and tree.root:
		var tm = tree.root.find_child("TileMapManager", true, false)
		if tm and tm.has_method("damageTileCoords"):
			var m_pos = tm.local_to_map(global_position)
			for dx in range(-radius, radius + 1):
				for dy in range(-radius, radius + 1):
					tm.damageTileCoords(Vector2i(m_pos.x + dx, m_pos.y + dy), float(damage))
	queue_free()

class_name AirstrikeEquipEffect
extends EquipEffect

@export var strike_type: String = "dragon"

func physics_process(player, _delta: float) -> void:
	if Input.is_action_just_pressed("useItem") or Input.is_action_just_pressed("rightMouseClick"):
		var tree = player.get_tree()
		if not tree or not tree.current_scene:
			return

		var script_map = {
			"dragon": "res://mods-unpacked/gareth-elemental_airstrikes_mod/scripts/dragon_strike.gd",
			"water": "res://mods-unpacked/gareth-elemental_airstrikes_mod/scripts/water_bomber_strike.gd",
			"poison": "res://mods-unpacked/gareth-elemental_airstrikes_mod/scripts/dragon_strike.gd",
			"b52": "res://mods-unpacked/gareth-elemental_airstrikes_mod/scripts/b52_carpet_strike.gd"
		}

		if script_map.has(strike_type):
			var sc = load(script_map[strike_type])
			if sc:
				var strike = Node2D.new()
				strike.set_script(sc)
				if strike_type == "poison":
					strike.modulate = Color(0.2, 1.0, 0.4)
				tree.current_scene.add_child(strike)

		var inv: Inventory = load("res://resources/Inventories/PlayerInventory.tres")
		if inv and is_instance_valid(player) and ("item_equipped" in player) and player.item_equipped:
			inv.sellFromInventory(player.item_equipped, 1)
			Bus.inventory_changed.emit()

class_name B52CarpetStrike
extends Node2D

const Textures = preload("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-elemental_airstrikes_mod/scripts/texture_factory.gd")
const BombScript = preload("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-elemental_airstrikes_mod/scripts/airstrike_bomb.gd")

var damage: float = 240.0
var drop_rate: float = 0.08
var speed: float = 220.0
var t: float = 0.0
var sprite: Sprite2D
var player_ref: Node2D = null

func _ready() -> void:
	z_index = 60
	sprite = Sprite2D.new()
	sprite.texture = Textures.create_b52_texture()
	sprite.scale = Vector2(2.2, 2.2)
	add_child(sprite)

	player_ref = get_tree().root.find_child("Player", true, false)
	if player_ref:
		global_position = Vector2(player_ref.global_position.x + 850.0, player_ref.global_position.y - 140.0)

func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	t += delta
	position.x -= delta * speed

	if t > drop_rate:
		_drop_carpet_bomb(global_position)
		t -= drop_rate

	if is_instance_valid(player_ref) and position.x < (player_ref.global_position.x - 900.0):
		queue_free()
	elif position.x < -1500.0:
		queue_free()

func _drop_carpet_bomb(pos: Vector2) -> void:
	var tree = get_tree()
	if not tree or not tree.current_scene:
		return
	var b = Node2D.new()
	b.set_script(BombScript)
	var tgt_y = pos.y + 450.0
	b.setup(pos, tgt_y, damage, 3, Textures.create_heavy_bomb_texture())
	tree.current_scene.add_child(b)

class_name DragonStrike
extends Node2D

const Textures = preload("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-elemental_airstrikes_mod/scripts/texture_factory.gd")
const BombScript = preload("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-elemental_airstrikes_mod/scripts/airstrike_bomb.gd")

var damage: float = 180.0
var bomb_rate: float = 0.06
var speed: float = 280.0
var t: float = 0.0
var sprite: Sprite2D
var player_ref: Node2D = null

func _ready() -> void:
	z_index = 60
	sprite = Sprite2D.new()
	sprite.texture = Textures.create_dragon_texture()
	sprite.scale = Vector2(2.0, 2.0)
	add_child(sprite)

	player_ref = get_tree().root.find_child("Player", true, false)
	if player_ref:
		global_position = Vector2(player_ref.global_position.x + 850.0, player_ref.global_position.y - 130.0)

func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	t += delta
	position.x -= delta * speed
	position.y += sin(t * 4.0) * 1.5

	if t > bomb_rate:
		_drop_fireball(global_position)
		t -= bomb_rate

	if is_instance_valid(player_ref) and position.x < (player_ref.global_position.x - 900.0):
		queue_free()
	elif position.x < -1500.0:
		queue_free()

	var tree = get_tree()
	if not tree or not tree.current_scene:
		return
	var b = Node2D.new()
	b.set_script(BombScript)
	var tgt_y = pos.y + 450.0
	b.setup(pos, tgt_y, damage, 2, Textures.create_fireball_texture())
	tree.current_scene.add_child(b)

extends Node

var canvas: CanvasLayer

var btn_water: Button

var btn_b52: Button

func _ready() -> void:
	canvas = CanvasLayer.new()
	canvas.layer = 94
	add_child(canvas)

	panel = PanelContainer.new()
	panel.anchor_left = 1.0
	panel.anchor_right = 1.0
	panel.anchor_top = 1.0

	panel.offset_top = -102.0

	var empty_sb = StyleBoxEmpty.new()
	panel.add_theme_stylebox_override("panel", empty_sb)

	var hbox = HBoxContainer.new()
	hbox.alignment = BoxContainer.ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	hbox.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 2)
	panel.add_child(hbox)

	btn_dragon = Button.new()
	btn_dragon.text = "🔥 7"
	btn_dragon.tooltip_text = "Dragon Firebombing (Press 7)"
	btn_dragon.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(34, 24)
	btn_dragon.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 9)
	btn_dragon.pressed.connect(_launch_dragon)
	hbox.add_child(btn_dragon)

	btn_water = Button.new()
	btn_water.text = "💧 8"
	btn_water.tooltip_text = "Water Bomber (Press 8)"
	btn_water.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(34, 24)
	btn_water.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 9)
	btn_water.pressed.connect(_launch_water)
	hbox.add_child(btn_water)

	btn_poison = Button.new()
	btn_poison.text = "☣️ 9"
	btn_poison.tooltip_text = "Poison Gas Strike (Press 9)"
	btn_poison.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(34, 24)
	btn_poison.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 9)
	btn_poison.pressed.connect(_launch_poison)
	hbox.add_child(btn_poison)

	btn_b52 = Button.new()
	btn_b52.text = "💣 0"
	btn_b52.tooltip_text = "B-52 Carpet Bomber (Press 0)"
	btn_b52.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(34, 24)
	btn_b52.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 9)
	btn_b52.pressed.connect(_launch_b52)
	hbox.add_child(btn_b52)

	canvas.add_child(panel)

func _process(_delta: float) -> void:
	var mining = _is_actively_mining()
	if panel and panel.visible != mining:
		panel.visible = mining

func _is_actively_mining() -> bool:
	var tree = get_tree()
	if not tree or not tree.root:
		return false
	var scene = tree.current_scene
	if scene and (scene.name == "MainMenu" or scene.name == "level_select"):
		return false

	var mgmt = tree.root.find_child("management_screen", true, false)
	if not mgmt:
		mgmt = tree.root.find_child("ManagementScreen", true, false)
	if mgmt and mgmt.is_visible_in_tree():
		return false

	var go_btn = tree.root.find_child("GoButton", true, false)
	if not go_btn:
		go_btn = tree.root.find_child("go_button", true, false)
	if go_btn and go_btn.is_visible_in_tree():
		return false

	var pause = tree.root.find_child("PauseMenu", true, false)
	if pause and pause.visible:
		return false

	var tm = tree.root.find_child("TileMapManager", true, false)
	var p = tree.root.find_child("Player", true, false)
	return is_instance_valid(tm) and is_instance_valid(p)

func _unhandled_input(event: InputEvent) -> void:
	if not panel.visible:
		return
	if event is InputEventKey and event.pressed and not event.echo:
		if event.keycode == KEY_7:
			_launch_dragon()
		elif event.keycode == KEY_8:
			_launch_water()
		elif event.keycode == KEY_9:
			_launch_poison()
		elif event.keycode == KEY_0:
			_launch_b52()

func _launch_dragon() -> void:
	var tree = get_tree()
	if not tree or not tree.current_scene:
		return
	var script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-elemental_airstrikes_mod/scripts/dragon_strike.gd")
	if script:
		var strike = Node2D.new()
		strike.set_script(script)
		tree.current_scene.add_child(strike)

func _launch_water() -> void:
	var tree = get_tree()
	if not tree or not tree.current_scene:
		return
	var script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-elemental_airstrikes_mod/scripts/water_bomber_strike.gd")
	if script:
		var strike = Node2D.new()
		strike.set_script(script)
		tree.current_scene.add_child(strike)

func _launch_poison() -> void:
	var tree = get_tree()
	if not tree or not tree.current_scene:
		return
	var script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-elemental_airstrikes_mod/scripts/dragon_strike.gd")
	if script:
		var strike = Node2D.new()
		strike.set_script(script)
		strike.modulate = Color(0.2, 1.0, 0.4)
		tree.current_scene.add_child(strike)

func _launch_b52() -> void:
	var tree = get_tree()
	if not tree or not tree.current_scene:
		return
	var script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-elemental_airstrikes_mod/scripts/b52_carpet_strike.gd")
	if script:
		var strike = Node2D.new()
		strike.set_script(script)
		tree.current_scene.add_child(strike)

class_name ElementalAirstrikeTextures
extends RefCounted

static func create_dragon_texture() -> ImageTexture:
	var img = Image.create(32, 32, false, Image.FORMAT_RGBA8)
	var red = Color(0.9, 0.15, 0.1, 1.0)
	var orange = Color(1.0, 0.55, 0.1, 1.0)
	var yellow = Color(1.0, 0.9, 0.2, 1.0)
	for x in range(32):
		for y in range(32):
			if (y >= 13 and y <= 18 and x >= 4 and x <= 27) or (x >= 12 and x <= 20 and y >= 6 and y <= 25):
				img.set_pixel(x, y, red)
			elif (y >= 10 and y <= 12 and x >= 8 and x <= 24) or (x >= 24 and x <= 28 and y >= 14 and y <= 17):
				img.set_pixel(x, y, orange)
			elif x >= 27 and x <= 30 and y >= 15 and y <= 16:
				img.set_pixel(x, y, yellow)
	return ImageTexture.create_from_image(img)

static func create_water_bomber_texture() -> ImageTexture:
	var img = Image.create(32, 32, false, Image.FORMAT_RGBA8)
	var blue = Color(0.1, 0.55, 0.95, 1.0)
	var white = Color(0.95, 0.95, 1.0, 1.0)
	var yellow = Color(1.0, 0.85, 0.2, 1.0)
	for x in range(32):
		for y in range(32):
			if (y >= 14 and y <= 17 and x >= 3 and x <= 28) or (x >= 13 and x <= 18 and y >= 8 and y <= 23):
				img.set_pixel(x, y, blue)
			elif (y in [13, 18] and x >= 10 and x <= 21):
				img.set_pixel(x, y, white)
			elif x >= 27 and x <= 30 and y >= 15 and y <= 16:
				img.set_pixel(x, y, yellow)
	return ImageTexture.create_from_image(img)

static func create_agent_orange_texture() -> ImageTexture:
	var img = Image.create(32, 32, false, Image.FORMAT_RGBA8)
	var green = Color(0.15, 0.75, 0.25, 1.0)
	var dark_green = Color(0.08, 0.45, 0.15, 1.0)
	var yellow = Color(1.0, 0.85, 0.15, 1.0)
	for x in range(32):
		for y in range(32):
			if (y >= 13 and y <= 17 and x >= 4 and x <= 28) or (x >= 12 and x <= 19 and y >= 9 and y <= 22):
				img.set_pixel(x, y, green)
			elif y in [12, 18] and x >= 8 and x <= 23:
				img.set_pixel(x, y, dark_green)
			elif x >= 27 and x <= 30 and y >= 14 and y <= 16:
				img.set_pixel(x, y, yellow)
	return ImageTexture.create_from_image(img)

static func create_b52_texture() -> ImageTexture:
	var img = Image.create(40, 40, false, Image.FORMAT_RGBA8)
	var camo_dark = Color(0.22, 0.26, 0.22, 1.0)
	var camo_light = Color(0.38, 0.44, 0.35, 1.0)
	var cockpit = Color(0.65, 0.85, 0.95, 1.0)
	for x in range(40):
		for y in range(40):
			if (y >= 17 and y <= 22 and x >= 2 and x <= 37) or (x >= 15 and x <= 24 and y >= 5 and y <= 34):
				img.set_pixel(x, y, camo_dark if (x + y) % 3 == 0 else camo_light)
			elif x in range(32, 36) and y in range(18, 21):
				img.set_pixel(x, y, cockpit)
	return ImageTexture.create_from_image(img)

static func create_fireball_texture() -> ImageTexture:
	var img = Image.create(16, 16, false, Image.FORMAT_RGBA8)
	var core = Color(1.0, 0.95, 0.3, 1.0)
	var outer = Color(0.95, 0.25, 0.1, 1.0)
	for x in range(16):
		for y in range(16):
			var dist = (x - 8)*(x - 8) + (y - 8)*(y - 8)
			if dist <= 16:
				img.set_pixel(x, y, core)
			elif dist <= 36:
				img.set_pixel(x, y, outer)
	return ImageTexture.create_from_image(img)

static func create_water_drop_texture() -> ImageTexture:
	var img = Image.create(16, 16, false, Image.FORMAT_RGBA8)
	var core = Color(0.4, 0.85, 1.0, 1.0)
	var outer = Color(0.1, 0.45, 0.9, 1.0)
	for x in range(16):
		for y in range(16):
			var dist = (x - 8)*(x - 8) + (y - 8)*(y - 8)
			if dist <= 16:
				img.set_pixel(x, y, core)
			elif dist <= 36:
				img.set_pixel(x, y, outer)
	return ImageTexture.create_from_image(img)

static func create_heavy_bomb_texture() -> ImageTexture:
	var img = Image.create(18, 18, false, Image.FORMAT_RGBA8)
	var iron = Color(0.2, 0.2, 0.22, 1.0)
	var yellow = Color(0.95, 0.8, 0.1, 1.0)
	for x in range(18):
		for y in range(18):
			var dist = (x - 9)*(x - 9) + (y - 9)*(y - 9)
			if dist <= 32:
				img.set_pixel(x, y, yellow if x % 4 == 0 else iron)
	return ImageTexture.create_from_image(img)

class_name WaterBomberStrike
extends Node2D

const Textures = preload("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-elemental_airstrikes_mod/scripts/texture_factory.gd")
const BombScript = preload("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-elemental_airstrikes_mod/scripts/airstrike_bomb.gd")

var damage: float = 120.0
var drop_rate: float = 0.05
var speed: float = 260.0
var t: float = 0.0
var sprite: Sprite2D
var player_ref: Node2D = null

func _ready() -> void:
	z_index = 60
	sprite = Sprite2D.new()
	sprite.texture = Textures.create_water_bomber_texture()
	sprite.scale = Vector2(2.0, 2.0)
	add_child(sprite)

	player_ref = get_tree().root.find_child("Player", true, false)
	if player_ref:
		global_position = Vector2(player_ref.global_position.x + 850.0, player_ref.global_position.y - 130.0)

func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	t += delta
	position.x -= delta * speed

	if t > drop_rate:
		_drop_water(global_position)
		t -= drop_rate

	if is_instance_valid(player_ref) and position.x < (player_ref.global_position.x - 900.0):
		queue_free()
	elif position.x < -1500.0:
		queue_free()

func _drop_water(pos: Vector2) -> void:
	var tree = get_tree()
	if not tree or not tree.current_scene:
		return
	var b = Node2D.new()
	b.set_script(BombScript)
	var tgt_y = pos.y + 450.0
	b.setup(pos, tgt_y, damage, 3, Textures.create_water_drop_texture())
	tree.current_scene.add_child(b)

--------
# Elemental Transmutation

**Namespace:** $namespace  
**Compatibility:** Coal LLC v1.4.1.1+ (ModLoader 7.0.1)

---

## Overview
Destroying tiles afflicted with elemental effects triggers economic bounties: burning tiles trigger thermal crystallization for cash bonuses, while corroded acid tiles yield scrap bounties.

---

## Technical Details (For Modders)
Hooks destroy_tile in tile_map_chunk.gd to check active fire and poison ticks, granting currency bonuses directly to player reserves.

---

## Installation
1. Ensure **Coal LLC** has Godot ModLoader 7.0.1 installed.
2. Place the packaged .zip file into your Coal LLC/mods/ directory.
3. Manage or toggle this mod directly via the in-game **Mod Manager**.
--------

const MOD_DIR := "gareth-elemental_transmutation_mod"

func _init() -> void:

	var extensions_dir_path: String = mod_dir_path.path_join("extensions")
	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks(

	)
	ModLoaderLog.info("Hooks successfully installed.", LOG_NAME)


func _ready() -> void:
	ModLoaderLog.info("Ready and active in scene!", LOG_NAME)

# Elemental Transmutation

**Namespace:** $namespace  
**Compatibility:** Coal LLC v1.4.1.1+ (ModLoader 7.0.1)

---

## Overview
Destroying tiles afflicted with elemental effects triggers economic bounties: burning tiles trigger thermal crystallization for cash bonuses, while corroded acid tiles yield scrap bounties.

---

## Technical Details (For Modders)
Hooks destroy_tile in tile_map_chunk.gd to check active fire and poison ticks, granting currency bonuses directly to player reserves.

---

## Installation
1. Ensure **Coal LLC** has Godot ModLoader 7.0.1 installed.
2. Place the packaged .zip file into your Coal LLC/mods/ directory.
3. Manage or toggle this mod directly via the in-game **Mod Manager**.

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-elemental_transmutation_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-elemental_transmutation_mod\extensions\scenes\tilemaps\tile_map_chunk.hooks.gd
==================================================
--------
extends Object

const LOG_NAME := "gareth-elemental_transmutation_mod:Hook"


func destroy_tile(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, idx: int, tile_properties: Dictionary) -> void:
	var chunk := chain.reference_object as TileMapChunk
	var had_poison: bool = chunk.poison_tiles.has(idx)
	var had_burn: bool = chunk.burn_tiles.has(idx)

	chain.execute_next([idx, tile_properties])

	if not had_poison and not had_burn:
		return

	var origin := chunk.array_to_global_pos(idx)
	var world_pos := Vector2(origin.x * 16, origin.y * 16)

	if had_burn:
		ModLoaderLog.info("[Transmutation] Burned tile destroyed at %s! Crystallized: +$35" % str(origin), LOG_NAME)
		if Gvars:
			Gvars.CashCount += 35
			Gvars.CashCount += 35
		Bus.BombExplode.emit(world_pos, 2.0, 30.0)

	if had_poison:
		ModLoaderLog.info("[Transmutation] Corroded tile destroyed at %s! Slag payout: +$15" % str(origin), LOG_NAME)
		if Gvars:
			Gvars.CashCount += 15
			Gvars.CashCount += 15

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-employee_passives_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-employee_passives_mod\extensions\scenes\AutoMiners\employee_miner_2.hooks.gd

extends Object

func _physics_process(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, delta: float) -> void:
	var miner = chain.reference_object
	if is_instance_valid(miner):
		var range_stat: float = _get_passive_stat("employee_miner_range")
		var speed_stat: float = _get_passive_stat("employee_miner_pickup_speed")

		if range_stat > 0.0 and ("pickaxe_range" in miner):
			miner.pickaxe_range = int(round(3.0 * (1.0 + range_stat)))
			miner.search_range = int(round(10.0 * (1.0 + range_stat)))

		var is_moving = false
		if ("state" in miner) and ("States" in miner):
			is_moving = (miner.state == miner.States.MOVING_TO_TARGET)

		if speed_stat > 0.0 and is_moving and ("velocity" in miner):
			miner.velocity.x *= (1.0 + speed_stat)

		if range_stat > 0.0 and is_moving and ("target_pos" in miner):
			var reach: float = 16.0 * (1.0 + range_stat)
			if abs(miner.global_position.x - miner.target_pos.x) < reach:
				if "mining_animation_player" in miner:
					miner.mining_animation_player.play("mining")
				miner.state = miner.States.MINING

		if speed_stat > 0.0:
			_vacuum_nearby_loot(miner, 52.0 * (1.0 + range_stat), 260.0 * (1.0 + speed_stat), delta)

	chain.execute_next([delta])

func _vacuum_nearby_loot(miner: Node2D, radius: float, pull_speed: float, delta: float) -> void:
	var tree = miner.get_tree()
	if not tree or not tree.root:
		return
	var loot_mgr = tree.root.find_child("LootDrops", true, false)
	if not is_instance_valid(loot_mgr):
		return
		
	var m_pos = miner.global_position
	var r_sq = radius * radius
	
	for child in loot_mgr.get_children():
		if is_instance_valid(child) and ("global_position" in child):
			var d_sq = m_pos.distance_squared_to(child.global_position)
			if d_sq <= r_sq:
				var dir = (m_pos - child.global_position).normalized()
				child.global_position += dir * pull_speed * delta

func _get_passive_stat(stat_name: String) -> float:
	if not Gvars:
		return 0.0
	if ("custom_passives" in Gvars) and (Gvars.custom_passives is Dictionary) and Gvars.custom_passives.has(stat_name):
		return float(Gvars.custom_passives[stat_name])
	if ("passives" in Gvars) and Gvars.passives:
		if Gvars.passives.has_meta(stat_name):
			return float(Gvars.passives.get_meta(stat_name))
		if stat_name in Gvars.passives:
			return float(Gvars.passives.get(stat_name))
	return 0.0

extends Object

func find_loot(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, collector) -> void:
	var ld = chain.reference_object
	if is_instance_valid(ld):
		var range_stat: float = _get_passive_stat("collector_range")
		if range_stat > 0.0 and ("collector_sample_live_limit" in ld):
			ld.collector_sample_live_limit = int(round(25.0 * (1.0 + range_stat)))
	chain.execute_next([collector])

func _get_passive_stat(stat_name: String) -> float:
	if not Gvars:
		return 0.0
	if ("custom_passives" in Gvars) and (Gvars.custom_passives is Dictionary) and Gvars.custom_passives.has(stat_name):
		return float(Gvars.custom_passives[stat_name])
	if ("passives" in Gvars) and Gvars.passives:
		if Gvars.passives.has_meta(stat_name):
			return float(Gvars.passives.get_meta(stat_name))
		if stat_name in Gvars.passives:
			return float(Gvars.passives.get(stat_name))
	return 0.0

==================================================
--------
extends Object

func _physics_process(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, delta: float) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([delta])
	var ufo = chain.reference_object
	if not is_instance_valid(ufo):
		return

	var range_stat: float = _get_passive_stat("collector_range")
	if range_stat <= 0.0:
		return

	var magnet_radius: float = 64.0 * (1.0 + range_stat)
	var pull_speed: float = 350.0 * (1.0 + range_stat * 0.5)
	var tree = ufo.get_tree()
	if not tree or not tree.root:
		return
	var loot_mgr = tree.root.find_child("LootDrops", true, false)
	if not loot_mgr:
		return

	var u_pos = ufo.global_position
	var r_sq = magnet_radius * magnet_radius
	for child in loot_mgr.get_children():
		if is_instance_valid(child) and ("global_position" in child):
			var d_sq = u_pos.distance_squared_to(child.global_position)
			if d_sq <= r_sq:
				var dir = (u_pos - child.global_position).normalized()
				child.global_position += dir * pull_speed * delta
				if d_sq < 324.0:
					if child.has_method("pickup"):
						child.pickup(ufo)
					elif ufo.has_method("on_ItemPickup") and ("pickupItem" in child):
						ufo.on_ItemPickup(child.pickupItem, ufo)
						child.queue_free()

func _get_passive_stat(stat_name: String) -> float:
	if not Gvars:
		return 0.0
	if ("custom_passives" in Gvars) and (Gvars.custom_passives is Dictionary) and Gvars.custom_passives.has(stat_name):
		return float(Gvars.custom_passives[stat_name])
	if ("passives" in Gvars) and Gvars.passives:
		if Gvars.passives.has_meta(stat_name):
			return float(Gvars.passives.get_meta(stat_name))
		if stat_name in Gvars.passives:
			return float(Gvars.passives.get(stat_name))
	return 0.0

# End-Game Staffs & Poison Guns Arsenal

**Namespace:** $namespace  
**Compatibility:** Coal LLC v1.4.1.1+ (ModLoader 7.0.1)

---

## Overview
Injects previously unreferenced Tiers 12 through 15 Magic Staffs (Spinel, Uranium, Moonstone, Onyx) and all 15 material grades of Caustic Poison Guns directly into the equipment shop.

Provides late-game elemental scaling and weapon choices beyond the standard tier 10 boundaries.

---

## Technical Details (For Modders)
Dynamically appends staff and poison gun .tres resources into item_unlocks.all_equipment_items and item_unlocks.all_items during mod initialization.

---

## Installation
1. Ensure **Coal LLC** has Godot ModLoader 7.0.1 installed.
2. Place the packaged .zip file into your Coal LLC/mods/ directory.
3. Manage or toggle this mod directly via the in-game **Mod Manager**.
--------
