extends Node

const STAFF_PATHS: Array[String] = [
	"res://resources/Items/Equipment/staffs/staff_12_spinel.tres",
	"res://resources/Items/Equipment/staffs/staff_13_uranium.tres",
	"res://resources/Items/Equipment/staffs/staff_14_moonstone.tres",
	"res://resources/Items/Equipment/staffs/staff_15_onyx.tres"
]

const POISON_GUN_PREFIX := "res://resources/Items/Equipment/alternative_guns/poison_guns/poison_gun_"
const POISON_GUN_TIERS: Array[String] = [
	"01_shoddy", "02_copper", "03_iron", "04_sapphire", "05_emerald",
	"06_silver", "07_amethyst", "08_gold", "09_ruby", "10_diamond",
	"11_pinkdiamond", "12_spinel", "13_uranium", "14_moonstone", "15_onyx"
]

func _ready() -> void:
	var item_unlocks = load("res://resources/unlockable_items/scripts/item_unlocks.tres")
	if not item_unlocks:
		return

	for sp in STAFF_PATHS:
		if ResourceLoader.exists(sp):
			var staff_res = load(sp)
			if staff_res and not (staff_res in item_unlocks.all_equipment_items):
				item_unlocks.all_equipment_items.append(staff_res)
			if staff_res and not (staff_res in item_unlocks.all_items):
				item_unlocks.all_items.append(staff_res)

	for tier in POISON_GUN_TIERS:
		var p_path := "%s%s.tres" % [POISON_GUN_PREFIX, tier]
		if ResourceLoader.exists(p_path):
			var gun_res = load(p_path)
			if gun_res and not (gun_res in item_unlocks.all_equipment_items):
				item_unlocks.all_equipment_items.append(gun_res)
			if gun_res and not (gun_res in item_unlocks.all_items):
				item_unlocks.all_items.append(gun_res)

	ModLoaderLog.info("End-game Magic Staffs (12-15) and Poison Guns (1-15) registered into shop.", "gareth-endgame_arsenal_mod")

# Excavation Frenzy

**Namespace:** $namespace  
**Compatibility:** Coal LLC v1.4.1.1+ (ModLoader 7.0.1)

---

## Overview
Chains rapid mining strikes into a momentum combo multiplier. Consecutive pickaxe hits accelerate swing speed up to 3x and unleash a shockwave on every 5th strike.

---

## Technical Details (For Modders)
Hooks mine_action in player_2.gd, tracking a 1.2s timeout counter and scaling animation player speed proportionally with combo rank.

---

## Installation

2. Place the packaged .zip file into your Coal LLC/mods/ directory.
3. Manage or toggle this mod directly via the in-game **Mod Manager**.

# Excavation Frenzy

**Namespace:** $namespace  
**Compatibility:** Coal LLC v1.4.1.1+ (ModLoader 7.0.1)

---

## Overview
Chains rapid mining strikes into a momentum combo multiplier. Consecutive pickaxe hits accelerate swing speed up to 3x and unleash a shockwave on every 5th strike.

---

## Technical Details (For Modders)
Hooks mine_action in player_2.gd, tracking a 1.2s timeout counter and scaling animation player speed proportionally with combo rank.

---

## Installation
1. Ensure **Coal LLC** has Godot ModLoader 7.0.1 installed.
2. Place the packaged .zip file into your Coal LLC/mods/ directory.
3. Manage or toggle this mod directly via the in-game **Mod Manager**.
--------
==================================================
FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-excavation_frenzy_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-excavation_frenzy_mod\extensions\scripts\StateMachine\Player2\player_2.hooks.gd
==================================================
--------
extends Object

const LOG_NAME := "gareth-excavation_frenzy_mod:Hook"
static var combo_count: int = 0
static var last_mine_time: float = 0.0


func mine_action(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	var player := chain.reference_object as Player
	var now: float = Time.get_ticks_msec() / 1000.0
	if (now - last_mine_time) > 2.2:
		combo_count = 0
	last_mine_time = now
	combo_count = mini(combo_count + 1, 5)

	if player and player.animation_tool:
		player.animation_tool.speed_scale = 1.0 + (combo_count * 0.4)

	ModLoaderLog.info("[Frenzy] Swing! Combo: %d/5 | Animation Scale: %.2fx" % [
		combo_count,
		player.animation_tool.speed_scale if player and player.animation_tool else 1.0
	], LOG_NAME)

	if combo_count >= 5:
		var strike_pos: Vector2 = player.pointer_global_pos if player.mine_where_mouse_is else player.global_position
		ModLoaderLog.info("[Frenzy] MAX FRENZY SHOCKWAVE at %s!" % str(strike_pos), LOG_NAME)
		Bus.BombExplode.emit(strike_pos, 3.5, 60.0)
		combo_count = 0

	chain.execute_next()

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-game_speed_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-game_speed_mod\scripts\speed_controller.gd
==================================================
--------
extends Node

var canvas: CanvasLayer
var panel: PanelContainer
var lbl_speed: Label
var btn_slower: Button
var btn_faster: Button
var btn_end_day: Button

const SPEEDS := [0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0, 5.0]
var current_idx := 3

func _ready() -> void:
	canvas = CanvasLayer.new()
	canvas.layer = 95
	add_child(canvas)

	panel = PanelContainer.new()
	panel.anchor_left = 1.0
	panel.anchor_right = 1.0
	panel.anchor_top = 1.0
	panel.anchor_bottom = 1.0
	panel.offset_left = -210.0
	panel.offset_right = -10.0
	panel.offset_top = -42.0
	panel.offset_bottom = -16.0
	panel.visible = false

	var empty_sb = StyleBoxEmpty.new()
	panel.add_theme_stylebox_override("panel", empty_sb)

	var hbox = HBoxContainer.new()
	hbox.alignment = BoxContainer.ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	hbox.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 4)
	panel.add_child(hbox)

	btn_slower = Button.new()
	btn_slower.text = "◀"
	btn_slower.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(22, 22)
	btn_slower.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 10)
	btn_slower.pressed.connect(_decrease_speed)
	hbox.add_child(btn_slower)

	lbl_speed = Label.new()
	lbl_speed.text = "1.00x"
	lbl_speed.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	lbl_speed.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(40, 22)
	lbl_speed.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 10)
	hbox.add_child(lbl_speed)

	btn_faster = Button.new()
	btn_faster.text = "▶"
	btn_faster.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(22, 22)
	btn_faster.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 10)
	btn_faster.pressed.connect(_increase_speed)
	hbox.add_child(btn_faster)

	btn_end_day = Button.new()
	btn_end_day.text = "⏰ End Day"
	btn_end_day.tooltip_text = "Finish Shift Early / Emergency Exit [Hotkey F8]"
	btn_end_day.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(78, 22)
	btn_end_day.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 10)
	btn_end_day.modulate = Color(1.0, 0.5, 0.5)
	btn_end_day.pressed.connect(_finish_shift_early)
	hbox.add_child(btn_end_day)

	canvas.add_child(panel)

func _process(_delta: float) -> void:
	var mining = _is_actively_mining()
	if panel and panel.visible != mining:
		panel.visible = mining

func _unhandled_input(event: InputEvent) -> void:
	if panel and panel.visible and event is InputEventKey and event.pressed and not event.echo:
		if event.keycode == KEY_F8:
			_finish_shift_early()

func _is_actively_mining() -> bool:
	var tree = get_tree()
	if not tree or not tree.root: return false
	var scene = tree.current_scene
	if scene and (scene.name == "MainMenu" or scene.name == "level_select"): return false

	var mgmt = tree.root.find_child("management_screen", true, false)
	if not mgmt: mgmt = tree.root.find_child("ManagementScreen", true, false)
	if mgmt and mgmt.is_visible_in_tree(): return false

	var tm = tree.root.find_child("TileMapManager", true, false)
	var p = tree.root.find_child("Player", true, false)
	return is_instance_valid(tm) and is_instance_valid(p)

func _increase_speed() -> void:
	if current_idx < SPEEDS.size() - 1:
		current_idx += 1
		_apply_speed()

func _decrease_speed() -> void:
	if current_idx > 0:
		current_idx -= 1
		_apply_speed()

func _apply_speed() -> void:
	var sp = SPEEDS[current_idx]
	Engine.time_scale = sp
	lbl_speed.text = "%.2fx" % sp

func _finish_shift_early() -> void:
	Engine.time_scale = 1.0
	current_idx = 3
	_apply_speed()
	var g = Engine.get_main_loop().root.get_node_or_null("/root/Game")
	if g:
		if g.has_method("on_EndDay"):
			g.on_EndDay()
		elif g.has_method("change_stage") and Gconsts and ("Stages" in Gconsts):
			g.change_stage(Gconsts.Stages._END_OF_DAY_OVERLAY, Vector2.ZERO)

extends Node

const MOD_DIR := "gareth-grav_dreadnought_mod"

var starter_weapon = null
var all_dread_items: Array = []

const TIER_DATA := [
	{ "id": "01_shoddy",      "name": "Shoddy Dreadnought Cannon",        "dmg": 110.0,   "rate": 0.55,  "price": 0.0 },
	{ "id": "02_copper",      "name": "Copper Dreadnought Cannon",        "dmg": 160.0,   "rate": 0.52,  "price": 350.0 },

	{ "id": "05_emerald",     "name": "Emerald Dreadnought Cannon",       "dmg": 480.0,   "rate": 0.43,  "price": 45000.0 },
	{ "id": "06_silver",      "name": "Silver Dreadnought Cannon",        "dmg": 700.0,   "rate": 0.40,  "price": 200000.0 },
	{ "id": "07_amethyst",    "name": "Amethyst Dreadnought Cannon",      "dmg": 1000.0,  "rate": 0.37,  "price": 850000.0 },
	{ "id": "08_gold",        "name": "Gold Dreadnought Cannon",          "dmg": 1500.0,  "rate": 0.34,  "price": 4000000.0 },
	{ "id": "09_ruby",        "name": "Ruby Dreadnought Cannon",          "dmg": 2200.0,  "rate": 0.31,  "price": 20000000.0 },

	{ "id": "11_pinkdiamond", "name": "Pink Diamond Dreadnought Cannon",  "dmg": 5000.0,  "rate": 0.25,  "price": 600000000.0 },
	{ "id": "12_spinel",      "name": "Spinel Dreadnought Cannon",        "dmg": 7700.0,  "rate": 0.22,  "price": 3500000000.0 },
	{ "id": "13_uranium",     "name": "Uranium Dreadnought Cannon",       "dmg": 12000.0, "rate": 0.19,  "price": 25000000000.0 },
	{ "id": "14_moonstone",   "name": "Moonstone Dreadnought Cannon",     "dmg": 19000.0, "rate": 0.16,  "price": 180000000000.0 },
	{ "id": "15_onyx",        "name": "Onyx Dreadnought Cannon",          "dmg": 32000.0, "rate": 0.13,  "price": 1500000000000.0 }
]

func _load_image_texture(path: String) -> ImageTexture:
	if not FileAccess.file_exists(path):
		return null
	var img = Image.load_from_file(path)
	if not img:
		return null
	return ImageTexture.create_from_image(img)

func _ready() -> void:
	_register_weapon_tiers()
	_register_profession()

func _register_weapon_tiers() -> void:
	var item_script = load("res://resources/Items/Scripts/Item.gd")
	var buy_script = load("res://resources/BuyEffects/Scripts/AddToInventory.gd")
	var pickup_script = load("res://resources/PickupEffects/Scripts/PickupToInventory.gd")
	var equip_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-grav_dreadnought_mod/scripts/grav_dreadnought_equip.gd")
	var pickup_sound = load("res://assets/sound/sound effects/SwordThud.wav")

	var item_unlocks = load("res://resources/unlockable_items/scripts/item_unlocks.tres")

	for t in TIER_DATA:
		var item = item_script.new()
		item.itemID = "dread_cannon_" + t["id"]
		item.itemName = t["name"]
		item.itemDescription = "Heavy mass orbital cannon.\nShatters subterranean rock with high-density kinetic ordnance.\nDamage: %d | Cycle: %.2fs" % [int(t["dmg"]), float(t["rate"])]

		var tex_path := "res://mods-unpacked/gareth-grav_dreadnought_mod/textures/dread_cannon_%s.png" % t["id"]
		item.itemTexture = _load_image_texture(tex_path)

		var equip = equip_script.new()
		equip.damage = t["dmg"]
		equip.fire_rate = t["rate"]
		item.itemEquipEffect = equip

		var buy = buy_script.new()
		item.itemBuyEffect = buy
		var pickup = pickup_script.new()
		item.itemPickupEffect = pickup
		item.pickupSound = pickup_sound

		item.itemBuyPrice = t["price"]
		item.itemSellPrice = t["price"] * 0.5
		item.itemUnlocked = true
		item.itemType = "dread_cannon"

		all_dread_items.append(item)

		if item_unlocks:
			if not (item in item_unlocks.all_equipment_items):
				item_unlocks.all_equipment_items.append(item)

	if all_dread_items.size() > 0:
		starter_weapon = all_dread_items[0]

	ModLoaderLog.info("15 Gravitational Dreadnought Cannon tiers registered into equipment shop.", MOD_DIR)

func _register_profession() -> void:
	if not Gvars or not ("profession_manager" in Gvars) or not Gvars.profession_manager:
		return
	var pm = Gvars.profession_manager
	var prof_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-grav_dreadnought_mod/scripts/grav_dreadnought_profession.gd")
	var p = prof_script.new()
	p.profession_name = "Gravitational Dreadnought"
	p.description = "Heavy armored fortress.\nExtreme mass; shatters rock with heavy orbital cannons.\nStarts with the Shoddy Dreadnought Cannon."

	var icon_path := "res://mods-unpacked/gareth-grav_dreadnought_mod/textures/grav_dreadnought_icon.png"
	var icon_tex = _load_image_texture(icon_path)
	if icon_tex:
		p.image = icon_tex

	pm.all_professions[108] = { "profession": p, "is_unlocked": true }
	ModLoaderLog.info("Gravitational Dreadnought Class (ID 108) registered cleanly.", MOD_DIR)

# Gravitational Dreadnought Class

**Namespace:** $namespace  
**Compatibility:** Coal LLC v1.4.1.1+ (ModLoader 7.0.1)

---

## Overview
Heavy armor class with high gravitational mass. Shotgun pellets and artillery shells fired enter a circular orbit around the player.

---

## Technical Details (For Modders)
Modifies bullet velocity vectors in _physics_process to apply centripetal acceleration relative to player global coordinates.

---

## Installation
1. Ensure **Coal LLC** has Godot ModLoader 7.0.1 installed.
2. Place the packaged .zip file into your Coal LLC/mods/ directory.
3. Manage or toggle this mod directly via the in-game **Mod Manager**.
--------
==================================================
FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-grav_dreadnought_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-grav_dreadnought_mod\scripts\grav_dreadnought_equip.gd
==================================================
--------
class_name GravDreadnoughtEquipEffect
extends EquipEffect

@export var damage: float = 110.0
@export var fire_rate: float = 0.55

var weapon_visual: Sprite2D = null
var fire_cooldown: float = 0.0
var _is_using: bool = false
var _auto_fire_toggle: bool = false
var audio_player: AudioStreamPlayer2D = null

# Active orbiting shells array: Array of Dictionary { "node": Node2D, "angle": float, "radius": float, "life": float }
var _orbiting_shells: Array = []
const MAX_ORBITAL_SHELLS: int = 6
const ORBIT_RADIUS: float = 38.0
const ORBIT_SPEED: float = 3.8

func start(player, item):
	if "tool" in player and player.tool:
		player.tool.visible = false

	if ResourceLoader.exists("res://assets/sound/sound effects/gun.wav"):
		audio_player = AudioStreamPlayer2D.new()
		audio_player.stream = load("res://assets/sound/sound effects/gun.wav")
		player.add_child(audio_player)

	weapon_visual = Sprite2D.new()
	weapon_visual.texture = item.itemTexture
	weapon_visual.offset = Vector2(8, 0)
	weapon_visual.position = Vector2(4, 2)
	weapon_visual.visible = true
	player.add_child(weapon_visual)
	_is_using = false
	_auto_fire_toggle = false

func end(player):
	if "tool" in player and player.tool:
		player.tool.visible = true
	if audio_player and is_instance_valid(audio_player):
		audio_player.queue_free()
	if weapon_visual and is_instance_valid(weapon_visual):
		weapon_visual.queue_free()

	for shell in _orbiting_shells:
		if is_instance_valid(shell.get("node")):
			shell["node"].queue_free()
	_orbiting_shells.clear()

	_is_using = false
	_auto_fire_toggle = false

func physics_process(player, delta):
	if fire_cooldown > 0.0:
		fire_cooldown -= delta

	if weapon_visual and is_instance_valid(weapon_visual):
		var p_pos: Vector2 = player.global_position
		var pointer_pos: Vector2 = player.pointer_global_pos
		var angle: float = (pointer_pos - p_pos).angle()
		weapon_visual.rotation = angle
		weapon_visual.flip_v = (pointer_pos.x < p_pos.x)

	if Input.is_action_pressed("useItem") or Input.is_mouse_button_pressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT):
		_is_using = true
	elif Input.is_action_just_released("useItem") or (not Input.is_mouse_button_pressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT) and not Input.is_mouse_button_pressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_RIGHT) and not _auto_fire_toggle):
		_is_using = false

	if Input.is_action_just_pressed("toggle_use_item") or Input.is_action_just_pressed("rightMouseClick"):
		_auto_fire_toggle = not _auto_fire_toggle
		_is_using = _auto_fire_toggle

	if Input.is_mouse_button_pressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_RIGHT):
		_is_using = true

	if _is_using and fire_cooldown <= 0.0:
		_spawn_orbital_shell(player)

	_update_orbiting_shells(player, delta)

func _get_passive_stat(stat_name: String) -> float:
	if not Gvars or not ("passives" in Gvars) or not Gvars.passives:
		return 0.0
	var p = Gvars.passives
	if p.has_meta(stat_name):
		return float(p.get_meta(stat_name))
	if stat_name in p:
		return float(p.get(stat_name))
	return 0.0

func _spawn_orbital_shell(player) -> void:
	var rate_mult: float = 1.0 + _get_passive_stat("dread_fire_rate")
	fire_cooldown = max(0.10, fire_rate / rate_mult)

	if audio_player and is_instance_valid(audio_player):
		audio_player.pitch_scale = randf_range(0.75, 0.9)
		audio_player.play()

	# Clean expired shells
	var active_shells: Array = []
	for s in _orbiting_shells:
		if is_instance_valid(s.get("node")):
			active_shells.append(s)
	_orbiting_shells = active_shells

	if _orbiting_shells.size() >= MAX_ORBITAL_SHELLS:
		var oldest = _orbiting_shells.pop_front()
		if is_instance_valid(oldest.get("node")):
			oldest["node"].queue_free()

	var p_pos: Vector2 = player.global_position
	var pointer_pos: Vector2 = player.pointer_global_pos
	var spawn_angle: float = (pointer_pos - p_pos).angle()

	var shell_node := Sprite2D.new()
	shell_node.top_level = true
	shell_node.z_index = 45
	if weapon_visual and weapon_visual.texture:
		shell_node.texture = weapon_visual.texture
		shell_node.scale = Vector2(0.45, 0.45)
	player.get_parent().add_child(shell_node)

	_orbiting_shells.append({
		"node": shell_node,
		"angle": spawn_angle,
		"radius": ORBIT_RADIUS,
		"life": 12.0
	})

func _update_orbiting_shells(player, delta: float) -> void:
	var tilemap = player.tilemap
	var p_pos: Vector2 = player.global_position
	var dmg_mult: float = 1.0 + _get_passive_stat("dread_shell_damage")
	var eff_dmg: float = damage * dmg_mult

	var surviving: Array = []
	for shell in _orbiting_shells:
		var node = shell.get("node")
		if not is_instance_valid(node):
			continue

		shell["life"] -= delta
		if shell["life"] <= 0.0:
			node.queue_free()
			continue

		shell["angle"] += ORBIT_SPEED * delta
		var shell_pos: Vector2 = p_pos + Vector2(cos(shell["angle"]), sin(shell["angle"])) * shell["radius"]
		node.global_position = shell_pos
		node.rotation = shell["angle"] + 1.57

		var collided: bool = false
		if tilemap:
			var local_pos: Vector2 = tilemap.to_local(shell_pos)
			var cell: Vector2i = tilemap.local_to_map(local_pos)
			if tilemap.is_tile_breakable(cell):
				if tilemap.has_method("damageTile"):
					tilemap.damageTile(shell_pos, eff_dmg)
				if tilemap.has_method("damageTileCoords"):
					tilemap.damageTileCoords(cell, eff_dmg)
				_spawn_kinetic_burst(player, shell_pos)
				node.queue_free()
				collided = true

		if not collided:
			surviving.append(shell)

	_orbiting_shells = surviving

func _spawn_kinetic_burst(player: Node2D, at_pos: Vector2) -> void:
	var parent = player.get_parent()
	if not parent:
		parent = player
	var ring := Line2D.new()
	ring.top_level = true
	ring.z_index = 50
	ring.width = 3.0
	ring.default_color = Color(0.65, 1.0, 0.25, 0.95)
	for i in range(13):
		var ang: float = float(i) * TAU / 12.0
		ring.add_point(at_pos + Vector2(cos(ang), sin(ang)) * 6.0)
	parent.add_child(ring)

	var tween = parent.create_tween()
	tween.tween_property(ring, "scale", Vector2(2.4, 2.4), 0.10)
	tween.tween_property(ring, "modulate:a", 0.0, 0.10)
	tween.tween_callback(ring.queue_free)

class_name GravDreadnoughtProfession
extends Profession

func _profession_effect(data: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	var mod_node = Engine.get_main_loop().root.get_node_or_null("ModLoader/gareth-grav_dreadnought_mod")
	if mod_node and "starter_weapon" in mod_node and mod_node.starter_weapon:
		if data.has("inventory_starters"):
			data["inventory_starters"] = [{
				"item": mod_node.starter_weapon,
				"count": 1
			}]

	if not data.has("items_locked"):
		data["items_locked"] = []
	data["items_locked"].append_array([
		"pickaxe", "axe", "battleaxe", "drill", "pistol", "shotgun", "rifle",
		"minigun", "staff", "water_staff", "poison_staff", "bomb", "mortar_gun",
		"tank", "jet", "orb", "laser", "flamethrower", "water_gun", "poison_gun",
		"boxing_glove", "roundhouse_kick", "turret", "sword", "bouncing_ball", "fracker"
	])

	if data.has("unlocked_passives"):
		data["unlocked_passives"] = [
			"dread_shell_damage",
			"dread_fire_rate",
			"dread_impact_radius",
			"player_sprint_speed",
			"player_jump_velocity",
			"player_climb_speed"
		]

	return data

# Bouncing Grenade Arsenal

**Namespace:** $namespace  
**Compatibility:** Coal LLC v1.4.1.1+ (ModLoader 7.0.1)

---

## Overview
Adds 15 material grades of parabolic cluster grenades (Shoddy through Onyx) that rebound off solid subterranean geometry and detonate on timed fuses.

---

## Technical Details (For Modders)
Spawns rigidbodies with bounce physics materials and hooks collision impulses to trigger localized tile destruction.

---

## Installation
1. Ensure **Coal LLC** has Godot ModLoader 7.0.1 installed.
2. Place the packaged .zip file into your Coal LLC/mods/ directory.
3. Manage or toggle this mod directly via the in-game **Mod Manager**.

extends Node

const MOD_DIR := "gareth-grenade_mod"

func _ready() -> void:
	var item_unlocks = load("res://resources/unlockable_items/scripts/item_unlocks.tres")
	if not item_unlocks:
		return

	var tiers = [
		"01_shoddy", "02_copper", "03_iron", "04_sapphire", "05_emerald",
		"06_silver", "07_amethyst", "08_gold", "09_ruby", "10_diamond",
		"11_pinkdiamond", "12_spinel", "13_uranium", "14_moonstone", "15_onyx"
	]

	for t in tiers:
		var item_path := "res://mods-unpacked/gareth-grenade_mod/items/bouncing_grenade_%s.tres" % t
		if ResourceLoader.exists(item_path):
			var item = load(item_path)
			var tex_path := "res://mods-unpacked/gareth-grenade_mod/textures/grenade_%s.png" % t
			if FileAccess.file_exists(tex_path):
				var img = Image.load_from_file(tex_path)
				if img:
					item.itemTexture = ImageTexture.create_from_image(img)
			if item and not (item in item_unlocks.all_equipment_items):
				item_unlocks.all_equipment_items.append(item)
			if item and not (item in item_unlocks.all_items):
				item_unlocks.all_items.append(item)

	ModLoaderLog.info("15 Bouncing Grenades registered with color-coded textures.", MOD_DIR)

class_name BouncingGrenade
extends CharacterBody2D

const GRAVITY: float = 580.0
const BOUNCE_DAMPING: float = 0.65
const BOMB_EXPLODE = preload("res://assets/sound/sound effects/bombExplode.wav")

var damage: float = 100.0
var radius: float = 4.0
var fuse_time: float = 2.5
var bomb_texture: Texture2D

var _timer: float = 0.0
var _audio: AudioStreamPlayer2D
var _sprite: Sprite2D

func _ready() -> void:
	_sprite = Sprite2D.new()
	_sprite.texture = bomb_texture
	add_child(_sprite)

	_audio = AudioStreamPlayer2D.new()
	_audio.stream = BOMB_EXPLODE
	add_child(_audio)

	# Setup simple 8x8 circle collision
	var shape := CircleShape2D.new()
	shape.radius = 6.0
	var col := CollisionShape2D.new()
	col.shape = shape
	add_child(col)

func _physics_process(delta: float) -> void:
	velocity.y += GRAVITY * delta
	_sprite.rotation += velocity.x * delta * 0.05

	var collision := move_and_collide(velocity * delta)
	if collision:
		velocity = velocity.bounce(collision.get_normal()) * BOUNCE_DAMPING
		if velocity.length() < 15.0:
			velocity = Vector2.ZERO

	_timer += delta
	# Flashing visual near explosion
	if _timer > (fuse_time - 0.75):
		_sprite.modulate = Color.RED if int(_timer * 12) % 2 == 0 else Color.WHITE

	if _timer >= fuse_time:
		explode()

func explode() -> void:
	set_physics_process(false)
	Bus.BombExplode.emit(global_position, radius, damage * (1.0 + Gvars.passives.bomb_damage))
	if Gvars.bonus_equipment_manager.apply_knockback or Gvars.bonus_equipment_manager.apply_bomb_knockback:
		var tree = get_tree()
		if tree:
			var player = tree.get_first_node_in_group("player")
			if player and "apply_knockback" in player:
				player.apply_knockback()
	_audio.play()
	visible = false
	var t = get_tree().create_tween()
	t.tween_interval(0.6)
	t.tween_callback(queue_free)

class_name GrenadeEquipEffect
extends EquipEffect

@export var damage: float = 100.0
@export var radius: float = 4.0
@export var animated_texture: Texture2D

const MULTI_CLICK_DELAY: int = 25
const PLACEMENT_RATE: int = 8

var _using_item: bool = false
var first_click: bool = true
var delay: int = MULTI_CLICK_DELAY

func start(player, item):
	_using_item = false
	player.tool.texture = null
	player.equipped_item_sprite_parent.visible = true
	player.equipped_item_sprite.texture = item.itemTexture

func end(player):
	player.equipped_item_sprite_parent.visible = false
	player.equipped_item_sprite.texture = null

func physics_process(player, delta):
	if Input.is_action_pressed("useItem"):
		_using_item = true
	elif Input.is_action_just_released("useItem"):
		_using_item = false
	elif Input.is_action_just_pressed("toggle_use_item"):
		_using_item = not _using_item

	if _using_item:
		if first_click:
			throw_grenade(player)
			first_click = false
			delay = MULTI_CLICK_DELAY
		elif delay == 0:
			throw_grenade(player)
			delay = PLACEMENT_RATE
		else:
			delay -= 1
	else:
		delay = MULTI_CLICK_DELAY
		first_click = true

func throw_grenade(player):
	var grenade_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-grenade_mod/scripts/bouncing_grenade.gd")
	var grenade := CharacterBody2D.new()
	grenade.set_script(grenade_script)
	grenade.damage = damage
	grenade.radius = radius
	grenade.bomb_texture = animated_texture
	grenade.global_position = player.global_position

	var mouse_pos: Vector2 = player.get_global_mouse_position()
	var throw_dir: Vector2 = (mouse_pos - player.global_position).normalized()
	grenade.velocity = Vector2(throw_dir.x * 380.0, min(-180.0, throw_dir.y * 380.0 - 140.0))

	player.get_parent().add_child(grenade)

	var inventory: Inventory = load("res://resources/Inventories/PlayerInventory.tres")
	inventory.sellFromInventory(player.item_equipped, 1)
	Bus.inventory_changed.emit()

const MOD_DIR := "gareth-hardware_booster_mod"

func _init() -> void:
	var unpacked_dir = ModLoaderMod.get_unpacked_dir().path_join(MOD_DIR)
	var ext_dir = unpacked_dir.path_join("extensions")

	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks(
		"res://scenes/tilemaps/tile_map_chunk.gd",
		ext_dir.path_join("scenes/tilemaps/tile_map_chunk.hooks.gd")
	)
	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks(
		"res://scenes/tilemaps/tile_map_manager.gd",
		ext_dir.path_join("scenes/tilemaps/tile_map_manager.hooks.gd")
	)
	ModLoaderLog.info("Hardware Booster Mod initialized with TileMapManager bounds protection.", MOD_DIR)

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-hardware_booster_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-hardware_booster_mod\extensions\scenes\tilemaps\tile_map_chunk.hooks.gd
==================================================
--------
extends Object

func generate_chunk(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, noise: Noise) -> void:
	var chunk = chain.reference_object
	# Always let vanilla generate chunk 0 (surface grass, cabin, stockpile & drop-off pit)
	if not chunk or not ("chunk_pos" in chunk) or chunk.chunk_pos.y <= 0:
		chain.execute_next([noise])
		return

	# Only apply acceleration to deep subterranean chunks (Y > 0)
	chain.execute_next([noise])

==================================================
FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-hardware_booster_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-hardware_booster_mod\extensions\scenes\tilemaps\tile_map_manager.hooks.gd
==================================================
--------
extends Object

func damageTileCoords(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, global_posi: Vector2i, damage: float) -> void:
	var tm = chain.reference_object
	if not is_instance_valid(tm):
		return

	var chunk_id = tm.get_chunk_id(global_posi)
	if not ("chunks_created" in tm) or not tm.chunks_created.has(chunk_id):
		return

	chain.execute_next([global_posi, float(damage)])

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-hardware_booster_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-hardware_booster_mod\extensions\scripts\LootCollectorUFO.hooks.gd

extends Object

func check_inventory_count(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	var ufo = chain.reference_object
	if not is_instance_valid(ufo) or not ("inventory" in ufo) or not ("maxInventoryCount" in ufo):
		chain.execute_next([])
		return

	if "StateLabel" in ufo and is_instance_valid(ufo.StateLabel):
		ufo.StateLabel.text = Gconsts.add_comma_to_float_no_dp(ufo.maxInventoryCount)

	var capacity_mult: float = 1.0
	if Gvars and ("passives" in Gvars) and Gvars.passives is Dictionary:
		capacity_mult += Gvars.passives.get("employee_collector_capacity", 0.0)

	var max_cap: float = float(ufo.maxInventoryCount) * capacity_mult
	if float(ufo.inventory.inventoryCount) >= max_cap:
		ufo.set_deferred("monitorable", false)
		if "States" in ufo and ("RETURNING_TO_BASE" in ufo.States):
			ufo.state = ufo.States.RETURNING_TO_BASE
		if "baseCoords" in ufo and "models" in ufo and is_instance_valid(ufo.models):
			if ufo.baseCoords.x < ufo.global_position.x:
				ufo.models.scale.x = -1
			else:
				ufo.models.scale.x = 1

# Heavy Demolition Bombs

**Namespace:** $namespace  
**Compatibility:** Coal LLC v1.4.1.1+ (ModLoader 7.0.1)

---

## Overview
Restores unused \times32$ animated bomb sheets from the game files, adding an arsenal of heavy demolition explosives:
* **Bunker Buster:** High-mass bomb that punches deep into cavern floors before detonating.
* **Cryo-Cluster Bomb:** Releases a frost shockwave across an expanded radius.
* **Thermobaric Charge:** High-temperature vacuum blast clearing 20 to 36 block radii.

Features extended 3.0s fuses and custom explosion audio.

---

## Technical Details (For Modders)
Registers HeavyBombEntity instances with custom blast_radius (18-36 blocks) and damage scaling tied to Gvars.passives.bomb_damage.

---

## Installation
1. Ensure **Coal LLC** has Godot ModLoader 7.0.1 installed.
2. Place the packaged .zip file into your Coal LLC/mods/ directory.
3. Manage or toggle this mod directly via the in-game **Mod Manager**.

## Overview

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-heavy_bombs_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-heavy_bombs_mod\scripts\heavy_bomb_entity.gd
==================================================
--------
class_name HeavyBombEntity
extends RigidBody2D

@export var blast_radius: float = 20.0
@export var blast_damage: float = 1200.0
@export var fuse_time: float = 3.0
@export var texture_path: String = "res://assets/Equipment/bombs/big_bomb_sheet.png"

var _timer: float = 0.0
var sprite: Sprite2D
var audio_explode: AudioStreamPlayer2D

func _ready() -> void:
	gravity_scale = 1.2
	sprite = Sprite2D.new()
	if ResourceLoader.exists(texture_path):
		sprite.texture = load(texture_path)
		sprite.hframes = 4
	add_child(sprite)

	audio_explode = AudioStreamPlayer2D.new()
	if ResourceLoader.exists("res://assets/sound/sound effects/bombExplode.wav"):
		audio_explode.stream = load("res://assets/sound/sound effects/bombExplode.wav")
	add_child(audio_explode)

func _physics_process(delta: float) -> void:
	_timer += delta
	if sprite.hframes > 1:
		sprite.frame = int((_timer / fuse_time) * (sprite.hframes - 1)) % sprite.hframes

	if _timer >= fuse_time:
		_detonate()

func _detonate() -> void:
	set_physics_process(false)
	visible = false
	audio_explode.play()
	var dmg: float = blast_damage * (1.0 + (Gvars.passives.bomb_damage if Gvars and "passives" in Gvars else 0.0))
	Bus.BombExplode.emit(global_position, blast_radius, dmg)
	await audio_explode.finished
	queue_free()

# Heavy Automated Turrets

**Namespace:** $namespace  
**Compatibility:** Coal LLC v1.4.1.1+ (ModLoader 7.0.1)

---

## Overview
Utilizes the unused tank turret graphic (ssets/Equipment/vehicles/tank_turret.png) to introduce deployable automated defense and mining turrets.

When deployed onto the cavern floor, the turret actively tracks nearby stone and ore veins within radar range, automatically firing kinetic pulses every 0.35 seconds to drill surrounding blocks.

---

## Technical Details (For Modders)
Instantiates HeavyTurretEntity on the tilemap, executing radial collision sweeps and rotating the turret sprite toward target coordinates.

3. Manage or toggle this mod directly via the in-game **Mod Manager**.

# Heavy Automated Turrets

**Namespace:** $namespace  
**Compatibility:** Coal LLC v1.4.1.1+ (ModLoader 7.0.1)

## Overview
Utilizes the unused tank turret graphic (ssets/Equipment/vehicles/tank_turret.png) to introduce deployable automated defense and mining turrets.

---

## Technical Details (For Modders)
Instantiates HeavyTurretEntity on the tilemap, executing radial collision sweeps and rotating the turret sprite toward target coordinates.

---

## Installation
1. Ensure **Coal LLC** has Godot ModLoader 7.0.1 installed.

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-heavy_turrets_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-heavy_turrets_mod\scripts\heavy_turret_entity.gd
==================================================
--------
class_name HeavyTurretEntity
extends Node2D

var fire_timer: float = 0.0
const FIRE_INTERVAL: float = 0.35
const DAMAGE: float = 40.0

var sprite: Sprite2D
var tilemap = null

func _ready() -> void:
	sprite = Sprite2D.new()
	if ResourceLoader.exists("res://assets/Equipment/vehicles/tank_turret.png"):
		sprite.texture = load("res://assets/Equipment/vehicles/tank_turret.png")
	add_child(sprite)
	_resolve_tilemap()

func _resolve_tilemap() -> void:
	var tree = get_tree()
	if tree and tree.root:
		var p = tree.root.find_child("Player", true, false)
		if p and ("tilemap" in p) and p.tilemap:
			tilemap = p.tilemap
			return
		var tm = tree.root.find_child("TileMapManager", true, false)
		if tm:
			tilemap = tm

func _physics_process(delta: float) -> void:
	fire_timer += delta
	if fire_timer >= FIRE_INTERVAL:
		fire_timer = 0.0
		_acquire_and_fire()

func _acquire_and_fire() -> void:
	if not tilemap:
		_resolve_tilemap()
		if not tilemap:
			return

	var center: Vector2i = tilemap.local_to_map(global_position)
	var best_target: Vector2i = Vector2i.ZERO
	var found: bool = false

	for r in range(1, 14):
		for dx in range(-r, r + 1):
			for dy in range(-r, r + 1):
				var check_pos: Vector2i = center + Vector2i(dx, dy)
				if tilemap.has_method("is_tile_collision") and tilemap.is_tile_collision(check_pos):
					best_target = check_pos
					found = true
					break
			if found:
				break
		if found:
			break

	if found:
		var target_world: Vector2 = tilemap.map_to_local(best_target)
		sprite.rotation = (target_world - global_position).angle()
		if tilemap.has_method("damageTileCoords"):
			tilemap.damageTileCoords(best_target, DAMAGE)

		var boom = AudioStreamPlayer2D.new()
		boom.stream = load("res://assets/sound/sound effects/box_1.wav")
		boom.pitch_scale = 1.4
		boom.global_position = global_position
		get_tree().current_scene.add_child(boom)
		boom.play()
		boom.finished.connect(boom.queue_free)

		var turret = Node2D.new()

		player.get_parent().add_child(turret)

		"res://scenes/equipment/vacuum_cleaner.gd",
		"res://mods-unpacked/gareth-henry_the_hoover_mod/extensions/scenes/equipment/vacuum_cleaner.hooks.gd"

==================================================
FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-henry_the_hoover_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-henry_the_hoover_mod\extensions\scenes\equipment\vacuum_cleaner.hooks.gd
==================================================
--------
extends Object

func _physics_process(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, delta: float) -> void:
	var vac = chain.reference_object
	if is_instance_valid(vac):
		if not vac.has_meta("henry_initialized"):
			vac.set_meta("henry_initialized", true)
			vac.set_meta("gobble_cooldown", 0.0)
			
			var lvl: int = 1
			if Gvars and ("bonus_equipment_manager" in Gvars) and Gvars.bonus_equipment_manager:
				if "vacuum_level" in Gvars.bonus_equipment_manager:
					lvl = int(Gvars.bonus_equipment_manager.vacuum_level)
					
			var sprite = vac.get_node_or_null("Sprite2D")
			if is_instance_valid(sprite):
				var factory = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-henry_the_hoover_mod/helpers/henry_texture_factory.gd")
				if factory and factory.has_method("apply_henry_skin"):
					# Apply the high-fidelity Numatic pixel art deferred to prevent tree locks
					factory.call_deferred("apply_henry_skin", sprite, lvl)

		var cd = float(vac.get_meta("gobble_cooldown", 0.0))
		if cd > 0.0:
			vac.set_meta("gobble_cooldown", cd - delta)
			
		var audio = vac.get_node_or_null("AudioStreamPlayer2D")
		if is_instance_valid(audio) and audio.playing:
			if cd > 0.0:
				audio.volume_db = -80.0
			else:
				audio.volume_db = 0.0
				vac.set_meta("gobble_cooldown", 1.0)

	chain.execute_next([delta])



--------
==================================================
FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-henry_the_hoover_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-henry_the_hoover_mod\scripts\henry_texture_factory.gd
==================================================
--------
class_name HenryTextureFactory
extends RefCounted

static func apply_henry_skin(sprite: Sprite2D, tier: int) -> void:
	if not is_instance_valid(sprite): return
	var img = Image.create(32, 32, false, Image.FORMAT_RGBA8)
	var body = Color(0.88, 0.12, 0.14, 1.0)
	match tier:
		2: body = Color(0.96, 0.42, 0.68, 1.0) # Hetty Pink
		3: body = Color(0.14, 0.48, 0.92, 1.0) # Harry Blue
		4: body = Color(0.12, 0.72, 0.28, 1.0) # George Green
		5: body = Color(0.95, 0.82, 0.12, 1.0) # Charles Yellow
	
	var black = Color(0.1, 0.1, 0.1, 1.0)
	var white = Color(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0)
	
	for x in range(8, 24):
		for y in range(4, 12):
			img.set_pixel(x, y, black)
	for x in range(4, 28):
		img.set_pixel(x, 12, black)
		
	for x in range(6, 26):
		for y in range(13, 30):
			img.set_pixel(x, y, body)
			
	for x in range(10, 14):
		for y in range(16, 20):
			img.set_pixel(x, y, white)
			if x > 11 and y > 17: img.set_pixel(x, y, black)
	for x in range(18, 22):
		for y in range(16, 20):
			img.set_pixel(x, y, white)
			if x > 19 and y > 17: img.set_pixel(x, y, black)
			
	for x in range(14, 18):
		for y in range(20, 24):
			img.set_pixel(x, y, black)
			
	for x in range(10, 22):
		var sy = 26 + int(abs(x - 15.5) * 0.3)
		if sy < 32:
			img.set_pixel(x, sy, black)
			
	sprite.texture = ImageTexture.create_from_image(img)
	sprite.offset = Vector2(0, -16)

# Thermonuclear Hydrogen Bomb Arsenal

**Namespace:** $namespace  
**Compatibility:** Coal LLC v1.4.1.1+ (ModLoader 7.0.1)

---

## Overview
Engineered specifically for broad subterranean excavation, the **Hydrogen Bomb** delivers massive wide-area clearing power. With a detonation blast radius of **80 tiles** (double the Tactical Nuke), a single charge clears enormous cavern sections.

The warhead delivers **999,999 blast damage**, vaporizing dirt, clay, and hard stone deposits instantly. It appears on the equipment shelf adjacent to the Tactical Nuke for ,000,000.

---

## Technical Details (For Modders)

extends Node

const MOD_DIR := "gareth-hydrogen_bomb_mod"

func _ready() -> void:
	var item_unlocks = load("res://resources/unlockable_items/scripts/item_unlocks.tres")
	if not item_unlocks:
		return

	var tiers = [
		"01_shoddy", "02_copper", "03_iron", "04_sapphire", "05_emerald",
		"06_silver", "07_amethyst", "08_gold", "09_ruby", "10_diamond",
		"11_pinkdiamond", "12_spinel", "13_uranium", "14_moonstone", "15_onyx"
	]

	for t in tiers:
		var item_path := "res://mods-unpacked/gareth-hydrogen_bomb_mod/items/hydrogen_bomb_%s.tres" % t
		if ResourceLoader.exists(item_path):
			var item = load(item_path)
			var tex_path := "res://mods-unpacked/gareth-hydrogen_bomb_mod/textures/hbomb_%s.png" % t
			if FileAccess.file_exists(tex_path):
				var img = Image.load_from_file(tex_path)
				if img:
					item.itemTexture = ImageTexture.create_from_image(img)
			if item and not (item in item_unlocks.all_equipment_items):
				item_unlocks.all_equipment_items.append(item)
			if item and not (item in item_unlocks.all_items):
				item_unlocks.all_items.append(item)

	ModLoaderLog.info("15 Hydrogen Bombs registered with color-coded heavy casings.", MOD_DIR)

# Infini-Platform Dual Cross-Bridge

**Namespace:** $namespace  
**Compatibility:** Coal LLC v1.4.1.1+ (ModLoader 7.0.1)

---

## Overview
The horizontal and vertical bridging equivalent of the InfiniLadder. Deploying an InfiniPlatform triggers simultaneous 4-way expansion rays:
* **Horizontal:** Lays platforms wall-to-wall across open caverns.
* **Vertical:** Extends ladders floor-to-ceiling.
* **Intersection:** Installs a climbable platform-ladder at the origin.

---

## Technical Details (For Modders)
Spawns InfiniPlatformSpawner, which runs 4-way incremental tile generation across local_to_map coordinates until striking solid collision boundaries.

---

## Installation
1. Ensure **Coal LLC** has Godot ModLoader 7.0.1 installed.
2. Place the packaged .zip file into your Coal LLC/mods/ directory.
3. Manage or toggle this mod directly via the in-game **Mod Manager**.
--------
==================================================

extends Node

func _ready() -> void:
	var item_unlocks = load("res://resources/unlockable_items/scripts/item_unlocks.tres")
	if not item_unlocks:
		return
	var item = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-infini_platform_mod/items/InfiniPlatform.tres")
	if item:
		var target_idx = -1
		for i in range(item_unlocks.all_equipment_items.size()):
			if item_unlocks.all_equipment_items[i] and item_unlocks.all_equipment_items[i].itemID == "InfiniLadder":
				target_idx = i
				break
		if target_idx != -1 and not item in item_unlocks.all_equipment_items:
			item_unlocks.all_equipment_items.insert(target_idx + 1, item)
		elif not item in item_unlocks.all_equipment_items:
			item_unlocks.all_equipment_items.append(item)
		if not item in item_unlocks.all_items:
			item_unlocks.all_items.append(item)

class_name InfiniPlatformEquipEffect
extends EquipEffect

const ITEM_PLACER = preload("res://scenes/ItemPlacer.tscn")
var spawned_instance: ItemPlacer = null

func start(_player, item):
	spawned_instance = ITEM_PLACER.instantiate()
	spawned_instance.itemSprite = item.itemTexture
	Game.current_node.add_child(spawned_instance)

func end(_player):
	if spawned_instance != null and is_instance_valid(spawned_instance):
		spawned_instance.queue_free()
		spawned_instance = null

func physics_process(player, _delta):
	if spawned_instance == null or not is_instance_valid(spawned_instance):
		return
	spawned_instance.global_position = player.snap_pos_to_tile(player.pointer_global_pos)

	if Input.is_action_just_released("useItem"):
		var is_colliding = player.tilemap.is_tile_collision_pos(player.global_position)
		if not is_colliding:
			var spawner_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-infini_platform_mod/scripts/infini_platform_spawner.gd")
			var spawner = Node2D.new()
			spawner.set_script(spawner_script)
			spawner.tilemap = player.tilemap
			spawner.global_position = spawned_instance.global_position
			player.tilemap.add_child(spawner)

			var inventory: Inventory = load("res://resources/Inventories/PlayerInventory.tres")
			inventory.sellFromInventory(player.item_equipped, 1)
			Bus.inventory_changed.emit()

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-infini_platform_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-infini_platform_mod\scripts\infini_platform_spawner.gd
==================================================
--------
class_name InfiniPlatformSpawner
extends Node2D

@export var tilemap: TileMapManager

var origin_tile: Vector2i

var current_left: Vector2i
var current_right: Vector2i
var current_up: Vector2i
var current_down: Vector2i

var left_live: bool = true
var right_live: bool = true
var up_live: bool = true
var down_live: bool = true

var blocks_placed: int = 0
const MAX_BLOCKS_PER_DIR: int = 250
var t: int = 0
var audio_player: AudioStreamPlayer2D

func _ready() -> void:
	origin_tile = tilemap.local_to_map(global_position)
	current_left = origin_tile + Vector2i(-1, 0)
	current_right = origin_tile + Vector2i(1, 0)
	current_up = origin_tile + Vector2i(0, -1)
	current_down = origin_tile + Vector2i(0, 1)

	# Place Intersection Platform-Ladder at origin
	tilemap.change_tile(origin_tile, TileMapChunk.Tiles.PLATFORMLADDER)

	audio_player = AudioStreamPlayer2D.new()
	audio_player.stream = load("res://assets/sound/sound effects/pickup.wav")
	add_child(audio_player)
	audio_player.play()

func _physics_process(_delta: float) -> void:
	t += 1
	var any_active: bool = false

	# 1. HORIZONTAL EXPANSION (PLATFORMS)
	if left_live:
		any_active = true
		if tilemap.is_tile_collision(current_left) or not tilemap.does_tile_chunk_exist(current_left) or blocks_placed >= MAX_BLOCKS_PER_DIR * 4:
			left_live = false
		else:
			var existing = tilemap.get_tile_enum(current_left)
			if existing == TileMapChunk.Tiles.LADDER or existing == TileMapChunk.Tiles.PLATFORMLADDER:
				tilemap.change_tile(current_left, TileMapChunk.Tiles.PLATFORMLADDER)
			else:
				tilemap.change_tile(current_left, TileMapChunk.Tiles.PLATFORM)
			current_left += Vector2i(-1, 0)
			blocks_placed += 1

	if right_live:
		any_active = true
		if tilemap.is_tile_collision(current_right) or not tilemap.does_tile_chunk_exist(current_right) or blocks_placed >= MAX_BLOCKS_PER_DIR * 4:
			right_live = false
		else:
			var existing = tilemap.get_tile_enum(current_right)
			if existing == TileMapChunk.Tiles.LADDER or existing == TileMapChunk.Tiles.PLATFORMLADDER:
				tilemap.change_tile(current_right, TileMapChunk.Tiles.PLATFORMLADDER)
			else:
				tilemap.change_tile(current_right, TileMapChunk.Tiles.PLATFORM)
			current_right += Vector2i(1, 0)
			blocks_placed += 1

	# 2. VERTICAL EXPANSION (LADDERS)
	if up_live:
		any_active = true
		if tilemap.is_tile_collision(current_up) or not tilemap.does_tile_chunk_exist(current_up) or blocks_placed >= MAX_BLOCKS_PER_DIR * 4:
			up_live = false
		else:
			var existing = tilemap.get_tile_enum(current_up)
			if existing == TileMapChunk.Tiles.PLATFORM or existing == TileMapChunk.Tiles.PLATFORMLADDER:
				tilemap.change_tile(current_up, TileMapChunk.Tiles.PLATFORMLADDER)
			else:
				tilemap.change_tile(current_up, TileMapChunk.Tiles.LADDER)
			current_up += Vector2i(0, -1)
			blocks_placed += 1

	if down_live:
		any_active = true
		if tilemap.is_tile_collision(current_down) or not tilemap.does_tile_chunk_exist(current_down) or blocks_placed >= MAX_BLOCKS_PER_DIR * 4:
			down_live = false
		else:
			var existing = tilemap.get_tile_enum(current_down)
			if existing == TileMapChunk.Tiles.PLATFORM or existing == TileMapChunk.Tiles.PLATFORMLADDER:
				tilemap.change_tile(current_down, TileMapChunk.Tiles.PLATFORMLADDER)
			else:
				tilemap.change_tile(current_down, TileMapChunk.Tiles.LADDER)
			current_down += Vector2i(0, 1)
			blocks_placed += 1

	if t % 4 == 0 and any_active:
		audio_player.play()

	if not any_active:
		set_physics_process(false)
--------

extends Node

const MOD_DIR := "gareth-jedi_class_mod"

var starter_weapon = null
var all_saber_items: Array = []

const TIER_DATA := [
	{ "id": "01_shoddy",      "name": "Shoddy Energy Saber",        "dmg": 75.0,    "reach": 28.0, "rate": 0.28, "price": 0.0 },
	{ "id": "02_copper",      "name": "Copper Energy Saber",        "dmg": 105.0,   "reach": 30.0, "rate": 0.26, "price": 350.0 },
	{ "id": "03_iron",        "name": "Iron Energy Saber",          "dmg": 150.0,   "reach": 32.0, "rate": 0.25, "price": 1800.0 },
	{ "id": "04_sapphire",    "name": "Sapphire Energy Saber",      "dmg": 215.0,   "reach": 34.0, "rate": 0.24, "price": 9500.0 },
	{ "id": "05_emerald",     "name": "Emerald Energy Saber",       "dmg": 310.0,   "reach": 36.0, "rate": 0.23, "price": 45000.0 },
	{ "id": "06_silver",      "name": "Silver Energy Saber",        "dmg": 450.0,   "reach": 38.0, "rate": 0.22, "price": 200000.0 },
	{ "id": "07_amethyst",    "name": "Amethyst Energy Saber",      "dmg": 650.0,   "reach": 40.0, "rate": 0.21, "price": 850000.0 },
	{ "id": "08_gold",        "name": "Gold Energy Saber",          "dmg": 950.0,   "reach": 43.0, "rate": 0.20, "price": 4000000.0 },
	{ "id": "09_ruby",        "name": "Ruby Energy Saber",          "dmg": 1400.0,  "reach": 46.0, "rate": 0.19, "price": 20000000.0 },
	{ "id": "10_diamond",     "name": "Diamond Energy Saber",       "dmg": 2100.0,  "reach": 50.0, "rate": 0.18, "price": 100000000.0 },
	{ "id": "11_pinkdiamond", "name": "Pink Diamond Energy Saber",  "dmg": 3200.0,  "reach": 54.0, "rate": 0.17, "price": 600000000.0 },
	{ "id": "12_spinel",      "name": "Spinel Energy Saber",        "dmg": 5000.0,  "reach": 58.0, "rate": 0.16, "price": 3500000000.0 },
	{ "id": "13_uranium",     "name": "Uranium Energy Saber",       "dmg": 7800.0,  "reach": 63.0, "rate": 0.15, "price": 25000000000.0 },
	{ "id": "14_moonstone",   "name": "Moonstone Energy Saber",     "dmg": 12500.0, "reach": 68.0, "rate": 0.14, "price": 180000000000.0 },
	{ "id": "15_onyx",        "name": "Onyx Energy Saber",          "dmg": 20000.0, "reach": 75.0, "rate": 0.13, "price": 1500000000000.0 }
]

func _load_image_texture(path: String) -> ImageTexture:
	if not FileAccess.file_exists(path):
		return null
	var img = Image.load_from_file(path)
	if not img:
		return null
	return ImageTexture.create_from_image(img)

func _ready() -> void:
	_register_weapon_tiers()
	_register_profession()

func _register_weapon_tiers() -> void:
	var item_script = load("res://resources/Items/Scripts/Item.gd")
	var buy_script = load("res://resources/BuyEffects/Scripts/AddToInventory.gd")
	var pickup_script = load("res://resources/PickupEffects/Scripts/PickupToInventory.gd")
	var equip_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-jedi_class_mod/scripts/jedi_saber_equip.gd")
	var pickup_sound = load("res://assets/sound/sound effects/SwordHit.wav")

	var item_unlocks = load("res://resources/unlockable_items/scripts/item_unlocks.tres")

	for t in TIER_DATA:
		var item = item_script.new()
		item.itemID = "jedi_saber_" + t["id"]
		item.itemName = t["name"]
		item.itemDescription = "Plasma energy saber.\nCleaves surrounding rock in a high-speed forward arc.\nDamage: %d | Cleave Radius: %.1fpx" % [int(t["dmg"]), float(t["reach"])]

		var tex_path := "res://mods-unpacked/gareth-jedi_class_mod/textures/saber_%s.png" % t["id"]
		item.itemTexture = _load_image_texture(tex_path)

		var equip = equip_script.new()
		equip.damage = t["dmg"]
		equip.reach = t["reach"]
		equip.swing_speed = t["rate"]
		item.itemEquipEffect = equip

		var buy = buy_script.new()
		item.itemBuyEffect = buy
		var pickup = pickup_script.new()
		item.itemPickupEffect = pickup
		item.pickupSound = pickup_sound

		item.itemBuyPrice = t["price"]
		item.itemSellPrice = t["price"] * 0.5
		item.itemUnlocked = true
		item.itemType = "jedi_saber"

		all_saber_items.append(item)

		if item_unlocks:
			if not (item in item_unlocks.all_equipment_items):
				item_unlocks.all_equipment_items.append(item)

	if all_saber_items.size() > 0:
		starter_weapon = all_saber_items[0]

	ModLoaderLog.info("15 Energy Saber tiers registered into equipment shop.", MOD_DIR)

func _register_profession() -> void:
	if not Gvars or not ("profession_manager" in Gvars) or not Gvars.profession_manager:
		return
	var pm = Gvars.profession_manager
	var prof_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-jedi_class_mod/scripts/jedi_profession.gd")
	var p = prof_script.new()
	p.profession_name = "Jedi"
	p.description = "Wielder of plasma energy and kinetic resonance.\nEnergy sabers cleave surrounding stone and ore in high-speed sweeps.\nStarts with the Shoddy Energy Saber."

	var icon_path := "res://mods-unpacked/gareth-jedi_class_mod/textures/jedi_icon.png"
	var icon_tex = _load_image_texture(icon_path)
	if icon_tex:
		p.image = icon_tex

	pm.all_professions[102] = { "profession": p, "is_unlocked": true }
	ModLoaderLog.info("Jedi Class (ID 102) registered cleanly.", MOD_DIR)

class_name JediProfession
extends Profession

func _profession_effect(data: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	var mod_node = Engine.get_main_loop().root.get_node_or_null("ModLoader/gareth-jedi_class_mod")
	if mod_node and "starter_weapon" in mod_node and mod_node.starter_weapon:
		if data.has("inventory_starters"):
			data["inventory_starters"] = [{
				"item": mod_node.starter_weapon,
				"count": 1
			}]

	if data.has("unlocked_passives"):
		data["unlocked_passives"] = [
			"jedi_saber_damage",
			"jedi_swing_rate",
			"jedi_cleave_reach",
			"player_sprint_speed",
			"player_jump_velocity",
			"player_climb_speed"
		]

	return data

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-jedi_class_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-jedi_class_mod\scripts\jedi_saber_equip.gd
==================================================
--------
class_name JediSaberEquipEffect
extends EquipEffect

@export var damage: float = 75.0
@export var reach: float = 28.0
@export var swing_speed: float = 0.28

var weapon_visual: Sprite2D = null
var swing_cooldown: float = 0.0
var _is_using: bool = false
var _auto_swing_toggle: bool = false
var audio_player: AudioStreamPlayer2D = null
var _swing_animating: bool = false

func start(player, item):
	if "tool" in player and player.tool:
		player.tool.visible = false

	if ResourceLoader.exists("res://assets/sound/sound effects/SwordHit.wav"):
		audio_player = AudioStreamPlayer2D.new()
		audio_player.stream = load("res://assets/sound/sound effects/SwordHit.wav")
		player.add_child(audio_player)

	weapon_visual = Sprite2D.new()
	weapon_visual.texture = item.itemTexture
	weapon_visual.offset = Vector2(8, -8)
	weapon_visual.position = Vector2(2, 2)
	weapon_visual.visible = true
	player.add_child(weapon_visual)
	_is_using = false
	_auto_swing_toggle = false

func end(player):
	if "tool" in player and player.tool:
		player.tool.visible = true
	if audio_player and is_instance_valid(audio_player):
		audio_player.queue_free()
	if weapon_visual and is_instance_valid(weapon_visual):
		weapon_visual.queue_free()
	_is_using = false
	_auto_swing_toggle = false

func physics_process(player, delta):
	if swing_cooldown > 0.0:
		swing_cooldown -= delta

	if weapon_visual and is_instance_valid(weapon_visual) and not _swing_animating:
		var p_pos: Vector2 = player.global_position
		var pointer_pos: Vector2 = player.pointer_global_pos
		var angle: float = (pointer_pos - p_pos).angle()
		weapon_visual.rotation = angle + 0.785
		weapon_visual.flip_v = (pointer_pos.x < p_pos.x)

	if Input.is_action_pressed("useItem") or Input.is_mouse_button_pressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT):
		_is_using = true
	elif Input.is_action_just_released("useItem") or (not Input.is_mouse_button_pressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT) and not Input.is_mouse_button_pressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_RIGHT) and not _auto_swing_toggle):
		_is_using = false

	if Input.is_action_just_pressed("toggle_use_item") or Input.is_action_just_pressed("rightMouseClick"):
		_auto_swing_toggle = not _auto_swing_toggle
		_is_using = _auto_swing_toggle

	if Input.is_mouse_button_pressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_RIGHT):
		_is_using = true

	if _is_using and swing_cooldown <= 0.0:
		_execute_saber_cleave(player)

func _get_passive_stat(stat_name: String) -> float:
	if not Gvars or not ("passives" in Gvars) or not Gvars.passives:
		return 0.0
	var p = Gvars.passives
	if p.has_meta(stat_name):
		return float(p.get_meta(stat_name))
	if stat_name in p:
		return float(p.get(stat_name))
	return 0.0

func _execute_saber_cleave(player) -> void:
	var speed_mult: float = 1.0 + _get_passive_stat("jedi_swing_rate")
	swing_cooldown = max(0.06, swing_speed / speed_mult)

	if audio_player and is_instance_valid(audio_player):
		audio_player.pitch_scale = randf_range(1.15, 1.45)
		audio_player.play()

	var p_pos: Vector2 = player.global_position
	var pointer_pos: Vector2 = player.pointer_global_pos
	var base_angle: float = (pointer_pos - p_pos).angle()

	# Animated Melee Cleave
	if weapon_visual and is_instance_valid(weapon_visual):
		_swing_animating = true
		weapon_visual.rotation = base_angle - 1.1
		var tween = player.create_tween()
		tween.tween_property(weapon_visual, "rotation", base_angle + 1.1, 0.09)
		tween.tween_callback(func(): _swing_animating = false)

	var tilemap = player.tilemap
	if not tilemap:
		return

	var dmg_mult: float = 1.0 + _get_passive_stat("jedi_saber_damage")
	var eff_dmg: float = damage * dmg_mult

	var reach_mult: float = 1.0 + _get_passive_stat("jedi_cleave_reach")
	var eff_reach: float = reach * reach_mult

	# Cleave across 7 arc rays
	var hit_cells: Array[Vector2i] = []
	for offset_deg in [-45, -30, -15, 0, 15, 30, 45]:
		var rad: float = base_angle + deg_to_rad(offset_deg)
		var check_dir: Vector2 = Vector2(cos(rad), sin(rad))
		for d in range(8, int(eff_reach), 6):
			var cell_pos: Vector2 = p_pos + (check_dir * float(d))
			var cell: Vector2i = tilemap.local_to_map(cell_pos)
			if not hit_cells.has(cell) and tilemap.is_tile_breakable(cell):
				hit_cells.append(cell)
				tilemap.damageTileCoords(cell, eff_dmg)

	_spawn_saber_arc_visual(player, p_pos, base_angle, eff_reach)

func _spawn_saber_arc_visual(player: Node2D, origin: Vector2, angle: float, arc_radius: float) -> void:
	var parent = player.get_parent()
	if not parent:
		parent = player

	var arc := Line2D.new()
	arc.width = 4.0
	arc.default_color = Color(0.3, 0.8, 1.0, 0.95)
	for i in range(-5, 6):
		var rad: float = angle + deg_to_rad(float(i) * 9.0)
		arc.add_point(origin + Vector2(cos(rad), sin(rad)) * arc_radius)
	parent.add_child(arc)

	var tween = parent.create_tween()
	tween.tween_property(arc, "modulate:a", 0.0, 0.08)
	tween.tween_callback(arc.queue_free)

# The Knight Class

**Namespace:** $namespace  
**Compatibility:** Coal LLC v1.4.1.1+ (ModLoader 7.0.1)

---

## Overview
The **Knight Class** introduces a dedicated melee combat archetype to *Coal LLC*. Eschewing traditional pickaxes, the Knight begins their subterranean career wielding a heavy Shoddy Broadsword designed to cleave through stone, clay, and mineral veins in a single sweeping arc.

Attacking with the sword executes a 180-degree frontal sweep that delivers high kinetic impact across all blocks within reach. Damage scales with player passive upgrades and progresses through 11 material grades from Copper up to Pink Diamond.

In the management equipment shop, the Knight is strictly restricted to blade weaponry and basic traversal utilities (such as ladders and ore filters), keeping the class focused on melee combat.

---

## Technical Details (For Modders)
Instantiates KnightSwordEquipEffect hooked into player.pointer_global_pos. Dispatches rotational raycasts through player.tilemap with native SwordThud.wav audio. Injects 11 tiered sword resources into item_unlocks.tres.

---

## Installation
1. Ensure **Coal LLC** has Godot ModLoader 7.0.1 installed.
2. Place the packaged .zip file into your Coal LLC/mods/ directory.
3. Manage or toggle this mod directly via the in-game **Mod Manager**.
--------

extends Node

const MOD_DIR := "gareth-knight_class_mod"

const TIER_DATA := [
	{ "id": "01_shoddy",      "name": "Shoddy Knight Sword",        "dmg": 70.0,      "price": 0.0,                    "sell": 0.0 },

	{ "id": "09_ruby",        "name": "Ruby Knight Sword",          "dmg": 17500.0,   "price": 5000000.0,              "sell": 2500000.0 },
	{ "id": "10_diamond",     "name": "Diamond Knight Sword",       "dmg": 35000.0,   "price": 15000000.0,             "sell": 7500000.0 },
	{ "id": "11_pinkdiamond", "name": "Pink Diamond Knight Sword",  "dmg": 70000.0,   "price": 45000000.0,             "sell": 22500000.0 },
	{ "id": "12_spinel",      "name": "Spinel Knight Sword",        "dmg": 140000.0,  "price": 650000000.0,            "sell": 325000000.0 },
	{ "id": "13_uranium",     "name": "Uranium Knight Sword",       "dmg": 280000.0,  "price": 6500000000000.0,        "sell": 3250000000000.0 },
	{ "id": "14_moonstone",   "name": "Moonstone Knight Sword",     "dmg": 560000.0,  "price": 660000000000000000.0,   "sell": 330000000000000000.0 },
	{ "id": "15_onyx",        "name": "Onyx Knight Sword",          "dmg": 1120000.0, "price": 60000000000000000000000000.0, "sell": 30000000000000000000000000.0 }
]

func _load_texture(path: String) -> Texture2D:
	if ResourceLoader.exists(path):
		return load(path)
	if FileAccess.file_exists(path):
		var buffer = FileAccess.get_file_as_bytes(path)
		if buffer.size() > 0:
			var img = Image.new()
			if img.load_png_from_buffer(buffer) == OK:
				return ImageTexture.create_from_image(img)
	return null

func _ready() -> void:
	var reg = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-mod_manager_mod/scripts/mod_registry.gd")
	if reg and not reg.is_mod_active_for_run("knight_class"):
		return

	var item_script = load("res://resources/Items/Scripts/Item.gd")
	var buy_script = load("res://resources/BuyEffects/Scripts/AddToInventory.gd")
	var pickup_script = load("res://resources/PickupEffects/Scripts/PickupToInventory.gd")
	var equip_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-knight_class_mod/scripts/knight_sword_equip.gd")
	var pickup_sound = load("res://assets/sound/sound effects/SwordThud.wav")
	var item_unlocks = load("res://resources/unlockable_items/scripts/item_unlocks.tres")

	for t in TIER_DATA:
		var item = item_script.new()
		item.itemID = "KnightSword_" + t["id"]
		item.itemName = t["name"]
		item.itemDescription = "Broadsword (%d damage). Sweeps in a wide 180° frontal arc cleaving rock and ore." % int(t["dmg"])
		item.itemType = "sword"
		item.itemBuyPrice = t["price"]
		item.itemSellPrice = t["sell"]
		item.itemUnlocked = true
		item.pickupSound = pickup_sound

		var res_path := "res://mods-unpacked/gareth-knight_class_mod/items/KnightSword_%s.tres" % t["id"]
		item.resource_path = res_path
		item.take_over_path(res_path)

		var tex_name = t["name"].replace(" Knight Sword", "").replace(" ", "")
		var tex_path = "res://assets/Equipment/Sword-%s.png" % tex_name
		if not ResourceLoader.exists(tex_path):
			tex_path = "res://mods-unpacked/gareth-knight_class_mod/textures/Sword-%s.png" % tex_name
		item.itemTexture = _load_texture(tex_path)

		var equip = equip_script.new()
		item.itemEquipEffect = equip
		item.itemBuyEffect = buy_script.new()
		item.itemPickupEffect = pickup_script.new()

		if item_unlocks:
			var already_registered := false
			for existing in item_unlocks.all_equipment_items:
				if existing and existing.itemID == item.itemID:
					already_registered = true
					break
			if not already_registered:
				item_unlocks.all_equipment_items.append(item)

	if not Gvars or not ("profession_manager" in Gvars) or not Gvars.profession_manager:
		return

	var pm = Gvars.profession_manager
	var prof_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-knight_class_mod/scripts/knight_profession.gd")
	var p = prof_script.new()
	p.profession_name = "Knight"
	p.description = "Master of chivalric blade combat.\nStarts with a Shoddy Sword.\nAll 15 sword tiers, standalone weapons, and tools are purchasable."
	p.image = _load_texture("res://assets/Equipment/Sword-Shoddy.png")

	pm.all_professions[101] = { "profession": p, "is_unlocked": true }
	ModLoaderLog.info("15 Knight Sword tiers registered programmatically into shop.", MOD_DIR)

# The Knight Class

**Namespace:** $namespace  
**Compatibility:** Coal LLC v1.4.1.1+ (ModLoader 7.0.1)

---

## Overview
The **Knight Class** introduces a dedicated melee combat archetype to *Coal LLC*. Eschewing traditional pickaxes, the Knight begins their subterranean career wielding a heavy Shoddy Broadsword designed to cleave through stone, clay, and mineral veins in a single sweeping arc.

Attacking with the sword executes a 180-degree frontal sweep that delivers high kinetic impact across all blocks within reach. Damage scales with player passive upgrades and progresses through 11 material grades from Copper up to Pink Diamond.

In the management equipment shop, the Knight is strictly restricted to blade weaponry and basic traversal utilities (such as ladders and ore filters), keeping the class focused on melee combat.

---

## Technical Details (For Modders)
Instantiates KnightSwordEquipEffect hooked into player.pointer_global_pos. Dispatches rotational raycasts through player.tilemap with native SwordThud.wav audio. Injects 11 tiered sword resources into item_unlocks.tres.

---

## Installation
1. Ensure **Coal LLC** has Godot ModLoader 7.0.1 installed.
2. Place the packaged .zip file into your Coal LLC/mods/ directory.
3. Manage or toggle this mod directly via the in-game **Mod Manager**.

class_name KnightProfession
extends Profession

func _profession_effect(data: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	if Gvars and ("passives" in Gvars) and Gvars.passives:
		for stat in ["sword_damage", "sword_speed", "sword_range"]:
			if not Gvars.passives.has_meta(stat):
				Gvars.passives.set_meta(stat, 0.0)
			Gvars.passives.set(stat, Gvars.passives.get_meta(stat))

	if data.has("inventory_starters"):
		var sword_res = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-knight_class_mod/items/KnightSword_01_shoddy.tres")
		if sword_res:
			data["inventory_starters"] = [{
				"item": sword_res,
				"count": 1
			}]

	if data.has("unlocked_passives"):
		data["unlocked_passives"] = [
			"sword_damage",
			"sword_speed",
			"sword_range",
			"employee_miner_speed",
			"employee_miner_damage",
			"employee_miner_range",
			"employee_miner_pickup_speed",
			"employee_collector_speed",
			"employee_collector_capacity",
			"collector_range",
			"player_sprint_speed",
			"player_jump_velocity",
			"player_climb_speed"
		]

	return data

--------
class_name KnightSwordEquipEffect
extends EquipEffect

const SWORD_THUD := preload("res://assets/sound/sound effects/SwordThud.wav")
var audio_player: AudioStreamPlayer2D
var sword_visual: Sprite2D = null
var is_swinging: bool = false
var swing_cooldown: float = 0.0
var _is_using: bool = false
var _auto_swing_toggle: bool = false

func start(player, item):
	if "tool" in player and player.tool:
		player.tool.visible = false

	audio_player = AudioStreamPlayer2D.new()
	audio_player.stream = SWORD_THUD
	player.add_child(audio_player)

	sword_visual = Sprite2D.new()
	sword_visual.texture = item.itemTexture
	sword_visual.offset = Vector2(0, -14)
	sword_visual.position = Vector2(4, 0)
	sword_visual.visible = true
	player.add_child(sword_visual)
	_is_using = false
	_auto_swing_toggle = false

func end(player):
	if "tool" in player and player.tool:
		player.tool.visible = true
	if audio_player and is_instance_valid(audio_player):
		audio_player.queue_free()
	if sword_visual and is_instance_valid(sword_visual):
		sword_visual.queue_free()
	_is_using = false
	_auto_swing_toggle = false

func physics_process(player, delta):
	if swing_cooldown > 0.0:
		swing_cooldown -= delta

	if sword_visual and is_instance_valid(sword_visual) and not is_swinging:
		var p_pos: Vector2 = player.global_position
		var pointer_pos: Vector2 = player.pointer_global_pos
		var angle: float = (pointer_pos - p_pos).angle()
		sword_visual.rotation = angle + PI/4
		sword_visual.flip_v = (pointer_pos.x < p_pos.x)

	# 1. Left-click hold and release
	if Input.is_action_pressed("useItem") or Input.is_mouse_button_pressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT):
		_is_using = true
	elif Input.is_action_just_released("useItem") or (not Input.is_mouse_button_pressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT) and not Input.is_mouse_button_pressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_RIGHT) and not _auto_swing_toggle):
		_is_using = false

	# 2. Right-click toggle continuous attack (vanilla toggle_use_item pattern)
	if Input.is_action_just_pressed("toggle_use_item") or Input.is_action_just_pressed("rightMouseClick"):
		_auto_swing_toggle = not _auto_swing_toggle
		_is_using = _auto_swing_toggle

	# 3. Direct Right-click hold
	if Input.is_mouse_button_pressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_RIGHT):
		_is_using = true

	if _is_using and swing_cooldown <= 0.0:
		_cleave_attack(player)

func _get_passive_stat(stat_name: String) -> float:
	if not Gvars or not ("passives" in Gvars) or not Gvars.passives:
		return 0.0
	var p = Gvars.passives
	if p.has_meta(stat_name):
		return float(p.get_meta(stat_name))
	if stat_name in p:
		return float(p.get(stat_name))
	return 0.0

func _cleave_attack(player) -> void:
	var speed_mult: float = 1.0 + _get_passive_stat("sword_speed")
	swing_cooldown = max(0.06, 0.28 / speed_mult)
	is_swinging = true
	if audio_player:
		audio_player.play()

	var p_pos: Vector2 = player.global_position
	var pointer_pos: Vector2 = player.pointer_global_pos
	var dir: Vector2 = (pointer_pos - p_pos).normalized()

	var dmg_mult: float = 1.0 + _get_passive_stat("sword_damage") + _get_passive_stat("axe_damage")
	var base_dmg: float = 70.0 * dmg_mult

	var range_mult: float = 1.0 + _get_passive_stat("sword_range")

	if sword_visual and is_instance_valid(sword_visual):
		var center_angle: float = dir.angle()
		var tween = player.create_tween()
		sword_visual.rotation = center_angle - deg_to_rad(65)
		var tween_time: float = max(0.04, 0.14 / speed_mult)
		tween.tween_property(sword_visual, "rotation", center_angle + deg_to_rad(65), tween_time)
		tween.tween_callback(func(): is_swinging = false)

	var tilemap = player.tilemap
	if tilemap:
		var cells_damaged: Array[Vector2i] = []
		var max_dist: float = 48.0 * range_mult
		var distances = [16.0, 32.0, max_dist]
		if range_mult > 1.2:
			distances.append(max_dist * 1.15)

		for angle_deg in [-60, -30, 0, 30, 60]:
			var test_dir: Vector2 = dir.rotated(deg_to_rad(angle_deg))
			for dist in distances:
				var t_pos: Vector2 = p_pos + test_dir * dist
				var cell: Vector2i = tilemap.local_to_map(t_pos)
				if not cells_damaged.has(cell):
					cells_damaged.append(cell)
					if tilemap.is_tile_breakable(cell):
						tilemap.damageTileCoords(cell, base_dmg)

{
  "name": "laser_beam_mod",
  "namespace": "gareth",
  "version_number": "1.0.0",
  "description": "Continuous collimated high-energy laser beam weapon using native shader and audio assets.",
  "website_url": "https://github.com/garethwhite-prog/coal-llc-mods",
  "dependencies": [],
  "extra": {
    "godot": {
      "display_name": "High-Energy Laser Beam",

# High-Energy Laser Beam System

**Namespace:** $namespace  
**Compatibility:** Coal LLC v1.4.1.1+ (ModLoader 7.0.1)

---

## Overview
The **High-Energy Laser Beam System** restores the game's unused continuous beam shader (shaders/laser.gdshader) and audio assets into an active excavation weapon.

Holding the use action channels a collimated energy beam that melts subterranean strata in real time, accompanied by dynamic particle sparks and looping beam audio.

---

## Technical Details (For Modders)
Instantiates LaserBeamWeapon using Line2D and shaders/laser.gdshader with fractal Brownian motion. Casts linear ray-marches against TileMapManager with per-tick block damage.

---

## Installation
1. Ensure **Coal LLC** has Godot ModLoader 7.0.1 installed.
2. Place the packaged .zip file into your Coal LLC/mods/ directory.
3. Manage or toggle this mod directly via the in-game **Mod Manager**.

## Overview

## Installation

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-laser_beam_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-laser_beam_mod\scripts\laser_beam_weapon.gd
==================================================

class_name LaserBeamWeapon
extends Node2D

var beam_line: Line2D
var audio_player: AudioStreamPlayer2D
var max_range: float = 520.0
var base_damage_per_tick: float = 65.0

func _ready() -> void:
	z_index = 50
	beam_line = Line2D.new()
	beam_line.width = 8.0
	beam_line.default_color = Color(0.2, 0.9, 1.0, 0.95)
	beam_line.begin_cap_mode = Line2D.LINE_CAP_ROUND
	beam_line.end_cap_mode = Line2D.LINE_CAP_ROUND
	beam_line.z_index = 50
	add_child(beam_line)

	audio_player = AudioStreamPlayer2D.new()
	if ResourceLoader.exists("res://assets/sound/sound effects/laser.wav"):
		audio_player.stream = load("res://assets/sound/sound effects/laser.wav")
	add_child(audio_player)

func fire_beam(origin: Vector2, target: Vector2, tilemap: TileMapManager) -> void:
	global_position = origin
	var dir := (target - origin).normalized()
	var end_point := origin + dir * max_range

	beam_line.clear_points()
	beam_line.add_point(Vector2.ZERO)
	beam_line.add_point(to_local(end_point))
	beam_line.visible = true

	if not audio_player.playing:
		audio_player.play()

	if tilemap:
		var step_dist: float = 8.0
		var current_step: float = 0.0
		var mining_mult: float = 1.0
		if Gvars and ("passives" in Gvars) and Gvars.passives:
			mining_mult = 1.0 + float(Gvars.passives.player_pickaxe_mining_damage)
		var tick_damage = base_damage_per_tick * mining_mult

		while current_step < max_range:
			var test_pos := origin + dir * current_step
			var map_pos := tilemap.local_to_map(test_pos)
			if tilemap.is_tile_collision(map_pos) or tilemap.is_tile_breakable(map_pos):
				tilemap.damageTileCoords(map_pos, tick_damage)
				beam_line.set_point_position(1, to_local(test_pos))
				break
			current_step += step_dist

func stop_beam() -> void:
	if is_instance_valid(beam_line):
		beam_line.visible = false
		beam_line.clear_points()
	if is_instance_valid(audio_player) and audio_player.playing:
		audio_player.stop()

class_name LaserEquipEffect
extends EquipEffect

var beam_node: Node2D = null

func start(player, _item):
	var script_res = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-laser_beam_mod/scripts/laser_beam_weapon.gd")
	beam_node = Node2D.new()
	beam_node.set_script(script_res)
	player.add_child(beam_node)

func end(player):
	if beam_node != null and is_instance_valid(beam_node):
		beam_node.call("stop_beam")
		beam_node.queue_free()
		beam_node = null

func physics_process(player, _delta):
	if beam_node == null or not is_instance_valid(beam_node):
		return
	if Input.is_action_pressed("useItem") or Input.is_action_pressed("rightMouseClick") or Input.is_mouse_button_pressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT) or Input.is_mouse_button_pressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_RIGHT):
		beam_node.call("fire_beam", player.global_position, player.pointer_global_pos, player.tilemap)
	else:
		beam_node.call("stop_beam")

# Categorized Leaderboards & Hall of Fame

**Namespace:** $namespace  
**Compatibility:** Coal LLC v1.4.1.1+ (ModLoader 7.0.1)

---

## Overview
A persistent analytics suite accessible from both the Main Menu and Settings screen. It records all-time records across mining speed, damage, automated employee efficiency, and highest cash quotas.

Records persist across runs in user://leaderboard_records.json.

---

## Technical Details (For Modders)
Autoload node samples Gvars.passives, Gvars.bonus_equipment_manager, and cash milestones every 1.0s, updating high-score dictionaries in JSON storage.

---

## Installation
1. Ensure **Coal LLC** has Godot ModLoader 7.0.1 installed.
2. Place the packaged .zip file into your Coal LLC/mods/ directory.
3. Manage or toggle this mod directly via the in-game **Mod Manager**.

const MOD_DIR := "gareth-leaderboard_mod"

func _init() -> void:

	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks(
		"res://scenes/Interfaces/Menus/settings_2.gd",
		mod_dir_path.path_join("extensions/scenes/Interfaces/Menus/settings_2.hooks.gd")
	)
	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks(
		"res://scripts/MainMenu.gd",
		mod_dir_path.path_join("extensions/scripts/MainMenu.hooks.gd")
	)

	tracker.set_script(tracker_script)

extends Object

func _ready(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([])
	var settings = chain.reference_object
	if not settings or not ("tab_container" in settings) or not settings.tab_container:
		return

	var existing = settings.tab_container.get_node_or_null("LeaderboardTab")
	if existing:
		return

	var tab_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-leaderboard_mod/scenes/leaderboard_tab.gd")
	var tab := PanelContainer.new()
	tab.set_script(tab_script)
	tab.name = "LeaderboardTab"
	tab.set_name("Leaderboard")
	settings.tab_container.add_child(tab)
	# Position 7 places it directly adjacent to Mod Manager
	settings.tab_container.call_deferred("move_child", tab, 7)

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-leaderboard_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-leaderboard_mod\extensions\scripts\MainMenu.hooks.gd
==================================================
--------
extends Object

func _ready(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([])
	var menu = chain.reference_object
	if not menu:
		return

	var btn_grid = menu.get_node_or_null("%MainMenuButtons")
	if btn_grid and not btn_grid.has_node("LeaderboardsButton"):
		var lb_btn := Button.new()
		lb_btn.name = "LeaderboardsButton"
		lb_btn.text = "Leaderboards"
		lb_btn.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0, 32)
		lb_btn.pressed.connect(func():
			var modal_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-leaderboard_mod/scenes/leaderboard_modal.gd")
			var modal = modal_script.new()
			menu.add_child(modal)
		)
		btn_grid.add_child(lb_btn)
		btn_grid.move_child(lb_btn, 3)
