class_name LeaderboardModal
extends Control

signal closed()

func _ready() -> void:
	process_mode = Node.PROCESS_MODE_ALWAYS
	var vp_rect := get_viewport_rect()
	position = Vector2.ZERO
	size = vp_rect.size
	custom_minimum_size = vp_rect.size
	mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_STOP
	z_index = 95

	var backdrop := ColorRect.new()
	backdrop.position = Vector2.ZERO
	backdrop.size = vp_rect.size
	backdrop.color = Color(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.85)
	backdrop.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_STOP
	add_child(backdrop)

	var center := CenterContainer.new()
	center.position = Vector2.ZERO
	center.size = vp_rect.size
	add_child(center)

	var panel := PanelContainer.new()
	panel.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(920, 560)
	center.add_child(panel)

	var margin := MarginContainer.new()
	margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_left", 24)
	margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_right", 24)
	margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_top", 16)
	margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_bottom", 16)
	panel.add_child(margin)

	var vbox := VBoxContainer.new()
	vbox.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 12)
	margin.add_child(vbox)

	var header := HBoxContainer.new()
	var title := RichTextLabel.new()
	title.bbcode_enabled = true
	title.text = "[b][font_size=22]Career Records & Hall of Fame[/font_size][/b]"
	title.fit_content = true
	title.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	header.add_child(title)

	var close_btn := Button.new()
	close_btn.text = " Close (Esc) "
	close_btn.pressed.connect(_on_close)
	header.add_child(close_btn)
	vbox.add_child(header)

	var sep := HSeparator.new()
	vbox.add_child(sep)

	var tab_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-leaderboard_mod/scenes/leaderboard_tab.gd")
	var tab_content = tab_script.new()
	tab_content.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	tab_content.size_flags_vertical = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	vbox.add_child(tab_content)

func _input(event: InputEvent) -> void:
	if event.is_action_pressed("ui_cancel") or event.is_action_pressed("menu"):
		_on_close()

func _on_close() -> void:
	closed.emit()
	queue_free()

const CATEGORIES: Array[Dictionary] = [

		"items": [

			{"key": "orb_damage", "label": "Orb Strike Damage", "unit": "%"},
			{"key": "tank_fire_rate", "label": "Tank Fire Rate", "unit": "%"},

			{"key": "highest_quota", "label": "Peak Coal Quota Cleared", "unit": "Count"}
		]
	}
]

func _ready() -> void:
	size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	size_flags_vertical = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL

	var margin := MarginContainer.new()
	margin.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	margin.size_flags_vertical = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_left", 32)
	margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_right", 32)
	margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_top", 16)
	margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_bottom", 16)
	add_child(margin)

	var scroll := ScrollContainer.new()
	scroll.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	scroll.size_flags_vertical = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	scroll.horizontal_scroll_mode = ScrollContainer.SCROLL_MODE_DISABLED
	margin.add_child(scroll)

	var vbox := VBoxContainer.new()
	vbox.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	vbox.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 6)
	scroll.add_child(vbox)

	var title := RichTextLabel.new()
	title.bbcode_enabled = true
	title.text = "[b][font_size=22]Quest Rewards & All-Time High Scores[/font_size][/b]"
	title.fit_content = true
	title.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0, 36)
	vbox.add_child(title)

	var tracker = get_node_or_null("/root/LeaderboardTracker")
	if tracker and tracker.has_method("sample_stats"):
		tracker.sample_stats()

	var records: Dictionary = tracker.records if tracker else {}
	var p = Gvars.passives if (Gvars and "passives" in Gvars) else null
	var bem = Gvars.bonus_equipment_manager if (Gvars and "bonus_equipment_manager" in Gvars) else null

	var rendered_keys: Dictionary = {}

	for cat in CATEGORIES:
		_add_section_header(vbox, cat["title"])
		for item in cat["items"]:
			var k: String = item["key"]
			rendered_keys[k] = true
			var all_time: float = records.get(k, 0.0)
			var current: float = _fetch_current(k, p, bem)
			_add_stat_row(vbox, item["label"], all_time, current, item["unit"])

	# Automatically list any extra passives that weren't in the hardcoded list
	var extra_keys: Array[String] = []
	if p:
		for prop in p.get_property_list():
			var pn: String = prop.name
			if not rendered_keys.has(pn) and not pn.begins_with("resource_") and not pn.begins_with("metadata/") and pn != "script":
				if pn in p:
					var val = p.get(pn)
					if val is float or val is int:
						extra_keys.append(pn)

	if extra_keys.size() > 0:
		_add_section_header(vbox, "Additional Active Passives")
		for ek in extra_keys:
			var formatted_name = ek.replace("_", " ").capitalize()
			var all_time: float = records.get(ek, 0.0)
			var current: float = float(p.get(ek))
			_add_stat_row(vbox, formatted_name, all_time, current, "%")

func _fetch_current(key: String, p: Object, bem: Object) -> float:
	if key == "vacuum_level":
		if bem and "current_vacuum" in bem and bem.current_vacuum != null:
			return float(bem.current_vacuum)
	elif key == "highest_cash":
		if Gvars and "cash" in Gvars and Gvars.cash != null:
			return float(Gvars.cash)
	elif key == "highest_quota":
		if Gvars and "quota" in Gvars and Gvars.quota != null:
			return float(Gvars.quota)
	elif p and (key in p):
		var v = p.get(key)
		if v != null:
			return float(v)
	return 0.0

func _add_section_header(parent: Node, title_text: String) -> void:
	var spacer := Control.new()
	spacer.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0, 10)
	parent.add_child(spacer)

	var lbl := RichTextLabel.new()
	lbl.bbcode_enabled = true
	lbl.text = "[color=#f2c94c][b][font_size=16]%s[/font_size][/b][/color]" % title_text
	lbl.fit_content = true
	lbl.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0, 24)
	parent.add_child(lbl)

	var sep := HSeparator.new()
	parent.add_child(sep)

func _add_stat_row(parent: Node, label_name: String, all_time: float, current: float, unit_type: String) -> void:
	var hbox := HBoxContainer.new()
	hbox.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL

	var name_lbl := Label.new()
	name_lbl.text = label_name
	name_lbl.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	name_lbl.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(260, 22)
	hbox.add_child(name_lbl)

	var record_lbl := Label.new()
	record_lbl.text = "Best: " + _format_val(all_time, unit_type)
	record_lbl.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(170, 22)
	record_lbl.modulate = Color(1.0, 0.85, 0.25)
	hbox.add_child(record_lbl)

	var curr_lbl := Label.new()
	curr_lbl.text = "Current: " + _format_val(current, unit_type)
	curr_lbl.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(170, 22)
	curr_lbl.modulate = Color(0.78, 0.78, 0.78)
	hbox.add_child(curr_lbl)

	parent.add_child(hbox)

func _format_val(val: float, unit_type: String) -> String:
	if unit_type == "%":
		return "+%d%%" % int(val * 100.0)
	elif unit_type == "Tier":
		return "Level %d" % int(val)
	elif unit_type == "Count":
		return Gconsts.add_comma_to_float_no_dp(val)
	elif unit_type == "Cash":
		if val >= 1e12:
			return "$%.2fe+12" % (val / 1e12)
		elif val >= 1e9:
			return "$%.2fe+9" % (val / 1e9)
		elif val >= 1e6:
			return "$%.2fe+6" % (val / 1e6)
		else:
			return "$%s" % Gconsts.add_comma_to_float_no_dp(val)
	return "%.2f" % val

static func celebrate_new_record(tree: SceneTree, record_name: String, value: String) -> void:
	var toast = Label.new()
	toast.text = "🎉 NEW LEADERBOARD RECORD! %s: %s" % [record_name, value]
	toast.anchor_left = 0.5
	toast.anchor_right = 0.5
	toast.offset_left = -280.0
	toast.offset_right = 280.0
	toast.offset_top = 130.0
	toast.offset_bottom = 165.0
	toast.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	toast.add_theme_color_override("font_color", Color(1.0, 0.85, 0.1, 1.0))
	tree.root.add_child(toast)

	var tw = tree.create_tween()
	tw.tween_interval(4.0)
	tw.tween_callback(toast.queue_free)

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-leaderboard_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-leaderboard_mod\scripts\record_tracker.gd
==================================================
--------
extends Node

const SAVE_PATH := "user://leaderboard_records.json"

var records: Dictionary = {}
var _poll_timer: float = 0.0

func _ready() -> void:
	load_records()

func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	_poll_timer += delta
	if _poll_timer >= 1.0:
		_poll_timer = 0.0
		sample_stats()

func sample_stats() -> void:
	if Gvars and ("passives" in Gvars) and Gvars.passives:
		var p = Gvars.passives
		for prop in p.get_property_list():
			var n: String = prop.name
			if n != "script" and not n.begins_with("resource_") and not n.begins_with("metadata/"):
				if n in p:
					var val = p.get(n)
					if (val is float or val is int) and val != null:
						_update_val(n, float(val))

	if Gvars and ("bonus_equipment_manager" in Gvars) and Gvars.bonus_equipment_manager:
		var bem = Gvars.bonus_equipment_manager
		if "current_vacuum" in bem and bem.current_vacuum != null:
			_update_val("vacuum_level", float(bem.current_vacuum))

	if Gvars and ("cash" in Gvars) and Gvars.cash != null:
		_update_val("highest_cash", float(Gvars.cash))

	if Gvars and ("quota" in Gvars) and Gvars.quota != null:
		_update_val("highest_quota", float(Gvars.quota))

	save_records()

func _update_val(key: String, current: float) -> void:
	if current > records.get(key, 0.0):
		records[key] = current

func load_records() -> void:
	if FileAccess.file_exists(SAVE_PATH):
		var file := FileAccess.open(SAVE_PATH, FileAccess.READ)
		if file:
			var json_text := file.get_as_text()
			var parsed = JSON.parse_string(json_text)
			if parsed is Dictionary:
				for k in parsed.keys():
					records[k] = float(parsed[k])

func save_records() -> void:
	var file := FileAccess.open(SAVE_PATH, FileAccess.WRITE)
	if file:
		file.store_string(JSON.stringify(records, "\t"))

# The Living Canvas (`gareth-living_canvas_mod`)

**Author:** Gareth White  
**Map ID:** 103  
**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Compatibility:** Coal LLC v1.4.1.1+ (ModLoader 7.0.1)

---

## Overview
**The Living Canvas** transforms subterranean strata into a playable mosaic sculpted directly from real images, photos, or screenshots. Using an intelligent Euclidean color quantization algorithm, pixels are converted into corresponding rock strata (`HARD_FIRE`, `HARD_ICE`, `HARD_GREEN`, `HARD_DARKBLUE`, etc.).

---

## Key Features
* **In-Game Image Picker:** Click the **??? Choose Image** button on the level card to open a native Windows file dialog and load any PNG or JPEG.
* **Auto-Downsampling:** Automatically downsamples large 1080p and 4K images to a 96-tile shaft width while preserving original aspect ratios.
* **Seamless Strata Looping:** Digging deep through the shaft smoothly loops the landscape pattern.
* **Bedrock Framing:** Borders beyond the canvas are encased in bedrock and dark slate to frame the subterranean artwork.

const MOD_ID := "gareth-living_canvas_mod"
const LOG_NAME := "gareth-living_canvas_mod"

func _init() -> void:
	ModLoaderLog.info("The Living Canvas Mod initializing...", LOG_NAME)
	install_hooks()

func _ready() -> void:
	var helper_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-living_canvas_mod/helpers/LivingCanvasHelper.gd")
	if helper_script and helper_script.has_method("ensure_level_registered"):
		helper_script.ensure_level_registered()
	ModLoaderLog.info("The Living Canvas Custom Map (ID 103) online!", LOG_NAME)

func install_hooks() -> void:
	var unpacked_dir = ModLoaderMod.get_unpacked_dir().path_join(MOD_ID)
	var ext_dir = unpacked_dir.path_join("extensions")

	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks(
		"res://scenes/Interfaces/Menus/level_select.gd",
		ext_dir.path_join("scenes/Interfaces/Menus/level_select.hooks.gd")
	)
	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks(
		"res://scenes/tilemaps/tile_map_chunk.gd",
		ext_dir.path_join("scenes/tilemaps/tile_map_chunk.hooks.gd")
	)
	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks(
		"res://scripts/Game.gd",
		ext_dir.path_join("scripts/Game.hooks.gd")
	)

**Author:** Gareth White  
**Map ID:** 103  
**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Compatibility:** Coal LLC v1.4.1.1+ (ModLoader 7.0.1)

## Overview
**The Living Canvas** transforms subterranean strata into a playable mosaic sculpted directly from real images, photos, or screenshots. Using an intelligent Euclidean color quantization algorithm, pixels are converted into corresponding rock strata (`HARD_FIRE`, `HARD_ICE`, `HARD_GREEN`, `HARD_DARKBLUE`, etc.).

---

## Key Features
* **In-Game Image Picker:** Click the **??? Choose Image** button on the level card to open a native Windows file dialog and load any PNG or JPEG.
* **Auto-Downsampling:** Automatically downsamples large 1080p and 4K images to a 96-tile shaft width while preserving original aspect ratios.

extends Object

func _ready(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	var ls = chain.reference_object
	if ls and ("LEVEL_ORDER" in ls):
		if not ls.LEVEL_ORDER.has(103):
			ls.LEVEL_ORDER.append(103)
	
	chain.execute_next([])
	
	if is_instance_valid(ls):
		var levels_grid = ls.get_node_or_null("%Levels")
		if levels_grid:
			for card in levels_grid.get_children():
				var is_map_103 := false
				if ("game_level" in card) and card.game_level and ("level" in card.game_level) and card.game_level.level == 103:
					is_map_103 = true
				elif ("level" in card) and card.level and ("level_id" in card.level) and card.level.level_id == 103:
					is_map_103 = true
				else:
					for lbl in card.find_children("*", "Label", true, false):
						if "Living Canvas" in lbl.text:
							is_map_103 = true
							break
							
				if is_map_103:
					var container = card.get_node_or_null("MarginContainer/VBoxContainer")
					if container and not container.has_node("CanvasImagePickerBtn"):
						var btn = Button.new()
						btn.name = "CanvasImagePickerBtn"
						btn.text = "Browse Custom Canvas..."
						btn.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0, 26)
						btn.modulate = Color(0.4, 0.9, 1.0)
						var helper = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-living_canvas_mod/helpers/LivingCanvasHelper.gd")
						if helper:
							btn.pressed.connect(Callable(helper.new(), "_on_browse_pressed"))
						container.call_deferred("add_child", btn)

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-living_canvas_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-living_canvas_mod\extensions\scenes\tilemaps\tile_map_chunk.hooks.gd

extends Object

func lvl_from_global_pos(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, pos: Vector2i) -> int:
	var helper_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-living_canvas_mod/helpers/LivingCanvasHelper.gd")
	if helper_script and helper_script.is_canvas_level():
		if pos.y < 0:
			return -2
		return 1
	return chain.execute_next([pos])

func generate_chunk(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, noise: Noise) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([noise])
	var helper_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-living_canvas_mod/helpers/LivingCanvasHelper.gd")
	if helper_script and helper_script.is_canvas_level():
		var chunk = chain.reference_object
		if chunk:
			helper_script.paint_chunk_from_canvas(chunk)

func damage_tile_idx(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, idx: int, damage: float) -> void:
	var helper_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-living_canvas_mod/helpers/LivingCanvasHelper.gd")
	if helper_script and helper_script.is_canvas_level():
		var chunk = chain.reference_object
		if chunk and chunk.has_method("array_to_global_pos"):
			var global_pos: Vector2i = Vector2i(chunk.array_to_global_pos(idx))
			# High damage multiplier so starter pickaxe easily carves into canvas art
			if global_pos.y > 12:
				damage *= 5.0
	chain.execute_next([idx, damage])

func change_stage(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, target: int, targetPosition: Vector2) -> void:
	var game = chain.reference_object
	if target == 2 and Gvars and ("level" in Gvars) and Gvars.level == 103:
		# Map 103: The Living Canvas - Route to MineRegion stage
		var mine_scene = load("res://scenes/Stages/MineRegion.tscn")
		if mine_scene and game:
			game.target_stage = mine_scene

	await chain.execute_next([target, targetPosition])

class_name LivingCanvasHelper
extends RefCounted

const CANVAS_LEVEL_ID: int = 103
const PREVIEW_PATH: String = "res://mods-unpacked/gareth-living_canvas_mod/assets/default_canvas.png"

static func is_canvas_level() -> bool:
	if Gvars:
		if "level" in Gvars and Gvars.level == CANVAS_LEVEL_ID:
			return true
		if "chosen_level" in Gvars and Gvars.chosen_level == CANVAS_LEVEL_ID:
			return true
		if "current_level" in Gvars and Gvars.current_level == CANVAS_LEVEL_ID:
			return true
	return false

static func ensure_level_registered() -> void:
	if not Gvars or not ("level_manager" in Gvars) or not Gvars.level_manager:
		return

	var lvl_mgr = Gvars.level_manager
	if "all_levels" in lvl_mgr and not lvl_mgr.all_levels.has(CANVAS_LEVEL_ID):
		var custom_level = GameLevel.new()
		custom_level.level_name = "The Living Canvas"
		custom_level.level = CANVAS_LEVEL_ID
		custom_level.mining_scene_path = "res://scenes/Stages/MineRegion.tscn"
		custom_level.level_description = "Subterranean strata sculpted directly from a real image or screenshot.\n\nDefault theme: 'Your Mum'. Mine through the beach mosaic or choose any custom image."
		custom_level.level_difficulty = GameLevel.Difficulty.REGULAR
		custom_level.unlock_description = ""

		if FileAccess.file_exists(PREVIEW_PATH):
			var bytes = FileAccess.get_file_as_bytes(PREVIEW_PATH)
			if bytes.size() > 0:
				var img = Image.new()
				if img.load_png_from_buffer(bytes) == OK:
					custom_level.level_image = ImageTexture.create_from_image(img)

		lvl_mgr.all_levels[CANVAS_LEVEL_ID] = {
			"level": custom_level,
			"is_unlocked": true
		}

	if "level_info" in Gvars and Gvars.level_info is Dictionary:
		if not Gvars.level_info.has(CANVAS_LEVEL_ID):
			Gvars.level_info[CANVAS_LEVEL_ID] = {
				"name": "The Living Canvas",
				"description": "Subterranean strata sculpted from real images.",
				"depth": 1000,
				"cost": 0,
				"unlocked": true
			}

# Lost Expedition Custom Map (Coal LLC Mod)

## Overview
Adds **Lost Expedition** (Map ID 101) to Coal LLC.
- **Unexploded Ordnance (UXO):** Highly volatile demolition charges embedded in the stone. Mining triggers destructive radial detonations with visual fractures, debris, and sound.
- **Cryo-Pod Shelters:** Fortified subterranean vaults holding trapped mining personnel. Breaching a vault and opening the pod unlocks and purchases an employee level into your active workforce alongside bonus salvage cash.
- **Mineral Strata:** High-yield undulating mineral fault seams that reward deep exploration.

# Lost Expedition Custom Map (Coal LLC Mod)

## Overview
Adds **Lost Expedition** (Map ID 101) to Coal LLC.
- **Unexploded Ordnance (UXO):** Highly volatile demolition charges embedded in the stone. Mining triggers destructive radial detonations with visual fractures, debris, and sound.
- **Cryo-Pod Shelters:** Fortified subterranean vaults holding trapped mining personnel. Breaching a vault and opening the pod unlocks and purchases an employee level into your active workforce alongside bonus salvage cash.
- **Mineral Strata:** High-yield undulating mineral fault seams that reward deep exploration.

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-lost_expedition_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-lost_expedition_mod\entities\CryoPod.gd
==================================================
--------
class_name CryoPod
extends Node2D

var in_range: bool = false
var looted: bool = false
var sprite: Sprite2D
var prompt_label: Label
var tex_closed: Texture2D
var tex_opened: Texture2D
var cached_player: Node2D = null

func _ready() -> void:
	z_index = 5
	
	var img_closed = Image.load_from_file("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-lost_expedition_mod/assets/cryo_pod.png")
	if img_closed:
		tex_closed = ImageTexture.create_from_image(img_closed)
	
	var img_opened = Image.load_from_file("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-lost_expedition_mod/assets/cryo_pod_opened.png")
	if img_opened:
		tex_opened = ImageTexture.create_from_image(img_opened)
	
	sprite = Sprite2D.new()
	sprite.texture = tex_closed
	add_child(sprite)
	
	prompt_label = Label.new()
	prompt_label.text = "[E] RESCUE"
	prompt_label.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	prompt_label.position = Vector2(-36, -26)
	prompt_label.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 10)
	prompt_label.add_theme_color_override("font_color", Color(0.2, 1.0, 0.9, 1.0))
	prompt_label.add_theme_color_override("font_outline_color", Color(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0))
	prompt_label.add_theme_constant_override("outline_size", 3)
	prompt_label.visible = false
	add_child(prompt_label)

func _get_player() -> Node2D:
	if cached_player and is_instance_valid(cached_player):
		return cached_player
	if Game and "player" in Game and is_instance_valid(Game.player):
		cached_player = Game.player
		return cached_player
	var tree = get_tree()
	if not tree:
		return null
	var p = tree.root.find_child("Player", true, false)
	if p and is_instance_valid(p) and p is Node2D:
		cached_player = p
		return cached_player
	var p2 = tree.root.find_child("Player2", true, false)
	if p2 and is_instance_valid(p2) and p2 is Node2D:
		cached_player = p2
		return cached_player
	for candidate in tree.get_nodes_in_group("player"):
		if is_instance_valid(candidate) and candidate is Node2D:
			cached_player = candidate
			return cached_player
	return null

func _process(_delta: float) -> void:
	if looted:
		return
	
	var player = _get_player()
	if not player:
		return
	
	var dist: float = global_position.distance_to(player.global_position)
	if dist <= 38.0:
		if not in_range:
			in_range = true
			prompt_label.visible = true
			if Bus and Bus.has_signal("ShowInputIndicator"):
				Bus.ShowInputIndicator.emit("interact", true, self)
		
		if Input.is_action_just_pressed("interact"):
			rescue_worker()
	else:
		if in_range:
			in_range = false
			prompt_label.visible = false
			if Bus and Bus.has_signal("ShowInputIndicator"):
				Bus.ShowInputIndicator.emit("interact", false, self)

func rescue_worker() -> void:
	if looted:
		return
	looted = true
	in_range = false
	prompt_label.visible = false
	
	if Bus and Bus.has_signal("ShowInputIndicator"):
		Bus.ShowInputIndicator.emit("interact", false, self)
	
	# 1. Swap visual to evacuated chamber
	if tex_opened:
		sprite.texture = tex_opened
	else:
		sprite.modulate = Color(0.35, 0.4, 0.45, 0.7)
	
	# 2. Audio feedback
	var sfx_stream = load("res://assets/sound/sound effects/coolDing.wav")
	if sfx_stream:
		var sfx = AudioStreamPlayer2D.new()
		sfx.stream = sfx_stream
		add_child(sfx)
		sfx.play()
	
	# 3. Direct Workforce Ledger Persistence
	var emp_mgr = Gvars.employee_manager
	var rescued_title: String = "Expedition Specialist"
	var chosen_emp: EmployeeLevel = null
	if emp_mgr and emp_mgr.ALL_EMPLOYEES.size() > 0:
		chosen_emp = emp_mgr.ALL_EMPLOYEES[randi() % emp_mgr.ALL_EMPLOYEES.size()]
		var emp_key = chosen_emp.level_name
		
		# Directly credit roster dictionary (bypasses store purchase requirements)
		if not emp_mgr.employees.has(emp_key):
			emp_mgr.employees[emp_key] = 0.0
		emp_mgr.employees[emp_key] += 1.0
		
		# Unlock this tier and predecessor tiers so they are not hidden by more_employees_mod
		var curr = chosen_emp
		while curr:
			if emp_mgr.locked_employees.has(curr.level_name):
				emp_mgr.locked_employees.erase(curr.level_name)
			if "upgrades_from" in curr and curr.upgrades_from != curr.EmployeeLevelNames.NOTHING:
				curr = emp_mgr.get_employee_level_from_enum(curr.upgrades_from)
			else:
				break
		
		if EmployeeLevel.EmployeeLevelNames.keys().size() > chosen_emp.level_name:
			rescued_title = EmployeeLevel.EmployeeLevelNames.keys()[chosen_emp.level_name].capitalize()
		elif chosen_emp.get("level_name") != null:
			rescued_title = str(chosen_emp.level_name)
	
	# 4. Physically instantiate live worker directly inside MineRegion for current shift
	if chosen_emp:
		var mine_region = get_tree().root.find_child("MineRegion", true, false)
		var worker_instance = null
		var is_collector: bool = false
		if "type" in chosen_emp and chosen_emp.type == EmployeeLevel.EmployeeType.COLLECTOR:
			is_collector = true
		
		if is_collector:
			var ufo_scene = load("res://scenes/LootCollectorUFO.tscn")
			if ufo_scene:
				worker_instance = ufo_scene.instantiate()
				worker_instance.employee_level = chosen_emp
				worker_instance.count = 1.0
				worker_instance.global_position = global_position
		else:
			var miner_scene = load("res://scenes/AutoMiners/multi_miner.tscn")
			if miner_scene:
				worker_instance = miner_scene.instantiate()
				worker_instance.employee_level = chosen_emp
				worker_instance.count = 1.0
				var tm = get_tree().root.find_child("TileMapManager", true, false)
				if tm:
					worker_instance.tilemap = tm
				worker_instance.global_position = global_position
		
		if worker_instance:
			if mine_region:
				mine_region.add_child(worker_instance)
			else:
				get_parent().add_child(worker_instance)
	
	Gvars.CashCount += 10000.0
	if Bus and Bus.has_signal("UpdateUI"):
		Bus.UpdateUI.emit()
	
	ModLoaderLog.info("Cryo-Pod breached! Rescued " + rescued_title + " added to Org Chart & active in mine! Salvaged $10,000.", "gareth-lost_expedition_mod")
	
	# 5. Passive selection card bonus
	var depth_mult: float = maxf(2.0, minf(6.0, 2.0 + abs(global_position.y) / 250.0))
	if Bus and Bus.has_signal("choose_passive"):
		Bus.choose_passive.emit(depth_mult)

func _ready(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	var helper_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-lost_expedition_mod/helpers/LostExpeditionHelper.gd")
	if helper_script:
		var helper = helper_script.new()
		helper.ensure_level_registered()
		var ls = chain.reference_object
		if ls and ("LEVEL_ORDER" in ls):
			if not ls.LEVEL_ORDER.has(helper.EXPEDITION_LEVEL_ID):
				ls.LEVEL_ORDER.append(helper.EXPEDITION_LEVEL_ID)
	chain.execute_next([])

func load_equipment_shop(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([])
	if Gvars.level == 101:
		var ms = chain.reference_object
		if ms and "equipment_shop" in ms and ms.equipment_shop:
			var allowed := ["pickaxe", "item_filter", "increase_stack_size", "ladder", "whistle", "collector_whistle", "mining_whistle"]
			for card in ms.equipment_shop.get_children():
				if "item" in card and card.item:
					var itype: String = str(card.item.itemType).to_lower()
					if not allowed.has(itype):
						card.visible = false

func lvl_from_global_pos(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, pos: Vector2i) -> int:
	if Gvars.level == 101:
		var helper_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-lost_expedition_mod/helpers/LostExpeditionHelper.gd")
		if helper_script:
			var helper = helper_script.new()
			return helper.calculate_tier(pos)
	return chain.execute_next([pos])

func generate_chunk(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, noise: Noise) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([noise])
	if Gvars.level == 101:
		var chunk = chain.reference_object
		if chunk:
			var helper_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-lost_expedition_mod/helpers/LostExpeditionHelper.gd")
			if helper_script:
				var helper = helper_script.new()
				helper.tag_uxo_tiles(chunk)

func destroy_tile(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, idx: int, tile_properties: Dictionary) -> void:
	if Gvars.level == 101:
		var chunk = chain.reference_object
		if chunk:
			var helper_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-lost_expedition_mod/helpers/LostExpeditionHelper.gd")
			if helper_script:
				var helper = helper_script.new()
				helper.handle_tile_destroyed(chunk, idx, tile_properties)
	chain.execute_next([idx, tile_properties])

func generate_chests(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, current_block_chest_locations: Array[Vector2i]) -> Array[Vector2i]:
	var locs: Array[Vector2i] = chain.execute_next([current_block_chest_locations])
	if Gvars.level == 101:
		var chunk = chain.reference_object
		if chunk:
			var helper_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-lost_expedition_mod/helpers/LostExpeditionHelper.gd")
			if helper_script:
				var helper = helper_script.new()
				locs = helper.spawn_cryo_pods(chunk, locs)
	return locs

func roll_loot(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([])
	if Gvars.level == 101:
		var chest = chain.reference_object
		if chest and chest.loot:
			var itype: String = str(chest.loot.itemType).to_lower()
			var iid: String = str(chest.loot.itemID).to_lower()
			# Replace non-pickaxe weapon scrolls with passive upgrades
			if itype == "scroll" and not iid.contains("pickaxe"):
				var pass_res = load("res://resources/Items/power_ups/PassiveUpgrade.tres")
				if pass_res:
					chest.loot = pass_res.duplicate()
					chest.loot_count = 1
			elif itype not in ["gem", "ore", "pickaxe", "passive_upgrade", "scroll"]:
				var pass_res = load("res://resources/Items/power_ups/PassiveUpgrade.tres")
				if pass_res:
					chest.loot = pass_res.duplicate()
					chest.loot_count = 1

extends Object

func change_stage(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, target: int, targetPosition: Vector2) -> void:
	var game = chain.reference_object
	var helper_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-lost_expedition_mod/helpers/LostExpeditionHelper.gd")
	if helper_script:
		var helper = helper_script.new()
		helper.ensure_level_registered()

	if target == 2 and Gvars.level == 101:
		if game and "MINE_REGION" in game:
			game.target_stage = game.MINE_REGION

	await chain.execute_next([target, targetPosition])

	if game:
		if game.has_node("ColorRect"):
			game.get_node("ColorRect").mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
		game.visible = false
		# Supply player & profession reference so nanobotz reads Gvars.profession
		if Gvars.profession:
			if not ("player" in game) or game.player == null:
				var player_node = game.get_tree().get_first_node_in_group("player")
				if player_node:
					game.player = player_node
			if "player" in game and game.player:
				game.player.set("profession", Gvars.profession)

class_name LostExpeditionHelper
extends RefCounted

const EXPEDITION_LEVEL_ID: int = 101

func ensure_level_registered() -> void:
	var lvl_mgr = Gvars.level_manager
	if not lvl_mgr:
		return
	
	if not lvl_mgr.all_levels.has(EXPEDITION_LEVEL_ID):
		var custom_level = GameLevel.new()
		custom_level.level_name = "Lost Expedition"
		custom_level.level = EXPEDITION_LEVEL_ID
		custom_level.mining_scene_path = "res://scenes/Stages/MineRegion.tscn"
		custom_level.level_description = "Abandoned subterranean expedition.\nDeep strata riddled with volatile unexploded ordnance.\nCryo-shelters contain trapped mining personnel."
		custom_level.level_difficulty = GameLevel.Difficulty.HARD
		custom_level.unlock_description = ""
		
		var img = Image.load_from_file("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-lost_expedition_mod/assets/lost_expedition_card.png")
		if img:
			custom_level.level_image = ImageTexture.create_from_image(img)
		
		lvl_mgr.all_levels[EXPEDITION_LEVEL_ID] = {
			"level": custom_level,
			"is_unlocked": true
		}

func calculate_tier(pos: Vector2i) -> int:
	var depth: float = float(pos.y)
	if depth < 0.0:
		return -2
	
	var wave: float = sin(float(pos.x) * 0.12) * 5.0
	var effective_y: float = depth + wave
	
	if effective_y < 12.0:
		return 0
	elif effective_y < 35.0:
		return 1
	elif effective_y < 70.0:
		return 2
	elif effective_y < 120.0:
		return 3
	elif effective_y < 200.0:
		return 4
	elif effective_y < 300.0:
		return 5
	elif effective_y < 450.0:
		return 6
	elif effective_y < 600.0:
		return 7
	elif effective_y < 750.0:
		return 8
	elif effective_y < 900.0:
		return 9
	elif effective_y < 1050.0:
		return 10
	elif effective_y < 1200.0:
		return 11
	else:
		return 12

func tag_uxo_tiles(chunk: Node) -> void:
	var uxo: Dictionary = {}
	var total_cells: int = chunk.chunk_size * chunk.chunk_size
	for idx in range(total_cells):
		var tile_id = chunk.tiles[idx]
		if tile_id != chunk.Tiles.EMPTY and chunk.TILE_PROPERTIES[tile_id]["breakable"]:
			if randf() < 0.035:
				uxo[idx] = true
	chunk.set_meta("expedition_uxo", uxo)

func handle_tile_destroyed(chunk: Node, idx: int, tile_properties: Dictionary = {}) -> void:
	if chunk.has_meta("expedition_uxo"):
		var uxo: Dictionary = chunk.get_meta("expedition_uxo")
		if uxo.has(idx):
			uxo.erase(idx)
			detonate_uxo(chunk, idx)
	
	if tile_properties and tile_properties.get("loot", "none") != "none":
		var global_pos = chunk.to_global(chunk.map_to_local(chunk.array_to_pos(idx)))
		if randf() < 0.35:
			var bonus_count: int = randi_range(2, 6)
			Bus.drop_loot.emit(global_pos + Vector2(randf_range(-6.0, 6.0), randf_range(-6.0, 6.0)), "res://resources/Items/Coal.tres", bonus_count)
		if randf() < 0.15:
			var salvage_cash: float = randf_range(250.0, 1500.0)
			Gvars.CashCount += salvage_cash

func detonate_uxo(chunk: Node, idx: int) -> void:
	var local_pos: Vector2 = chunk.map_to_local(chunk.array_to_pos(idx))
	var world_center: Vector2 = chunk.to_global(local_pos)
	var manager = chunk.get_parent()
	for dx in range(-2, 3):
		for dy in range(-2, 3):
			var offset_world: Vector2 = world_center + Vector2(dx * 16.0, dy * 16.0)
			if manager and manager.has_method("damageTile"):
				manager.damageTile(offset_world, 450)

func spawn_cryo_pods(chunk: Node, current_locs: Array[Vector2i]) -> Array[Vector2i]:
	if randf() > 0.60:
		return current_locs
	
	# Strict: Surface chunks (y <= 0) must never contain subterranean vaults
	if "chunk_pos" in chunk and chunk.chunk_pos.y < 1:
		return current_locs
	
	var width: int = 3
	var height: int = 3
	var start_tile: Vector2i = Vector2i(
		randi_range(0, chunk.chunk_size - width),
		randi_range(0, chunk.chunk_size - height)
	)
	
	var local_start: Vector2 = chunk.map_to_local(start_tile)
	var world_start: Vector2 = chunk.to_global(local_start)
	var global_cell: Vector2i = Vector2i(world_start / 16.0)
	if global_cell.y < 16:
		return current_locs
	
	# Vault walls dynamically match the hardness of the surrounding stratum
	var pod_tier: int = calculate_tier(global_cell)
	var wall_tile: int = chunk.chest_block_from_level(pod_tier)
	if wall_tile == chunk.Tiles.EMPTY:
		wall_tile = chunk.Tiles.HARD_CLAY
	
	for n in range(5):
		if start_tile in current_locs:
			start_tile = Vector2i(
				randi_range(0, chunk.chunk_size - width),
				randi_range(0, chunk.chunk_size - height)
			)
	
	for x in range(start_tile.x - width, start_tile.x + width):
		for y in range(start_tile.y - height, start_tile.y + height):
			current_locs.append(Vector2i(x, y))
	
	for x in range(start_tile.x, start_tile.x + width):
		for y in range(start_tile.y, start_tile.y + height):
			var c_idx: int = chunk.pos_to_array(Vector2i(x, y))
			if (x == start_tile.x + 1) and (y == start_tile.y + 1):
				chunk.tiles[c_idx] = chunk.Tiles.EMPTY
				chunk.build_tile(c_idx)
				var pod_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-lost_expedition_mod/entities/CryoPod.gd")
				if pod_script:
					var pod = pod_script.new()
					pod.position = chunk.map_to_local(Vector2i(x, y))
					chunk.add_child.call_deferred(pod)
			else:
				chunk.tiles[c_idx] = wall_tile
				chunk.build_tile(c_idx)
	
	return current_locs

# Martial Artist Legends Expansion

**Namespace:** $namespace  
**Compatibility:** Coal LLC v1.4.1.1+ (ModLoader 7.0.1)

---

## Overview
Adds high-tier legendary boxing gloves and roundhouse kicks inspired by legendary fighters, featuring screen-clearing kinetic impact.

---

## Technical Details (For Modders)
Hooks attack_tile in boxing_gloves.gd and damage_tiles in kick.gd to apply compounding passive punch/kick damage.

---

## Installation
1. Ensure **Coal LLC** has Godot ModLoader 7.0.1 installed.
2. Place the packaged .zip file into your Coal LLC/mods/ directory.
3. Manage or toggle this mod directly via the in-game **Mod Manager**.

extends Node

const MOD_DIR := "gareth-martial_legends_mod"

func _init() -> void:
	var mod_dir_path: String = ModLoaderMod.get_unpacked_dir().path_join(MOD_DIR)
	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks(
		"res://scenes/equipment/boxing_gloves.gd",
		mod_dir_path.path_join("extensions/scenes/equipment/boxing_gloves.hooks.gd")
	)
	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks(
		"res://scenes/equipment/kick.gd",
		mod_dir_path.path_join("extensions/scenes/equipment/kick.hooks.gd")
	)
	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks(
		"res://resources/professions/scripts/martial_artist.gd",
		mod_dir_path.path_join("extensions/resources/professions/scripts/martial_artist.hooks.gd")
	)

func _ready() -> void:
	var item_unlocks = load("res://resources/unlockable_items/scripts/item_unlocks.tres")
	if not item_unlocks:
		return
	var dir_path := "res://mods-unpacked/gareth-martial_legends_mod/items"
	var dir := DirAccess.open(dir_path)
	if dir:
		dir.list_dir_begin()
		var file_name := dir.get_next()
		while file_name != "":
			if file_name.ends_with(".tres"):
				var item = load(dir_path.path_join(file_name))
				if item:
					if not item in item_unlocks.all_equipment_items:
						item_unlocks.all_equipment_items.append(item)
					if not item in item_unlocks.all_items:
						item_unlocks.all_items.append(item)
			file_name = dir.get_next()
	ModLoaderLog.info("Martial Artist Legends expansion loaded cleanly.", MOD_DIR)

func _profession_effect(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, _data: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	var res: Dictionary = chain.execute_next([_data])
	if res.has("unlocked_passives"):
		if not "punch_damage" in res["unlocked_passives"]:
			res["unlocked_passives"].append("punch_damage")
		if not "punch_speed" in res["unlocked_passives"]:
			res["unlocked_passives"].append("punch_speed")
		if not "kick_damage" in res["unlocked_passives"]:
			res["unlocked_passives"].append("kick_damage")
		if not "kick_speed" in res["unlocked_passives"]:
			res["unlocked_passives"].append("kick_speed")
	return res

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-martial_legends_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-martial_legends_mod\extensions\scenes\equipment\boxing_gloves.hooks.gd

extends Object

var punch_counter: int = 0

func attack_tile(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, count: float) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([count])
	var bg = chain.reference_object
	if not bg or not bg.tilemap:
		return

	var tree = bg.get_tree()
	if not tree:
		return
	var player = tree.get_first_node_in_group("player")
	if not player or not ("item_equipped" in player) or not player.item_equipped:
		return

	var item_id: String = player.item_equipped.itemID
	var player_tile: Vector2i = bg.tilemap.local_to_map(bg.global_position)
	var dir_vec: Vector2 = (bg.attack_direction.global_position - bg.global_position).normalized()
	var direction := Vector2i(round(dir_vec.x), round(dir_vec.y))
	if direction == Vector2i.ZERO:
		direction = Vector2i.RIGHT

	punch_counter += 1

	# MIKE TYSON EXPANSION
	if item_id == "boxing_gloves_mike_tyson":
		bg.attacks_per_second = 10.0
		var combo: int = punch_counter % 3
		if combo == 0:
			_berbick_left_hook(bg, player_tile, direction, count)
		elif combo == 1:
			_frazier_uppercut(bg, player_tile, direction, count)
		elif combo == 2:
			_spinks_uppercut(bg, player_tile, direction, count)
	elif item_id == "boxing_gloves_tyson_frazier":
		bg.attacks_per_second = 9.0
		_frazier_uppercut(bg, player_tile, direction, count)
	elif item_id == "boxing_gloves_tyson_berbick":
		bg.attacks_per_second = 8.0
		_berbick_left_hook(bg, player_tile, direction, count)
	elif item_id == "boxing_gloves_tyson_spinks":
		bg.attacks_per_second = 8.0
		_spinks_uppercut(bg, player_tile, direction, count)
	elif item_id == "boxing_gloves_chuck_norris":
		bg.attacks_per_second = 12.0
		for dist in range(1, 4):
			var target: Vector2i = player_tile + (direction * dist)
			if bg.tilemap.is_tile_breakable(target):
				bg.tilemap.damageTileCoords(target, bg.damage * 3.0 * count)
				bg.tilemap.sparks_manager.add_spark(bg.tilemap.map_to_local(target))
		if "kick_instance" in player and player.kick_instance:
			player.kick_instance.rotation_speed = 36.0

func _berbick_left_hook(bg: Node2D, pt: Vector2i, dir: Vector2i, count: float) -> void:
	var perp := Vector2i(-dir.y, dir.x)
	var targets := [
		pt + dir,
		pt + dir + perp,
		pt + dir - perp
	]
	for t in targets:
		if bg.tilemap.is_tile_breakable(t):
			bg.tilemap.damageTileCoords(t, bg.damage * 1.5 * count)
			bg.tilemap.sparks_manager.add_spark(bg.tilemap.map_to_local(t))

func _frazier_uppercut(bg: Node2D, pt: Vector2i, dir: Vector2i, count: float) -> void:
	var vert_dir := Vector2i.UP
	if dir.y > 0:
		vert_dir = Vector2i.DOWN
	for h in range(1, 4):
		var t: Vector2i = pt + (vert_dir * h)
		if bg.tilemap.is_tile_breakable(t):
			bg.tilemap.damageTileCoords(t, bg.damage * 2.0 * count)
			bg.tilemap.sparks_manager.add_spark(bg.tilemap.map_to_local(t))

func _spinks_uppercut(bg: Node2D, pt: Vector2i, dir: Vector2i, count: float) -> void:
	var target: Vector2i = pt + dir
	if bg.tilemap.is_tile_breakable(target):
		bg.tilemap.damageTileCoords(target, bg.damage * 10.0 * count)
		bg.tilemap.sparks_manager.add_spark(bg.tilemap.map_to_local(target))

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-martial_legends_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-martial_legends_mod\extensions\scenes\equipment\kick.hooks.gd

extends Object

func _process(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, delta: float) -> void:
	var kick = chain.reference_object
	if kick:
		var tree = kick.get_tree()
		if tree:
			var player = tree.get_first_node_in_group("player")
			if player and ("item_equipped" in player) and player.item_equipped:
				var item_id: String = player.item_equipped.itemID
				if item_id.begins_with("kick_jcvd"):
					kick.rotation_speed = 32.0
				elif item_id.begins_with("kick_chuck_norris"):
					kick.rotation_speed = 40.0
					if "boxing_gloves_instance" in player and player.boxing_gloves_instance:
						player.boxing_gloves_instance.attacks_per_second = 15.0
	chain.execute_next([delta])

func damage_tiles(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, damage_idxes: Array[int]) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([damage_idxes])
	var kick = chain.reference_object
	if not kick or not kick.tilemap:
		return

	var tree = kick.get_tree()
	if not tree:
		return
	var player = tree.get_first_node_in_group("player")
	if not player or not ("item_equipped" in player) or not player.item_equipped:
		return

	var item_id: String = player.item_equipped.itemID
	var centre_tile: Vector2i = kick.tilemap.local_to_map(kick.global_position)

	# JCVD: The Epic Split
	if item_id == "kick_jcvd_epic_split":
		for dist in range(1, 5):
			var left_tile: Vector2i = centre_tile + Vector2i(-dist, 0)
			var right_tile: Vector2i = centre_tile + Vector2i(dist, 0)
			if kick.tilemap.is_tile_breakable(left_tile):
				kick.tilemap.damageTileCoords(left_tile, kick.damage * 1.5)
				kick.tilemap.sparks_manager.add_spark(kick.tilemap.map_to_local(left_tile))
			if kick.tilemap.is_tile_breakable(right_tile):
				kick.tilemap.damageTileCoords(right_tile, kick.damage * 1.5)
				kick.tilemap.sparks_manager.add_spark(kick.tilemap.map_to_local(right_tile))

	# JCVD: Helicopter Split-Kick
	elif item_id == "kick_jcvd_helicopter":
		for x in range(-5, 6):
			for y in range(-5, 6):
				if abs(x) == 5 or abs(y) == 5 or (abs(x) == 4 and abs(y) == 4):
					var outer_tile: Vector2i = centre_tile + Vector2i(x, y)
					if kick.tilemap.is_tile_breakable(outer_tile):
						kick.tilemap.damageTileCoords(outer_tile, kick.damage * 1.0)

	# Chuck Norris: Roundhouse Kick (8-tile shockwave)
	elif item_id == "kick_chuck_norris_roundhouse":
		var facing := Vector2i.RIGHT
		if "facing_direction" in player:
			var fd: Vector2 = player.facing_direction
			facing = Vector2i(round(fd.x), round(fd.y))
		if facing == Vector2i.ZERO:
			facing = Vector2i.RIGHT
		for dist in range(1, 9):
			var shock_tile: Vector2i = centre_tile + (facing * dist)
			if kick.tilemap.is_tile_breakable(shock_tile):
				kick.tilemap.damageTileCoords(shock_tile, kick.damage * 5.0)
				kick.tilemap.sparks_manager.add_spark(kick.tilemap.map_to_local(shock_tile))

	# Chuck Norris: Spinning Back Heel (Crushing point impact)
	elif item_id == "kick_chuck_norris_spinning_heel":
		for dx in [-1, 0, 1]:
			for dy in [-1, 0, 1]:
				var close_tile: Vector2i = centre_tile + Vector2i(dx, dy)
				if kick.tilemap.is_tile_breakable(close_tile):
					kick.tilemap.damageTileCoords(close_tile, kick.damage * 12.0)

--------
# Universal Mod Manager & Mutator Selector

**Namespace:** $namespace  
**Compatibility:** Coal LLC v1.4.1.1+ (ModLoader 7.0.1)

---

## Overview
The **Universal Mod Manager** provides seamless runtime control over every installed modification for *Coal LLC*. It dynamically hooks the Main Menu, in-game Pause Menu, and the native Settings screen, offering both an executive-level overview of installed mods and a full-screen pre-run mutator selector that categorizes add-ons into Weapons, Progression, Automation, Hardcore Mutators, and Performance utilities.

The mod automatically scans ModLoaderStore.mod_data on engine boot, discovering present and future .zip packages without hardcoded registry lists. When starting a career run, the pre-run modal captures your custom toggle presets and writes them into user://saves/slot_X/run_config.json, preserving independent mod loadouts across different save profiles.

Additionally, this framework includes an active shop filtering layer that intercepts management_screen.gd. It removes disabled weapons from the store shelves and enforces class-specific weapon whitelists, ensuring classes only encounter equipment matching their chosen archetype.

---

## Technical Details (For Modders)
Hooks MainMenu.gd, settings_2.gd, PauseMenu.gd, choose_passive.gd, and management_screen.gd. Intercepts load_equipment_shop() to filter shop cards by run_config.json toggles and class whitelists.

---

## Installation
1. Ensure **Coal LLC** has Godot ModLoader 7.0.1 installed.
2. Place the packaged .zip file into your Coal LLC/mods/ directory.
3. Manage or toggle this mod directly via the in-game **Mod Manager**.
--------

extends Object

func initialise_slots(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	var inv = chain.reference_object
	if not is_instance_valid(inv):
		chain.execute_next([])
		return

	var target_slots: int = 10
	if "maxSlots" in inv and inv.maxSlots > 0:
		target_slots = int(inv.maxSlots)

	var empty_res = load("res://resources/Items/Empty.tres")
	var current_size: int = inv.items.size()

	for n in range(current_size, target_slots):
		var empty_slot = InventoryItem.new()
		if empty_res:
			empty_slot.set("item", empty_res)
		empty_slot.count = 0.0
		inv.items.append(empty_slot)

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-mod_manager_mod\extensions\scenes\Interfaces\Management\passive_bonuses.hooks.gd
==================================================
--------
extends Object

var bonus_search: LineEdit = null
var bonus_sort: OptionButton = null
var bonus_chk_afford: CheckBox = null
var is_bonus_affordable_only: bool = false

func refresh(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([])
	var panel = chain.reference_object
	if not is_instance_valid(panel):
		return

	_ensure_filter_bar(panel)
	_apply_bonus_filter(panel)

func _ensure_filter_bar(panel: Control) -> void:
	var scroll = panel.get_parent()
	var v_box = scroll.get_parent() if scroll else null
	if not v_box:
		return

	if v_box.has_node("BonusFilterBar"):
		var existing = v_box.get_node("BonusFilterBar")
		bonus_search = existing.get_node_or_null("BonusSearch")
		bonus_sort = existing.get_node_or_null("BonusSort")
		bonus_chk_afford = existing.get_node_or_null("BonusAfford")
		return

	var bar = HBoxContainer.new()
	bar.name = "BonusFilterBar"
	bar.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 10)

	var search = LineEdit.new()
	search.name = "BonusSearch"
	search.placeholder_text = "Search bonus upgrades..."
	search.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	search.clear_button_enabled = true
	search.text_changed.connect(func(_t): _apply_bonus_filter(panel))
	bar.add_child(search)
	bonus_search = search

	var sort_btn = OptionButton.new()
	sort_btn.name = "BonusSort"
	sort_btn.add_item("Sort: Default", 0)
	sort_btn.add_item("Name: A-Z", 1)
	sort_btn.item_selected.connect(func(_idx): _apply_bonus_filter(panel))
	bar.add_child(sort_btn)
	bonus_sort = sort_btn

	var chk = CheckBox.new()
	chk.name = "BonusAfford"
	chk.text = "Affordable Only"
	chk.tooltip_text = "Show only upgrades you can purchase right now."
	chk.toggled.connect(func(pressed: bool):
		is_bonus_affordable_only = pressed
		_apply_bonus_filter(panel)
	)
	bar.add_child(chk)
	bonus_chk_afford = chk

	v_box.add_child(bar)
	v_box.move_child(bar, scroll.get_index())

func _apply_bonus_filter(panel: Control) -> void:
	if not is_instance_valid(panel) or not is_instance_valid(bonus_search):
		return
	var q: String = bonus_search.text.strip_edges().to_lower()
	var cur_cash: float = Gvars.CashCount if Gvars else 0.0

	for item in panel.get_children():
		if not is_instance_valid(item):
			continue
		var label = item.find_child("*Label*", true, false)
		var text: String = label.text.to_lower() if (label and "text" in label) else ""
		var match_q: bool = q.is_empty() or q in text

		var cost: float = 0.0
		if "cost" in item:
			cost = float(item.cost)
		elif "price" in item:
			cost = float(item.price)

		var match_afford: bool = not is_bonus_affordable_only or (cost <= cur_cash or cost <= 0.0)
		item.visible = match_q and match_afford

extends Node

const MOD_ID := "gareth-mod_manager_mod"
const LOG_NAME := "gareth-mod_manager_mod"

func _init() -> void:
	var unpacked_dir = ModLoaderMod.get_unpacked_dir().path_join(MOD_ID)
	var ext_dir = unpacked_dir.path_join("extensions")

	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks("res://resources/Inventories/Scripts/Inventory.gd", ext_dir.path_join("resources/Inventories/Scripts/Inventory.hooks.gd"))
	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks("res://resources/BuyEffects/Scripts/AddToInventory.gd", ext_dir.path_join("resources/BuyEffects/Scripts/AddToInventory.hooks.gd"))
	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks("res://scenes/Interfaces/Management/shop_item_card.gd", ext_dir.path_join("scenes/Interfaces/Management/shop_item_card.hooks.gd"))
	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks("res://scripts/Stockpile.gd", ext_dir.path_join("scripts/Stockpile.hooks.gd"))
	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks("res://scenes/Interfaces/in_game/choose_passive.gd", ext_dir.path_join("scenes/Interfaces/in_game/choose_passive.hooks.gd"))
	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks("res://scenes/Interfaces/Management/passive_bonuses.gd", ext_dir.path_join("scenes/Interfaces/Management/passive_bonuses.hooks.gd"))
	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks("res://scripts/MainMenu.gd", ext_dir.path_join("scripts/MainMenu.hooks.gd"))
	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks("res://scripts/Gvars.gd", ext_dir.path_join("scripts/Gvars.hooks.gd"))
	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks("res://scenes/Stages/management_screen.gd", ext_dir.path_join("scenes/Stages/management_screen.hooks.gd"))
	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks("res://scripts/Camera.gd", ext_dir.path_join("scripts/Camera.hooks.gd"))
	ModLoaderLog.info("Universal Mod Manager initializing with Inventory hooks...", LOG_NAME)

func _ready() -> void:
	ModLoaderLog.info("Universal Mod Manager active with full Class, Shop & Input gating.", LOG_NAME)

extends Object

func get_max_count(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, price: float, count: float) -> float:
	if price <= 0.0:
		return count
	if Gvars and is_nan(Gvars.CashCount):
		Gvars.CashCount = 0.0
	if Gvars and Gvars.CashCount <= 0.0:
		return 0.0
	var _max = floor(Gvars.CashCount / price)
	return min(_max, count)

func buy_effect(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, shop_item: Item, count: int) -> void:
	if not shop_item:
		chain.execute_next([shop_item, count])
		return

	if Gvars and is_nan(Gvars.CashCount):
		Gvars.CashCount = 0.0

	var price: float = float(shop_item.itemBuyPrice)
	# 0g starter items bypass wallet deduction cleanly
	if price <= 0.0:
		var inventory: Inventory = load("res://resources/Inventories/PlayerInventory.tres")
		if inventory:
			inventory.addToInventory(shop_item, count)
			inventory.emit_signal("inventory_changed")
			Bus.inventory_changed.emit()
		return

	# Pass exact vanilla signature: [Item, int]
	chain.execute_next([shop_item, count])

const CUSTOM_PASSIVE_DEFAULTS: Dictionary = {
	"ball_blast_damage": 0.05, "ball_blast_radius": 0.05, "ball_fire_rate": 0.05,
	"chrono_tick_rate": 0.05, "chrono_shockwave_damage": 0.05, "chrono_stasis_duration": 0.05,
	"exec_damage": 0.05, "exec_fire_rate": 0.05, "exec_bounty": 0.05,
	"drill_speed": 0.05, "axe_speed": 0.05, "gun_rate": 0.05,
	"dread_shell_damage": 0.05, "dread_fire_rate": 0.05, "dread_impact_radius": 0.05,
	"jedi_saber_damage": 0.05, "jedi_swing_rate": 0.05, "jedi_cleave_reach": 0.05,
	"sword_damage": 0.05, "sword_speed": 0.05, "sword_range": 0.05,
	"employee_miner_range": 0.05, "employee_miner_pickup_speed": 0.05, "collector_range": 0.05,
	"sinclair_melt_damage": 0.05, "sinclair_beam_width": 0.05, "sinclair_spectrum_ticks": 0.05,
	"fracker_pulse_damage": 0.05, "fracker_seam_resonance": 0.05, "fracker_fire_rate": 0.05,
	"void_implosion_damage": 0.05, "void_pull_radius": 0.05, "void_event_horizon": 0.05
}

func set_up_buttons(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	var screen = chain.reference_object
	if not is_instance_valid(screen) or not ("choose_between" in screen):
		chain.execute_next([])
		return

	var buttons = [screen.button, screen.button_2, screen.button_3]
	for i in range(0, min(3, screen.choose_between.size())):
		var stat_name: String = str(screen.choose_between[i])
		var base_val: float = 0.05
		if screen.ALL_PASSIVES.has(stat_name):
			base_val = screen.ALL_PASSIVES[stat_name]
		elif CUSTOM_PASSIVE_DEFAULTS.has(stat_name):
			base_val = CUSTOM_PASSIVE_DEFAULTS[stat_name]

		var pct_str: String = str(screen.multiplier * (base_val * 100.0))
		buttons[i].text = "Increase " + stat_name.replace("_", " ").capitalize() + " +" + pct_str + "%"

func _on_button_pressed(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	_handle_button_click(chain, 0)

func _on_button_2_pressed(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	_handle_button_click(chain, 1)

func _on_button_3_pressed(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	_handle_button_click(chain, 2)

func _handle_button_click(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, button_idx: int) -> void:
	var screen = chain.reference_object
	if not is_instance_valid(screen) or not screen.live or not ("choose_between" in screen):
		chain.execute_next([])
		return

	if button_idx >= screen.choose_between.size():
		chain.execute_next([])
		return

	var stat_name: String = str(screen.choose_between[button_idx])
	if screen.ALL_PASSIVES.has(stat_name):
		chain.execute_next([])
		return

	screen.live = false
	var base_val: float = CUSTOM_PASSIVE_DEFAULTS.get(stat_name, 0.05)
	var add_amount: float = screen.multiplier * base_val

	if Gvars and ("passives" in Gvars) and Gvars.passives:
		var p: Resource = Gvars.passives
		var cur_meta: float = 0.0
		if p.has_meta(stat_name):
			cur_meta = float(p.get_meta(stat_name))
		p.set_meta(stat_name, cur_meta + add_amount)

	if Gvars:
		if not ("custom_passives" in Gvars) or Gvars.custom_passives == null:
			Gvars.set("custom_passives", {})
		var cp = Gvars.get("custom_passives")
		if cp is Dictionary:
			cp[stat_name] = cp.get(stat_name, 0.0) + add_amount

	screen.get_tree().paused = false
	screen.queue_free()

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-mod_manager_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-mod_manager_mod\extensions\scenes\Interfaces\Management\equipment_shop.hooks.gd

extends Object

func _ready(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([])
	var shop = chain.reference_object
	if not is_instance_valid(shop):
		return

	var bar = shop.find_child("HBoxContainer", true, false)
	if not bar:
		bar = shop.find_child("SortContainer", true, false)

	if bar and not bar.has_node("CheckAffordable"):
		var chk = CheckBox.new()
		chk.name = "CheckAffordable"
		chk.text = "Affordable Only"
		chk.tooltip_text = "Hide items that exceed your current wallet balance."
		chk.toggled.connect(func(pressed: bool):
			Gvars.show_only_affordable = pressed
			if shop.has_method("refresh_shop"):
				shop.refresh_shop()
			else:
				for card in shop.find_children("*", "PanelContainer", true, false):
					if card.has_method("refreshDetails"):
						card.refreshDetails()
		)
		bar.add_child(chk)

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-mod_manager_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-mod_manager_mod\extensions\scenes\Interfaces\Management\passive_bonuses.hooks.gd

extends Object

var bonus_search: LineEdit = null
var bonus_sort: OptionButton = null
var bonus_chk_afford: CheckBox = null
var is_bonus_affordable_only: bool = false

func refresh(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([])
	var panel = chain.reference_object
	if not is_instance_valid(panel):
		return

	_ensure_filter_bar(panel)
	_apply_bonus_filter(panel)

func _ensure_filter_bar(panel: Control) -> void:
	var scroll = panel.get_parent()
	var v_box = scroll.get_parent() if scroll else null
	if not v_box:
		return

	if v_box.has_node("BonusFilterBar"):
		var existing = v_box.get_node("BonusFilterBar")
		bonus_search = existing.get_node_or_null("BonusSearch")
		bonus_sort = existing.get_node_or_null("BonusSort")
		bonus_chk_afford = existing.get_node_or_null("BonusAfford")
		return

	var bar = HBoxContainer.new()
	bar.name = "BonusFilterBar"
	bar.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 10)

	var search = LineEdit.new()
	search.name = "BonusSearch"
	search.placeholder_text = "Search bonus upgrades..."
	search.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	search.clear_button_enabled = true
	search.text_changed.connect(func(_t): _apply_bonus_filter(panel))
	bar.add_child(search)
	bonus_search = search

	var sort_btn = OptionButton.new()
	sort_btn.name = "BonusSort"
	sort_btn.add_item("Sort: Default", 0)
	sort_btn.add_item("Name: A-Z", 1)
	sort_btn.item_selected.connect(func(_idx): _apply_bonus_filter(panel))
	bar.add_child(sort_btn)
	bonus_sort = sort_btn

	var chk = CheckBox.new()
	chk.name = "BonusAfford"
	chk.text = "Affordable Only"
	chk.tooltip_text = "Show only upgrades you can purchase right now."
	chk.toggled.connect(func(pressed: bool):
		is_bonus_affordable_only = pressed
		_apply_bonus_filter(panel)
	)
	bar.add_child(chk)
	bonus_chk_afford = chk

	v_box.add_child(bar)
	v_box.move_child(bar, scroll.get_index())

func _apply_bonus_filter(panel: Control) -> void:
	if not is_instance_valid(panel) or not is_instance_valid(bonus_search):
		return
	var q: String = bonus_search.text.strip_edges().to_lower()
	var cur_cash: float = Gvars.CashCount if Gvars else 0.0

	for item in panel.get_children():
		if not is_instance_valid(item):
			continue
		var label = item.find_child("*Label*", true, false)
		var text: String = label.text.to_lower() if (label and "text" in label) else ""
		var match_q: bool = q.is_empty() or q in text

		var cost: float = 0.0
		if "cost" in item:
			cost = float(item.cost)
		elif "price" in item:
			cost = float(item.price)

		var match_afford: bool = not is_bonus_affordable_only or (cost <= cur_cash or cost <= 0.0)
		item.visible = match_q and match_afford

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-mod_manager_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-mod_manager_mod\extensions\scenes\Interfaces\Management\shop_item_card.hooks.gd

extends Object

func refreshDetails(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([])
	var card = chain.reference_object
	if not is_instance_valid(card) or not ("item" in card) or not card.item:
		return

	if Gvars and is_nan(Gvars.CashCount):
		Gvars.CashCount = 0.0

	var cost: float = float(card.item.itemBuyPrice)
	if card.purchaseable and (cost <= 0.0 or Gvars.CashCount >= cost):
		if "purchase_button" in card and is_instance_valid(card.purchase_button):
			card.purchase_button.disabled = false

func _on_purchase_button_pressed(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	var card = chain.reference_object
	if not is_instance_valid(card) or not ("item" in card) or not card.item:
		chain.execute_next([])
		return

	if Gvars and is_nan(Gvars.CashCount):
		Gvars.CashCount = 0.0

	var cost: float = float(card.item.itemBuyPrice)
	# For free 0g items, trigger buy effect directly
	if cost <= 0.0:
		if card.item.itemBuyEffect:
			card.item.itemBuyEffect.buy_effect(card.item, 1)
		card.refreshDetails()
		card.shop_button_pressed.emit()
		return

	# For regular items, let vanilla buy_effect perform the single deduction
	chain.execute_next([])

extends Object

func _ready(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([])
	var ps = chain.reference_object
	if not ps or not ("professions" in ps) or not ps.professions:
		return

	var reg = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-mod_manager_mod/scripts/mod_registry.gd")
	if not reg:
		return

	for card in ps.professions.get_children():
		if "profession" in card and card.profession:
			var pname: String = str(card.profession.profession_name).to_lower().replace(" ", "_").replace("-", "_")
			if not reg.is_mod_active_for_run(pname):
				card.visible = false

extends Object

func _ready(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([])
	var settings = chain.reference_object
	if not settings or not ("tab_container" in settings) or not settings.tab_container:
		return

	var tc: TabContainer = settings.tab_container

	# 1. Add Mod Manager Tab ONLY if missing
	var mm_tab = tc.get_node_or_null("ModManagerTab")
	if not mm_tab:
		var tab := PanelContainer.new()
		tab.name = "ModManagerTab"
		tab.set_name("Mod Manager")
		tab.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
		tab.size_flags_vertical = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL

		var margin := MarginContainer.new()
		margin.set_anchors_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
		margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_left", 32)
		margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_right", 32)
		margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_top", 20)
		margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_bottom", 20)
		tab.add_child(margin)

		var vbox := VBoxContainer.new()
		vbox.set_anchors_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
		vbox.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
		vbox.size_flags_vertical = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
		vbox.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 14)
		margin.add_child(vbox)

		var header_bar := HBoxContainer.new()
		var h_lbl := RichTextLabel.new()
		h_lbl.bbcode_enabled = true
		h_lbl.text = "[b][font_size=20]Installed Mod Manager & Runtime Toggles[/font_size][/b]"
		h_lbl.fit_content = true
		h_lbl.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
		header_bar.add_child(h_lbl)

		var launch_full_btn := Button.new()
		launch_full_btn.text = " Open Fullscreen Mod Manager "
		launch_full_btn.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(240, 36)
		launch_full_btn.pressed.connect(func():
			var view_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-mod_manager_mod/scenes/mod_manager_view.gd")
			var view = view_script.new()
			view.is_pre_run_mode = false
			settings.add_child(view)
		)
		header_bar.add_child(launch_full_btn)
		vbox.add_child(header_bar)

		var sep := HSeparator.new()
		vbox.add_child(sep)

		var desc := Label.new()
		desc.text = "Configure installed mods and difficulty mutators live during gameplay."
		desc.modulate = Color(0.75, 0.75, 0.75)
		vbox.add_child(desc)

		tc.add_child(tab)
		mm_tab = tab

	# 2. Position Mod Manager at Index 6
	tc.move_child(mm_tab, 6)

	# 3. Align Leaderboard Tab at Index 7 and eliminate duplicate / unnamed tabs
	var found_lb: bool = false
	for child in tc.get_children():
		var c_name := child.name
		if c_name.to_lower().contains("leaderboard"):
			if not found_lb:
				child.name = "Leaderboard"
				child.set_name("Leaderboard")
				tc.move_child(child, 7)
				found_lb = true
			else:
				child.queue_free()
		elif c_name.begins_with("@PanelContainer@"):
			child.queue_free()

extends Object

var search_input: LineEdit = null
var sort_dropdown: OptionButton = null
var chk_affordable: CheckBox = null
var is_affordable_only: bool = false

var bonus_search_input: LineEdit = null
var bonus_sort_dropdown: OptionButton = null
var bonus_chk_affordable: CheckBox = null
var is_bonus_affordable_only: bool = false

func load_equipment_shop(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	var screen = chain.reference_object
	if is_instance_valid(screen):
		if Gvars and is_nan(Gvars.CashCount):
			Gvars.CashCount = 0.0
		_ensure_search_and_sort_bar(screen)
		_sort_equipment_catalog()

	chain.execute_next([])

	if is_instance_valid(screen):
		Callable(self, "_apply_filter").call_deferred(screen)

func load_bonus_shop(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	var screen = chain.reference_object
	if is_instance_valid(screen):
		_ensure_bonus_filter_bar(screen)

	chain.execute_next([])

	if is_instance_valid(screen):
		Callable(self, "_apply_bonus_filter").call_deferred(screen)

func _ensure_search_and_sort_bar(screen: Node) -> void:
	if not is_instance_valid(screen):
		return
	var shop = screen.find_child("EquipmentShop", true, false)
	if not shop:
		return

	var scroll = shop.get_parent()
	var v_box = scroll.get_parent() if scroll else null
	if not v_box or v_box.has_node("EquipmentFilterBar"):
		return

	var bar = HBoxContainer.new()
	bar.name = "EquipmentFilterBar"
	bar.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 10)

	var search = LineEdit.new()
	search.name = "SearchInput"
	search.placeholder_text = "Search equipment by name or type..."
	search.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	search.clear_button_enabled = true
	search.text_changed.connect(func(_new_text): _apply_filter(screen))
	bar.add_child(search)
	search_input = search

	var sort_btn = OptionButton.new()
	sort_btn.name = "SortOption"
	sort_btn.add_item("Sort: Tier (Default)", 0)
	sort_btn.add_item("Sort: Price (Low to High)", 1)
	sort_btn.add_item("Sort: Price (High to Low)", 2)
	sort_btn.add_item("Sort: Alphabetical (A-Z)", 3)
	sort_btn.item_selected.connect(func(_idx):
		_sort_equipment_catalog()
		if screen.has_method("load_equipment_shop"):
			screen.load_equipment_shop()
	)
	bar.add_child(sort_btn)
	sort_dropdown = sort_btn

	var chk = CheckBox.new()
	chk.name = "AffordableOnly"
	chk.text = "Affordable Only"
	chk.tooltip_text = "Show only items you can afford with current cash."
	chk.toggled.connect(func(pressed: bool):
		is_affordable_only = pressed
		_apply_filter(screen)
	)
	bar.add_child(chk)
	chk_affordable = chk

	v_box.add_child(bar)
	v_box.move_child(bar, scroll.get_index())

func _ensure_bonus_filter_bar(screen: Node) -> void:
	if not is_instance_valid(screen):
		return
	var shop = screen.find_child("BonusShop", true, false)
	if not shop:
		return

	var scroll = shop.get_parent()
	var v_box = scroll.get_parent() if scroll else null
	if not v_box or v_box.has_node("BonusFilterBar"):
		return

	var bar = HBoxContainer.new()
	bar.name = "BonusFilterBar"
	bar.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 10)

	var search = LineEdit.new()
	search.name = "BonusSearchInput"
	search.placeholder_text = "Search bonuses by name..."
	search.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	search.clear_button_enabled = true
	search.text_changed.connect(func(_new_text): _apply_bonus_filter(screen))
	bar.add_child(search)
	bonus_search_input = search

	var chk = CheckBox.new()
	chk.name = "BonusAffordableOnly"
	chk.text = "Affordable Only"
	chk.tooltip_text = "Show only bonuses you can afford."
	chk.toggled.connect(func(pressed: bool):
		is_bonus_affordable_only = pressed
		_apply_bonus_filter(screen)
	)
	bar.add_child(chk)
	bonus_chk_affordable = chk

	v_box.add_child(bar)
	v_box.move_child(bar, scroll.get_index())

func _sort_equipment_catalog() -> void:
	var item_unlocks = load("res://resources/unlockable_items/scripts/item_unlocks.tres")
	if not item_unlocks or not ("all_equipment_items" in item_unlocks):
		return

	var mode: int = sort_dropdown.selected if is_instance_valid(sort_dropdown) else 0
	if mode == 1:
		item_unlocks.all_equipment_items.sort_custom(func(a, b): return a.itemBuyPrice < b.itemBuyPrice)
	elif mode == 2:
		item_unlocks.all_equipment_items.sort_custom(func(a, b): return a.itemBuyPrice > b.itemBuyPrice)
	elif mode == 3:
		item_unlocks.all_equipment_items.sort_custom(func(a, b): return str(a.itemName).nocasecmp_to(str(b.itemName)) < 0)

func _apply_filter(screen: Node) -> void:
	if not is_instance_valid(screen) or not is_instance_valid(search_input):
		return
	var query: String = search_input.text.strip_edges().to_lower()
	var cur_cash: float = Gvars.CashCount if Gvars else 0.0

	var shop = screen.find_child("EquipmentShop", true, false)
	if not shop:
		return

	for card in shop.find_children("*", "PanelContainer", true, false):
		if not is_instance_valid(card) or not ("item" in card) or not card.item:
			continue
		var it = card.item
		var it_name: String = str(it.itemName).to_lower()
		var it_id: String = str(it.itemID).to_lower()
		var it_type: String = str(it.itemType).to_lower()

		var match_query: bool = query.is_empty() or query in it_name or query in it_id or query in it_type
		var match_afford: bool = not is_affordable_only or (it.itemBuyPrice <= cur_cash or it.itemBuyPrice <= 0.0)

		card.visible = match_query and match_afford

func _apply_bonus_filter(screen: Node) -> void:
	if not is_instance_valid(screen) or not is_instance_valid(bonus_search_input):
		return
	var query: String = bonus_search_input.text.strip_edges().to_lower()
	var cur_cash: float = Gvars.CashCount if Gvars else 0.0

	var shop = screen.find_child("BonusShop", true, false)
	if not shop:
		return

	for card in shop.find_children("*", "PanelContainer", true, false):
		if not is_instance_valid(card) or not ("item" in card) or not card.item:
			continue
		var it = card.item
		var it_name: String = str(it.itemName).to_lower()
		var match_query: bool = query.is_empty() or query in it_name
		var match_afford: bool = not is_bonus_affordable_only or (it.itemBuyPrice <= cur_cash or it.itemBuyPrice <= 0.0)

		card.visible = match_query and match_afford

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-mod_manager_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-mod_manager_mod\extensions\scripts\Camera.hooks.gd

extends Object

func _process(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, delta: float) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([delta])
	var cam = chain.reference_object
	if not is_instance_valid(cam):
		return

	# Force camera following and re-acquire player if target was detached
	cam.free_move = false
	var target = null
	if ("TargetNode" in cam) and is_instance_valid(cam.TargetNode):
		target = cam.TargetNode
	else:
		var tree = cam.get_tree()
		if tree:
			var players = tree.get_nodes_in_group("player")
			if players.size() > 0 and is_instance_valid(players[0]):
				target = players[0]
				cam.TargetNode = target
			elif is_instance_valid(cam.get_node_or_null("../Player")):
				target = cam.get_node("../Player")
				cam.TargetNode = target

	if is_instance_valid(target):
		cam.global_position = target.global_position

	# Dedicated zoom controls (freed up [ and ] for game speed)
	if Gvars and ("settings" in Gvars) and Gvars.settings:
		var cur_zoom: float = Gvars.settings.zoom_level
		if Input.is_action_just_pressed("zoomOut") or Input.is_key_pressed(KEY_MINUS):
			Gvars.settings.zoom_level = clamp(cur_zoom - 0.2, 0.2, 4.0)
			cam.zoom = Vector2(Gvars.settings.zoom_level, Gvars.settings.zoom_level)
		elif Input.is_action_just_pressed("zoomIn") or Input.is_key_pressed(KEY_EQUAL):
			Gvars.settings.zoom_level = clamp(cur_zoom + 0.2, 0.2, 4.0)
			cam.zoom = Vector2(Gvars.settings.zoom_level, Gvars.settings.zoom_level)
		elif Input.is_key_pressed(KEY_0):
			Gvars.settings.zoom_level = 1.0
			cam.zoom = Vector2(1.0, 1.0)

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-mod_manager_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-mod_manager_mod\extensions\scripts\Gvars.hooks.gd

extends Object

func reset_resources(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	var gvars = chain.reference_object
	if is_instance_valid(gvars) and ("resources_to_save" in gvars) and (gvars.resources_to_save is Array):
		var clean_res: Array = []
		for r in gvars.resources_to_save:
			if is_instance_valid(r) and ("resource_path" in r) and str(r.resource_path) != "" and ResourceLoader.exists(r.resource_path):
				clean_res.append(r)
		gvars.resources_to_save = clean_res
	chain.execute_next([])

func save_Gvars(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> Dictionary:
	var result: Dictionary = chain.execute_next([])
	if not result.has("Gvars"):
		result["Gvars"] = {}

	var g = chain.reference_object
	if g and ("profession" in g) and g.profession and ("profession_manager" in g) and g.profession_manager:
		var pm = g.profession_manager
		for p_id in pm.all_professions.keys():
			if pm.all_professions[p_id]["profession"] == g.profession:
				result["Gvars"]["profession"] = p_id
				break
	return result

func load_Gvars(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, save: Dictionary) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([save])
	var g = chain.reference_object
	if not g or not save.has("Gvars") or not save["Gvars"].has("profession"):
		return

	var saved_id: int = int(save["Gvars"]["profession"])
	if ("profession_manager" in g) and g.profession_manager:
		var pm = g.profession_manager
		if pm.all_professions.has(saved_id):
			g.profession = pm.all_professions[saved_id]["profession"]
