extends Object

func _ready(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([])
	var menu = chain.reference_object
	if not is_instance_valid(menu):
		return

	# Check for an optional external branded logo using raw byte buffers
	var logo_loaded := false
	var title_node = menu.find_child("Title", true, false)
	if not title_node:
		title_node = menu.find_child("TextureRect", true, false)

	var logo_path := "res://mods-unpacked/gareth-mod_manager_mod/assets/branded_logo.png"
	if FileAccess.file_exists(logo_path) and is_instance_valid(title_node) and title_node is TextureRect:
		var bytes := FileAccess.get_file_as_bytes(logo_path)
		if bytes.size() > 0:
			var img := Image.new()
			if img.load_png_from_buffer(bytes) == OK:
				var tex := ImageTexture.create_from_image(img)
				title_node.texture = tex
				title_node.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(760, 310)
				title_node.stretch_mode = TextureRect.STRETCH_KEEP_ASPECT_CENTERED
				logo_loaded = true

	# Procedural Hanging Plaque with Iron Chains beneath the Title Sign
	if not logo_loaded and not menu.has_node("HangingTitlePlaque"):
		var plaque_container := Control.new()
		plaque_container.name = "HangingTitlePlaque"
		plaque_container.set_anchors_preset(Control.PRESET_CENTER_TOP)
		plaque_container.position = Vector2(0, 0)
		menu.add_child(plaque_container)

		# Left Iron Chain Links
		var chain_l := ColorRect.new()
		chain_l.color = Color(0.28, 0.28, 0.32, 0.95)
		chain_l.position = Vector2(488, 252)
		chain_l.size = Vector2(8, 22)
		plaque_container.add_child(chain_l)

		# Right Iron Chain Links
		var chain_r := ColorRect.new()
		chain_r.color = Color(0.28, 0.28, 0.32, 0.95)
		chain_r.position = Vector2(784, 252)
		chain_r.size = Vector2(8, 22)
		plaque_container.add_child(chain_r)

		# Hanging Wooden Plaque Board
		var wood_panel := PanelContainer.new()
		wood_panel.position = Vector2(440, 274)
		wood_panel.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(400, 36)

		var sb := StyleBoxFlat.new()
		sb.bg_color = Color(0.55, 0.32, 0.18, 0.98) # Warm timber brown
		sb.border_color = Color(0.22, 0.12, 0.06, 1.0) # Dark wooden bark rim
		sb.set_border_width_all(3)
		sb.corner_radius_top_left = 6
		sb.corner_radius_top_right = 6
		sb.corner_radius_bottom_left = 6
		sb.corner_radius_bottom_right = 6
		sb.expand_margin_top = 2
		sb.expand_margin_bottom = 2
		wood_panel.add_theme_stylebox_override("panel", sb)

		var plaque_label := RichTextLabel.new()
		plaque_label.bbcode_enabled = true
		plaque_label.text = "[center][b][color=#38bdf8]✦ ENHANCED BY GARETH WHITE & GEMINI ✦[/color][/b][/center]"
		plaque_label.fit_content = true
		plaque_label.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
		wood_panel.add_child(plaque_label)

		plaque_container.add_child(wood_panel)

	# Main Menu Buttons Configuration
	var btn_grid = menu.get_node_or_null("%MainMenuButtons")
	if btn_grid:
		btn_grid.offset_top = -68.0
		btn_grid.offset_bottom = 176.0
		btn_grid.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 4)

		if not btn_grid.has_node("ModManagerButton"):
			var mod_btn := Button.new()
			mod_btn.name = "ModManagerButton"
			mod_btn.text = "Mod Manager"
			mod_btn.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0, 30)
			mod_btn.pressed.connect(func():
				var view_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-mod_manager_mod/scenes/mod_manager_view.gd")
				if view_script:
					var view: Control = Control.new()
					view.set_script(view_script)
					view.set_meta("is_pre_run_mode", false)
					menu.add_child(view)
			)
			btn_grid.add_child(mod_btn)
			btn_grid.move_child(mod_btn, 2)

		if not btn_grid.has_node("ModStoreButton"):
			var store_btn := Button.new()
			store_btn.name = "ModStoreButton"
			store_btn.text = "Mod & Map Store"
			store_btn.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0, 30)
			store_btn.modulate = Color(1.0, 0.88, 0.35)
			store_btn.pressed.connect(func():
				var store_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-mod_manager_mod/scenes/mod_store_view.gd")
				if store_script:
					var store_view = store_script.new()
					menu.add_child(store_view)
			)
			btn_grid.add_child(store_btn)
			btn_grid.move_child(store_btn, 3)

		if not btn_grid.has_node("LeaderboardsButton"):
			var lb_btn := Button.new()
			lb_btn.name = "LeaderboardsButton"
			lb_btn.text = "Leaderboards"
			lb_btn.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0, 30)
			lb_btn.pressed.connect(func():
				var modal_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-leaderboard_mod/scenes/leaderboard_modal.gd")
				if modal_script:
					var modal = modal_script.new()
					menu.add_child(modal)
			)
			btn_grid.add_child(lb_btn)
			btn_grid.move_child(lb_btn, 4)

func on_profession_selected(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, mode: int, prof: Object, hard_modifier: Object) -> void:
	var menu = chain.reference_object
	if not menu:
		chain.execute_next([mode, prof, hard_modifier])
		return

	var p_select = menu.get_node_or_null("ProfessionSelect")
	if not p_select and "profession_select" in menu:
		p_select = menu.profession_select
	if p_select:
		p_select.visible = false

	var view_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-mod_manager_mod/scenes/mod_manager_view.gd")
	if view_script:
		var view: Control = Control.new()
		view.set_script(view_script)
		view.name = "PreRunModSelector"
		view.set_meta("is_pre_run_mode", true)

		view.confirmed.connect(func():
			chain.execute_next([mode, prof, hard_modifier])
		)

		view.back_pressed.connect(func():
			if p_select:
				p_select.visible = true
		)

		menu.add_child(view)
	else:
		chain.execute_next([mode, prof, hard_modifier])

extends Object

func _ready(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([])
	var pause_menu = chain.reference_object
	if not pause_menu:
		return

	var resume_btn = null
	for child in pause_menu.find_children("*", "Button", true, false):
		if child.text.to_lower().contains("resume"):
			resume_btn = child
			break

	if resume_btn and resume_btn.get_parent():
		var parent_box = resume_btn.get_parent()
		if not parent_box.has_node("PauseModManagerButton"):
			var mod_btn := Button.new()
			mod_btn.name = "PauseModManagerButton"
			mod_btn.text = "Mod Manager"
			mod_btn.custom_minimum_size = resume_btn.custom_minimum_size
			mod_btn.size_flags_horizontal = resume_btn.size_flags_horizontal

			var btn_font = resume_btn.get_theme_font("font")
			if btn_font:
				mod_btn.add_theme_font_override("font", btn_font)
			var font_sz = resume_btn.get_theme_font_size("font_size")
			if font_sz > 0:
				mod_btn.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", font_sz)

			mod_btn.pressed.connect(func():
				var view_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-mod_manager_mod/scenes/mod_manager_view.gd")
				var view = view_script.new()
				view.is_pre_run_mode = false
				pause_menu.add_child(view)
			)
			parent_box.add_child(mod_btn)
			var r_idx = resume_btn.get_index()
			parent_box.move_child(mod_btn, r_idx + 1)

		if not parent_box.has_node("PauseLeaderboardsButton"):
			var lb_btn := Button.new()
			lb_btn.name = "PauseLeaderboardsButton"
			lb_btn.text = "Leaderboards"
			lb_btn.custom_minimum_size = resume_btn.custom_minimum_size
			lb_btn.size_flags_horizontal = resume_btn.size_flags_horizontal

			var btn_font2 = resume_btn.get_theme_font("font")
			if btn_font2:
				lb_btn.add_theme_font_override("font", btn_font2)
			var font_sz2 = resume_btn.get_theme_font_size("font_size")
			if font_sz2 > 0:
				lb_btn.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", font_sz2)

			lb_btn.pressed.connect(func():
				var lb_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-leaderboard_mod/scripts/leaderboard_overlay.gd")
				if lb_script:
					var lb = lb_script.new()
					pause_menu.add_child(lb)
			)
			parent_box.add_child(lb_btn)
			var m_idx = parent_box.get_node("PauseModManagerButton").get_index()
			parent_box.move_child(lb_btn, m_idx + 1)

extends Object

func drop_off_coal(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	var sp = chain.reference_object
	# If player reference is lost at day transition, return safely without crashing
	if not is_instance_valid(sp) or not ("player" in sp) or not is_instance_valid(sp.player) or not ("inventory" in sp.player) or not sp.player.inventory:
		return
	chain.execute_next([])

class_name ModManagerView
extends Control

signal confirmed()
signal back_pressed()

@export var is_pre_run_mode: bool = true

var toggles: Dictionary = {}
const CONFIG_PATH := "user://gareth_mods_config.json"

func _ready() -> void:
	call_deferred("_inject_search_bar")
	process_mode = Node.PROCESS_MODE_ALWAYS
	var vp_rect := get_viewport_rect()
	position = Vector2.ZERO
	size = vp_rect.size
	custom_minimum_size = vp_rect.size
	mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_STOP
	z_index = 85

	var bg := ColorRect.new()
	bg.position = Vector2.ZERO
	bg.size = vp_rect.size
	bg.color = Color(0.06, 0.07, 0.09, 0.98)
	bg.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_STOP
	add_child(bg)

	var margin := MarginContainer.new()
	margin.position = Vector2.ZERO
	margin.size = vp_rect.size
	margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_left", 40)
	margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_right", 40)
	margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_top", 28)
	margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_bottom", 28)
	add_child(margin)

	var vbox := VBoxContainer.new()
	vbox.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	vbox.size_flags_vertical = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	vbox.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 16)
	margin.add_child(vbox)

	var top_bar := HBoxContainer.new()
	top_bar.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0, 48)
	top_bar.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	vbox.add_child(top_bar)

	var back_btn := Button.new()
	back_btn.text = " < Back " if not is_pre_run_mode else " < Back to Class Select "
	back_btn.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(210, 40)
	back_btn.size_flags_vertical = Control.SIZE_SHRINK_CENTER
	back_btn.pressed.connect(func():
		back_pressed.emit()
		queue_free()
	)
	top_bar.add_child(back_btn)

	var title := Label.new()
	title.text = "MOD MANAGER — INSTALLED MODS & MUTATORS" if not is_pre_run_mode else "ACTIVE MOD EXPANSIONS & MUTATORS FOR THIS RUN"
	title.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	title.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	title.size_flags_vertical = Control.SIZE_SHRINK_CENTER
	title.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 18)
	top_bar.add_child(title)

	var confirm_btn := Button.new()
	confirm_btn.text = " Save & Close " if not is_pre_run_mode else " Continue to Map Select > "
	confirm_btn.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(220, 40)
	confirm_btn.size_flags_vertical = Control.SIZE_SHRINK_CENTER
	confirm_btn.modulate = Color(0.4, 1.0, 0.5)
	confirm_btn.pressed.connect(_on_confirm)
	top_bar.add_child(confirm_btn)

	var sep := HSeparator.new()
	vbox.add_child(sep)

	var scroll := ScrollContainer.new()
	scroll.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	scroll.size_flags_vertical = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	vbox.add_child(scroll)

	var content := VBoxContainer.new()
	content.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	content.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 22)
	scroll.add_child(content)

	_populate_cards(content)

func _populate_cards(content: VBoxContainer) -> void:
	var registry_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-mod_manager_mod/scripts/mod_registry.gd")
	var mods: Array = registry_script.get_all_installed_mods()
	var saved_config := _load_existing_config()

	var category_order := [
		"Classes & Professions",
		"Progression & Upgrades",
		"Custom Maps & Strata",
		"Weapons & Arsenal",
		"Employees & Automation",
		"Quality of Life",
		"Performance & System",
		"Community Add-Ons",
		"Other Mods"
	]

	var grouped: Dictionary = {}
	for cat in category_order:
		grouped[cat] = []

	for m in mods:
		var c: String = m["category"]
		if not grouped.has(c):
			grouped[c] = []
		grouped[c].append(m)

	for cat in category_order:
		var list: Array = grouped[cat]
		if list.size() == 0:
			continue

		var cat_lbl := Label.new()
		cat_lbl.text = "%s (%d)" % [cat.to_upper(), list.size()]
		cat_lbl.modulate = Color(1.0, 0.85, 0.3)
		cat_lbl.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 16)
		content.add_child(cat_lbl)

		var grid := GridContainer.new()
		grid.columns = 2
		grid.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
		grid.add_theme_constant_override("h_separation", 20)
		grid.add_theme_constant_override("v_separation", 12)
		content.add_child(grid)

		for m in list:
			var k: String = m["key"]
			var default_active: bool = bool(saved_config.get(k, true))
			if cat == "Hardcore Mutators" and not saved_config.has(k):
				default_active = false
			toggles[k] = default_active

			var card := PanelContainer.new()
			card.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL

			var c_margin := MarginContainer.new()
			c_margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_left", 14)
			c_margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_right", 14)
			c_margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_top", 10)
			c_margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_bottom", 10)
			card.add_child(c_margin)

			var hbox := HBoxContainer.new()
			hbox.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
			hbox.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 12)
			c_margin.add_child(hbox)

			var cb := CheckBox.new()
			cb.button_pressed = default_active
			cb.disabled = bool(m.get("is_core", false))
			cb.toggled.connect(func(val: bool): toggles[k] = val)
			hbox.add_child(cb)

			var info_vbox := VBoxContainer.new()
			info_vbox.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL

			var title_lbl := Label.new()
			title_lbl.text = "%s  —  by %s [v%s]" % [m["name"], m["author"], m["version"]]
			title_lbl.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 13)
			info_vbox.add_child(title_lbl)

			var desc_lbl := Label.new()
			desc_lbl.text = m["description"]
			desc_lbl.modulate = Color(0.7, 0.7, 0.7)
			desc_lbl.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 11)
			desc_lbl.autowrap_mode = TextServer.AUTOWRAP_WORD_SMART
			info_vbox.add_child(desc_lbl)

			hbox.add_child(info_vbox)
			grid.add_child(card)

func _load_existing_config() -> Dictionary:
	if FileAccess.file_exists(CONFIG_PATH):
		var f := FileAccess.open(CONFIG_PATH, FileAccess.READ)
		if f:
			var parsed = JSON.parse_string(f.get_as_text())
			if parsed is Dictionary and parsed.has("mods"):
				return parsed["mods"]
	return {}

func _on_confirm() -> void:
	var cfg := {"mods": toggles}
	var f := FileAccess.open(CONFIG_PATH, FileAccess.WRITE)
	if f:
		f.store_string(JSON.stringify(cfg, "\t"))
		f.close()

	var ssm = get_node_or_null("/root/SaveSlotManager")
	if ssm and is_pre_run_mode:
		var slot_cfg := FileAccess.open(ssm.get_slot_config_path(ssm.current_slot), FileAccess.WRITE)
		if slot_cfg:
			slot_cfg.store_string(JSON.stringify(cfg, "\t"))
			slot_cfg.close()

	confirmed.emit()
	queue_free()





func _inject_search_bar() -> void:
	var header = find_child("HeaderContainer", true, false)
	if not header: header = self
	var search = LineEdit.new()
	search.placeholder_text = "Search active mods..."
	search.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(300, 35)
	search.text_changed.connect(_on_search_filter)
	header.add_child(search)

func _on_search_filter(query: String) -> void:
	var q = query.to_lower()
	var list = find_child("ModListContainer", true, false)
	if is_instance_valid(list):
		for card in list.get_children():
			if q.is_empty():
				card.visible = true
			else:
				var mod_name = card.name.to_lower()
				var lbl = card.find_child("*Label*", true, false)
				if is_instance_valid(lbl): mod_name += lbl.text.to_lower()
				card.visible = q in mod_name

func _on_repo_selected(idx: int) -> void:
	active_repo_idx = idx
	custom_repo_edit.visible = (idx >= KNOWN_REPOS.size())
	_load_catalog_for_selected_repo()

func _load_catalog_for_selected_repo() -> void:
	if active_repo_idx == 1:
		# Der-Floh Catalogs with mature versions (1.2.0 - 1.0.4)
		all_packages = [
			{"id": "der_floh-ore_value_mod", "name": "Ore Value Refresh", "tag": "v1.2.0", "desc": "Refreshes and scales daily ore sell prices dynamically on each new shift.", "cat": "Workforce & Automation", "author": "Der-Floh"},
			{"id": "der_floh-teleport_mod", "name": "Recall Teleporter", "tag": "v1.0.4", "desc": "Portable emergency recall device that instantly transports the miner back to the surface cabin.", "cat": "Quality of Life", "author": "Der-Floh"},
			{"id": "der_floh-more_stats_mod", "name": "Advanced Shift Statistics", "tag": "v1.1.0", "desc": "Detailed analytics breakdown for mined ore counts, cash velocity, and drill efficiency.", "cat": "Quality of Life", "author": "Der-Floh"},
			{"id": "der_floh-speed_run_timer_mod", "name": "Quota Speedrun Stopwatch", "tag": "v1.0.2", "desc": "On-screen high-precision timer tracking daily quota completion splits.", "cat": "Quality of Life", "author": "Der-Floh"},
			{"id": "der_floh-pickaxeaoe_mod", "name": "Pickaxe AOE Damage", "tag": "v1.3.1", "desc": "AoE and electric pickaxe now deal equal damage to all ores around swing radius.", "cat": "Classes & Professions", "author": "Der-Floh"},
			{"id": "der_floh-no_holes_mod", "name": "No Holes Backfill", "tag": "v1.2.5", "desc": "Automatically backfills hollow chambers as you tunnel downward to prevent collapsing.", "cat": "Quality of Life", "author": "Der-Floh"},
			{"id": "der_floh-passive_drop_mod", "name": "Dynamic Passive Chest Loot Drops", "tag": "v1.2.0", "desc": "Boosts chances of passive unlocks dropping inside deep mine chests based on tier count.", "cat": "Workforce & Automation", "author": "Der-Floh"}
		]
		status_lbl.text = "Der-Floh (Community Classics): 7 genuine packages loaded."
		status_lbl.modulate = Color(0.4, 1.0, 0.6)
	else:
		# Seed ALL 58+ identified Gareth White officially released packages with elevated versions

func _render_cards() -> void:
	for child in cards_vbox.get_children():
		child.queue_free()

	var query: String = search_edit.text.strip_edges().to_lower()
	var selected_cat: int = cat_dropdown.selected
	var selected_status: int = status_dropdown.selected

	var match_count = 0
	for pkg in all_packages:
		var pkg_id: String = str(pkg.get("id", ""))
		var pkg_name: String = str(pkg.get("name", "Package"))
		var pkg_tag: String = str(pkg.get("tag", "v1.0.0"))
		var pkg_desc: String = str(pkg.get("desc", ""))
		var pkg_cat: String = str(pkg.get("cat", "General Mod"))
		var pkg_author: String = str(pkg.get("author", "Gareth White"))

		# 1. Interactive Search Filter
		if not query.is_empty() and not (query in pkg_name.to_lower() or query in pkg_desc.to_lower() or query in pkg_author.to_lower()):
			continue
		
		# 2. Category Filter
		if selected_cat > 0:
			var cat_name: String = cat_dropdown.get_item_text(selected_cat)
			if pkg_cat != cat_name:
				continue

		# File verification checks on Windows Steam isolation paths
		var base_dir = OS.get_executable_path().get_base_dir()
		var is_installed = FileAccess.file_exists(base_dir.path_join("mods").path_join("%s.zip" % pkg_id))

		# 3. Status Filter
		if selected_status == 1 and not is_installed:
			continue
		if selected_status == 2 and is_installed:
			continue

		match_count += 1

		var card = PanelContainer.new()
		var margin = MarginContainer.new()
		margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_left", 16)
		margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_top", 10)
		margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_right", 16)
		margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_bottom", 10)
		card.add_child(margin)

		var hbox = HBoxContainer.new()
		hbox.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 16)
		margin.add_child(hbox)

		var info_vbox = VBoxContainer.new()
		info_vbox.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL

		var title_lbl = RichTextLabel.new()
		title_lbl.bbcode_enabled = true
		title_lbl.text = "[b][font_size=15][color=#ffffff]%s[/color][/font_size][/b]  [color=#4ade80]%s[/color]  [color=#fbbf24][%s][/color]  [color=#94a3b8]by %s[/color]" % [pkg_name, pkg_tag, pkg_cat, pkg_author]
		title_lbl.fit_content = true
		info_vbox.add_child(title_lbl)

		var desc_lbl = Label.new()
		desc_lbl.text = pkg_desc
		desc_lbl.modulate = Color(0.88, 0.90, 0.94)
		desc_lbl.autowrap_mode = TextServer.AUTOWRAP_WORD_SMART
		info_vbox.add_child(desc_lbl)

		hbox.add_child(info_vbox)

		var btn_action = Button.new()
		btn_action.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(140, 36)

		if is_installed:
			btn_action.text = "Uninstall"
			btn_action.modulate = Color(1.0, 0.45, 0.45)
			btn_action.pressed.connect(func(): _uninstall_mod(pkg_id))
		else:
			btn_action.text = "Install"
			btn_action.modulate = Color(0.45, 0.95, 0.55)
			btn_action.pressed.connect(func(): _install_mod(pkg_id))

		hbox.add_child(btn_action)
		cards_vbox.add_child(card)

	# Active search feedback directly to the operator
	if not query.is_empty():
		status_lbl.text = "🔍 Found %d of %d packages matching '%s'" % [match_count, all_packages.size(), query]
		status_lbl.modulate = Color(0.3, 0.8, 1.0)
	else:
		status_lbl.text = "📚 Repository contains %d active packages for staging." % [all_packages.size()]
		status_lbl.modulate = Color(0.4, 1.0, 0.6)

func _install_mod(mod_id: String) -> void:
	var base_dir = OS.get_executable_path().get_base_dir()
	var src = base_dir.path_join("downloaded_mods").path_join("%s.zip" % mod_id)
	var dst = base_dir.path_join("mods").path_join("%s.zip" % mod_id)

	if FileAccess.file_exists(src):
		var err = DirAccess.copy_absolute(src, dst)
		if err == OK:
			status_lbl.text = "🚀 Installed %s.zip to active mods directory! (Restart game to load)." % mod_id
			status_lbl.modulate = Color(0.4, 1.0, 0.6)
		else:
			status_lbl.text = "❌ Failed to copy pack: Error %d" % err
			status_lbl.modulate = Color(1.0, 0.4, 0.4)
	else:
		status_lbl.text = "❌ Package '%s.zip' not staged in downloaded_mods/." % mod_id
		status_lbl.modulate = Color(1.0, 0.4, 0.4)

	_render_cards()

func _uninstall_mod(mod_id: String) -> void:
	var base_dir = OS.get_executable_path().get_base_dir()
	var dst = base_dir.path_join("mods").path_join("%s.zip" % mod_id)

	if FileAccess.file_exists(dst):
		var err = DirAccess.remove_absolute(dst)
		if err == OK:
			status_lbl.text = "🗑️ Uninstalled %s.zip. Deleted from active mods directory." % mod_id
			status_lbl.modulate = Color(1.0, 0.5, 0.5)
		else:
			# If Windows filesystem holds read locks, write target as a pending purge
			var pend = FileAccess.open(base_dir.path_join("mods").path_join("%s.pending_delete" % mod_id), FileAccess.WRITE)
			if pend: pend.close()
			status_lbl.text = "⏳ Windows locked %s.zip. Slated for sync deletion on next launch." % mod_id
			status_lbl.modulate = Color(1.0, 0.85, 0.3)

	_render_cards()



--------
==================================================
FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-mod_manager_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-mod_manager_mod\scripts\mod_registry.gd
==================================================
--------
class_name ModRegistry
extends RefCounted

const CORE_MODS: Array[String] = [
	"gareth-mod_manager_mod",
	"gareth-multisave_mod",
	"gareth-game_speed_mod",
	"gareth-lost_expedition_mod",
	"gareth-tectonic_faultline_mod",
	"gareth-living_canvas_mod",
	"gareth-abyssal_trench_mod",
	"gareth-diagonal_map_mod"
]

static func is_mod_active_for_run(mod_key: String) -> bool:
	if mod_key in CORE_MODS:
		return true

	var ssm = Engine.get_main_loop().root.get_node_or_null("/root/SaveSlotManager")
	if ssm and ssm.has_method("is_mod_active_for_run"):
		return ssm.is_mod_active_for_run(mod_key)

	var cfg_path := "user://gareth_mods_config.json"
	if FileAccess.file_exists(cfg_path):
		var f := FileAccess.open(cfg_path, FileAccess.READ)
		if f:
			var parsed = JSON.parse_string(f.get_as_text())
			if parsed is Dictionary and parsed.has("mods"):
				return bool(parsed["mods"].get(mod_key, true))
	return true

static func get_all_installed_mods() -> Array[Dictionary]:
	return get_all_mods()

static func get_all_mods() -> Array[Dictionary]:
	var list: Array[Dictionary] = []
	var mod_dict: Dictionary = {}

	var mls = Engine.get_main_loop().root.get_node_or_null("/root/ModLoaderStore")
	if mls and ("mod_data" in mls) and mls.mod_data is Dictionary and mls.mod_data.size() > 0:
		mod_dict = mls.mod_data
	elif ModLoader and ("mod_data" in ModLoader) and ModLoader.mod_data is Dictionary and ModLoader.mod_data.size() > 0:
		mod_dict = ModLoader.mod_data

	if mod_dict.size() > 0:
		for mod_id in mod_dict.keys():
			var data = mod_dict[mod_id]
			if not data or not ("manifest" in data) or not data.manifest:
				continue
			list.append(_dict_from_manifest(mod_id, data.manifest, data.is_active if "is_active" in data else true))
		return list

	var dir := DirAccess.open("res://mods-unpacked")
	if dir:
		dir.list_dir_begin()
		var folder := dir.get_next()
		while folder != "":
			if dir.current_is_dir() and not folder.begins_with("."):
				var mpath := "res://mods-unpacked/%s/manifest.json" % folder
				if FileAccess.file_exists(mpath):
					var f := FileAccess.open(mpath, FileAccess.READ)
					if f:
						var parsed = JSON.parse_string(f.get_as_text())
						if parsed is Dictionary:
							list.append(_dict_from_manifest(folder, parsed, true))
			folder = dir.get_next()

	return list

static func _dict_from_manifest(mod_id: String, m, active: bool) -> Dictionary:
	var display_name: String = _format_display_name(m)
	var desc: String = str(m.description) if "description" in m else ""
	var author: String = "Community"
	if "extra" in m and m.extra:
		if ("godot" in m.extra) and ("authors" in m.extra.godot) and m.extra.godot.authors.size() > 0:
			author = str(m.extra.godot.authors[0])
	var version: String = str(m.version_number) if "version_number" in m else "1.0.0"
	var cat: String = _determine_category(m)
	var is_core: bool = (mod_id in CORE_MODS)

	return {
		"id": mod_id,
		"key": mod_id,
		"name": display_name,
		"description": desc,
		"author": author,
		"version": version,
		"category": cat,
		"is_active": active,
		"is_core": is_core
	}

static func _determine_category(m) -> String:
	var id: String = str(m.name).to_lower() if "name" in m else ""
	if "map" in id or "trench" in id or "faultline" in id or "canvas" in id or "expedition" in id or "diagonal" in id:
		return "Custom Maps & Strata"
	if "class" in id or "profession" in id or "jedi" in id or "knight" in id or "sinclairity" in id:
		return "Classes & Professions"
	if "weapon" in id or "bomb" in id or "laser" in id or "nuke" in id or "turret" in id or "mortar" in id or "grenade" in id or "arsenal" in id or "tank" in id or "strike" in id:
		return "Weapons & Arsenal"
	if "employee" in id or "collector" in id or "drill" in id or "vacuum" in id:
		return "Employees & Automation"
	if "speed" in id or "inventory" in id or "save" in id or "leaderboard" in id or "multisave" in id or "settings" in id or "scrap" in id or "delivery" in id or "ladder" in id:
		return "Quality of Life"
	if "hardware" in id or "limit" in id or "performance" in id or "booster" in id or "holes" in id:
		return "Performance & System"
	if "spirit" in id or "variance" in id or "upgrade" in id or "passive" in id or "order" in id or "beacon" in id:
		return "Progression & Upgrades"
	return "Community Add-Ons"

static func _format_display_name(m) -> String:
	if "extra" in m and m.extra and ("godot" in m.extra) and ("display_name" in m.extra.godot):
		return str(m.extra.godot.display_name)
	var raw: String = str(m.name).replace("_mod", "").replace("mod_", "").replace("-", " ").replace("_", " ") if "name" in m else "Mod"
	return raw.capitalize()

class_name ModStoreClient
extends Node

signal repo_fetch_completed(repo_name: String, releases: Array)
signal mod_download_completed(mod_id: String, success: bool)

const DEFAULT_REPOS := [
	"garethwhite-prog/coal-llc-mods",
	"Der-Floh/coal-llc-mods"
]

var http_client: HTTPRequest

func _ready() -> void:
	http_client = HTTPRequest.new()
	add_child(http_client)

func fetch_all_available_mods() -> void:
	var repos = _load_repo_list()
	for r in repos:
		_fetch_repo_releases(r)

func _load_repo_list() -> Array:
	var list := DEFAULT_REPOS.duplicate()
	var path := "user://mod_store_repos.json"
	if FileAccess.file_exists(path):
		var f := FileAccess.open(path, FileAccess.READ)
		if f:
			var data = JSON.parse_string(f.get_as_text())
			if data is Array:
				for custom_r in data:
					if not custom_r in list:
						list.append(custom_r)
	return list

func _fetch_repo_releases(repo: String) -> void:
	var url := "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/garethwhite-prog/coal-llc-mods/main/mod_catalog.json?v=" + str(Time.get_unix_time_from_system())
	var headers := ["User-Agent: Coal-LLC-Mod-Store/1.0"]
	http_client.request(url, headers)
	var result = await http_client.request_completed
	if result[1] == 200:
		var json = JSON.parse_string(result[3].get_string_from_utf8())
		if json is Array:
			repo_fetch_completed.emit(repo, json)

func download_mod_zip(download_url: String, target_filename: String) -> void:
	var dl_http = HTTPRequest.new()
	add_child(dl_http)
	var local_repo_dir := OS.get_executable_path().get_base_dir().path_join("mods_repository")
	var dest_path := local_repo_dir.path_join(target_filename)
	dl_http.download_file = dest_path
	dl_http.request(download_url, ["User-Agent: Coal-LLC-Mod-Store/1.0"])
	var res = await dl_http.request_completed
	dl_http.queue_free()
	mod_download_completed.emit(target_filename, res[1] == 200)

class_name SaveProfileSync
extends RefCounted

static func sync_slot_mods_to_runtime(slot_id: int) -> void:
	var exe_dir := OS.get_executable_path().get_base_dir()
	var repo_dir := exe_dir.path_join("mods_repository")
	var active_dir := exe_dir.path_join("mods")

	var slot_config_path := "user://slot_%d_active_mods.json" % slot_id
	if not FileAccess.file_exists(slot_config_path):
		return

	var f := FileAccess.open(slot_config_path, FileAccess.READ)
	if not f:
		return
	var active_map = JSON.parse_string(f.get_as_text())
	if not (active_map is Dictionary):
		return

	# Copy enabled zips from master mods_repository into active mods/
	var dir := DirAccess.open(repo_dir)
	if dir:
		dir.list_dir_begin()
		var file_name = dir.get_next()
		while file_name != "":
			if not dir.current_is_dir() and file_name.ends_with(".zip"):
				var mod_key = file_name.replace(".zip", "")
				var is_active = bool(active_map.get(mod_key, true))
				var src = repo_dir.path_join(file_name)
				var dst = active_dir.path_join(file_name)
				if is_active:
					dir.copy(src, dst)
				else:
					if FileAccess.file_exists(dst):
						DirAccess.remove_absolute(dst)
			file_name = dir.get_next()

func _add_header(parent: Control, text: String) -> void:
	var h := RichTextLabel.new()
	h.bbcode_enabled = true
	h.text = "\n[b][color=gold]" + text + "[/color][/b]"
	h.fit_content = true
	h.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0, 32)
	parent.add_child(h)

func _add_mod_toggle(parent: Control, label_text: String, mod_key: String) -> void:
	var b: SettingBool = BOOL_SCENE.instantiate()
	b.default = true
	parent.add_child(b)
	b.setting_label.text = label_text
	b.check_button.button_pressed = GarethSettings.is_mod_enabled(mod_key)
	b.new_value.connect(_on_mod_toggle_changed.bind(mod_key))

func _on_mod_toggle_changed(value: bool, mod_key: String) -> void:
	GarethSettings.set_mod_enabled(mod_key, value)

func _add_slider(parent: Control, label_text: String, slider_key: String, def_val: float, min_val: float, max_val: float, step_val: float) -> void:
	var s: SettingSlider = SLIDER_SCENE.instantiate()
	s.default = def_val
	parent.add_child(s)
	s.setting_label.text = label_text
	s.slider.min_value = min_val
	s.slider.max_value = max_val
	s.slider.step = step_val
	s.slider.value = GarethSettings.get_slider(slider_key, def_val)
	s.value_label.text = Gconsts.two_dp(s.slider.value)
	s.new_value.connect(_on_slider_changed.bind(slider_key))

func _on_slider_changed(value: float, slider_key: String) -> void:
	GarethSettings.set_slider(slider_key, value)

extends RefCounted

const CONFIG_PATH := "user://gareth_mods_config.json"

static var config: Dictionary = {
	"mods": {
		"tank_arsenal": true,
		"hydrogen_bomb": true,
		"neutron_bomb": true,
		"more_layer_shifts": true,
		"more_inventory_slots": true,
		"more_inventory_stacks": true,
		"buyable_vacuums": true,
		"more_mortars": true,
		"more_drills": true,
		"infini_platform": true,
		"more_employees": true,
		"more_collectors": true,
		"weapon_stacker": true,
		"elemental_transmutation": true,
		"excavation_frenzy": true,
		"plasma_raygun": true,
		"prospectors_beacon": true,
		"seismic_hazards": true
	},
	"sliders": {
		"hbomb_radius": 80.0,
		"nbomb_damage_mult": 1.0,
		"infini_platform_max": 200.0,
		"minigun_stack_scaling": 1.0
	}
}

static var _loaded: bool = false

static func ensure_loaded() -> void:
	if _loaded:
		return
	_loaded = true
	if not FileAccess.file_exists(CONFIG_PATH):
		save_config()
		return
	var file := FileAccess.open(CONFIG_PATH, FileAccess.READ)
	if file:
		var json_text := file.get_as_text()
		file.close()
		var parsed = JSON.parse_string(json_text)
		if parsed is Dictionary:
			for cat in parsed.keys():
				if config.has(cat) and parsed[cat] is Dictionary:
					for k in parsed[cat].keys():
						config[cat][k] = parsed[cat][k]

static func save_config() -> void:
	var file := FileAccess.open(CONFIG_PATH, FileAccess.WRITE)
	if file:
		file.store_string(JSON.stringify(config, "\t"))
		file.close()

static func is_mod_enabled(mod_name: String) -> bool:
	ensure_loaded()
	return bool(config["mods"].get(mod_name, true))

static func set_mod_enabled(mod_name: String, enabled: bool) -> void:
	ensure_loaded()
	config["mods"][mod_name] = enabled
	save_config()

static func get_slider(key: String, default_val: float) -> float:
	ensure_loaded()
	return float(config["sliders"].get(key, default_val))

static func set_slider(key: String, value: float) -> void:
	ensure_loaded()
	config["sliders"][key] = value
	save_config()

# Cosmic Collector Drones

**Namespace:** $namespace  
**Compatibility:** Coal LLC v1.4.1.1+ (ModLoader 7.0.1)

---

## Overview
The **Cosmic Collectors Mod** expands autonomous surface logistics with four ultra-high-tier Collector drones designed for massive late-game production:
* **Vortex Collector (Level 32 - Speed Branch):** Flight Speed 6.0, Inventory 60,000 items (,000,000).
* **Quantum Collector (Level 33 - Speed Branch):** Flight Speed 9.0, Inventory 180,000 items (,250,000,000).
* **Abyssal Collector (Level 34 - Capacity Branch):** Flight Speed 2.6, Inventory 300,000 items (,000,000).
* **Singularity Collector (Level 35 - Capacity Branch):** Flight Speed 3.5, Inventory 2,500,000 items (,500,000,000).

All employee tooltips feature click-through mouse filters to prevent shop interface blocking, while drone state persistence integrates safely with native career save files.

---

## Technical Details (For Modders)
Extends employee_manager.gd hierarchy and patches employee_shop_tooltip.gd to prevent UI mouse blocking. Bypasses Godot typed array limitations via hook chains.

---

## Installation
1. Ensure **Coal LLC** has Godot ModLoader 7.0.1 installed.
2. Place the packaged .zip file into your Coal LLC/mods/ directory.
3. Manage or toggle this mod directly via the in-game **Mod Manager**.

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-more_collectors_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-more_collectors_mod\extensions\resources\Employees\Scripts\employee_manager.hooks.gd

extends Object

var custom_levels: Dictionary = {}

func _init() -> void:
	var paths := [
		"res://mods-unpacked/gareth-more_collectors_mod/levels/vortex_collector.tres",
		"res://mods-unpacked/gareth-more_collectors_mod/levels/quantum_collector.tres",
		"res://mods-unpacked/gareth-more_collectors_mod/levels/abyssal_collector.tres",
		"res://mods-unpacked/gareth-more_collectors_mod/levels/singularity_collector.tres"
	]
	for p in paths:
		var lvl = load(p)
		if lvl:
			custom_levels[lvl.level_name] = lvl

func get_employee_level_from_enum(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, employee_enum: int) -> EmployeeLevel:
	if custom_levels.has(employee_enum):
		return custom_levels[employee_enum]
	return chain.execute_next([employee_enum])

func purchase_employee_level(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, employee_level: EmployeeLevel, count: float) -> float:
	var mgr = chain.reference_object
	if mgr:
		_cascade_locks(mgr)
		if employee_level.level_name in mgr.locked_employees:
			return 0.0
	return chain.execute_next([employee_level, count])

func _cascade_locks(mgr) -> void:
	var changed := true
	while changed:
		changed = false
		for lvl_name in custom_levels.keys():
			var lvl: EmployeeLevel = custom_levels[lvl_name]
			if lvl.upgrades_from in mgr.locked_employees:
				if not (lvl.level_name in mgr.locked_employees):
					mgr.locked_employees.append(lvl.level_name)
					changed = true

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-more_collectors_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-more_collectors_mod\extensions\scenes\Interfaces\Management\employee_shop_tooltip.hooks.gd
==================================================

extends Object

const CUSTOM_NAMES := {
	32: "Vortex Collector",
	33: "Quantum Collector",
	34: "Abyssal Collector",
	35: "Singularity Collector"
}

func _ready(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	var tooltip := chain.reference_object as EmployeeShopTooltip
	if not tooltip:
		chain.execute_next([])
		return

	tooltip.scale = Vector2(1.88, 1.88)
	_apply_click_through(tooltip)

	if not tooltip.employee_level:
		chain.execute_next([])
		return

	var lvl = tooltip.employee_level
	if CUSTOM_NAMES.has(lvl.level_name):
		var name_str: String = CUSTOM_NAMES[lvl.level_name]
		var info: String = "[b]" + name_str + " - [i]" + Gconsts.add_comma_to_int(int(lvl.upgrade_cost)) + "g[/i][/b]\n"
		info += "Type: Collector\n"
		info += "Flight Speed: " + str(lvl.fly_speed) + "\n"
		info += "Capacity: " + Gconsts.add_comma_to_int(int(lvl.inventory_space)) + " items\n"
		tooltip.text_label.text = info
		return
	chain.execute_next([])
	_apply_click_through(tooltip)

func _apply_click_through(node: Node) -> void:
	if node is Control:
		node.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	for child in node.get_children():
		_apply_click_through(child)

const SPACER_SCENE = preload("res://scenes/Interfaces/Management/employee_level_icon_spacer.tscn")

func _ready(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([])
	var chart := chain.reference_object as OrganisationChart2
	if not chart:
		return

	var levels := [
		"res://mods-unpacked/gareth-more_collectors_mod/levels/vortex_collector.tres",
		"res://mods-unpacked/gareth-more_collectors_mod/levels/quantum_collector.tres",
		"res://mods-unpacked/gareth-more_collectors_mod/levels/abyssal_collector.tres",
		"res://mods-unpacked/gareth-more_collectors_mod/levels/singularity_collector.tres"
	]

	var vbox = chart.get_node_or_null("VBoxContainer")
	if not vbox:
		return

	var new_row := HBoxContainer.new()
	new_row.name = "HBoxContainer_CosmicCollectors"
	vbox.add_child(new_row)

	for lvl_path in levels:
		var lvl = load(lvl_path)
		if lvl:
			var icon: EmployeeLevelIcon2 = ICON2_SCENE.instantiate()
			icon.employee_level = lvl
			icon.add_to_group("employee_icons")
			new_row.add_child(icon)
			icon.try_to_purchase.connect(chart.on_try_to_purchase)
			icon.try_to_sell.connect(chart.on_try_to_sell)
			new_row.add_child(SPACER_SCENE.instantiate())

	chart.register_all_employee_icons()
	chart.refresh_all_icons()
	ModLoaderLog.info("Cosmic Collector tier icons injected into Organisation Chart.", "gareth-more_collectors_mod")

extends Node

func _ready() -> void:
	var item_unlocks = load("res://resources/unlockable_items/scripts/item_unlocks.tres")
	if not item_unlocks:
		return

	var target_idx = -1
	for i in range(item_unlocks.all_equipment_items.size()):
		if item_unlocks.all_equipment_items[i] and item_unlocks.all_equipment_items[i].itemID == "drill_04":
			target_idx = i
			break

	var new_items: Array[Item] = []
	for t in ["05", "06", "07", "08", "09", "10"]:
		var it = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-more_drills_mod/items/drill_%s.tres" % t)
		if it:
			new_items.append(it)
			if not it in item_unlocks.all_items:
				item_unlocks.all_items.append(it)

	if target_idx != -1:
		for offset in range(new_items.size()):
			item_unlocks.all_equipment_items.insert(target_idx + 1 + offset, new_items[offset])
	else:
		for it in new_items:
			item_unlocks.all_equipment_items.append(it)

# Executive Workforce Hierarchy

**Namespace:** $namespace  
**Compatibility:** Coal LLC v1.4.1.1+ (ModLoader 7.0.1)

---

## Overview
The **Executive Workforce Hierarchy** expands the autonomous Miner workforce line past Senior VP Miner with four high-yield corporate tiers:
* **Executive VP Miner (Level 24):** Mining Speed 2.4, Ruby Pickaxe (,500,000).
* **Chief Mining Officer (Level 25):** Mining Speed 3.5, Pink Diamond Pickaxe (,000,000).
* **Managing Director Miner (Level 26):** Mining Speed 5.0, Moonstone Pickaxe (,000,000).
* **Titan Miner (Level 27):** Mining Speed 7.5, Onyx Pickaxe (,000,000,000).

The mod applies a 53% uncoupled scale to the organisation board ( = 350, Y = 80$), keeping all employee ranks clearly interactable above the grass line. Locked or disallowed parent branches automatically cascade their restrictions upward, preventing orphaned icons across the board.

Dynamic orthogonal connection lines are rendered using a custom _draw() hook, displaying clean red and green routing across all workforce branches.

---

## Technical Details (For Modders)
Hooks organisation_chart_2.gd _ready, refresh_all_icons, and _draw to inject custom rows and connection vectors. Patches employee_manager.gd to register levels 24-27.

---

## Installation
1. Ensure **Coal LLC** has Godot ModLoader 7.0.1 installed.
2. Place the packaged .zip file into your Coal LLC/mods/ directory.
3. Manage or toggle this mod directly via the in-game **Mod Manager**.
--------

# Executive Workforce Hierarchy

**Namespace:** $namespace  
**Compatibility:** Coal LLC v1.4.1.1+ (ModLoader 7.0.1)

---

## Overview
The **Executive Workforce Hierarchy** expands the autonomous Miner workforce line past Senior VP Miner with four high-yield corporate tiers:
* **Executive VP Miner (Level 24):** Mining Speed 2.4, Ruby Pickaxe (,500,000).
* **Chief Mining Officer (Level 25):** Mining Speed 3.5, Pink Diamond Pickaxe (,000,000).
* **Managing Director Miner (Level 26):** Mining Speed 5.0, Moonstone Pickaxe (,000,000).
* **Titan Miner (Level 27):** Mining Speed 7.5, Onyx Pickaxe (,000,000,000).

The mod applies a 53% uncoupled scale to the organisation board ( = 350, Y = 80$), keeping all employee ranks clearly interactable above the grass line. Locked or disallowed parent branches automatically cascade their restrictions upward, preventing orphaned icons across the board.

Dynamic orthogonal connection lines are rendered using a custom _draw() hook, displaying clean red and green routing across all workforce branches.

---

## Technical Details (For Modders)
Hooks organisation_chart_2.gd _ready, refresh_all_icons, and _draw to inject custom rows and connection vectors. Patches employee_manager.gd to register levels 24-27.

---

## Installation
1. Ensure **Coal LLC** has Godot ModLoader 7.0.1 installed.
2. Place the packaged .zip file into your Coal LLC/mods/ directory.
3. Manage or toggle this mod directly via the in-game **Mod Manager**.

extends Object

var custom_levels: Dictionary = {}

func _init() -> void:
	var paths := [
		"res://mods-unpacked/gareth-more_employees_mod/levels/executive_vp_miner.tres",
		"res://mods-unpacked/gareth-more_employees_mod/levels/chief_mining_officer.tres",
		"res://mods-unpacked/gareth-more_employees_mod/levels/managing_director_miner.tres",
		"res://mods-unpacked/gareth-more_employees_mod/levels/titan_miner.tres"
	]
	for p in paths:
		var lvl = load(p)
		if lvl:
			custom_levels[lvl.level_name] = lvl

func get_employee_level_from_enum(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, employee_enum: int) -> EmployeeLevel:
	if custom_levels.has(employee_enum):
		return custom_levels[employee_enum]
	return chain.execute_next([employee_enum])

func purchase_employee_level(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, employee_level: EmployeeLevel, count: float) -> float:
	var mgr = chain.reference_object
	if mgr:
		_cascade_locks(mgr)
		if employee_level.level_name in mgr.locked_employees:
			return 0.0
	return chain.execute_next([employee_level, count])

func _cascade_locks(mgr) -> void:
	var changed := true
	while changed:
		changed = false
		for lvl_name in custom_levels.keys():
			var lvl: EmployeeLevel = custom_levels[lvl_name]
			if lvl.upgrades_from in mgr.locked_employees:
				if not (lvl.level_name in mgr.locked_employees):
					mgr.locked_employees.append(lvl.level_name)
					changed = true

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-more_employees_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-more_employees_mod\extensions\scenes\Interfaces\Management\employee_level_icon_2.hooks.gd

extends Object

func _ready(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([])
	var icon := chain.reference_object as EmployeeLevelIcon2
	if not icon:
		return
	if icon.tooltip_instance:
		_set_ignore(icon.tooltip_instance)
	if icon.sell_tooltip_instance:
		_set_ignore(icon.sell_tooltip_instance)

func position_tooltip(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, tooltip_ins: Control) -> void:
	var icon := chain.reference_object as EmployeeLevelIcon2
	if not icon or not tooltip_ins:
		chain.execute_next([tooltip_ins])
		return

	_set_ignore(tooltip_ins)

	var scaled_w: float = tooltip_ins.size.x * tooltip_ins.scale.x
	var scaled_h: float = tooltip_ins.size.y * tooltip_ins.scale.y
	var mouse_pos: Vector2 = icon.get_local_mouse_position()
	var global_mouse: Vector2 = icon.get_global_mouse_position()

	var offset_x: float = 0.0
	var offset_y: float = 0.0

	if global_mouse.x > 960.0:
		offset_x = -45.0 - scaled_w
	else:
		offset_x = 45.0

	if global_mouse.y > 540.0:
		offset_y = -45.0 - scaled_h
	else:
		offset_y = 45.0

	tooltip_ins.position = mouse_pos + Vector2(offset_x, offset_y)

func _set_ignore(node: Node) -> void:
	if node is Control:
		node.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	for child in node.get_children():
		_set_ignore(child)

extends Object

const CUSTOM_NAMES := {
	24: "Executive VP Miner",
	25: "Chief Mining Officer",
	26: "Managing Director Miner",
	27: "Titan Miner"
}

func _ready(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	var tooltip := chain.reference_object as EmployeeShopTooltip
	if not tooltip:
		chain.execute_next([])
		return

	tooltip.scale = Vector2(1.88, 1.88)
	_apply_click_through(tooltip)

	if not tooltip.employee_level:
		chain.execute_next([])
		return

	var lvl = tooltip.employee_level
	if CUSTOM_NAMES.has(lvl.level_name):
		var name_str: String = CUSTOM_NAMES[lvl.level_name]
		var info: String = "[b]" + name_str + " - [i]" + Gconsts.add_comma_to_int(int(lvl.upgrade_cost)) + "g[/i][/b]\n"
		info += "Type: Miner\n"
		info += "Mining Speed: " + str(lvl.mining_speed) + "\n"
		if lvl.equipment:
			info += "Pickaxe: " + str(lvl.equipment.itemName) + "\n"
		tooltip.text_label.text = info
		return
	chain.execute_next([])
	_apply_click_through(tooltip)

func _apply_click_through(node: Node) -> void:
	if node is Control:
		node.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	for child in node.get_children():
		_apply_click_through(child)

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-more_employees_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-more_employees_mod\extensions\scenes\Interfaces\Management\organisation_chart_2.hooks.gd
==================================================
--------
extends Object

const ICON2_SCENE = preload("res://scenes/Interfaces/Management/employee_level_icon_2.tscn")
const SPACER_SCENE = preload("res://scenes/Interfaces/Management/employee_level_icon_spacer.tscn")

func _ready(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([])
	var chart := chain.reference_object as OrganisationChart2
	if not chart:
		return

	var vbox = chart.get_node_or_null("VBoxContainer")
	if not vbox:
		return

	if chart.has_node("TreeWrapper"):
		return

	var wrapper := Control.new()
	wrapper.name = "TreeWrapper"
	wrapper.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	chart.add_child(wrapper)
	chart.remove_child(vbox)
	wrapper.add_child(vbox)

	var to_remove: Array[Node] = []
	for child in vbox.get_children():
		if child is HBoxContainer:
			var has_icon := false
			for sub in child.get_children():
				if sub is EmployeeLevelIcon2:
					has_icon = true
					break
			if not has_icon:
				to_remove.append(child)
			else:
				child.size_flags_vertical = Control.SIZE_SHRINK_BEGIN
				child.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0, 128)

	for r in to_remove:
		vbox.remove_child(r)
		r.queue_free()

	var titan = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-more_employees_mod/levels/titan_miner.tres")
	var md = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-more_employees_mod/levels/managing_director_miner.tres")
	var cmo = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-more_employees_mod/levels/chief_mining_officer.tres")
	var exec = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-more_employees_mod/levels/executive_vp_miner.tres")

	var vortex = null
	var quantum = null
	var abyssal = null
	var singularity = null
	if ResourceLoader.exists("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-more_collectors_mod/levels/vortex_collector.tres"):
		vortex = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-more_collectors_mod/levels/vortex_collector.tres")
		quantum = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-more_collectors_mod/levels/quantum_collector.tres")
		abyssal = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-more_collectors_mod/levels/abyssal_collector.tres")
		singularity = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-more_collectors_mod/levels/singularity_collector.tres")

	var r9 := _create_grid_row(chart, titan, null, null)
	r9.name = "Tier_9_Titan"
	vbox.add_child(r9)
	vbox.move_child(r9, 0)

	var r8 := _create_grid_row(chart, md, null, null)
	r8.name = "Tier_8_MD"
	vbox.add_child(r8)
	vbox.move_child(r8, 1)

	var r7 := _create_grid_row(chart, cmo, quantum, singularity)
	r7.name = "Tier_7_CMO"
	vbox.add_child(r7)
	vbox.move_child(r7, 2)

	var r6 := _create_grid_row(chart, exec, vortex, abyssal)
	r6.name = "Tier_6_Exec"
	vbox.add_child(r6)
	vbox.move_child(r6, 3)

	vbox.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 4)
	vbox.scale = Vector2(0.53, 0.53)
	vbox.position = Vector2(350, 80)

	chart.register_all_employee_icons()
	_cascade_and_update_visibility(chart)
	chart.refresh_all_icons()
	ModLoaderLog.info("Workforce chart initialized cleanly.", "gareth-more_employees_mod")

func _create_grid_row(chart: OrganisationChart2, miner_lvl: EmployeeLevel, speed_col_lvl: EmployeeLevel, cap_col_lvl: EmployeeLevel) -> HBoxContainer:
	var row := HBoxContainer.new()
	row.alignment = BoxContainer.ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	row.size_flags_vertical = Control.SIZE_SHRINK_BEGIN
	row.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0, 128)
	for i in range(15):
		if i == 9 and miner_lvl:
			var icon: EmployeeLevelIcon2 = ICON2_SCENE.instantiate()
			icon.employee_level = miner_lvl
			icon.add_to_group("employee_icons")
			row.add_child(icon)
			icon.try_to_purchase.connect(chart.on_try_to_purchase)
			icon.try_to_sell.connect(chart.on_try_to_sell)
		elif i == 11 and speed_col_lvl:
			var icon: EmployeeLevelIcon2 = ICON2_SCENE.instantiate()
			icon.employee_level = speed_col_lvl
			icon.add_to_group("employee_icons")
			row.add_child(icon)
			icon.try_to_purchase.connect(chart.on_try_to_purchase)
			icon.try_to_sell.connect(chart.on_try_to_sell)
		elif i == 13 and cap_col_lvl:
			var icon: EmployeeLevelIcon2 = ICON2_SCENE.instantiate()
			icon.employee_level = cap_col_lvl
			icon.add_to_group("employee_icons")
			row.add_child(icon)
			icon.try_to_purchase.connect(chart.on_try_to_purchase)
			icon.try_to_sell.connect(chart.on_try_to_sell)
		else:
			row.add_child(SPACER_SCENE.instantiate())
	return row

func refresh_all_icons(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	var chart := chain.reference_object as OrganisationChart2
	if not chart:
		chain.execute_next([])
		return
	_cascade_and_update_visibility(chart)
	chain.execute_next([])
	_cascade_and_update_visibility(chart)
	chart.queue_redraw()

func _cascade_and_update_visibility(chart: OrganisationChart2) -> void:
	var mgr = Gvars.employee_manager
	if not mgr:
		return

	var changed := true
	while changed:
		changed = false
		for icon in chart.all_employee_icons:
			if not icon or not is_instance_valid(icon) or not icon.employee_level:
				continue
			var lvl = icon.employee_level
			if lvl.upgrades_from != EmployeeLevel.EmployeeLevelNames.NOTHING:
				if lvl.upgrades_from in mgr.locked_employees:
					if not (lvl.level_name in mgr.locked_employees):
						mgr.locked_employees.append(lvl.level_name)
						changed = true

	for icon in chart.all_employee_icons:
		if not icon or not is_instance_valid(icon) or not icon.employee_level:
			continue
		var is_locked = (icon.employee_level.level_name in mgr.locked_employees)
		if is_locked:
			icon.visible = false
			icon.purchaseable = false

func _draw(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	var chart := chain.reference_object as OrganisationChart2
	if not chart:
		chain.execute_next([])
		return

	if chart.all_employee_icons.is_empty():
		chart.register_all_employee_icons()

	var mgr = Gvars.employee_manager
	var points_lock: Array[Vector2] = []
	var points_unlock: Array[Vector2] = []

	for icon in chart.all_employee_icons:
		if not icon or not is_instance_valid(icon) or not icon.visible or not icon.employee_level:
			continue
		if mgr and (icon.employee_level.level_name in mgr.locked_employees):
			continue

		var upgrades_from = icon.employee_level.upgrades_from
		if upgrades_from == EmployeeLevel.EmployeeLevelNames.NOTHING:
			continue

		for icon_2 in chart.all_employee_icons:
			if not icon_2 or not is_instance_valid(icon_2) or not icon_2.visible or not icon_2.employee_level:
				continue
			if mgr and (icon_2.employee_level.level_name in mgr.locked_employees):
				continue

			if icon_2.employee_level.level_name == upgrades_from:
				var p1: Vector2
				if icon_2.employee_node:
					p1 = icon_2.employee_node.global_position - chart.global_position
				else:
					p1 = icon_2.global_position - chart.global_position

				var p2: Vector2
				if icon.employee_node:
					p2 = icon.employee_node.global_position - chart.global_position
				else:
					p2 = icon.global_position - chart.global_position

				if p1.length_squared() < 100.0 or p2.length_squared() < 100.0:
					continue

				var mid_y: float = (p1.y + p2.y) / 2.0
				var pts: Array[Vector2] = points_unlock if icon.purchaseable else points_lock

				pts.append(p1)
				pts.append(Vector2(p1.x, mid_y))

				pts.append(Vector2(p1.x, mid_y))
				pts.append(Vector2(p2.x, mid_y))

				pts.append(Vector2(p2.x, mid_y))
				pts.append(p2)

	if not points_lock.is_empty():
		chart.draw_multiline(PackedVector2Array(points_lock), Color(0.6, 0.0, 0.0), 3.5, false)

	if not points_unlock.is_empty():
		chart.draw_multiline(PackedVector2Array(points_unlock), Color(0.0, 0.6, 0.0), 5.5, false)

extends Node

func _init() -> void:
	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks(
		"res://scenes/Interfaces/InventoryWindow.gd",
		"res://mods-unpacked/gareth-more_inventory_slots_mod/extensions/scenes/Interfaces/InventoryWindow.hooks.gd"
	)

func _ready() -> void:
	var item_unlocks = load("res://resources/unlockable_items/scripts/item_unlocks.tres")
	if not item_unlocks:
		return

	var cleaned: Array[Item] = []
	for it in item_unlocks.all_bonus_items:
		if it and it.itemID.begins_with("AddInventorySlots") and not it.itemID in ["AddInventorySlots1", "AddInventorySlots2", "AddInventorySlots3"]:
			continue
		cleaned.append(it)
	item_unlocks.all_bonus_items = cleaned

	var target_idx = -1
	for i in range(item_unlocks.all_bonus_items.size()):
		if item_unlocks.all_bonus_items[i] and item_unlocks.all_bonus_items[i].itemID == "AddInventorySlots3":
			target_idx = i
			break

	var new_items: Array[Item] = []
	for t in range(4, 11):
		var it = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-more_inventory_slots_mod/items/add_inventory_slots_%d.tres" % t)
		if it:
			new_items.append(it)
			if not it in item_unlocks.all_items:
				item_unlocks.all_items.append(it)

	if target_idx != -1:
		for offset in range(new_items.size()):
			item_unlocks.all_bonus_items.insert(target_idx + 1 + offset, new_items[offset])
	else:
		for it in new_items:
			item_unlocks.all_bonus_items.append(it)

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-more_inventory_slots_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-more_inventory_slots_mod\helpers\InventoryScrollHelper.gd

class_name InventoryScrollHelper
extends RefCounted

static func setup_scroll(window: GridContainer) -> void:
	if not window or not is_instance_valid(window):
		return
	_apply_scroll_wrapper.call_deferred(window)

static func update_scroll(window: GridContainer) -> void:
	if not window or not is_instance_valid(window):
		return
	_adjust_scroll_size.call_deferred(window)

static func _apply_scroll_wrapper(window: GridContainer) -> void:
	if not window or not is_instance_valid(window):
		return

	var parent = window.get_parent()
	if not parent or not is_instance_valid(parent):
		return

	if parent is ScrollContainer or parent.name == "InventoryScrollContainer":
		_adjust_scroll_size(window)
		return

	var idx = window.get_index()
	var orig_owner = window.owner

	var scroll = ScrollContainer.new()
	scroll.name = "InventoryScrollContainer"
	scroll.horizontal_scroll_mode = ScrollContainer.SCROLL_MODE_DISABLED
	scroll.vertical_scroll_mode = ScrollContainer.SCROLL_MODE_AUTO

	scroll.size_flags_horizontal = window.size_flags_horizontal
	scroll.size_flags_vertical = Control.SIZE_SHRINK_CENTER

	var dims = _calculate_dimensions(window)
	scroll.custom_minimum_size = dims

	window.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	window.size_flags_vertical = Control.SIZE_SHRINK_BEGIN

	parent.remove_child(window)
	parent.add_child(scroll)
	parent.move_child(scroll, idx)
	scroll.add_child(window)

	if orig_owner:
		scroll.owner = orig_owner
		window.owner = orig_owner

	_wire_slot_mouse_scroll(window, scroll)

static func _adjust_scroll_size(window: GridContainer) -> void:
	if not window or not is_instance_valid(window):
		return
	var scroll = window.get_parent() as ScrollContainer
	if not scroll:
		return
	var dims = _calculate_dimensions(window)
	scroll.custom_minimum_size = dims
	_wire_slot_mouse_scroll(window, scroll)

static func _calculate_dimensions(window: GridContainer) -> Vector2:
	var slots = window.get_children()
	var slot_w: float = 64.0
	var slot_h: float = 64.0
	var h_sep: float = window.get_theme_constant("h_separation")
	var v_sep: float = window.get_theme_constant("v_separation")
	if h_sep <= 0.0: h_sep = 6.0
	if v_sep <= 0.0: v_sep = 6.0

	if slots.size() > 0 and slots[0] is Control:
		var s = slots[0] as Control
		if s.size.x > 10.0: slot_w = s.size.x
		elif s.custom_minimum_size.x > 10.0: slot_w = s.custom_minimum_size.x
		if s.size.y > 10.0: slot_h = s.size.y
		elif s.custom_minimum_size.y > 10.0: slot_h = s.custom_minimum_size.y

	var total_w = (slot_w * 10.0) + (h_sep * 9.0) + 26.0
	var max_h   = (slot_h * 4.0) + (v_sep * 3.0) + 4.0
	return Vector2(total_w, max_h)

static func _wire_slot_mouse_scroll(window: GridContainer, scroll: ScrollContainer) -> void:
	for child in window.get_children():
		if child is Control and not child.has_meta("scroll_wired"):
			child.set_meta("scroll_wired", true)
			child.gui_input.connect(func(event: InputEvent):
				if event is InputEventMouseButton and event.is_pressed():
					var v_bar = scroll.get_v_scroll_bar()
					if not v_bar:
						return
					if event.button_index == MOUSE_BUTTON_WHEEL_UP:
						v_bar.value -= v_bar.page * 0.25
					elif event.button_index == MOUSE_BUTTON_WHEEL_DOWN:
						v_bar.value += v_bar.page * 0.25
			)

extends Node

const MOD_DIR := "gareth-more_layer_shifts_mod"

func _init() -> void:
	var mod_dir_path: String = ModLoaderMod.get_unpacked_dir().path_join(MOD_DIR)
	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks(
		"res://scenes/tilemaps/tile_map_chunk.gd",
		mod_dir_path.path_join("extensions/scenes/tilemaps/tile_map_chunk.hooks.gd")
	)

func _ready() -> void:
	var item_unlocks = load("res://resources/unlockable_items/scripts/item_unlocks.tres")
	if item_unlocks:
		for lvl in range(6, 13):
			var path = "res://mods-unpacked/gareth-more_layer_shifts_mod/items/shift_layers_%d.tres" % lvl
			if ResourceLoader.exists(path):
				var item = load(path)
				if item:
					if not item in item_unlocks.all_bonus_items:
						item_unlocks.all_bonus_items.append(item)
					if not item in item_unlocks.all_items:
						item_unlocks.all_items.append(item)
	ModLoaderLog.info("More Layer Shifts 6-12 active with extended stratum progression.", MOD_DIR)

# Deeper Cavern Layer Shifts (6-12)

**Namespace:** $namespace  
**Compatibility:** Coal LLC v1.4.1.1+ (ModLoader 7.0.1)

---

## Overview
Extends subterranean mine generation from 6 up to 12 distinct geological strata, elevating high-tier mineral veins closer to reachable mining depths.

---

## Technical Details (For Modders)
Hooks adjust_level_bonus in tile_map_chunk.gd to extrapolate depth parameters beyond vanilla stratum bounds.

---

## Installation
1. Ensure **Coal LLC** has Godot ModLoader 7.0.1 installed.
2. Place the packaged .zip file into your Coal LLC/mods/ directory.
3. Manage or toggle this mod directly via the in-game **Mod Manager**.

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-more_layer_shifts_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-more_layer_shifts_mod\extensions\scenes\tilemaps\tile_map_chunk.hooks.gd
==================================================
--------
extends Object

func adjust_level_bonus(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, lvl: int, rank: int) -> int:
	if lvl < 0:
		return lvl

	var chunk = chain.reference_object
	if not chunk or not ("upgrade_table" in chunk):
		return chain.execute_next([lvl, rank])

	var table: Array = chunk.upgrade_table
	if lvl >= table.size():
		return 19

	var row: Array = table[lvl]
	var max_vanilla_rank: int = row.size() - 1

	# Ranks 0-5 use the vanilla table directly
	if rank <= max_vanilla_rank:
		return row[rank]

	# Ranks 6-12 extend progression smoothly toward layer 19 (deepest rock)
	var extra_shift: int = rank - max_vanilla_rank
	var base_val: int = row[max_vanilla_rank]
	var calculated_lvl: int = mini(19, base_val + extra_shift)
	return calculated_lvl

extends Node

func _ready() -> void:
	var item_unlocks = load("res://resources/unlockable_items/scripts/item_unlocks.tres")
	if not item_unlocks:
		return

	var target_idx = -1
	for i in range(item_unlocks.all_equipment_items.size()):
		if item_unlocks.all_equipment_items[i] and item_unlocks.all_equipment_items[i].itemID == "mortar_gun_05":
			target_idx = i
			break

	var new_items: Array[Item] = []
	for t in ["06", "07", "08", "09", "10"]:
		var it = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-more_mortars_mod/items/mortar_gun_%s.tres" % t)
		if it:
			new_items.append(it)
			if not it in item_unlocks.all_items:
				item_unlocks.all_items.append(it)

	if target_idx != -1:
		for offset in range(new_items.size()):
			item_unlocks.all_equipment_items.insert(target_idx + 1 + offset, new_items[offset])
	else:
		for it in new_items:
			item_unlocks.all_equipment_items.append(it)

extends Node

const MOD_DIR := "gareth-more_nukes_mod"

func _ready() -> void:
	var item_unlocks = load("res://resources/unlockable_items/scripts/item_unlocks.tres")
	if not item_unlocks:
		return

	var tiers = [
		"01_shoddy", "02_copper", "03_iron", "04_sapphire", "05_emerald",
		"06_silver", "07_amethyst", "08_gold", "09_ruby", "10_diamond",
		"11_pinkdiamond", "12_spinel", "13_uranium", "14_moonstone", "15_onyx"
	]

	for t in tiers:
		var item_path := "res://mods-unpacked/gareth-more_nukes_mod/items/tactical_nuke_%s.tres" % t
		if ResourceLoader.exists(item_path):
			var item = load(item_path)
			var tex_path := "res://mods-unpacked/gareth-more_nukes_mod/textures/nuke_%s.png" % t
			if FileAccess.file_exists(tex_path):
				var img = Image.load_from_file(tex_path)
				if img:
					item.itemTexture = ImageTexture.create_from_image(img)
			if item and not (item in item_unlocks.all_equipment_items):
				item_unlocks.all_equipment_items.append(item)
			if item and not (item in item_unlocks.all_items):
				item_unlocks.all_items.append(item)

	ModLoaderLog.info("15 Tactical Nukes registered with color-coded warheads.", MOD_DIR)

extends Node

const MOD_DIR := "gareth-more_vacuum_quests_mod"

func _init() -> void:
	var mod_dir_path: String = ModLoaderMod.get_unpacked_dir().path_join(MOD_DIR)
	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks(
		"res://scripts/equipment_manager.gd",
		mod_dir_path.path_join("extensions/scripts/equipment_manager.hooks.gd")
	)
	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks(
		"res://scenes/NPCs/quest_scene_manager.gd",
		mod_dir_path.path_join("extensions/scenes/NPCs/quest_scene_manager.hooks.gd")
	)
	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks(
		"res://resources/Quests/quest_rewards/reward_vacuum.gd",
		mod_dir_path.path_join("extensions/resources/Quests/quest_rewards/reward_vacuum.hooks.gd")
	)

func _ready() -> void:
	var item_unlocks = load("res://resources/unlockable_items/scripts/item_unlocks.tres")
	if item_unlocks:
		for lvl in range(5, 9):
			var path = "res://mods-unpacked/gareth-more_vacuum_quests_mod/items/vacuum_cleaner_%d.tres" % lvl
			if ResourceLoader.exists(path):
				var item = load(path)
				if item:
					if not item in item_unlocks.all_bonus_items:
						item_unlocks.all_bonus_items.append(item)
					if not item in item_unlocks.all_items:
						item_unlocks.all_items.append(item)
	ModLoaderLog.info("Tabatha Quests & Vacuum Levels 5-8 online.", MOD_DIR)

# Tabatha Quests & Vacuum Levels 5-8

**Namespace:** $namespace  
**Compatibility:** Coal LLC v1.4.1.1+ (ModLoader 7.0.1)

---

## Overview
Extends NPC Tabatha's questline with Diamond, Uranium, and Moonstone tiers that reward high-capacity deep-strata vacuums.

---

## Technical Details (For Modders)
Hooks quest_scene_manager.gd to inject extended quest objectives and connects reward_vacuum.gd to high-tier vacuum equipment.

---

## Installation
1. Ensure **Coal LLC** has Godot ModLoader 7.0.1 installed.
2. Place the packaged .zip file into your Coal LLC/mods/ directory.
3. Manage or toggle this mod directly via the in-game **Mod Manager**.

extends Object

func reward(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([])
	var rw = chain.reference_object
	if not rw or not Gvars.quest_manager:
		return
	if rw.vacuum == BonusEquipmentManager.Vacuums.LEVEL_4:
		if Gvars.quest_manager.quests.has(100):
			if Gvars.quest_manager.quests[100]["state"] == QuestManager.States.UNAVAILABLE_CHAIN:
				Gvars.quest_manager.change_quest_state(100, QuestManager.States.AVAILABLE)

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-more_vacuum_quests_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-more_vacuum_quests_mod\extensions\scenes\NPCs\quest_scene_manager.hooks.gd

extends Object

const QUEST_POINT_SCENE = preload("res://scenes/NPCs/quest_point.tscn")
const CUST_REWARD_SCRIPT = preload("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-more_vacuum_quests_mod/scripts/custom_vacuum_reward.gd")

func _ready(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([])
	var qsm := chain.reference_object as QuestSceneManager
	if not qsm:
		return
	_setup_extended_mermaid_quests(qsm)

func refresh_all_quest_points(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	var qsm := chain.reference_object as QuestSceneManager
	if qsm:
		_setup_extended_mermaid_quests(qsm)
	chain.execute_next([])

func _setup_extended_mermaid_quests(qsm: QuestSceneManager) -> void:
	var qm := Gvars.quest_manager
	var bem := Gvars.bonus_equipment_manager
	if not qm or not bem:
		return

	# 1. Initialize quest entries in QuestManager
	var custom_ids := [100, 101, 102, 103]
	for q_id in custom_ids:
		if not qm.quests.has(q_id):
			qm.quests[q_id] = {
				"state": QuestManager.States.UNAVAILABLE_CHAIN,
				"count_so_far": 0
			}

	# 2. Check Diamond completion
	var diamond_done := false
	if qm.quests.has(QuestManager.Quests.MERMAID_05):
		diamond_done = (qm.quests[QuestManager.Quests.MERMAID_05]["state"] == QuestManager.States.COMPLETE)

	if int(bem.current_vacuum) >= 4 or diamond_done:
		if qm.quests[100]["state"] == QuestManager.States.UNAVAILABLE_CHAIN:
			qm.quests[100]["state"] = QuestManager.States.AVAILABLE

	# 3. Synchronize with bonus shop purchases
	if int(bem.current_vacuum) >= 5:
		qm.quests[100]["state"] = QuestManager.States.COMPLETE
		if qm.quests[101]["state"] == QuestManager.States.UNAVAILABLE_CHAIN:
			qm.quests[101]["state"] = QuestManager.States.AVAILABLE
	if int(bem.current_vacuum) >= 6:
		qm.quests[101]["state"] = QuestManager.States.COMPLETE
		if qm.quests[102]["state"] == QuestManager.States.UNAVAILABLE_CHAIN:
			qm.quests[102]["state"] = QuestManager.States.AVAILABLE
	if int(bem.current_vacuum) >= 7:
		qm.quests[102]["state"] = QuestManager.States.COMPLETE
		if qm.quests[103]["state"] == QuestManager.States.UNAVAILABLE_CHAIN:
			qm.quests[103]["state"] = QuestManager.States.AVAILABLE
	if int(bem.current_vacuum) >= 8:
		qm.quests[103]["state"] = QuestManager.States.COMPLETE

	# 4. Locate Tabatha (MermaidNPC)
	var tree := qsm.get_tree()
	var mermaid: NPC = null
	for node in tree.get_nodes_in_group("npcs"):
		if node is NPC and node.name == "MermaidNPC":
			mermaid = node
			break
	if not mermaid:
		for node in tree.get_nodes_in_group("quest_points"):
			if node is QuestPoint and node.quest_giver and node.quest_giver.name == "MermaidNPC":
				mermaid = node.quest_giver
				break

	if not mermaid:
		return

	var quests_node := mermaid.get_parent().get_node_or_null("../Quests")
	if not quests_node:
		quests_node = mermaid.get_parent()

	# 5. Inject QuestPoints
	_build_quest_point(qsm, quests_node, mermaid, 100, 5, 101,
		"res://resources/Items/Gems/Uranium.tres", 60,
		"RADIOACTIVE SHINES !!! I NEED 60 URANIUM GEMS PLEASE !!! I WILL MAKE YOUR VACUUM EVEN LONGER TO REACH DEEP GREEN ROCKS !!!",
		"WAHOO !!! SPARKLY AND DANGEROUS !!! YOUR VACUUM REACHES 220 TILES DEEP NOW !!!")

	_build_quest_point(qsm, quests_node, mermaid, 101, 6, 102,
		"res://resources/Items/Gems/Moonstone.tres", 75,
		"CAN YOU BRING ME 75 MOONSTONES PLEASE ??? SHINY BLUE GLOW WILL POWER A SUPER VACUUM FOR DEEP CAVERNS !!!",
		"WAHOO !!! MOON MAGIC POWERS YOUR HOSE !!! NOW YOU CAN REACH 300 TILES DEEP !!!")

	_build_quest_point(qsm, quests_node, mermaid, 102, 7, 103,
		"res://resources/Items/Gems/Onyx.tres", 100,
		"BLACK AS NIGHT !!! BRING ME 100 ONYX GEMS PLEASE !!! ALMOST AT THE CORE OF THE EARTH !!!",
		"WAHOO !!! IT'S SO HEAVY AND STRONG !!! VACUUM REACH EXTENDED TO 420 TILES !!!")

	_build_quest_point(qsm, quests_node, mermaid, 103, 8, -1,
		"res://resources/Items/Gems/PinkDiamond.tres", 150,
		"ULTIMATE CHALLENGE !!! 150 PINK DIAMONDS FOR THE LEGENDARY ABYSSAL VACUUM !!! REACH THE VERY BOTTOM OF THE WORLD !!!",
		"UNBELIEVABLE !!! THE ULTIMATE VACUUM IS YOURS !!! 600 TILES OF RAW SUCTION POWER !!! THANK YOU BEST FRIEND !!!")

func _build_quest_point(qsm: QuestSceneManager, parent_node: Node, mermaid: NPC, q_id: int, target_lvl: int, next_q: int, gem_path: String, count_req: int, start_txt: String, end_txt: String) -> void:
	var qp_name := "QuestPointTabatha%d" % (q_id - 94)
	if parent_node.has_node(qp_name):
		return

	var qp: QuestPoint = QUEST_POINT_SCENE.instantiate()
	qp.name = qp_name
	qp.quest = q_id
	qp.quest_giver = mermaid
	qp.quest_start_dialogue = start_txt
	qp.quest_complete_dialogue = end_txt
	qp.quest_item = load(gem_path)
	qp.count_required = count_req

	var rew = CUST_REWARD_SCRIPT.new()
	rew.target_vacuum_level = target_lvl
	rew.next_quest_id = next_q
	qp.quest_reward = rew

	var qm = Gvars.quest_manager
	if qm and qm.quests.has(q_id):
		qp.state = qm.quests[q_id]["state"]
		qp.count_so_far = qm.quests[q_id]["count_so_far"]
	else:
		qp.state = QuestManager.States.UNAVAILABLE_CHAIN
		qp.count_so_far = 0

	parent_node.add_child(qp)
	qp.add_to_group("quest_points")
	qsm.all_quest_points[q_id] = qp
	mermaid.no_dialogue_left.connect(qp.interact_with_quest)
	qp.refresh_state()

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-more_vacuum_quests_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-more_vacuum_quests_mod\extensions\scripts\equipment_manager.hooks.gd

extends Object

func generate_vacuum_cleaner(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([])
	var mgr = chain.reference_object
	if not mgr or not mgr.bem:
		return
	var cur_lvl = int(mgr.bem.current_vacuum)
	var target_len = 0
	match cur_lvl:
		5: target_len = 220 * 16
		6: target_len = 300 * 16
		7: target_len = 420 * 16
		8: target_len = 600 * 16
		_: target_len = 0

	if target_len > 0:
		for child in mgr.get_children():
			if child is VacuumCleaner:
				child.vacuum_length = target_len
				ModLoaderLog.info("Upgraded vacuum tether length to %d tiles (%d px)." % [target_len / 16, target_len], "gareth-more_vacuum_quests_mod")


--------

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-more_vacuum_quests_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-more_vacuum_quests_mod\scripts\custom_vacuum_reward.gd
==================================================
--------
class_name CustomVacuumReward
extends QuestReward

var target_vacuum_level: int = 5
var next_quest_id: int = 101

func reward() -> void:
	if Gvars.bonus_equipment_manager:
		Gvars.bonus_equipment_manager.current_vacuum = target_vacuum_level
	if Gvars.equipment_manager:
		Gvars.equipment_manager.generate_vacuum_cleaner()
	if next_quest_id > 0 and Gvars.quest_manager:
		Gvars.quest_manager.change_quest_state(next_quest_id, QuestManager.States.AVAILABLE)

# Multi-Save Profile Manager

**Namespace:** $namespace  
**Compatibility:** Coal LLC v1.4.1.1+ (ModLoader 7.0.1)

---

## Overview
Introduces 6 independent save profile slots accessible from the Main Menu, each tracking career progress, day count, cash reserves, and active classes.

The manager automatically migrates single-save career files into Slot 1 and validates career badges on boot to protect unlocked classes and achievements.

---

## Technical Details (For Modders)
Passive filesystem listener monitors user://v04_savegame.save timestamps and mirrors to user://saves/slot_X/. Reads and validates user://v04_saveglobals.save on startup.

---

## Installation
1. Ensure **Coal LLC** has Godot ModLoader 7.0.1 installed.
2. Place the packaged .zip file into your Coal LLC/mods/ directory.
3. Manage or toggle this mod directly via the in-game **Mod Manager**.
--------

		"res://scripts/MainMenu.gd",

	var ssm = Node.new()

	ssm.set_script(ssm_script)

	ModLoaderLog.info("Multi-Save Slot Manager v2.2 active without Gvars hooks.", MOD_DIR)

# Multi-Save Profile Manager

**Namespace:** $namespace  
**Compatibility:** Coal LLC v1.4.1.1+ (ModLoader 7.0.1)

---

## Overview
Introduces 6 independent save profile slots accessible from the Main Menu, each tracking career progress, day count, cash reserves, and active classes.

The manager automatically migrates single-save career files into Slot 1 and validates career badges on boot to protect unlocked classes and achievements.

---

## Technical Details (For Modders)
Passive filesystem listener monitors user://v04_savegame.save timestamps and mirrors to user://saves/slot_X/. Reads and validates user://v04_saveglobals.save on startup.

---

## Installation
1. Ensure **Coal LLC** has Godot ModLoader 7.0.1 installed.
2. Place the packaged .zip file into your Coal LLC/mods/ directory.
3. Manage or toggle this mod directly via the in-game **Mod Manager**.
--------
==================================================
FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-multisave_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-multisave_mod\extensions\scripts\Gvars.hooks.gd
==================================================
--------
extends Object

func save_game(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([])
	var ssm = Gvars.get_node_or_null("/root/SaveSlotManager")
	if ssm:
		ssm.sync_active_slot_from_vanilla()

func load_game(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	var ssm = Gvars.get_node_or_null("/root/SaveSlotManager")
	if ssm:
		ssm.prepare_vanilla_from_active_slot()
	chain.execute_next([])

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-multisave_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-multisave_mod\extensions\scripts\MainMenu.hooks.gd
==================================================
--------
extends Object

func _ready(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([])
	var menu = chain.reference_object
	if not menu:
		return

	var ssm = menu.get_node_or_null("/root/SaveSlotManager")
	if ssm and ssm.has_method("restore_career_progression"):
		ssm.restore_career_progression()

	var slot_num: int = ssm.current_slot if ssm else 1

	if "continue_game_button" in menu and menu.continue_game_button:
		menu.continue_game_button.text = "Resume Profile (Slot %d)" % slot_num
		if ssm and ssm.has_save(slot_num):
			menu.continue_game_button.disabled = false

	var btn_grid = menu.get_node_or_null("%MainMenuButtons")
	if btn_grid and not btn_grid.has_node("SaveProfilesButton"):
		var profile_btn := Button.new()
		profile_btn.name = "SaveProfilesButton"
		profile_btn.text = "Save Profiles"
		profile_btn.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0, 32)
		profile_btn.pressed.connect(func(): _open_profiles_modal(menu))
		btn_grid.add_child(profile_btn)
		btn_grid.move_child(profile_btn, 1)

func _open_profiles_modal(menu: Control) -> void:
	var modal_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-multisave_mod/scenes/save_slot_modal.gd")
	var modal: Control = Control.new()
	modal.set_script(modal_script)

	modal.slot_chosen_play.connect(func(slot_id: int):
		var ssm = menu.get_node_or_null("/root/SaveSlotManager")
		if ssm:
			ssm.current_slot = slot_id
			ssm.prepare_vanilla_from_active_slot()
		modal.queue_free()
		menu.on_pressed_continuegame()
	)

	modal.slot_chosen_new.connect(func(slot_id: int):
		var ssm = menu.get_node_or_null("/root/SaveSlotManager")
		if ssm:
			ssm.current_slot = slot_id
			ssm.delete_slot(slot_id)
		modal.queue_free()
		menu.on_pressed_newgame()
	)

	menu.add_child(modal)
