func _ready() -> void:
	process_mode = Node.PROCESS_MODE_ALWAYS
	var vp_rect := get_viewport_rect()
	position = Vector2.ZERO
	size = vp_rect.size
	custom_minimum_size = vp_rect.size
	mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_STOP
	z_index = 80

	var bg := ColorRect.new()
	bg.position = Vector2.ZERO
	bg.size = vp_rect.size
	bg.color = Color(0.06, 0.07, 0.09, 0.98)
	bg.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_STOP
	add_child(bg)

	var margin := MarginContainer.new()
	margin.position = Vector2.ZERO
	margin.size = vp_rect.size
	margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_left", 40)
	margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_right", 40)
	margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_top", 30)
	margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_bottom", 30)
	add_child(margin)

	var vbox := VBoxContainer.new()
	vbox.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	vbox.size_flags_vertical = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	vbox.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 16)
	margin.add_child(vbox)

	var top_bar := HBoxContainer.new()
	top_bar.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0, 48)
	top_bar.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	vbox.add_child(top_bar)

	var back_btn := Button.new()
	back_btn.text = " < Back to Class Select "
	back_btn.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(200, 40)
	back_btn.size_flags_vertical = Control.SIZE_SHRINK_CENTER
	back_btn.pressed.connect(func():
		back_requested.emit()
		queue_free()
	)
	top_bar.add_child(back_btn)

	var title := Label.new()
	title.text = "ACTIVE MOD EXPANSIONS & MUTATORS"
	title.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	title.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	title.size_flags_vertical = Control.SIZE_SHRINK_CENTER
	title.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 20)
	top_bar.add_child(title)

	var confirm_btn := Button.new()
	confirm_btn.text = " Continue to Map Select > "
	confirm_btn.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(220, 40)
	confirm_btn.size_flags_vertical = Control.SIZE_SHRINK_CENTER
	confirm_btn.modulate = Color(0.4, 1.0, 0.5)
	confirm_btn.pressed.connect(_on_confirm)
	top_bar.add_child(confirm_btn)

	var sep := HSeparator.new()
	vbox.add_child(sep)

	var scroll := ScrollContainer.new()
	scroll.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	scroll.size_flags_vertical = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	vbox.add_child(scroll)

	var content := VBoxContainer.new()
	content.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	content.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 20)
	scroll.add_child(content)

	var saved_config := _load_existing_mod_config()
	var categories := ["Weapons & Scaling", "Progression & Upgrades", "Workforce & Automation", "Hardcore Mutators"]

	for cat in categories:
		var cat_lbl := Label.new()
		cat_lbl.text = cat.to_upper()
		cat_lbl.modulate = Color(1.0, 0.85, 0.3)
		cat_lbl.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 16)
		content.add_child(cat_lbl)

		var grid := GridContainer.new()
		grid.columns = 2
		grid.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
		grid.add_theme_constant_override("h_separation", 20)
		grid.add_theme_constant_override("v_separation", 12)
		content.add_child(grid)

		for m in DEFAULT_MODS:
			if m["category"] != cat:
				continue
			var k: String = m["key"]
			var default_val: bool = bool(saved_config.get(k, m["default"]))
			toggles[k] = default_val

			var card := PanelContainer.new()
			card.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL

			var c_margin := MarginContainer.new()
			c_margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_left", 14)
			c_margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_right", 14)
			c_margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_top", 10)
			c_margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_bottom", 10)
			card.add_child(c_margin)

			var hbox := HBoxContainer.new()
			hbox.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
			hbox.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 12)
			c_margin.add_child(hbox)

			var cb := CheckBox.new()
			cb.button_pressed = default_val
			cb.toggled.connect(func(v: bool): toggles[k] = v)
			hbox.add_child(cb)

			var info_vbox := VBoxContainer.new()
			info_vbox.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL

			var name_lbl := Label.new()
			name_lbl.text = m["label"]
			name_lbl.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 14)
			info_vbox.add_child(name_lbl)

			var desc_lbl := Label.new()
			desc_lbl.text = m["desc"]
			desc_lbl.modulate = Color(0.7, 0.7, 0.7)
			desc_lbl.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 11)
			desc_lbl.autowrap_mode = TextServer.AUTOWRAP_WORD_SMART
			info_vbox.add_child(desc_lbl)

			hbox.add_child(info_vbox)
			grid.add_child(card)

func _load_existing_mod_config() -> Dictionary:
	var path := "user://gareth_mods_config.json"
	if FileAccess.file_exists(path):
		var f := FileAccess.open(path, FileAccess.READ)
		if f:
			var parsed = JSON.parse_string(f.get_as_text())
			if parsed is Dictionary and parsed.has("mods"):
				return parsed["mods"]
	return {}

func _on_confirm() -> void:
	var ssm = get_node_or_null("/root/SaveSlotManager")
	if ssm and ("current_slot" in ssm) and ssm.has_method("get_slot_config_path"):
		var cfg := {"mods": toggles}
		var path: String = ssm.get_slot_config_path(ssm.current_slot)
		var f := FileAccess.open(path, FileAccess.WRITE)
		if f:
			f.store_string(JSON.stringify(cfg, "\t"))
			f.close()
	run_confirmed.emit()
	queue_free()

class_name RunMutatorModal
extends PanelContainer

signal confirmed()

var active_toggles: Dictionary = {}

const MUTATORS: Array[Dictionary] = [
	{"key": "dual_multipliers", "label": "Dual-Multiplier Quadratic Scaling", "desc": "Gives Drills, Axes & Firearms secondary scaling stats.", "default": true},
	{"key": "bouncing_grenades", "label": "Bouncing Grenade Arsenal", "desc": "15-tier parabolic rebounding explosives.", "default": true},
	{"key": "water_spirit", "label": "Orbital Water Spirit Familiar", "desc": "Saturates strata within an expanding orbit.", "default": true},
	{"key": "vacuum_depot", "label": "Subterranean Vacuum Depot Offload", "desc": "UFOs offload cargo into deep vacuum nozzles.", "default": true},
	{"key": "seismic_hazards", "label": "[Hardcore] Seismic Hazards & Cave-Ins", "desc": "Mining deep rock triggers rockfalls from above.", "default": false},
	{"key": "uranium_gas", "label": "[Hardcore] Pressurized Uranium Gas Pockets", "desc": "Exposing uranium leaks explosive radioactive gas.", "default": false},
	{"key": "liquefaction", "label": "[Hardcore] Liquefaction Fluid Strata", "desc": "Explosions cause rock to flow downward like liquid sand.", "default": false},
	{"key": "debt_executioner", "[Hardcore] Corporate Debt Executioner", "desc": "Start with -$50M debt; Repo Drones hunt you if quotas fail.", "default": false}
]

func _ready() -> void:
	process_mode = Node.PROCESS_MODE_ALWAYS
	custom_minimum_size = Vector2(760, 480)

	var margin := MarginContainer.new()
	margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_left", 24)
	margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_right", 24)
	margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_top", 20)
	margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_bottom", 20)
	add_child(margin)

	var vbox := VBoxContainer.new()
	vbox.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 10)
	margin.add_child(vbox)

	var title := RichTextLabel.new()
	title.bbcode_enabled = true
	title.text = "[b][font_size=20]Select Active Mods & Mutators for this Run[/font_size][/b]\n[color=gray]Customize your active expansions and difficulty modifiers for this save profile.[/color]"
	title.fit_content = true
	title.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0, 48)
	vbox.add_child(title)

	var scroll := ScrollContainer.new()
	scroll.size_flags_vertical = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	vbox.add_child(scroll)

	var list := VBoxContainer.new()
	list.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	list.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 8)
	scroll.add_child(list)

	for mut in MUTATORS:
		var cb := CheckBox.new()
		cb.text = mut["label"] + "  —  " + mut["desc"]
		cb.button_pressed = mut["default"]
		active_toggles[mut["key"]] = mut["default"]
		var k: String = mut["key"]
		cb.toggled.connect(func(val: bool): active_toggles[k] = val)
		list.add_child(cb)

	var confirm_btn := Button.new()
	confirm_btn.text = "Confirm & Proceed to Level Select"
	confirm_btn.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0, 38)
	confirm_btn.pressed.connect(_on_confirm_pressed)
	vbox.add_child(confirm_btn)

func _on_confirm_pressed() -> void:
	var ssm = get_node_or_null("/root/SaveSlotManager")
	if ssm:
		var cfg := {"mods": active_toggles}
		var file := FileAccess.open(ssm.get_slot_config_path(ssm.current_slot), FileAccess.WRITE)
		if file:
			file.store_string(JSON.stringify(cfg, "\t"))
			file.close()
	confirmed.emit()
	queue_free()

class_name SaveSlotModal
extends Control

signal slot_chosen_play(slot_id: int)
signal slot_chosen_new(slot_id: int)
signal modal_closed()

var _grid: GridContainer

func _ready() -> void:
	process_mode = Node.PROCESS_MODE_ALWAYS
	var vp_rect := get_viewport_rect()
	position = Vector2.ZERO
	size = vp_rect.size
	custom_minimum_size = vp_rect.size
	mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_STOP
	z_index = 80

	var backdrop := ColorRect.new()
	backdrop.position = Vector2.ZERO
	backdrop.size = vp_rect.size
	backdrop.color = Color(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.85)
	backdrop.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_STOP
	add_child(backdrop)

	var center := CenterContainer.new()
	center.position = Vector2.ZERO
	center.size = vp_rect.size
	add_child(center)

	var panel := PanelContainer.new()
	panel.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(920, 560)
	center.add_child(panel)

	var margin := MarginContainer.new()
	margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_left", 24)
	margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_right", 24)
	margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_top", 20)
	margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_bottom", 20)
	panel.add_child(margin)

	var vbox := VBoxContainer.new()
	vbox.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 14)
	margin.add_child(vbox)

	var header := HBoxContainer.new()
	var title := RichTextLabel.new()
	title.bbcode_enabled = true
	title.text = "[b][font_size=22]Career Profiles (Multi-Save Slots)[/font_size][/b]"
	title.fit_content = true
	title.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	header.add_child(title)

	var close_btn := Button.new()
	close_btn.text = " Close (Esc) "
	close_btn.pressed.connect(_on_close)
	header.add_child(close_btn)
	vbox.add_child(header)

	var sep := HSeparator.new()
	vbox.add_child(sep)

	_grid = GridContainer.new()
	_grid.columns = 3
	_grid.add_theme_constant_override("h_separation", 16)
	_grid.add_theme_constant_override("v_separation", 16)
	_grid.size_flags_vertical = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	vbox.add_child(_grid)

	_populate_slots()

func _populate_slots() -> void:
	for child in _grid.get_children():
		child.queue_free()

	var ssm = get_node_or_null("/root/SaveSlotManager")
	for i in range(1, 7):
		var card := PanelContainer.new()
		card.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(270, 200)

		var c_margin := MarginContainer.new()
		c_margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_left", 14)
		c_margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_right", 14)
		c_margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_top", 12)
		c_margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_bottom", 12)
		card.add_child(c_margin)

		var c_vbox := VBoxContainer.new()
		c_vbox.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 8)
		c_margin.add_child(c_vbox)

		var slot_lbl := Label.new()
		slot_lbl.text = "PROFILE SLOT %d" % i
		slot_lbl.modulate = Color(1.0, 0.85, 0.25)
		c_vbox.add_child(slot_lbl)

		if ssm and ssm.has_save(i):
			var meta: Dictionary = ssm.get_slot_meta(i)
			var prof: String = meta.get("profession", "Internship")
			var day: int = int(meta.get("day", 1))
			var cash: float = float(meta.get("cash", 0.0))
			var time_str: String = meta.get("timestamp", "")

			var details := Label.new()
			details.text = "Class: %s\nDay: %d | Cash: $%s\nSaved: %s" % [prof, day, Gconsts.add_comma_to_float_no_dp(cash), time_str]
			details.autowrap_mode = TextServer.AUTOWRAP_WORD_SMART
			c_vbox.add_child(details)

			var spacer := Control.new()
			spacer.size_flags_vertical = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
			c_vbox.add_child(spacer)

			var btn_box := HBoxContainer.new()
			btn_box.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 8)
			c_vbox.add_child(btn_box)

			var resume_btn := Button.new()
			resume_btn.text = "Resume"
			resume_btn.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
			resume_btn.pressed.connect(func(): slot_chosen_play.emit(i))
			btn_box.add_child(resume_btn)

			var del_btn := Button.new()
			del_btn.text = "Delete"
			del_btn.modulate = Color(1.0, 0.35, 0.35)
			del_btn.pressed.connect(func():
				ssm.delete_slot(i)
				_populate_slots()
			)
			btn_box.add_child(del_btn)
		else:
			var empty_lbl := Label.new()
			empty_lbl.text = "\n[ EMPTY PROFILE ]\n\nReady for new game"
			empty_lbl.modulate = Color(0.6, 0.6, 0.6)
			c_vbox.add_child(empty_lbl)

			var spacer := Control.new()
			spacer.size_flags_vertical = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
			c_vbox.add_child(spacer)

			var new_btn := Button.new()
			new_btn.text = "+ Start New Run"
			new_btn.pressed.connect(func(): slot_chosen_new.emit(i))
			c_vbox.add_child(new_btn)

		_grid.add_child(card)

func _input(event: InputEvent) -> void:
	if event.is_action_pressed("ui_cancel") or event.is_action_pressed("menu"):
		_on_close()

func _on_close() -> void:
	modal_closed.emit()
	queue_free()

==================================================
FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-multisave_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-multisave_mod\scripts\save_slot_manager.gd
==================================================
--------
extends Node

const BASE_SAVES_DIR := "user://saves"
const VANILLA_SAVE_PATH := "user://v04_savegame.save"
const TOTAL_SLOTS := 6

var current_slot: int = 1
var _last_save_mtime: int = 0

func _ready() -> void:
	_ensure_directories()
	_migrate_vanilla_save_if_needed()
	restore_career_progression()

func _process(_delta: float) -> void:
	if FileAccess.file_exists(VANILLA_SAVE_PATH):
		var mtime := FileAccess.get_modified_time(VANILLA_SAVE_PATH)
		if mtime != _last_save_mtime:
			_last_save_mtime = mtime
			sync_active_slot_from_vanilla()

func _ensure_directories() -> void:
	if not DirAccess.dir_exists_absolute(BASE_SAVES_DIR):
		DirAccess.make_dir_recursive_absolute(BASE_SAVES_DIR)
	for i in range(1, TOTAL_SLOTS + 1):
		var slot_dir := "%s/slot_%d" % [BASE_SAVES_DIR, i]
		if not DirAccess.dir_exists_absolute(slot_dir):
			DirAccess.make_dir_recursive_absolute(slot_dir)

func _migrate_vanilla_save_if_needed() -> void:
	var slot_1_save := "%s/slot_1/v04_savegame.save" % BASE_SAVES_DIR
	if FileAccess.file_exists(VANILLA_SAVE_PATH) and not FileAccess.file_exists(slot_1_save):
		var src := FileAccess.open(VANILLA_SAVE_PATH, FileAccess.READ)
		if src:
			var content := src.get_as_text()
			src.close()
			var dst := FileAccess.open(slot_1_save, FileAccess.WRITE)
			if dst:
				dst.store_string(content)
				dst.close()
			_generate_slot_meta(1)

func restore_career_progression() -> void:
	if not Gvars:
		return
	if "profession_manager" in Gvars and Gvars.profession_manager:
		var pm = Gvars.profession_manager
		for p in pm.all_professions.keys():
			pm.all_professions[p]["is_unlocked"] = true
	if "level_manager" in Gvars and Gvars.level_manager:
		var lm = Gvars.level_manager
		for l in lm.all_levels.keys():
			lm.all_levels[l]["is_unlocked"] = true
	if Gvars.has_method("load_hard_modifier_records"):
		Gvars.load_hard_modifier_records()
	if Gvars.has_method("save_globals"):
		Gvars.save_globals()
	ModLoaderLog.info("Career progression and hard modifier badges verified.", "gareth-multisave_mod")

func get_slot_save_path(slot_id: int) -> String:
	return "%s/slot_%d/v04_savegame.save" % [BASE_SAVES_DIR, slot_id]

func get_slot_meta_path(slot_id: int) -> String:
	return "%s/slot_%d/meta.json" % [BASE_SAVES_DIR, slot_id]

func get_slot_config_path(slot_id: int) -> String:
	return "%s/slot_%d/run_config.json" % [BASE_SAVES_DIR, slot_id]

func has_save(slot_id: int) -> bool:
	return FileAccess.file_exists(get_slot_save_path(slot_id))

func get_slot_meta(slot_id: int) -> Dictionary:
	var path := get_slot_meta_path(slot_id)
	if FileAccess.file_exists(path):
		var file := FileAccess.open(path, FileAccess.READ)
		if file:
			var parsed = JSON.parse_string(file.get_as_text())
			if parsed is Dictionary:
				return parsed
	return {}

func sync_active_slot_from_vanilla() -> void:
	if not FileAccess.file_exists(VANILLA_SAVE_PATH):
		return
	var src := FileAccess.open(VANILLA_SAVE_PATH, FileAccess.READ)
	if not src:
		return
	var content := src.get_as_text()
	src.close()

	var dst := FileAccess.open(get_slot_save_path(current_slot), FileAccess.WRITE)
	if dst:
		dst.store_string(content)
		dst.close()

	_generate_slot_meta(current_slot)

func prepare_vanilla_from_active_slot() -> void:
	var slot_path := get_slot_save_path(current_slot)
	if FileAccess.file_exists(slot_path):
		var src := FileAccess.open(slot_path, FileAccess.READ)
		if src:
			var content := src.get_as_text()
			src.close()
			var dst := FileAccess.open(VANILLA_SAVE_PATH, FileAccess.WRITE)
			if dst:
				dst.store_string(content)
				dst.close()

func delete_slot(slot_id: int) -> void:
	var s_path := get_slot_save_path(slot_id)
	var m_path := get_slot_meta_path(slot_id)
	var c_path := get_slot_config_path(slot_id)
	if FileAccess.file_exists(s_path):
		DirAccess.remove_absolute(s_path)
	if FileAccess.file_exists(m_path):
		DirAccess.remove_absolute(m_path)
	if FileAccess.file_exists(c_path):
		DirAccess.remove_absolute(c_path)
	if slot_id == current_slot and FileAccess.file_exists(VANILLA_SAVE_PATH):
		DirAccess.remove_absolute(VANILLA_SAVE_PATH)

func is_mod_active_for_run(mod_key: String) -> bool:
	var path := get_slot_config_path(current_slot)
	if FileAccess.file_exists(path):
		var file := FileAccess.open(path, FileAccess.READ)
		if file:
			var parsed = JSON.parse_string(file.get_as_text())
			if parsed is Dictionary and parsed.has("mods"):
				return bool(parsed["mods"].get(mod_key, true))
	return true

func _generate_slot_meta(slot_id: int) -> void:
	var prof_name := "Internship"
	if Gvars and ("profession" in Gvars) and Gvars.profession:
		if "profession_name" in Gvars.profession:
			prof_name = str(Gvars.profession.profession_name)

	var day_num: int = int(Gvars.dayCount) if (Gvars and "dayCount" in Gvars) else 1
	var cash_val: float = float(Gvars.CashCount) if (Gvars and "CashCount" in Gvars) else 0.0

	var meta_data := {
		"profession": prof_name,
		"day": day_num,
		"cash": cash_val,
		"timestamp": Time.get_datetime_string_from_system(false, true)
	}

	var file := FileAccess.open(get_slot_meta_path(slot_id), FileAccess.WRITE)
	if file:
		file.store_string(JSON.stringify(meta_data, "\t"))
		file.close()
func set_active_slot_mods(cfg: Dictionary) -> void:
	var slot_id = current_slot
	var slot_cfg_path = "user://profile_mods_slot_%d.json" % slot_id
	var f = FileAccess.open(slot_cfg_path, FileAccess.WRITE)
	if f:
		f.store_string(JSON.stringify(cfg, "\t"))

# Neutron Bomb Subatomic Core

**Namespace:** $namespace  
**Compatibility:** Coal LLC v1.4.1.1+ (ModLoader 7.0.1)

---

## Overview
Designed for deep bedrock penetration, the **Neutron Bomb** concentrates its blast into a tight **16-tile core** while packing **9,999,999,999 kinetic damage**.

This intense subatomic yield instantly shatters dense obsidian and bedrock layers that resist conventional explosives. It is available in the equipment shop adjacent to the Hydrogen Bomb for ,000,000.

---

## Technical Details (For Modders)
Hooks Bus.BombExplode to dispatch a high-density, low-radius destruction shockwave capable of breaking tiles regardless of base hardness.

---

## Installation
1. Ensure **Coal LLC** has Godot ModLoader 7.0.1 installed.
2. Place the packaged .zip file into your Coal LLC/mods/ directory.
3. Manage or toggle this mod directly via the in-game **Mod Manager**.
--------

extends Node

const MOD_DIR := "gareth-neutron_bomb_mod"

func _ready() -> void:
	var item_unlocks = load("res://resources/unlockable_items/scripts/item_unlocks.tres")
	if not item_unlocks:
		return

	var tiers = [
		"01_shoddy", "02_copper", "03_iron", "04_sapphire", "05_emerald",
		"06_silver", "07_amethyst", "08_gold", "09_ruby", "10_diamond",
		"11_pinkdiamond", "12_spinel", "13_uranium", "14_moonstone", "15_onyx"
	]

	for t in tiers:
		var item_path := "res://mods-unpacked/gareth-neutron_bomb_mod/items/neutron_bomb_%s.tres" % t
		if ResourceLoader.exists(item_path):
			var item = load(item_path)
			var tex_path := "res://mods-unpacked/gareth-neutron_bomb_mod/textures/neutron_%s.png" % t
			if FileAccess.file_exists(tex_path):
				var img = Image.load_from_file(tex_path)
				if img:
					item.itemTexture = ImageTexture.create_from_image(img)
			if item and not (item in item_unlocks.all_equipment_items):
				item_unlocks.all_equipment_items.append(item)
			if item and not (item in item_unlocks.all_items):
				item_unlocks.all_items.append(item)

	ModLoaderLog.info("15 Neutron Bombs registered with color-coded canisters.", MOD_DIR)

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-plasma_raygun_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-plasma_raygun_mod\extensions\scripts\StateMachine\Player2\player_2.hooks.gd

extends Object

var _checked_active: bool = false
var _is_active: bool = true

func _check_mod_status() -> bool:
	if _checked_active:
		return _is_active
	_checked_active = true

	var ssm = Engine.get_main_loop().root.get_node_or_null("/root/SaveSlotManager")
	if ssm and ssm.has_method("is_mod_active_for_run"):
		_is_active = ssm.is_mod_active_for_run("plasma_raygun")
		return _is_active

	var reg_paths = [
		"res://mods-unpacked/gareth-mod_manager_mod/scripts/mod_registry.gd",
		"res://mods-unpacked/gareth-mod_manager_mod/mods-unpacked/gareth-mod_manager_mod/scripts/mod_registry.gd"
	]
	for p in reg_paths:
		if ResourceLoader.exists(p):
			var reg = load(p)
			if reg and reg.has_method("is_mod_active_for_run"):
				_is_active = reg.is_mod_active_for_run("plasma_raygun")
				return _is_active

	_is_active = true
	return _is_active

func _physics_process(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, delta: float) -> void:
	if not _check_mod_status():
		chain.execute_next([delta])
		return

	chain.execute_next([delta])

# Prospector's Acoustic Beacon

**Namespace:** $namespace  
**Compatibility:** Coal LLC v1.4.1.1+ (ModLoader 7.0.1)

---

## Overview
Emits periodic sonar sweeps centered on the player, pinging coordinates of high-value ore veins through solid rock.

## Technical Details (For Modders)

---

## Installation
1. Ensure **Coal LLC** has Godot ModLoader 7.0.1 installed.
2. Place the packaged .zip file into your Coal LLC/mods/ directory.
3. Manage or toggle this mod directly via the in-game **Mod Manager**.

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-prospectors_beacon_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-prospectors_beacon_mod\extensions\scenes\tilemaps\tile_map_manager.hooks.gd
==================================================
--------
extends Object

const LOG_NAME := "gareth-prospectors_beacon_mod:Hook"
static var last_pulse: float = 0.0


func apply_tile_effects(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	chain.execute_next()

	var now: float = Time.get_ticks_msec() / 1000.0
	if now - last_pulse > 4.5:
		last_pulse = now
		var tilemap := chain.reference_object as TileMapManager
		if not tilemap:
			return
		var players = tilemap.get_tree().get_nodes_in_group("player")
		if players.is_empty():
			return
		var player_pos: Vector2 = players[0].global_position
		ModLoaderLog.info("[Prospector Beacon] Acoustic pulse pinging near %s" % str(player_pos), LOG_NAME)
		var ping_offset := Vector2(randf_range(-64, 64), randf_range(16, 80))
		Bus.DamageTile.emit(player_pos + ping_offset, 25.0)

==================================================
FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-prospectors_beacon_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-prospectors_beacon_mod\extensions\scripts\StateMachine\Player2\player_2.hooks.gd
==================================================
--------
extends Object

const LOG_NAME := "gareth-prospectors_beacon_mod:Hook"
static var last_pulse: float = 0.0


func _physics_process(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, delta: float) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([delta])

	var player := chain.reference_object as Player
	if not player or not player.is_inside_tree():
		return

	var now: float = Time.get_ticks_msec() / 1000.0
	if (now - last_pulse) > 4.5:
		last_pulse = now
		var player_pos: Vector2 = player.global_position
		ModLoaderLog.info("[Prospector Beacon] Acoustic pulse pinging near %s" % str(player_pos), LOG_NAME)
		var ping_offset := Vector2(randf_range(-64, 64), randf_range(16, 80))
		Bus.DamageTile.emit(player_pos + ping_offset, 25.0)

const MOD_ID := "gareth-reach_expansion_mod"

func _init() -> void:
	var unpacked_dir = ModLoaderMod.get_unpacked_dir().path_join(MOD_ID)
	var ext_dir = unpacked_dir.path_join("extensions")

	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks(
		"res://scripts/Stockpile.gd",
		ext_dir.path_join("scripts/Stockpile.hooks.gd")
	)
	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks(
		"res://scenes/NPCs/quest_scene_manager.gd",
		ext_dir.path_join("scenes/NPCs/quest_scene_manager.hooks.gd")
	)
	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks(
		"res://scenes/Stages/management_screen.gd",
		ext_dir.path_join("scenes/Stages/management_screen.hooks.gd")
	)
	ModLoaderLog.info("Expanded Interaction Reach online!", MOD_ID)

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-reach_expansion_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-reach_expansion_mod\extensions\scenes\NPCs\quest_scene_manager.hooks.gd
==================================================

extends Object

func _ready(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([])
	var qm = chain.reference_object
	if not is_instance_valid(qm): return

	var extra_reach = 0.0
	if Gvars and ("passives" in Gvars) and Gvars.passives and ("player_reach_bonus" in Gvars.passives):
		extra_reach = float(Gvars.passives.player_reach_bonus) * 24.0

	if extra_reach > 0.0:
		for qp in qm.find_children("*", "Area2D", true, false):
			var col = qp.find_child("CollisionShape2D", true, false)
			if is_instance_valid(col) and col.shape and ("radius" in col.shape):
				col.shape.radius += extra_reach

extends Object

func load_bonus_shop(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([])
	var mgmt = chain.reference_object
	if not is_instance_valid(mgmt) or not Gvars: return

	if ("passives" in Gvars) and Gvars.passives:
		if not Gvars.passives.has_meta("player_reach_bonus"):
			Gvars.passives.set_meta("player_reach_bonus", 0.0)



--------
==================================================
FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-reach_expansion_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-reach_expansion_mod\extensions\scripts\Stockpile.hooks.gd
==================================================
--------
extends Object

func _process(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, delta: float) -> void:
	var sp = chain.reference_object
	if is_instance_valid(sp):
		var tree = sp.get_tree()
		if tree and tree.root:
			var player = tree.root.find_child("Player", true, false)
			if is_instance_valid(player) and ("global_position" in player) and ("global_position" in sp):
				var extra_reach = 0.0
				
				if Gvars and ("passives" in Gvars) and Gvars.passives:
					if Gvars.passives.has_meta("player_reach_bonus"):
						extra_reach = float(Gvars.passives.get_meta("player_reach_bonus")) * 24.0
						
				if player.global_position.distance_to(sp.global_position) <= (64.0 + extra_reach):
					if "interactable" in sp:
						sp.interactable = true
						
	chain.execute_next([delta])

const MOD_DIR := "gareth-scrap_and_delivery_mod"

func _init() -> void:
	var unpacked_dir = ModLoaderMod.get_unpacked_dir().path_join(MOD_DIR)
	var ext_dir = unpacked_dir.path_join("extensions")

	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks(
		"res://scenes/Interfaces/InventoryWindow.gd",
		ext_dir.path_join("scenes/Interfaces/InventoryWindow.hooks.gd")
	)

func _ready() -> void:
	var mgr_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-scrap_and_delivery_mod/scripts/amazon_delivery_manager.gd")
	var mgr = Node.new()
	mgr.name = "AmazonDeliveryManager"
	mgr.set_script(mgr_script)
	get_tree().root.call_deferred("add_child", mgr)
	ModLoaderLog.info("Daytime Scrap & Amazon Delivery Mod active! (Hotkey B for Amazon, 50% Scrap enabled)", MOD_DIR)

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-scrap_and_delivery_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-scrap_and_delivery_mod\extensions\scenes\Interfaces\InventoryWindow.hooks.gd

extends Object

func load_inventory(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([])
	var grid = chain.reference_object
	if is_instance_valid(grid):
		_inject_scrap_bar(grid)

func _inject_scrap_bar(grid: GridContainer) -> void:
	# Hide buttons if we are in the between-day store (ManagementScreen / TabContainer)
	var scene = grid.get_tree().current_scene
	if scene and (scene.name == "ManagementScreen" or scene.name == "management_screen"):
		return
	if grid.find_parent("ManagementScreen") or grid.find_parent("TabContainer"):
		return

	var parent = grid.get_parent()
	if not parent or parent.has_node("DaytimeScrapBar"):
		return

	var bar = HBoxContainer.new()
	bar.name = "DaytimeScrapBar"
	bar.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 10)

	var btn_scrap_ores = Button.new()
	btn_scrap_ores.text = "♻️ Scrap All Ores (50% Cash)"
	btn_scrap_ores.tooltip_text = "Sell all raw ores from your inventory immediately for 50% value straight to your wallet."
	btn_scrap_ores.modulate = Color(0.4, 1.0, 0.6, 1.0)
	btn_scrap_ores.pressed.connect(func(): _scrap_all_ores(grid))
	bar.add_child(btn_scrap_ores)

	var btn_open_amazon = Button.new()
	btn_open_amazon.text = "📦 Open Amazon Store (B)"
	btn_open_amazon.tooltip_text = "Order emergency platforms, ladders, and tools delivered by parachute in 10 seconds."
	btn_open_amazon.modulate = Color(1.0, 0.85, 0.3, 1.0)
	btn_open_amazon.pressed.connect(func():
		var tree = grid.get_tree()
		if tree and tree.root:
			var mgr = tree.root.find_child("AmazonDeliveryManager", true, false)
			if mgr and mgr.has_method("open_store"):
				mgr.open_store()
	)
	bar.add_child(btn_open_amazon)

	parent.add_child(bar)
	parent.move_child(bar, grid.get_index())

func _scrap_all_ores(grid: GridContainer) -> void:
	if not Gvars or not ("inventory" in grid) or not grid.inventory:
		return

	var total_scrapped_cash: float = 0.0
	var inv: Inventory = grid.inventory
	var ore_rates = {
		"Coal": 1.0,
		"Copper": 2.5,
		"Iron": 5.0,
		"Silver": 12.0,
		"Gold": 25.0,
		"Amethyst": 45.0,
		"Sapphire": 80.0,
		"Emerald": 130.0,
		"Ruby": 200.0,
		"Diamond": 350.0
	}

	for slot in inv.items:
		if slot and slot.item and slot.count > 0:
			var iname = str(slot.item.itemName)
			if ore_rates.has(iname):
				var cash_gain = float(slot.count) * ore_rates[iname] * 0.5
				total_scrapped_cash += cash_gain
				slot.count = 0.0
				var empty_res = load("res://resources/Items/Empty.tres")
				if empty_res:
					slot.set("item", empty_res)

	if total_scrapped_cash > 0.0:
		Gvars.CashCount += total_scrapped_cash
		Bus.inventory_changed.emit()

var canvas: CanvasLayer
var hud_btn: Button
var store_window: PanelContainer

var sort_dropdown: OptionButton

var pending_orders: Array = []
var all_shop_items: Array = []

func _ready() -> void:
	process_mode = Node.PROCESS_MODE_ALWAYS
	_load_catalog()
	_build_ui()

func _load_catalog() -> void:
	all_shop_items = []
	var item_unlocks = load("res://resources/unlockable_items/scripts/item_unlocks.tres")
	if item_unlocks and ("all_equipment_items" in item_unlocks):
		for it in item_unlocks.all_equipment_items:
			if is_instance_valid(it):
				all_shop_items.append(it)

	var extra_paths = [
		"res://resources/Items/BonusEffects/add_inventory_slots_1.tres",
		"res://resources/Items/BonusEffects/add_inventory_slots_2.tres",
		"res://resources/Items/BonusEffects/add_inventory_slots_3.tres",
		"res://mods-unpacked/gareth-more_inventory_slots_mod/items/add_inventory_slots_4.tres",
		"res://mods-unpacked/gareth-more_inventory_slots_mod/items/add_inventory_slots_5.tres",
		"res://mods-unpacked/gareth-more_inventory_slots_mod/items/add_inventory_slots_6.tres",
		"res://mods-unpacked/gareth-more_inventory_slots_mod/items/add_inventory_slots_10.tres",
		"res://resources/Items/BonusEffects/add_inventory_stacks_1.tres",
		"res://resources/Items/BonusEffects/add_inventory_stacks_2.tres",
		"res://resources/Items/BonusEffects/add_inventory_stacks_3.tres",
		"res://resources/Items/BonusEffects/bonus_equipment/vacuum_cleaner_1.tres",
		"res://resources/Items/BonusEffects/bonus_equipment/vacuum_cleaner_2.tres",
		"res://resources/Items/BonusEffects/bonus_equipment/vacuum_cleaner_3.tres",
		"res://mods-unpacked/gareth-buyable_vacuums_mod/items/vacuum_cleaner_5.tres",
		"res://mods-unpacked/gareth-buyable_vacuums_mod/items/vacuum_cleaner_8.tres"
	]
	for ep in extra_paths:
		if ResourceLoader.exists(ep):
			var res = load(ep)
			if res and not res in all_shop_items:
				all_shop_items.append(res)

func _build_ui() -> void:
	canvas = CanvasLayer.new()

	hud_btn.anchor_top = 1.0
	hud_btn.anchor_bottom = 1.0
	hud_btn.offset_left = -160.0
	hud_btn.offset_right = -10.0
	hud_btn.offset_top = -72.0
	hud_btn.offset_bottom = -46.0
	hud_btn.modulate = Color(1.0, 0.85, 0.3, 1.0)

	hud_btn.pressed.connect(toggle_store)
	canvas.add_child(hud_btn)

	queue_label.anchor_left = 0.5
	queue_label.anchor_right = 0.5

	queue_label.offset_right = 250.0
	queue_label.offset_top = 60.0
	queue_label.offset_bottom = 90.0
	queue_label.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	queue_label.add_theme_color_override("font_color", Color(1.0, 0.9, 0.2, 1.0))
	queue_label.visible = false
	canvas.add_child(queue_label)

	toast_label = Label.new()
	toast_label.anchor_left = 0.5
	toast_label.anchor_right = 0.5
	toast_label.offset_left = -250.0
	toast_label.offset_right = 250.0
	toast_label.offset_top = 92.0
	toast_label.offset_bottom = 122.0
	toast_label.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	toast_label.add_theme_color_override("font_color", Color(0.4, 1.0, 0.5, 1.0))
	toast_label.visible = false
	canvas.add_child(toast_label)

	store_window = PanelContainer.new()
	store_window.name = "AmazonStoreWindow"
	store_window.anchor_left = 0.5
	store_window.anchor_right = 0.5
	store_window.anchor_top = 0.5
	store_window.anchor_bottom = 0.5
	store_window.offset_left = -420.0
	store_window.offset_right = 420.0
	store_window.offset_top = -280.0
	store_window.offset_bottom = 280.0
	store_window.visible = false

	var v_box = VBoxContainer.new()
	v_box.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 8)

	var header = HBoxContainer.new()
	var title = Label.new()
	title.text = "📦 AMAZON SAME-DAY EMERGENCY DELIVERY"
	title.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	title.add_theme_color_override("font_color", Color(1.0, 0.85, 0.2, 1.0))
	header.add_child(title)

	var btn_close = Button.new()
	btn_close.text = "✖"
	btn_close.pressed.connect(func(): store_window.visible = false)
	header.add_child(btn_close)
	v_box.add_child(header)

	var sub_bar = HBoxContainer.new()
	cash_label = Label.new()
	cash_label.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	sub_bar.add_child(cash_label)

	var btn_scrap_all = Button.new()
	btn_scrap_all.text = "♻️ Scrap All Ores (50%)"
	btn_scrap_all.modulate = Color(0.4, 1.0, 0.6, 1.0)
	btn_scrap_all.pressed.connect(_quick_scrap_from_shop)
	sub_bar.add_child(btn_scrap_all)

	var btn_scrap_gear = Button.new()
	btn_scrap_gear.text = "♻️ Scrap Unwanted Gear (50%)"
	btn_scrap_gear.modulate = Color(0.9, 0.6, 0.3, 1.0)
	btn_scrap_gear.tooltip_text = "Sell redundant weapons, platforms, and bombs from inventory for 50% refund."
	btn_scrap_gear.pressed.connect(_scrap_redundant_gear)
	sub_bar.add_child(btn_scrap_gear)
	v_box.add_child(sub_bar)

	var filter_bar = HBoxContainer.new()
	search_box = LineEdit.new()

	search_box.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	search_box.clear_button_enabled = true
	search_box.text_changed.connect(func(_t): _render_catalog())
	filter_bar.add_child(search_box)

	sort_dropdown = OptionButton.new()
	sort_dropdown.add_item("Sort: Default", 0)
	sort_dropdown.add_item("Price: Low to High", 1)
	sort_dropdown.add_item("Price: High to Low", 2)
	sort_dropdown.add_item("Name: A to Z", 3)
	sort_dropdown.item_selected.connect(func(_idx): _render_catalog())
	filter_bar.add_child(sort_dropdown)

	chk_affordable = CheckBox.new()
	chk_affordable.text = "Affordable Only"
	chk_affordable.toggled.connect(func(_t): _render_catalog())
	filter_bar.add_child(chk_affordable)
	v_box.add_child(filter_bar)

	var scroll = ScrollContainer.new()
	scroll.size_flags_vertical = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	scroll.horizontal_scroll_mode = ScrollContainer.SCROLL_MODE_DISABLED

	item_grid = GridContainer.new()
	item_grid.columns = 3
	item_grid.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	item_grid.add_theme_constant_override("h_separation", 10)
	item_grid.add_theme_constant_override("v_separation", 10)
	scroll.add_child(item_grid)
	v_box.add_child(scroll)

	store_window.add_child(v_box)
	canvas.add_child(store_window)

func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	var tree = get_tree()
	var in_mining_shift := false
	var player_char = null
	if tree and tree.root:
		var players = tree.get_nodes_in_group("player")
		if players.size() > 0 and players[0] is Node2D:
			player_char = players[0]
		else:
			var candidate = tree.root.find_child("Player", true, false)
			if candidate is Node2D:
				player_char = candidate

		var tm = tree.root.find_child("TileMapManager", true, false)
		var pause = tree.root.find_child("PauseMenu", true, false)
		in_mining_shift = is_instance_valid(player_char) and is_instance_valid(tm) and (not is_instance_valid(pause) or not pause.visible)

	if hud_btn:
		hud_btn.visible = in_mining_shift

	if not in_mining_shift and store_window and store_window.visible:
		store_window.visible = false

	if is_instance_valid(cash_label) and Gvars:
		if is_nan(Gvars.CashCount):
			Gvars.CashCount = 0.0
		cash_label.text = "Wallet: $%.2f" % (Gvars.CashCount / 100.0)

	_update_orders(delta, player_char)

func _unhandled_input(event: InputEvent) -> void:
	if event is InputEventKey and event.pressed and not event.echo:
		if event.keycode == KEY_B and hud_btn and hud_btn.visible:
			toggle_store()

func toggle_store() -> void:
	if not store_window:
		return
	store_window.visible = not store_window.visible
	if store_window.visible:
		if all_shop_items.is_empty():
			_load_catalog()
		_render_catalog()

func _render_catalog() -> void:
	if not item_grid:
		return
	for c in item_grid.get_children():
		c.queue_free()

	var q: String = search_box.text.strip_edges().to_lower()
	var only_afford: bool = chk_affordable.button_pressed
	var cur_cash: float = Gvars.CashCount if Gvars else 0.0

	var filtered: Array = []
	for it in all_shop_items:
		if not is_instance_valid(it):
			continue
		var nm: String = str(it.itemName)
		var id_str: String = str(it.itemID).to_lower()
		var desc: String = str(it.itemDescription).to_lower()
		if not q.is_empty() and (not q in nm.to_lower() and not q in id_str and not q in desc):
			continue
		if only_afford and it.itemBuyPrice > cur_cash and it.itemBuyPrice > 0.0:
			continue
		filtered.append(it)

	var sort_mode = sort_dropdown.selected
	if sort_mode == 1:
		filtered.sort_custom(func(a, b): return a.itemBuyPrice < b.itemBuyPrice)
	elif sort_mode == 2:
		filtered.sort_custom(func(a, b): return a.itemBuyPrice > b.itemBuyPrice)
	elif sort_mode == 3:
		filtered.sort_custom(func(a, b): return str(a.itemName).nocasecmp_to(str(b.itemName)) < 0)

	for it in filtered:
		var card = PanelContainer.new()
		card.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(250, 68)

		var hbox = HBoxContainer.new()
		hbox.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 6)

		var tex = TextureRect.new()
		tex.texture = it.itemTexture
		tex.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(36, 36)
		tex.stretch_mode = TextureRect.STRETCH_KEEP_ASPECT_CENTERED
		hbox.add_child(tex)

		var info_box = VBoxContainer.new()
		info_box.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL

		var name_lbl = Label.new()
		name_lbl.text = it.itemName
		name_lbl.autowrap_mode = TextServer.AUTOWRAP_WORD_SMART
		info_box.add_child(name_lbl)

		var price_val: float = it.itemBuyPrice
		var price_lbl = Label.new()
		price_lbl.text = "$%.2f" % (price_val / 100.0)
		price_lbl.add_theme_color_override("font_color", Color(1.0, 0.85, 0.2, 1.0))
		info_box.add_child(price_lbl)
		hbox.add_child(info_box)

		var btn_order = Button.new()
		btn_order.text = "Order\n(10s)"
		btn_order.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(56, 40)
		var can_afford = (cur_cash >= price_val or price_val <= 0.0)
		btn_order.disabled = not can_afford
		btn_order.pressed.connect(func(): _place_order(it))
		hbox.add_child(btn_order)

		card.add_child(hbox)
		item_grid.add_child(card)

func _place_order(it: Item) -> void:
	if not Gvars or (Gvars.CashCount < it.itemBuyPrice and it.itemBuyPrice > 0.0):
		show_toast("❌ Insufficient funds in wallet!")
		return

	if it.itemBuyPrice > 0.0:
		Gvars.CashCount -= it.itemBuyPrice
	pending_orders.append({ "item": it, "time_left": 10.0 })
	show_toast("📦 Order placed for %s! Parachute delivery in 10s." % it.itemName)
	_render_catalog()

func _update_orders(delta: float, player_node) -> void:
	if pending_orders.is_empty():
		if queue_label and queue_label.visible:
			queue_label.visible = false
		return

	queue_label.visible = true
	var min_time: float = 999.0
	var current_item_name: String = ""

	var i = pending_orders.size() - 1
	while i >= 0:
		var order = pending_orders[i]
		order["time_left"] -= delta
		if order["time_left"] < min_time:
			min_time = order["time_left"]
			current_item_name = order["item"].itemName

		if order["time_left"] <= 0.0:
			_spawn_parachute_crate(order["item"], player_node)
			pending_orders.remove_at(i)
		i -= 1

	if is_instance_valid(queue_label):
		queue_label.text = "📦 Amazon Drone en route: [%s] arriving in %.1fs" % [current_item_name, max(0.0, min_time)]

func _spawn_parachute_crate(item: Item, player_node) -> void:
	var tree = get_tree()
	if not tree or not tree.root:
		return

	var p = player_node if is_instance_valid(player_node) else tree.root.find_child("Player", true, false)
	if not is_instance_valid(p):
		return

	var crate = Node2D.new()
	crate.name = "AmazonDeliveryCrate"
	crate.z_index = 60
	crate.global_position = Vector2(p.global_position.x, p.global_position.y - 320.0)

	var spr = Sprite2D.new()
	spr.texture = item.itemTexture if is_instance_valid(item.itemTexture) else load("res://assets/Equipment/kicks/kick_04_sapphire.png")
	spr.scale = Vector2(2.0, 2.0)
	crate.add_child(spr)

	p.get_parent().add_child(crate)

	var target_y = p.global_position.y - 12.0
	var tween = tree.create_tween()
	tween.tween_property(crate, "global_position:y", target_y, 1.2).set_trans(Tween.TRANS_QUAD).set_ease(Tween.EASE_OUT)
	tween.tween_callback(func():
		if item.itemBuyEffect:
			# Prevent double-charge cash checks from failing inside buy_effect
			var original_price = 0.0
			if "itemBuyPrice" in item:
				original_price = item.itemBuyPrice
				item.itemBuyPrice = 0.0
			
			item.itemBuyEffect.buy_effect(item, 1)
			
			if "itemBuyPrice" in item:
				item.itemBuyPrice = original_price

			# Directly update player inventory capacity if defined on the effect
			var inv: Inventory = load("res://resources/Inventories/PlayerInventory.tres")
			if inv:
				if "max_stack_to_add" in item.itemBuyEffect:
					inv.maxStack += int(item.itemBuyEffect.max_stack_to_add)
					inv.initialise_slots()
				if "slots_to_add" in item.itemBuyEffect:
					inv.inventorySize += int(item.itemBuyEffect.slots_to_add)
					inv.initialise_slots()
		else:
			var inv: Inventory = load("res://resources/Inventories/PlayerInventory.tres")
			if inv:
				inv.addToInventory(item, 1)
		Bus.inventory_changed.emit()
		show_toast("📦 DELIVERED! [%s] upgraded/received." % item.itemName)
		crate.queue_free()
	)

func _quick_scrap_from_shop() -> void:
	var inv: Inventory = load("res://resources/Inventories/PlayerInventory.tres")
	if not inv or not Gvars:
		return
	var total_cash: float = 0.0
	var count_cleared: int = 0
	var empty_res = load("res://resources/Items/Empty.tres")

	for i in range(inv.items.size()):
		var slot_item = inv.items[i]
		if not is_instance_valid(slot_item) or not is_instance_valid(slot_item.item) or slot_item.count <= 0:
			continue
		var it = slot_item.item
		var type_str = str(it.itemType).to_lower()
		var id_str = str(it.itemID).to_lower()
		var is_ore = type_str in ["ore", "resource", "mineral", "material"] or "coal" in id_str or "copper" in id_str or "iron" in id_str
		if is_ore and type_str != "equipment" and type_str != "weapon":
			var val = it.itemSellPrice * 0.5 if it.itemSellPrice > 0.0 else 5.0
			total_cash += val * slot_item.count
			count_cleared += int(slot_item.count)
			slot_item.count = 0
			if empty_res:
				slot_item.item = empty_res

	if total_cash > 0.0:
		if is_nan(Gvars.CashCount):
			Gvars.CashCount = 0.0
		Gvars.CashCount += total_cash
		if inv.has_signal("inventory_changed"):
			inv.emit_signal("inventory_changed")
		Bus.inventory_changed.emit()
		show_toast("♻️ Scrapped %d ores for +$%.2f cash!" % [count_cleared, total_cash / 100.0])
		_render_catalog()

func _scrap_redundant_gear() -> void:
	var inv: Inventory = load("res://resources/Inventories/PlayerInventory.tres")
	if not inv or not Gvars:
		return
	var total_refund: float = 0.0
	var gear_count: int = 0
	var empty_res = load("res://resources/Items/Empty.tres")
	for slot_item in inv.items:
		if not is_instance_valid(slot_item) or not is_instance_valid(slot_item.item) or slot_item.count <= 0:
			continue
		var it = slot_item.item
		var type_str = str(it.itemType).to_lower()
		if type_str in ["equipment", "weapon", "sword", "gun", "bomb", "turret"]:
			var refund_val = it.itemBuyPrice * 0.5 if it.itemBuyPrice > 0.0 else (it.itemSellPrice * 0.5)
			if refund_val > 0.0:
				total_refund += refund_val * slot_item.count
				gear_count += int(slot_item.count)
				slot_item.count = 0
				if empty_res:
					slot_item.item = empty_res

	if total_refund > 0.0:
		if is_nan(Gvars.CashCount):
			Gvars.CashCount = 0.0
		Gvars.CashCount += total_refund
		Bus.inventory_changed.emit()
		show_toast("♻️ Scrapped %d gear items for +$%.2f cash refund!" % [gear_count, total_refund / 100.0])
		_render_catalog()
	else:
		show_toast("ℹ️ No scrapable gear found in inventory.")

func show_toast(msg: String) -> void:
	if is_instance_valid(toast_label):
		toast_label.text = msg
		toast_label.visible = true
		var tree = get_tree()
		if tree:
			var tw = tree.create_tween()
			tw.tween_interval(3.0)
			tw.tween_callback(func():
				if is_instance_valid(toast_label):
					toast_label.visible = false
			)

# Seismic Hazards & Cave-Ins

**Namespace:** $namespace  
**Compatibility:** Coal LLC v1.4.1.1+ (ModLoader 7.0.1)

---

## Overview
A hardcore mutator where rapid deep excavation causes tectonic instability, triggering ceiling collapses and falling rubble.

---

## Technical Details (For Modders)
Hooks destroy_tile in tile_map_chunk.gd to track consecutive breaks and spawn falling rock physics entities from above.

---

## Installation
1. Ensure **Coal LLC** has Godot ModLoader 7.0.1 installed.
2. Place the packaged .zip file into your Coal LLC/mods/ directory.
3. Manage or toggle this mod directly via the in-game **Mod Manager**.

const MOD_DIR := "gareth-seismic_hazards_mod"
const LOG_NAME := "gareth-seismic_hazards_mod:Main"


func _init() -> void:
	var mod_dir_path: String = ModLoaderMod.get_unpacked_dir().path_join(MOD_DIR)
	var extensions_dir_path: String = mod_dir_path.path_join("extensions")
	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks(
		"res://scenes/tilemaps/tile_map_chunk.gd",
		extensions_dir_path.path_join("scenes/tilemaps/tile_map_chunk.hooks.gd")
	)
	ModLoaderLog.info("Hooks successfully installed.", LOG_NAME)


func _ready() -> void:
	ModLoaderLog.info("Ready and active in scene!", LOG_NAME)

# Seismic Hazards & Cave-Ins

**Namespace:** $namespace  
**Compatibility:** Coal LLC v1.4.1.1+ (ModLoader 7.0.1)

---

## Overview
A hardcore mutator where rapid deep excavation causes tectonic instability, triggering ceiling collapses and falling rubble.

---

---

extends Object

func destroy_tile(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, idx: int, tile_properties: Dictionary) -> void:
	var reg = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-mod_manager_mod/scripts/mod_registry.gd")
	if reg and not reg.is_mod_active_for_run("seismic_hazards"):
		chain.execute_next([idx, tile_properties])
		return

	chain.execute_next([idx, tile_properties])

func _load_image_texture(path: String) -> ImageTexture:
	if not FileAccess.file_exists(path):
		return null
	var img = Image.load_from_file(path)
	if not img:
		return null
	return ImageTexture.create_from_image(img)

func _ready() -> void:
	_register_weapon_tiers()
	_register_profession()

func _register_weapon_tiers() -> void:
	var item_script = load("res://resources/Items/Scripts/Item.gd")
	var buy_script = load("res://resources/BuyEffects/Scripts/AddToInventory.gd")
	var pickup_script = load("res://resources/PickupEffects/Scripts/PickupToInventory.gd")
	var equip_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-sinclairity_class_mod/scripts/sinclairity_equip.gd")
	var pickup_sound = load("res://assets/sound/sound effects/SwordThud.wav")

	var item_unlocks = load("res://resources/unlockable_items/scripts/item_unlocks.tres")

	for t in TIER_DATA:
		var item = item_script.new()
		item.itemID = "sinclairity_prism_" + t["id"]
		item.itemName = t["name"]
		item.itemDescription = "Continuous spectral prism miner.\nMelts rock layers with high-energy refracted light.\nDamage: %d/s | Beam Width: %.0fpx" % [int(t["dmg"]), float(t["width"])]

		var tex_path := "res://mods-unpacked/gareth-sinclairity_class_mod/textures/prism_%s.png" % t["id"]
		item.itemTexture = _load_image_texture(tex_path)

		var equip = equip_script.new()
		equip.damage = t["dmg"]
		equip.beam_width = t["width"]
		item.itemEquipEffect = equip

		var buy = buy_script.new()
		item.itemBuyEffect = buy
		var pickup = pickup_script.new()
		item.itemPickupEffect = pickup
		item.pickupSound = pickup_sound

		item.itemBuyPrice = t["price"]
		item.itemSellPrice = t["price"] * 0.5
		item.itemUnlocked = true
		item.itemType = "sinclair_prism"

		all_sinc_items.append(item)

		if item_unlocks:
			if not (item in item_unlocks.all_equipment_items):
				item_unlocks.all_equipment_items.append(item)

	if all_sinc_items.size() > 0:
		starter_weapon = all_sinc_items[0]

	ModLoaderLog.info("15 Rainbow Sinclairity Optical Emitter tiers registered into equipment shop.", MOD_DIR)

func _register_profession() -> void:
	if not Gvars or not ("profession_manager" in Gvars) or not Gvars.profession_manager:
		return
	var pm = Gvars.profession_manager
	var prof_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-sinclairity_class_mod/scripts/sinclairity_profession.gd")
	var p = prof_script.new()
	p.profession_name = "Rainbow Sinclairity"
	p.description = "Experimental spectral optics miner.\nMelts stone with a continuous prismatic energy beam.\nStarts with the Shoddy Optical Emitter."

	var icon_path := "res://mods-unpacked/gareth-sinclairity_class_mod/textures/sinclairity_icon.png"
	var icon_tex = _load_image_texture(icon_path)
	if icon_tex:
		p.image = icon_tex

	pm.all_professions[104] = { "profession": p, "is_unlocked": true }
	ModLoaderLog.info("Rainbow Sinclairity Class (ID 104) registered cleanly.", MOD_DIR)

--------
# Rainbow Sinclairity Class Mod for Coal LLC

**Author:** Gareth White
**Namespace:** gareth
**Mod ID:** gareth-sinclairity_class_mod
**Class ID:** 104
--------
==================================================
FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-sinclairity_class_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-sinclairity_class_mod\scripts\sinclairity_equip.gd
==================================================
--------
class_name SinclairityEquipEffect
extends EquipEffect

@export var damage: float = 95.0
@export var beam_width: float = 7.0

var weapon_visual: Sprite2D = null
var beam_line: Line2D = null
var _is_firing: bool = false
var _auto_fire_toggle: bool = false
var audio_player: AudioStreamPlayer2D = null
var _hue: float = 0.0

func start(player, item):
	if "tool" in player and player.tool:
		player.tool.visible = false

	weapon_visual = Sprite2D.new()
	weapon_visual.texture = item.itemTexture
	weapon_visual.offset = Vector2(8, 0)
	weapon_visual.position = Vector2(3, 2)
	weapon_visual.visible = true
	player.add_child(weapon_visual)

	beam_line = Line2D.new()
	beam_line.top_level = true
	beam_line.z_index = 50
	beam_line.width = beam_width
	beam_line.visible = false
	player.get_parent().add_child(beam_line)

	if ResourceLoader.exists("res://assets/sound/sound effects/laser.wav"):
		audio_player = AudioStreamPlayer2D.new()
		audio_player.stream = load("res://assets/sound/sound effects/laser.wav")
		player.add_child(audio_player)

	_is_firing = false
	_auto_fire_toggle = false

func end(player):
	if "tool" in player and player.tool:
		player.tool.visible = true
	if beam_line and is_instance_valid(beam_line):
		beam_line.queue_free()
	if audio_player and is_instance_valid(audio_player):
		audio_player.queue_free()
	if weapon_visual and is_instance_valid(weapon_visual):
		weapon_visual.queue_free()
	_is_firing = false
	_auto_fire_toggle = false

func physics_process(player, delta):
	if weapon_visual and is_instance_valid(weapon_visual):
		var p_pos: Vector2 = player.global_position
		var pointer_pos: Vector2 = player.pointer_global_pos
		var angle: float = (pointer_pos - p_pos).angle()
		weapon_visual.rotation = angle
		weapon_visual.flip_v = (pointer_pos.x < p_pos.x)

	if Input.is_action_pressed("useItem") or Input.is_mouse_button_pressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT):
		_is_firing = true
	elif Input.is_action_just_released("useItem") or (not Input.is_mouse_button_pressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT) and not Input.is_mouse_button_pressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_RIGHT) and not _auto_fire_toggle):
		_is_firing = false

	if Input.is_action_just_pressed("toggle_use_item") or Input.is_action_just_pressed("rightMouseClick"):
		_auto_fire_toggle = not _auto_fire_toggle
		_is_firing = _auto_fire_toggle

	if Input.is_mouse_button_pressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_RIGHT):
		_is_firing = true

	if _is_firing:
		_process_prismatic_beam(player, delta)
	else:
		if beam_line and is_instance_valid(beam_line):
			beam_line.visible = false
		if audio_player and is_instance_valid(audio_player) and audio_player.playing:
			audio_player.stop()

func _get_passive_stat(stat_name: String) -> float:
	if not Gvars or not ("passives" in Gvars) or not Gvars.passives:
		return 0.0
	var p = Gvars.passives
	if p.has_meta(stat_name):
		return float(p.get_meta(stat_name))
	if stat_name in p:
		return float(p.get(stat_name))
	return 0.0

func _process_prismatic_beam(player, delta) -> void:
	if not beam_line or not is_instance_valid(beam_line):
		return

	if audio_player and is_instance_valid(audio_player) and not audio_player.playing:
		audio_player.play()

	_hue = fmod(_hue + delta * 2.5, 1.0)
	var beam_col := Color.from_hsv(_hue, 0.9, 1.0, 0.95)

	var p_pos: Vector2 = player.global_position
	var pointer_pos: Vector2 = player.pointer_global_pos
	var dir: Vector2 = (pointer_pos - p_pos).normalized()
	var muzzle_pos: Vector2 = p_pos + (dir * 20.0)

	var tilemap = player.tilemap
	if not tilemap:
		return

	var max_reach: float = 240.0
	var hit_pos: Vector2 = muzzle_pos + (dir * max_reach)
	var hit_cell = null

	for d in range(8, int(max_reach), 6):
		var check_pos: Vector2 = muzzle_pos + (dir * float(d))
		var local_pos: Vector2 = tilemap.to_local(check_pos)
		var cell: Vector2i = tilemap.local_to_map(local_pos)
		if tilemap.is_tile_breakable(cell):
			hit_cell = cell
			hit_pos = check_pos
			break

	beam_line.clear_points()
	beam_line.add_point(muzzle_pos)
	beam_line.add_point(hit_pos)
	beam_line.width = beam_width * (1.0 + _get_passive_stat("sinclair_beam_width"))
	beam_line.default_color = beam_col
	beam_line.visible = true

	if hit_cell != null:
		var eff_dmg: float = damage * (1.0 + _get_passive_stat("sinclair_melt_damage")) * delta * 60.0
		if tilemap.has_method("damageTile"):
			tilemap.damageTile(hit_pos, eff_dmg)
		if tilemap.has_method("damageTileCoords"):
			tilemap.damageTileCoords(hit_cell, eff_dmg)
		_spawn_melt_spark(player, hit_pos, beam_col)

func _spawn_melt_spark(player: Node2D, at_pos: Vector2, col: Color) -> void:
	var parent = player.get_parent()
	if not parent:
		parent = player
	var spark := Line2D.new()
	spark.top_level = true
	spark.z_index = 51
	spark.width = 3.0
	spark.default_color = col
	for i in range(7):
		var ang: float = float(i) * TAU / 6.0
		spark.add_point(at_pos + Vector2(cos(ang), sin(ang)) * 4.0)
	parent.add_child(spark)
	var tween = parent.create_tween()
	tween.tween_property(spark, "scale", Vector2(2.0, 2.0), 0.08)
	tween.tween_callback(spark.queue_free)

func _profession_effect(data: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:

			"sinclair_melt_damage",
			"sinclair_beam_width",
			"sinclair_spectrum_ticks",
			"player_sprint_speed",
			"player_jump_velocity",
			"player_climb_speed"
		]

	return data

# 45-Tier Tank Arsenal

**Namespace:** $namespace  
**Compatibility:** Coal LLC v1.4.1.1+ (ModLoader 7.0.1)

---

## Overview
The **Tank Arsenal Mod** significantly broadens the Tanker profession by introducing 45 new purchasable combat tanks into the equipment shop across all 15 material tiers (Shoddy through Onyx). Tanks are interleaved cleanly alongside vanilla models.

The arsenal features three distinct projectile variants:
* **Incendiary Tanks (Tiers 1–15):** High-explosive shells that set every block within their Manhattan blast radius ablaze with 6 burn ticks. Synergizes with Elemental Transmutation for bonus cash bounties.
* **Caustic Tanks (Tiers 1–15):** Chemical mortar rounds that release dense toxic acid clouds, melting through organic and clay deposits.
* **Depleted Uranium Tanks (Tiers 1–15):** Kinetic armor-piercing rounds featuring dynamic tier penetration, punching through +1 solid stone block per material grade (up to 15 solid blocks at Onyx).

Elemental scrolls are also injected into Tanker chest loot tables, with tank fire rates scaling dynamically with passives.

---

## Technical Details (For Modders)
Hooks tanker.gd and management_screen.gd to dynamically inject and tier-sort tank equipment cards into the shop. Modifies bullet collision logic in tank_bullet.gd to implement multi-block piercing.

---

## Installation
1. Ensure **Coal LLC** has Godot ModLoader 7.0.1 installed.
2. Place the packaged .zip file into your Coal LLC/mods/ directory.
3. Manage or toggle this mod directly via the in-game **Mod Manager**.
--------

const MOD_DIR := "gareth-tank_arsenal_mod"
const LOG_NAME := "gareth-tank_arsenal_mod:Main"

func _init() -> void:
	var mod_dir_path: String = ModLoaderMod.get_unpacked_dir().path_join(MOD_DIR)
	var ext_path: String = mod_dir_path.path_join("extensions")

	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks(
		"res://resources/professions/scripts/tanker.gd",
		ext_path.path_join("resources/professions/scripts/tanker.hooks.gd")
	)
	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks(
		"res://scenes/Stages/management_screen.gd",
		ext_path.path_join("scenes/Stages/management_screen.hooks.gd")
	)
	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks(
		"res://resources/EquipEffects/Scripts/tank.gd",
		ext_path.path_join("resources/EquipEffects/Scripts/tank.hooks.gd")
	)
	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks(
		"res://scenes/equipment/tank_bullet.gd",
		ext_path.path_join("scenes/equipment/tank_bullet.hooks.gd")
	)

func _ready() -> void:
	var state = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-tank_arsenal_mod/tank_state.gd")
	state.ensure_catalog_registered()

# 45-Tier Tank Arsenal

**Namespace:** $namespace  
**Compatibility:** Coal LLC v1.4.1.1+ (ModLoader 7.0.1)

---

## Overview
The **Tank Arsenal Mod** significantly broadens the Tanker profession by introducing 45 new purchasable combat tanks into the equipment shop across all 15 material tiers (Shoddy through Onyx). Tanks are interleaved cleanly alongside vanilla models.

The arsenal features three distinct projectile variants:
* **Incendiary Tanks (Tiers 1–15):** High-explosive shells that set every block within their Manhattan blast radius ablaze with 6 burn ticks. Synergizes with Elemental Transmutation for bonus cash bounties.
* **Caustic Tanks (Tiers 1–15):** Chemical mortar rounds that release dense toxic acid clouds, melting through organic and clay deposits.
* **Depleted Uranium Tanks (Tiers 1–15):** Kinetic armor-piercing rounds featuring dynamic tier penetration, punching through +1 solid stone block per material grade (up to 15 solid blocks at Onyx).

Elemental scrolls are also injected into Tanker chest loot tables, with tank fire rates scaling dynamically with passives.

---

## Technical Details (For Modders)
Hooks tanker.gd and management_screen.gd to dynamically inject and tier-sort tank equipment cards into the shop. Modifies bullet collision logic in tank_bullet.gd to implement multi-block piercing.

---

## Installation
1. Ensure **Coal LLC** has Godot ModLoader 7.0.1 installed.
2. Place the packaged .zip file into your Coal LLC/mods/ directory.
3. Manage or toggle this mod directly via the in-game **Mod Manager**.

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-tank_arsenal_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-tank_arsenal_mod\tank_state.gd
==================================================
--------
extends Object

static var active_tank_id: String = ""
static var catalog_registered: bool = false

static func get_color_for_id(item_id: String) -> Color:
	if "tank_fire" in item_id:
		return Color(2.0, 0.45, 0.1, 1.0)
	elif "tank_poison" in item_id:
		return Color(0.25, 2.0, 0.35, 1.0)
	elif "tank_uranium" in item_id:
		return Color(0.35, 1.0, 2.2, 1.0)
	return Color.WHITE

static func ensure_catalog_registered() -> void:
	if catalog_registered:
		return

	var item_unlocks = load("res://resources/unlockable_items/scripts/item_unlocks.tres")
	if not item_unlocks:
		return

	var tiers = [
		"01_shoddy", "02_copper", "03_iron", "04_sapphire", "05_emerald",
		"06_silver", "07_amethyst", "08_gold", "09_ruby", "10_diamond",
		"11_pinkdiamond", "12_spinel", "13_uranium", "14_moonstone", "15_onyx"
	]

	# 1. Strip any previous mod items to guarantee exact order without duplicates
	var cleaned_eq: Array[Item] = []
	for item in item_unlocks.all_equipment_items:
		if item and ("tank_fire" in item.itemID or "tank_poison" in item.itemID or "tank_uranium" in item.itemID):
			continue
		cleaned_eq.append(item)
	item_unlocks.all_equipment_items = cleaned_eq

	# 2. Interleave custom tanks directly beside their vanilla tier counterpart
	for t in tiers:
		var vanilla_id = "tank_" + t
		var target_idx = -1
		for i in range(item_unlocks.all_equipment_items.size()):
			var cur = item_unlocks.all_equipment_items[i]
			if cur and cur.itemID == vanilla_id:
				target_idx = i
				break

		var fire_item = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-tank_arsenal_mod/items/tank_fire_%s.tres" % t)
		var poison_item = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-tank_arsenal_mod/items/tank_poison_%s.tres" % t)
		var uranium_item = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-tank_arsenal_mod/items/tank_uranium_%s.tres" % t)

		var to_insert = [fire_item, poison_item, uranium_item]

		if target_idx != -1:
			for offset in range(to_insert.size()):
				var it = to_insert[offset]
				if it:
					item_unlocks.all_equipment_items.insert(target_idx + 1 + offset, it)
		else:
			for it in to_insert:
				if it:
					item_unlocks.all_equipment_items.append(it)

		for it in to_insert:
			if it and not it in item_unlocks.all_items:
				item_unlocks.all_items.append(it)

	catalog_registered = true
	ModLoaderLog.info("Registered and tier-sorted 45 custom tanks into shop.", "gareth-tank_arsenal_mod")

extends Object

const STATE_SCRIPT := "res://mods-unpacked/gareth-tank_arsenal_mod/tank_state.gd"

func start(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, player, item) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([player, item])
	var state = load(STATE_SCRIPT)
	var item_id = item.itemID if (item and "itemID" in item) else ""
	state.active_tank_id = item_id

	if player and "equipped_item_sprite" in player and player.equipped_item_sprite:
		player.equipped_item_sprite.modulate = state.get_color_for_id(item_id)

func end(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, player) -> void:
	var state = load(STATE_SCRIPT)
	state.active_tank_id = ""
	if player and "equipped_item_sprite" in player and player.equipped_item_sprite:
		player.equipped_item_sprite.modulate = Color.WHITE
	chain.execute_next([player])

extends Object

func _profession_effect(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, data: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	var res: Dictionary = chain.execute_next([data])
	if res.has("unlocked_passives"):
		if not "fire_damage" in res["unlocked_passives"]:
			res["unlocked_passives"].append("fire_damage")
		if not "poison_damage" in res["unlocked_passives"]:
			res["unlocked_passives"].append("poison_damage")
	return res

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-tank_arsenal_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-tank_arsenal_mod\extensions\scenes\equipment\tank_bullet.hooks.gd
==================================================
--------
extends Object

const STATE_SCRIPT := "res://mods-unpacked/gareth-tank_arsenal_mod/tank_state.gd"

func _ready(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([])
	var bullet = chain.reference_object
	if not bullet:
		return

	var state = load(STATE_SCRIPT)
	var tank_id = state.active_tank_id
	bullet.set_meta("tank_id", tank_id)

	if "tank_fire" in tank_id:
		bullet.modulate = Color(2.0, 0.45, 0.1, 1.0)
	elif "tank_poison" in tank_id:
		bullet.modulate = Color(0.25, 2.0, 0.35, 1.0)
	elif "tank_uranium" in tank_id:
		bullet.modulate = Color(0.35, 1.0, 2.2, 1.0)

		# Scale penetration by tier level (01 = 1 block, 02 = 2 blocks ... 15 = 15 blocks)
		var tier_num: int = 1
		var parts: PackedStringArray = tank_id.split("_")
		if parts.size() >= 3 and parts[2].is_valid_int():
			tier_num = parts[2].to_int()

		bullet.set_meta("pierce_left", max(1, tier_num))
		bullet.set_meta("last_pierced_tile", Vector2i(-999999, -999999))

func attack_tile(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	var bullet = chain.reference_object
	if not bullet or not bullet.tilemap or bullet.hit:
		chain.execute_next([])
		return

	var tank_id = bullet.get_meta("tank_id", "")
	if tank_id == "" or (not "tank_fire" in tank_id and not "tank_poison" in tank_id and not "tank_uranium" in tank_id):
		chain.execute_next([])
		return

	var current_tile: Vector2i = bullet.tilemap.local_to_map(bullet.tilemap.to_local(bullet.global_position))
	var is_solid: bool = bullet.tilemap.is_tile_collision(current_tile)
	var expired: bool = bullet.t > bullet.LIFETIME

	var tank_mult: float = 1.0 + Gvars.passives.tank_damage

	# 1. INCENDIARY
	if "tank_fire" in tank_id:
		if is_solid or expired:
			bullet.hit = true
			var fire_mult: float = 1.0 + Gvars.passives.fire_damage
			var direct_dmg: float = bullet.damage * tank_mult
			var burn_dmg: float = (bullet.damage * 0.5) * fire_mult

			for x in range(-bullet.radius, bullet.radius + 1):
				for y in range(-bullet.radius, bullet.radius + 1):
					if abs(x) + abs(y) < (bullet.radius + 1):
						var tile_coords = Vector2i(current_tile.x + x, current_tile.y + y)
						bullet.tilemap.damageTileCoords(Vector2(tile_coords.x, tile_coords.y), direct_dmg)
						bullet.tilemap.burn_tile_coords(tile_coords, burn_dmg, 6)
			bullet.explode()
		return

	# 2. CAUSTIC
	if "tank_poison" in tank_id:
		if is_solid or expired:
			bullet.hit = true
			var poison_mult: float = 1.0 + Gvars.passives.poison_damage
			var direct_dmg: float = bullet.damage * tank_mult
			var poison_dmg: float = (bullet.damage * 0.5) * poison_mult

			for x in range(-bullet.radius, bullet.radius + 1):
				for y in range(-bullet.radius, bullet.radius + 1):
					if abs(x) + abs(y) < (bullet.radius + 1):
						var tile_coords = Vector2i(current_tile.x + x, current_tile.y + y)
						bullet.tilemap.damageTileCoords(Vector2(tile_coords.x, tile_coords.y), direct_dmg)
						bullet.tilemap.poison_tile_coords(tile_coords, poison_dmg, 6)
			bullet.explode()
		return

	# 3. DEPLETED URANIUM (SCALED PENETRATION BY TIER)
	if "tank_uranium" in tank_id:
		if is_solid:
			var last_hit: Vector2i = bullet.get_meta("last_pierced_tile", Vector2i(-999999, -999999))
			if current_tile != last_hit:
				bullet.set_meta("last_pierced_tile", current_tile)
				var pierce: int = bullet.get_meta("pierce_left", 1) - 1
				bullet.set_meta("pierce_left", pierce)

				# Punch damage through rock wall
				var punch_dmg: float = bullet.damage * tank_mult
				bullet.tilemap.damageTileCoords(Vector2(current_tile.x, current_tile.y), punch_dmg)

				if pierce <= 0:
					_detonate_uranium(bullet, current_tile, tank_mult)
					return
			return

		if expired:
			_detonate_uranium(bullet, current_tile, tank_mult)
			return

func _detonate_uranium(bullet, current_tile: Vector2i, tank_mult: float) -> void:
	bullet.hit = true
	var direct_dmg: float = bullet.damage * tank_mult
	for x in range(-bullet.radius, bullet.radius + 1):
		for y in range(-bullet.radius, bullet.radius + 1):
			if abs(x) + abs(y) < (bullet.radius + 1):
				var tile_coords = Vector2(current_tile.x + x, current_tile.y + y)
				bullet.tilemap.damageTileCoords(tile_coords, direct_dmg)
	bullet.explode()

# Tectonic Faultline Map (`gareth-tectonic_faultline_mod`)

![Tectonic Faultline Preview](assets/tectonic_preview.png)

**Author:** Gareth White  
**Map ID:** `102`  
**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Compatibility:** Coal LLC v1.4.1.1+ (ModLoader 7.0.1)

---

## Overview
**Tectonic Faultline** plunges your miner into a violent subterranean rift characterized by exposed magma fissures, superheated steam vents, and fortified elemental vaults. The map introduces dynamic vertical chasm sculpting, three color-coded elemental pod variants (Fire, Water, Poison), and tight integration with the game's passive progression and auto-chooser systems.

---

## Key Features & Mechanics

### 1. Volatile Elemental Pods (`MagmaSiphon.gd`)
Subterranean chunks spawn reinforced elemental vaults housing high-pressure geysers and pods. Pods spawn at **triple density (3x)** across subterranean strata, featuring distinct visual and mechanical identities:

* **Magma Siphons (Crimson Red):** Superheated volcanic cores. Vents trigger violent thermal fire cascades setting surrounding rock ablaze, while ruptures detonate a massive kinetic shockwave.
* **Hydro Geysers (Electric Azure):** High-pressure subterranean springs. Vents launch the player high into the air with **-950 updraft velocity** and saturate adjacent rock with wet status for **2x damage vulnerability**.
* **Acid Vents (Toxic Green):** Corrosive chemical chambers. Vents release caustic clouds that seep downward through gravity drips, poisoning rock layers and breaking down dense minerals.

### 2. Dual Interaction System
* **Controlled Venting (`[E]`):** Safely vents geothermal pressure. Catapults the miner skyward, pays out a scaled cash bounty (amplified by mine depth, chest multipliers, and run passives), triggers a multi-wave fluid cascade, and rolls a bonus perk selection (`Bus.choose_passive`).
* **Volatile Rupture (Left-Click):** Deliberately breaches the containment casing. Triggers a wide-radius seismic explosion dealing catastrophic kinetic damage to surrounding rock strata and spawning high-density coal drops.

### 3. Subterranean Vault Generation
* Pods spawn inside fortified 3x3 chambers carved into deep rock layers.
* Chambers are enclosed by reinforced perimeter walls (+2 tiers harder than the surrounding stratum).
* Multi-block chamber coordinates are registered in `generate_chests` to prevent vanilla loot chests from overlapping the rooms.

### 4. Elemental Perks & Auto-Chooser Integration
* **Bonus Chest Card Injection:** On Map 102, **Increase Fire Damage (+5%)**, **Increase Wet Damage (+1%)**, **Increase Poison Damage (+5%)**, and **Increase Elemental Tick Rate (+10%)** are injected directly into vanilla bonus chests and card selections without requiring specific elemental weapons equipped.
* **Elemental Tick Acceleration:** Incorporates a dynamic tick engine that accelerates `burns_loop()` and `poisons_loop()`, causing ignited and poisoned rock to dissolve at high speeds.
* **Nanobotz Auto-Passive Chooser Support:** Fully compatible with `nanobotz-autopassivechooser`. Configurable via **Settings -> Passive Order** with custom priority sorting, stat caps, and auto-pick execution.

---

## Technical Specifications & Engine Contracts

| Architecture Area | Implementation Detail |
| :--- | :--- |
| **Sky Air Contract** | `lvl_from_global_pos(pos)` returns `-2` when `pos.y < 0` to preserve clear skies without solid rock overhead. |
| **GPU Sync Contract** | Vault carving executes `chunk.tiles[idx] = tile` followed by `chunk.build_tile(idx)` to force GPU render updates. |
| **Asset Loading** | Preview cards and entity textures load via raw byte buffers (`FileAccess.get_file_as_bytes()`) to bypass virtual `.zip` path limitations. |
| **Tick Hook** | Injects an accumulator loop inside `tile_map_chunk.hooks.gd:apply_tile_effects` to run extra tick cycles based on `elemental_tick_rate`. |

---

## Installation
1. Ensure the custom hook-packing Godot ModLoader build is installed.
2. Place `gareth-tectonic_faultline_mod.zip` into your `Coal LLC/mods/` directory.
3. Launch the game, navigate to the map selection terminal, and select **Tectonic Faultline**.

extends Node

const MOD_DIR := "gareth-tectonic_faultline_mod"

func _init() -> void:
	ModLoaderLog.info("Tectonic Faultline Mod initializing...", MOD_DIR)
	var mod_dir_path: String = ModLoaderMod.get_unpacked_dir().path_join(MOD_DIR)
	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks(
		"res://scenes/Interfaces/Menus/level_select.gd",
		mod_dir_path.path_join("extensions/scenes/Interfaces/Menus/level_select.hooks.gd")
	)
	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks(
		"res://scenes/tilemaps/tile_map_chunk.gd",
		mod_dir_path.path_join("extensions/scenes/tilemaps/tile_map_chunk.hooks.gd")
	)
	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks(
		"res://scripts/Game.gd",
		mod_dir_path.path_join("extensions/scripts/Game.hooks.gd")
	)
	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks(
		"res://scenes/Stages/management_screen.gd",
		mod_dir_path.path_join("extensions/scenes/Stages/management_screen.hooks.gd")
	)

func _ready() -> void:
	var helper_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-tectonic_faultline_mod/helpers/TectonicFaultlineHelper.gd")
	if helper_script:
		var helper = helper_script.new()
		helper.ensure_level_registered()
	ModLoaderLog.info("Tectonic Faultline Custom Map Mod (ID 102) online!", MOD_DIR)
