# Tectonic Faultline Map (`gareth-tectonic_faultline_mod`)

![Tectonic Faultline Preview](assets/tectonic_preview.png)

**Author:** Gareth White  
**Map ID:** `102`  
**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Compatibility:** Coal LLC v1.4.1.1+ (ModLoader 7.0.1)

---

## Overview
**Tectonic Faultline** plunges your miner into a violent subterranean rift characterized by exposed magma fissures, superheated steam vents, and fortified elemental vaults. The map introduces dynamic vertical chasm sculpting, three color-coded elemental pod variants (Fire, Water, Poison), and tight integration with the game's passive progression and auto-chooser systems.

---

## Key Features & Mechanics

### 1. Volatile Elemental Pods (`MagmaSiphon.gd`)
Subterranean chunks spawn reinforced elemental vaults housing high-pressure geysers and pods. Pods spawn at **triple density (3x)** across subterranean strata, featuring distinct visual and mechanical identities:

* **Magma Siphons (Crimson Red):** Superheated volcanic cores. Vents trigger violent thermal fire cascades setting surrounding rock ablaze, while ruptures detonate a massive kinetic shockwave.
* **Hydro Geysers (Electric Azure):** High-pressure subterranean springs. Vents launch the player high into the air with **-950 updraft velocity** and saturate adjacent rock with wet status for **2x damage vulnerability**.
* **Acid Vents (Toxic Green):** Corrosive chemical chambers. Vents release caustic clouds that seep downward through gravity drips, poisoning rock layers and breaking down dense minerals.

### 2. Dual Interaction System
* **Controlled Venting (`[E]`):** Safely vents geothermal pressure. Catapults the miner skyward, pays out a scaled cash bounty (amplified by mine depth, chest multipliers, and run passives), triggers a multi-wave fluid cascade, and rolls a bonus perk selection (`Bus.choose_passive`).
* **Volatile Rupture (Left-Click):** Deliberately breaches the containment casing. Triggers a wide-radius seismic explosion dealing catastrophic kinetic damage to surrounding rock strata and spawning high-density coal drops.

### 3. Subterranean Vault Generation
* Pods spawn inside fortified 3x3 chambers carved into deep rock layers.
* Chambers are enclosed by reinforced perimeter walls (+2 tiers harder than the surrounding stratum).
* Multi-block chamber coordinates are registered in `generate_chests` to prevent vanilla loot chests from overlapping the rooms.

### 4. Elemental Perks & Auto-Chooser Integration
* **Bonus Chest Card Injection:** On Map 102, **Increase Fire Damage (+5%)**, **Increase Wet Damage (+1%)**, **Increase Poison Damage (+5%)**, and **Increase Elemental Tick Rate (+10%)** are injected directly into vanilla bonus chests and card selections without requiring specific elemental weapons equipped.
* **Elemental Tick Acceleration:** Incorporates a dynamic tick engine that accelerates `burns_loop()` and `poisons_loop()`, causing ignited and poisoned rock to dissolve at high speeds.
* **Nanobotz Auto-Passive Chooser Support:** Fully compatible with `nanobotz-autopassivechooser`. Configurable via **Settings -> Passive Order** with custom priority sorting, stat caps, and auto-pick execution.

---

## Technical Specifications & Engine Contracts

| Architecture Area | Implementation Detail |
| :--- | :--- |
| **Sky Air Contract** | `lvl_from_global_pos(pos)` returns `-2` when `pos.y < 0` to preserve clear skies without solid rock overhead. |
| **GPU Sync Contract** | Vault carving executes `chunk.tiles[idx] = tile` followed by `chunk.build_tile(idx)` to force GPU render updates. |
| **Asset Loading** | Preview cards and entity textures load via raw byte buffers (`FileAccess.get_file_as_bytes()`) to bypass virtual `.zip` path limitations. |
| **Tick Hook** | Injects an accumulator loop inside `tile_map_chunk.hooks.gd:apply_tile_effects` to run extra tick cycles based on `elemental_tick_rate`. |

---

## Installation
1. Ensure the custom hook-packing Godot ModLoader build is installed.
2. Place `gareth-tectonic_faultline_mod.zip` into your `Coal LLC/mods/` directory.
3. Launch the game, navigate to the map selection terminal, and select **Tectonic Faultline**.

class_name MagmaSiphon
extends Node2D

enum ElementType { FIRE, WATER, POISON }

var element_type: ElementType = ElementType.FIRE
var in_range: bool = false
var vented: bool = false
var sprite: Sprite2D
var prompt_label: Label
var tex_active: Texture2D
var tex_vented: Texture2D
var cached_player: Node2D = null

# Animated Shockwave Ring
var fx_radius: float = 0.0
var fx_alpha: float = 0.0
var fx_color: Color = Color(1.0, 0.15, 0.15)

func _ready() -> void:
	z_index = 6
	
	var img_active = Image.load_from_file("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-tectonic_faultline_mod/assets/magma_siphon_active.png")
	if img_active:
		tex_active = ImageTexture.create_from_image(img_active)
	
	var img_vented = Image.load_from_file("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-tectonic_faultline_mod/assets/magma_siphon_vented.png")
	if img_vented:
		tex_vented = ImageTexture.create_from_image(img_vented)
	
	sprite = Sprite2D.new()
	sprite.texture = tex_active
	add_child(sprite)
	
	prompt_label = Label.new()
	prompt_label.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	prompt_label.position = Vector2(-85, -28)
	prompt_label.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 9)
	prompt_label.add_theme_constant_override("outline_size", 3)
	prompt_label.visible = false
	add_child(prompt_label)
	
	_apply_visual_styling()

func set_element(type_str: String) -> void:
	match type_str.to_lower():
		"water":
			element_type = ElementType.WATER
		"poison":
			element_type = ElementType.POISON
		_:
			element_type = ElementType.FIRE
	_apply_visual_styling()

func _apply_visual_styling() -> void:
	if not sprite or not prompt_label:
		return
	match element_type:
		ElementType.WATER:
			# Vivid Electric Blue
			sprite.modulate = Color(0.2, 0.55, 1.4, 1.0)
			fx_color = Color(0.2, 0.6, 1.0)
			prompt_label.text = "[E] VENT GEYSER  |  [CLICK] RUPTURE"
			prompt_label.add_theme_color_override("font_color", Color(0.3, 0.75, 1.0, 1.0))
			prompt_label.add_theme_color_override("font_outline_color", Color(0.0, 0.15, 0.4, 1.0))
		ElementType.POISON:
			# Vivid Toxic Green
			sprite.modulate = Color(0.25, 1.3, 0.25, 1.0)
			fx_color = Color(0.25, 0.95, 0.25)
			prompt_label.text = "[E] VENT ACID  |  [CLICK] DISSOLVE"
			prompt_label.add_theme_color_override("font_color", Color(0.4, 1.0, 0.35, 1.0))
			prompt_label.add_theme_color_override("font_outline_color", Color(0.05, 0.3, 0.05, 1.0))
		_:
			# Vivid Crimson Red (Magma)
			sprite.modulate = Color(1.4, 0.2, 0.2, 1.0)
			fx_color = Color(1.0, 0.15, 0.15)
			prompt_label.text = "[E] VENT MAGMA  |  [CLICK] DETONATE"
			prompt_label.add_theme_color_override("font_color", Color(1.0, 0.35, 0.25, 1.0))
			prompt_label.add_theme_color_override("font_outline_color", Color(0.35, 0.05, 0.05, 1.0))

func _get_player() -> Node2D:
	if cached_player and is_instance_valid(cached_player):
		return cached_player
	if Game and "player" in Game and is_instance_valid(Game.player):
		cached_player = Game.player
		return cached_player
	var tree = get_tree()
	if not tree:
		return null
	var p2 = tree.root.find_child("Player2", true, false)
	if p2 and is_instance_valid(p2) and p2 is Node2D:
		cached_player = p2
		return cached_player
	var p = tree.root.find_child("Player", true, false)
	if p and is_instance_valid(p) and p is Node2D:
		cached_player = p
		return cached_player
	return null

func _get_tile_map_manager() -> Node:
	var n = get_parent()
	while n:
		if n.has_method("burn_tile") and n.has_method("damageTile"):
			return n
		n = n.get_parent()
	var tree = get_tree()
	if tree and tree.root:
		return tree.root.find_child("TileMapManager", true, false)
	return null

func _get_scaling_multiplier() -> float:
	var depth_stratum: float = maxf(1.0, 1.0 + abs(global_position.y) / 160.0)
	var depth_factor: float = pow(depth_stratum, 1.45)
	
	var chest_mult: float = 1.0
	if "chest_bonus" in Gvars:
		chest_mult += float(Gvars.chest_bonus)
	if "passives" in Gvars and "chest_rewards" in Gvars.passives:
		chest_mult += float(Gvars.passives.chest_rewards)
	if "multiplier" in Gvars:
		chest_mult *= maxf(1.0, float(Gvars.multiplier))
	
	return depth_factor * chest_mult

func _process(_delta: float) -> void:
	if vented:
		return
	
	var player = _get_player()
	if not player:
		return
	
	var dist: float = global_position.distance_to(player.global_position)
	if dist <= 54.0:
		if not in_range:
			in_range = true
			prompt_label.visible = true
			if Bus and Bus.has_signal("ShowInputIndicator"):
				Bus.ShowInputIndicator.emit("interact", true, self)
		
		# 1. Controlled Safe Venting via [E]
		if Input.is_action_just_pressed("interact"):
			vent_controlled(player)
		
		# 2. Volatile Rupture via Left-Click
		elif Input.is_mouse_button_pressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT):
			var mouse_pos = get_global_mouse_position()
			if global_position.distance_to(mouse_pos) <= 54.0 or dist <= 38.0:
				breach_volatile()
	else:
		if in_range:
			in_range = false
			prompt_label.visible = false
			if Bus and Bus.has_signal("ShowInputIndicator"):
				Bus.ShowInputIndicator.emit("interact", false, self)

func vent_controlled(player: Node2D) -> void:
	if vented:
		return
	vented = true
	in_range = false
	prompt_label.visible = false
	if Bus and Bus.has_signal("ShowInputIndicator"):
		Bus.ShowInputIndicator.emit("interact", false, self)
	
	_trigger_visual_eruption(false)
	
	# Updraft velocity (Water geyser has highest skyward launch)
	if player and ("velocity" in player):
		match element_type:
			ElementType.WATER:
				player.velocity.y = -950.0
			ElementType.POISON:
				player.velocity.y = -700.0
			_:
				player.velocity.y = -800.0
	
	var scale_mult = _get_scaling_multiplier()
	var bounty: float = (20000.0 + (abs(global_position.y) * 14.0)) * maxf(1.0, scale_mult * 0.4)
	Gvars.CashCount += bounty
	if Bus and Bus.has_signal("UpdateUI"):
		Bus.UpdateUI.emit()
	
	_spawn_loot(false)
	
	var core_damage: float = 35000.0 * scale_mult
	_start_fluid_cascade(3.5, core_damage, false)
	
	var depth_mult: float = maxf(2.5, minf(8.0, 2.5 + abs(global_position.y) / 180.0))
	if Bus and Bus.has_signal("choose_passive"):
		Bus.choose_passive.emit(depth_mult)
	
	ModLoaderLog.info("Elemental Pod vented (%s)! Updraft + Scaled Bounty: $%d" % [ElementType.keys()[element_type], int(bounty)], "gareth-tectonic_faultline_mod")

func breach_volatile() -> void:
	if vented:
		return
	vented = true
	in_range = false
	prompt_label.visible = false
	if Bus and Bus.has_signal("ShowInputIndicator"):
		Bus.ShowInputIndicator.emit("interact", false, self)
	
	_trigger_visual_eruption(true)
	
	var scale_mult = _get_scaling_multiplier()
	var bounty: float = (12000.0 + (abs(global_position.y) * 8.0)) * maxf(1.0, scale_mult * 0.35)
	Gvars.CashCount += bounty
	if Bus and Bus.has_signal("UpdateUI"):
		Bus.UpdateUI.emit()
	
	_spawn_loot(true)
	
	var core_damage: float = 120000.0 * scale_mult
	_start_fluid_cascade(5.5, core_damage, true)
	
	var depth_mult: float = maxf(2.0, minf(6.5, 2.0 + abs(global_position.y) / 220.0))
	if Bus and Bus.has_signal("choose_passive"):
		Bus.choose_passive.emit(depth_mult)
	
	ModLoaderLog.info("Elemental Pod breached (%s)! Massive scaled seismic detonation!" % ElementType.keys()[element_type], "gareth-tectonic_faultline_mod")

func _spawn_loot(is_breach: bool) -> void:
	if not Bus or not Bus.has_signal("drop_loot"):
		return
	var item_path = "res://resources/Items/Coal.tres"
	var count = randi_range(24, 45) if is_breach else randi_range(10, 20)
	Bus.drop_loot.emit(global_position + Vector2(randf_range(-12.0, 12.0), randf_range(-12.0, 12.0)), item_path, count)

func _start_fluid_cascade(radius: float, damage: float, is_breach: bool) -> void:
	var tm_mgr = _get_tile_map_manager()
	var scale_mult = _get_scaling_multiplier()
	
	# Wave 1: Immediate explosive core shockwave
	if Bus and Bus.has_signal("BombExplode"):
		Bus.BombExplode.emit(global_position, radius, damage)
	
	var r_int: int = int(ceil(radius))
	for dx in range(-r_int, r_int + 1):
		for dy in range(-r_int, r_int + 1):
			if Vector2(dx, dy).length() <= radius:
				var target_pos = global_position + Vector2(dx * 16.0, dy * 16.0)
				_apply_element_to_tile(tm_mgr, target_pos, damage, scale_mult)
	
	# Wave 2: Lateral splatter spread after 0.14s
	get_tree().create_timer(0.14).timeout.connect(func():
		var lat_radius = radius + 1.5
		for dx in range(-int(lat_radius), int(lat_radius) + 1):
			for dy in range(-1, 2):
				var target_pos = global_position + Vector2(dx * 16.0, dy * 16.0)
				_apply_element_to_tile(tm_mgr, target_pos, damage * 0.6, scale_mult)
	)
	
	# Wave 3: Downward gravitational drip & seep after 0.28s
	get_tree().create_timer(0.28).timeout.connect(func():
		var drip_depth = 6 if is_breach else 4
		for dy in range(1, drip_depth + 1):
			for dx in range(-2, 3):
				var drip_pos = global_position + Vector2(dx * 16.0, dy * 16.0)
				_apply_element_to_tile(tm_mgr, drip_pos, damage * 0.45, scale_mult)
	)

func _apply_element_to_tile(tm_mgr: Node, pos: Vector2, dmg: float, scale_mult: float) -> void:
	if Bus and Bus.has_signal("DamageTile"):
		Bus.DamageTile.emit(pos, dmg)
	
	if not tm_mgr:
		return
	
	match element_type:
		ElementType.FIRE:
			var burn_dps: float = 250.0 * scale_mult
			var burn_ticks: int = 6
			if tm_mgr.has_method("burn_tile"):
				tm_mgr.burn_tile(pos, burn_dps, burn_ticks)
		ElementType.WATER:
			if tm_mgr.has_method("wet_tile"):
				tm_mgr.wet_tile(pos)
			if Bus and Bus.has_signal("DamageTile"):
				Bus.DamageTile.emit(pos, dmg * 0.5)
		ElementType.POISON:
			var poison_dps: float = 320.0 * scale_mult
			var poison_ticks: int = 8
			if tm_mgr.has_method("poison_tile"):
				tm_mgr.poison_tile(pos, poison_dps, poison_ticks)

func _trigger_visual_eruption(is_explosion: bool) -> void:
	if tex_vented:
		sprite.texture = tex_vented
	sprite.modulate = Color(0.25, 0.25, 0.3, 0.85)
	
	var sfx_stream = load("res://assets/sound/sound effects/chestOpen.wav")
	if sfx_stream:
		var sfx = AudioStreamPlayer2D.new()
		sfx.stream = sfx_stream
		sfx.pitch_scale = 0.65 if is_explosion else 1.25
		add_child(sfx)
		sfx.play()
	
	# Primary Elemental Particle Burst
	var p_elem = CPUParticles2D.new()
	p_elem.emitting = true
	p_elem.one_shot = true
	p_elem.explosiveness = 0.95
	p_elem.amount = 65 if is_explosion else 35
	p_elem.lifetime = 0.9
	p_elem.direction = Vector2(0, -1)
	p_elem.spread = 180.0 if is_explosion else 55.0
	p_elem.gravity = Vector2(0, 420)
	p_elem.initial_velocity_min = 220.0 if is_explosion else 270.0
	p_elem.initial_velocity_max = 420.0 if is_explosion else 480.0
	p_elem.scale_amount_min = 4.0
	p_elem.scale_amount_max = 9.5
	
	match element_type:
		ElementType.WATER:
			p_elem.color = Color(0.2, 0.6, 1.0, 0.95)
		ElementType.POISON:
			p_elem.color = Color(0.25, 0.95, 0.25, 0.95)
		_:
			p_elem.color = Color(1.0, 0.15, 0.15, 1.0)
	add_child(p_elem)
	
	# Gravity Drip / Droplet Particles
	var p_drip = CPUParticles2D.new()
	p_drip.emitting = true
	p_drip.one_shot = true
	p_drip.explosiveness = 0.8
	p_drip.amount = 40
	p_drip.lifetime = 1.3
	p_drip.direction = Vector2(0, 1)
	p_drip.spread = 60.0
	p_drip.gravity = Vector2(0, 500)
	p_drip.initial_velocity_min = 80.0
	p_drip.initial_velocity_max = 180.0
	p_drip.scale_amount_min = 3.0
	p_drip.scale_amount_max = 6.5
	p_drip.color = p_elem.color
	add_child(p_drip)
	
	# Expanding Shockwave Ring
	var tw = create_tween()
	fx_radius = 8.0
	fx_alpha = 1.0
	var target_radius = 120.0 if is_explosion else 75.0
	tw.tween_property(self, "fx_radius", target_radius, 0.52).set_trans(Tween.TRANS_QUAD).set_ease(Tween.EASE_OUT)
	tw.parallel().tween_property(self, "fx_alpha", 0.0, 0.52)
	tw.tween_callback(func(): queue_redraw())

func _draw() -> void:
	if fx_alpha > 0.01:
		draw_arc(Vector2.ZERO, fx_radius, 0.0, TAU, 48, Color(fx_color.r, fx_color.g, fx_color.b, fx_alpha), 5.5)
		draw_arc(Vector2.ZERO, fx_radius * 0.7, 0.0, TAU, 48, Color(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, fx_alpha * 0.8), 2.8)
		queue_redraw()

func _ready(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	var helper_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-tectonic_faultline_mod/helpers/TectonicFaultlineHelper.gd")
	if helper_script:
		var helper = helper_script.new()
		helper.ensure_level_registered()
		var ls = chain.reference_object
		if ls and ("LEVEL_ORDER" in ls):
			if not ls.LEVEL_ORDER.has(helper.FAULTLINE_LEVEL_ID):
				ls.LEVEL_ORDER.append(helper.FAULTLINE_LEVEL_ID)
	chain.execute_next([])

extends Object

func load_equipment_shop(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([])
	if Gvars.level == 102:
		var ms = chain.reference_object
		if ms and "equipment_shop" in ms and ms.equipment_shop:
			# Allows pickaxes, heavy drills, and tectonic equipment; filters out delicate cold/water items
			var allowed := ["pickaxe", "drill", "tectonic_fracker", "item_filter", "increase_stack_size", "ladder", "whistle", "collector_whistle", "mining_whistle"]
			for card in ms.equipment_shop.get_children():
				if "item" in card and card.item:
					var itype: String = str(card.item.itemType).to_lower()
					if not allowed.has(itype):
						card.visible = false

func lvl_from_global_pos(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, pos: Vector2i) -> int:
	if Gvars.level == 102:
		var helper_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-tectonic_faultline_mod/helpers/TectonicFaultlineHelper.gd")
		if helper_script:
			return helper_script.calculate_faultline_tier(pos)
	return chain.execute_next([pos])

func generate_chunk(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, noise: Noise) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([noise])
	if Gvars.level == 102:
		var chunk = chain.reference_object
		if chunk and "chunk_pos" in chunk and chunk.chunk_pos.y >= 0:
			var helper_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-tectonic_faultline_mod/helpers/TectonicFaultlineHelper.gd")
			if helper_script:
				var fnl = FastNoiseLite.new()
				fnl.seed = Gvars.map_seed if ("map_seed" in Gvars) else 1337
				fnl.frequency = 0.035
				helper_script.generate_faultline_strata(chunk, fnl)

func generate_chests(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, current_block_chest_locations: Array[Vector2i]) -> Array[Vector2i]:
	var locs: Array[Vector2i] = chain.execute_next([current_block_chest_locations])
	if Gvars.level == 102:
		var chunk = chain.reference_object
		if chunk:
			var helper_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-tectonic_faultline_mod/helpers/TectonicFaultlineHelper.gd")
			if helper_script:
				locs = helper_script.spawn_magma_siphons(chunk, locs)
	return locs

func destroy_tile(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, idx: int, tile_properties: Dictionary) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([idx, tile_properties])

func apply_tile_effects(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([])
	var chunk = chain.reference_object
	if chunk and ("level" in Gvars) and Gvars.level == 102:
		var helper_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-tectonic_faultline_mod/helpers/TectonicFaultlineHelper.gd")
		if helper_script:
			var tick_bonus: float = helper_script.get_elemental_tick_bonus()
			if tick_bonus > 0.0:
				var acc: float = chunk.get_meta("elem_tick_acc", 0.0) + tick_bonus
				while acc >= 1.0:
					acc -= 1.0
					if chunk.has_method("poisons_loop"):
						chunk.poisons_loop()
					if chunk.has_method("burns_loop"):
						chunk.burns_loop()
				chunk.set_meta("elem_tick_acc", acc)

extends Object

func change_stage(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, target: int, targetPosition: Vector2) -> void:
	var game = chain.reference_object
	var helper_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-tectonic_faultline_mod/helpers/TectonicFaultlineHelper.gd")
	if helper_script:
		var helper = helper_script.new()
		helper.ensure_level_registered()

	if target == 2 and Gvars.level == 102:
		if game and "MINE_REGION" in game:
			game.target_stage = game.MINE_REGION

	await chain.execute_next([target, targetPosition])

	if game:
		if game.has_node("ColorRect"):
			game.get_node("ColorRect").mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
		game.visible = false
		if Gvars.profession:
			if not ("player" in game) or game.player == null:
				var player_node = game.get_tree().get_first_node_in_group("player")
				if player_node:
					game.player = player_node
			if "player" in game and game.player:
				game.player.set("profession", Gvars.profession)

class_name TectonicFaultlineHelper
extends RefCounted

const FAULTLINE_LEVEL_ID: int = 102
const CHUNK_SIZE: int = 16

static func is_faultline_level() -> bool:
	if Gvars:
		if "level" in Gvars and Gvars.level == FAULTLINE_LEVEL_ID:
			return true
		if "chosen_level" in Gvars and Gvars.chosen_level == FAULTLINE_LEVEL_ID:
			return true
		if "current_level" in Gvars and Gvars.current_level == FAULTLINE_LEVEL_ID:
			return true
	return false

static func get_elemental_tick_bonus() -> float:
	if Gvars and ("custom_passives" in Gvars) and (Gvars.custom_passives is Dictionary):
		return float(Gvars.custom_passives.get("elemental_tick_rate", 0.0))
	return 0.0

func ensure_level_registered() -> void:
	register_faultline_level()

static func register_faultline_level() -> void:
	if not Gvars:
		return
	
	if "level_manager" in Gvars and Gvars.level_manager and ("all_levels" in Gvars.level_manager):
		var lvl_mgr = Gvars.level_manager
		if not lvl_mgr.all_levels.has(FAULTLINE_LEVEL_ID):
			var custom_level = GameLevel.new()
			custom_level.level_name = "Tectonic Faultline"
			custom_level.level = FAULTLINE_LEVEL_ID
			custom_level.mining_scene_path = "res://scenes/Stages/MineRegion.tscn"
			custom_level.level_description = "Seismic faultline with superheated vents, magma fissures, and elemental chambers.\n\nElemental pods contain explosive fluids and geothermal updrafts."
			custom_level.level_difficulty = GameLevel.Difficulty.HARD
			custom_level.unlock_description = ""
			
			var preview_path = "res://mods-unpacked/gareth-tectonic_faultline_mod/assets/tectonic_preview.png"
			var bytes = FileAccess.get_file_as_bytes(preview_path)
			if bytes and bytes.size() > 0:
				var f_img = Image.new()
				if f_img.load_png_from_buffer(bytes) == OK:
					custom_level.level_image = ImageTexture.create_from_image(f_img)
			
			lvl_mgr.all_levels[FAULTLINE_LEVEL_ID] = {
				"level": custom_level,
				"is_unlocked": true
			}
	
	if "level_info" in Gvars and Gvars.level_info is Dictionary:
		if not Gvars.level_info.has(FAULTLINE_LEVEL_ID):
			Gvars.level_info[FAULTLINE_LEVEL_ID] = {
				"name": "Tectonic Faultline",
				"description": "Seismic faultline with superheated vents, magma fissures, and elemental chambers.",
				"depth": 1400,
				"cost": 0,
				"unlocked": true
			}

static func calculate_faultline_tier(pos: Vector2i) -> int:
	var y_tile: int = pos.y
	if y_tile < 0:
		return -2
	elif y_tile < 24:
		return 1
	elif y_tile < 48:
		return 2
	elif y_tile < 80:
		return 3
	elif y_tile < 120:
		return 4
	elif y_tile < 170:
		return 5
	elif y_tile < 230:
		return 6
	elif y_tile < 300:
		return 7
	else:
		return min(12, 8 + int((y_tile - 300) / 80))

static func pick_faultline_block(chunk: Node, global_cell: Vector2i, noise: FastNoiseLite) -> int:
	if global_cell.y <= 0:
		return chunk.Tiles.EMPTY
	
	var tier: int = calculate_faultline_tier(global_cell)
	if tier < 0:
		return chunk.Tiles.EMPTY
	
	var rift_noise: float = abs(noise.get_noise_2d(float(global_cell.x) * 4.5, float(global_cell.y) * 1.2))
	if rift_noise < 0.09 and global_cell.y > 10:
		return chunk.Tiles.EMPTY
	
	var fissure_noise: float = noise.get_noise_2d(float(global_cell.x) * 2.8 + 50.0, float(global_cell.y) * 2.8)
	if fissure_noise > 0.42 and global_cell.y > 18:
		return chunk.Tiles.HARD_FIRE
	
	var base_tile: int = chunk.chest_block_from_level(tier)
	if base_tile == chunk.Tiles.EMPTY:
		base_tile = chunk.Tiles.HARD_FIRE
	return base_tile

static func generate_faultline_strata(chunk: Node, noise: FastNoiseLite) -> void:
	if not ("tiles" in chunk) or not ("chunk_size" in chunk):
		return
	
	for x in range(chunk.chunk_size):
		for y in range(chunk.chunk_size):
			var local_pos = Vector2i(x, y)
			var global_posi: Vector2i = Vector2i(chunk.to_global(chunk.map_to_local(local_pos)) / 16.0)
			
			if global_posi.y > 3:
				var idx: int = chunk.pos_to_array(local_pos) if chunk.has_method("pos_to_array") else (x + y * chunk.chunk_size)
				if idx >= 0 and idx < chunk.tiles.size():
					if chunk.tiles[idx] != chunk.Tiles.EMPTY:
						var new_block = pick_faultline_block(chunk, global_posi, noise)
						chunk.tiles[idx] = new_block
						chunk.build_tile(idx)

static func spawn_magma_siphons(chunk: Node, current_locs: Array[Vector2i]) -> Array[Vector2i]:
	if "chunk_pos" in chunk and chunk.chunk_pos.y < 1:
		return current_locs
	
	var pods_to_spawn: int = 3
	var elements: Array[String] = ["fire", "water", "poison"]
	elements.shuffle()
	
	var width: int = 3
	var height: int = 3
	
	for p_idx in range(pods_to_spawn):
		var found_spot: bool = false
		var start_tile: Vector2i = Vector2i.ZERO
		
		for attempt in range(12):
			var candidate = Vector2i(
				randi_range(1, chunk.chunk_size - width - 1),
				randi_range(1, chunk.chunk_size - height - 1)
			)
			
			var overlaps: bool = false
			for ox in range(candidate.x - 1, candidate.x + width + 1):
				for oy in range(candidate.y - 1, candidate.y + height + 1):
					if Vector2i(ox, oy) in current_locs:
						overlaps = true
						break
				if overlaps:
					break
			
			if not overlaps:
				start_tile = candidate
				found_spot = true
				break
		
		if not found_spot:
			continue
		
		var local_start: Vector2 = chunk.map_to_local(start_tile)
		var world_start: Vector2 = chunk.to_global(local_start)
		var global_cell: Vector2i = Vector2i(world_start / 16.0)
		if global_cell.y < 10:
			continue
		
		var pod_tier: int = calculate_faultline_tier(global_cell)
		var wall_tier: int = min(max(1, pod_tier + 2), 12)
		var wall_tile: int = chunk.chest_block_from_level(wall_tier)
		if wall_tile == chunk.Tiles.EMPTY:
			wall_tile = chunk.Tiles.HARD_DARK if global_cell.y > 40 else chunk.Tiles.HARD_FIRE
		
		for x in range(start_tile.x - 1, start_tile.x + width + 1):
			for y in range(start_tile.y - 1, start_tile.y + height + 1):
				current_locs.append(Vector2i(x, y))
		
		for x in range(start_tile.x, start_tile.x + width):
			for y in range(start_tile.y, start_tile.y + height):
				var local_coord = Vector2i(x, y)
				var c_idx: int = chunk.pos_to_array(local_coord)
				var is_wall = (x == start_tile.x or x == start_tile.x + width - 1 or y == start_tile.y or y == start_tile.y + height - 1)
				
				if is_wall:
					chunk.tiles[c_idx] = wall_tile
					chunk.build_tile(c_idx)
				else:
					chunk.tiles[c_idx] = chunk.Tiles.EMPTY
					chunk.build_tile(c_idx)
		
		var SiphonScene = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-tectonic_faultline_mod/entities/MagmaSiphon.gd")
		if SiphonScene:
			var siphon = SiphonScene.new()
			var center_tile = Vector2i(start_tile.x + 1, start_tile.y + 1)
			siphon.position = chunk.map_to_local(center_tile)
			siphon.set_element(elements[p_idx % elements.size()])
			chunk.add_child(siphon)
	
	return current_locs

# Tectonic Fracker Class Mod for Coal LLC

**Author:** Gareth White
**Namespace:** gareth
**Mod ID:** gareth-tectonic_fracker_mod
**Class ID:** 109

## Overview
The Tectonic Fracker is an industrial seismic mining class designed to shatter continuous ore seams using acoustic pulse resonance.

## Progression Tiers (15 Mineral Grades)
1. Shoddy (80 Dmg, 18 Blocks) - Starter Gear
2. Copper (115 Dmg, 24 Blocks, 350g)
3. Iron (160 Dmg, 32 Blocks, 1,800g)
4. Sapphire (230 Dmg, 42 Blocks, 9,500g)
5. Emerald (330 Dmg, 54 Blocks, 45,000g)
6. Silver (480 Dmg, 68 Blocks, 200,000g)
7. Amethyst (700 Dmg, 85 Blocks, 850,000g)
8. Gold (1,000 Dmg, 110 Blocks, 4,000,000g)
9. Ruby (1,500 Dmg, 145 Blocks, 20,000,000g)
10. Diamond (2,300 Dmg, 190 Blocks, 100,000,000g)
11. Pink Diamond (3,500 Dmg, 250 Blocks, 600,000,000g)
12. Spinel (5,500 Dmg, 330 Blocks, 3.5B g)
13. Uranium (8,500 Dmg, 440 Blocks, 25B g)
14. Moonstone (13,500 Dmg, 600 Blocks, 180B g)
15. Onyx (22,000 Dmg, 850 Blocks, 1.5T g)

const MOD_DIR := "gareth-tectonic_fracker_mod"

var starter_weapon = null
var all_fracker_items: Array = []

const TIER_DATA := [
	{ "id": "01_shoddy",      "name": "Shoddy Tectonic Fracker",        "dmg": 80.0,    "seam": 18,  "price": 0.0 },
	{ "id": "02_copper",      "name": "Copper Tectonic Fracker",        "dmg": 115.0,   "seam": 24,  "price": 350.0 },

func _load_image_texture(path: String) -> ImageTexture:
	if not FileAccess.file_exists(path):
		return null
	var img = Image.load_from_file(path)
	if not img:
		return null
	return ImageTexture.create_from_image(img)

func _ready() -> void:
	_register_weapon_tiers()
	_register_profession()

func _register_weapon_tiers() -> void:
	var item_script = load("res://resources/Items/Scripts/Item.gd")
	var buy_script = load("res://resources/BuyEffects/Scripts/AddToInventory.gd")
	var pickup_script = load("res://resources/PickupEffects/Scripts/PickupToInventory.gd")
	var equip_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-tectonic_fracker_mod/scripts/tectonic_fracker_equip.gd")
	var pickup_sound = load("res://assets/sound/sound effects/SwordThud.wav")

	var item_unlocks = load("res://resources/unlockable_items/scripts/item_unlocks.tres")

	for t in TIER_DATA:
		var item = item_script.new()
		item.itemID = "tectonic_fracker_" + t["id"]
		item.itemName = t["name"]
		item.itemDescription = "Industrial seismic harvester.\nFires acoustic fracture pulses that shatter continuous ore seams.\nDamage: %d | Max Seam: %d\nScales with Fracker upgrades." % [int(t["dmg"]), int(t["seam"])]

		var tex_path := "res://mods-unpacked/gareth-tectonic_fracker_mod/textures/fracker_%s.png" % t["id"]
		item.itemTexture = _load_image_texture(tex_path)

		var equip = equip_script.new()
		equip.damage = t["dmg"]
		equip.seam_limit = t["seam"]
		item.itemEquipEffect = equip

		var buy = buy_script.new()
		item.itemBuyEffect = buy
		var pickup = pickup_script.new()
		item.itemPickupEffect = pickup
		item.pickupSound = pickup_sound

		item.itemBuyPrice = t["price"]
		item.itemSellPrice = t["price"] * 0.5
		item.itemUnlocked = true
		item.itemType = "fracker"

		all_fracker_items.append(item)

		if item_unlocks:
			if not (item in item_unlocks.all_equipment_items):
				item_unlocks.all_equipment_items.append(item)

	if all_fracker_items.size() > 0:
		starter_weapon = all_fracker_items[0]

	ModLoaderLog.info("15 Tectonic Fracker tiers registered into equipment shop.", MOD_DIR)

func _register_profession() -> void:
	if not Gvars or not ("profession_manager" in Gvars) or not Gvars.profession_manager:
		return
	var pm = Gvars.profession_manager
	var prof_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-tectonic_fracker_mod/scripts/tectonic_fracker_profession.gd")
	var p = prof_script.new()
	p.profession_name = "Tectonic Fracker"
	p.description = "Industrial seismic harvester.\nFires acoustic fracture pulses that shatter entire continuous ore seams simultaneously.\nScales with bespoke Fracker upgrades."

	var icon_path := "res://mods-unpacked/gareth-tectonic_fracker_mod/textures/tectonic_fracker_icon.png"
	var icon_tex = _load_image_texture(icon_path)
	if icon_tex:
		p.image = icon_tex

	pm.all_professions[109] = { "profession": p, "is_unlocked": true }
	ModLoaderLog.info("Tectonic Fracker Class (ID 109) registered cleanly.", MOD_DIR)

# Tectonic Fracker Class Mod for Coal LLC

**Author:** Gareth White
**Namespace:** gareth
**Mod ID:** gareth-tectonic_fracker_mod
**Class ID:** 109

## Overview
The Tectonic Fracker is an industrial seismic mining class designed to shatter continuous ore seams using acoustic pulse resonance.

## Progression Tiers (15 Mineral Grades)
1. Shoddy (80 Dmg, 18 Blocks) - Starter Gear
2. Copper (115 Dmg, 24 Blocks, 350g)
3. Iron (160 Dmg, 32 Blocks, 1,800g)
4. Sapphire (230 Dmg, 42 Blocks, 9,500g)
5. Emerald (330 Dmg, 54 Blocks, 45,000g)
6. Silver (480 Dmg, 68 Blocks, 200,000g)
7. Amethyst (700 Dmg, 85 Blocks, 850,000g)
8. Gold (1,000 Dmg, 110 Blocks, 4,000,000g)
9. Ruby (1,500 Dmg, 145 Blocks, 20,000,000g)
10. Diamond (2,300 Dmg, 190 Blocks, 100,000,000g)
11. Pink Diamond (3,500 Dmg, 250 Blocks, 600,000,000g)
12. Spinel (5,500 Dmg, 330 Blocks, 3.5B g)
13. Uranium (8,500 Dmg, 440 Blocks, 25B g)
14. Moonstone (13,500 Dmg, 600 Blocks, 180B g)
15. Onyx (22,000 Dmg, 850 Blocks, 1.5T g)

class_name TectonicFrackerEquipEffect
extends EquipEffect

@export var damage: float = 80.0
@export var seam_limit: int = 18

var weapon_visual: Sprite2D = null
var fire_cooldown: float = 0.0
var _is_using: bool = false
var _auto_fire_toggle: bool = false
var audio_player: AudioStreamPlayer2D = null

func start(player, item):
	if "tool" in player and player.tool:
		player.tool.visible = false

	if ResourceLoader.exists("res://assets/sound/sound effects/SwordThud.wav"):
		audio_player = AudioStreamPlayer2D.new()
		audio_player.stream = load("res://assets/sound/sound effects/SwordThud.wav")
		player.add_child(audio_player)

	weapon_visual = Sprite2D.new()
	weapon_visual.texture = item.itemTexture
	weapon_visual.offset = Vector2(10, 0)
	weapon_visual.position = Vector2(2, 2)
	weapon_visual.visible = true
	player.add_child(weapon_visual)
	_is_using = false
	_auto_fire_toggle = false

func end(player):
	if "tool" in player and player.tool:
		player.tool.visible = true
	if audio_player and is_instance_valid(audio_player):
		audio_player.queue_free()
	if weapon_visual and is_instance_valid(weapon_visual):
		weapon_visual.queue_free()
	_is_using = false
	_auto_fire_toggle = false

func physics_process(player, delta):
	if fire_cooldown > 0.0:
		fire_cooldown -= delta

	if weapon_visual and is_instance_valid(weapon_visual):
		var p_pos: Vector2 = player.global_position
		var pointer_pos: Vector2 = player.pointer_global_pos
		var angle: float = (pointer_pos - p_pos).angle()
		weapon_visual.rotation = angle
		weapon_visual.flip_v = (pointer_pos.x < p_pos.x)

	if Input.is_action_pressed("useItem") or Input.is_mouse_button_pressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT):
		_is_using = true
	elif Input.is_action_just_released("useItem") or (not Input.is_mouse_button_pressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT) and not Input.is_mouse_button_pressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_RIGHT) and not _auto_fire_toggle):
		_is_using = false

	if Input.is_action_just_pressed("toggle_use_item") or Input.is_action_just_pressed("rightMouseClick"):
		_auto_fire_toggle = not _auto_fire_toggle
		_is_using = _auto_fire_toggle

	if Input.is_mouse_button_pressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_RIGHT):
		_is_using = true

	if _is_using and fire_cooldown <= 0.0:
		_fire_seismic_pulse(player)

func _get_passive_stat(stat_name: String) -> float:
	if not Gvars or not ("passives" in Gvars) or not Gvars.passives:
		return 0.0
	var p = Gvars.passives
	if p.has_meta(stat_name):
		return float(p.get_meta(stat_name))
	if stat_name in p:
		return float(p.get(stat_name))
	return 0.0

func _fire_seismic_pulse(player) -> void:
	var fire_rate_mult: float = 1.0 + _get_passive_stat("fracker_fire_rate")
	fire_cooldown = max(0.06, 0.26 / fire_rate_mult)

	if audio_player and is_instance_valid(audio_player):
		audio_player.pitch_scale = randf_range(0.72, 0.88)
		audio_player.play()

	var p_pos: Vector2 = player.global_position
	var pointer_pos: Vector2 = player.pointer_global_pos
	var dir: Vector2 = (pointer_pos - p_pos).normalized()
	var muzzle_pos: Vector2 = p_pos + (dir * 18.0)

	if weapon_visual and is_instance_valid(weapon_visual):
		var orig_pos = weapon_visual.position
		weapon_visual.position -= dir * 5.0
		var tween = player.create_tween()
		tween.tween_property(weapon_visual, "position", orig_pos, 0.08)

	var tilemap = player.tilemap
	if not tilemap:
		return

	var max_reach: float = 140.0
	var hit_cell = null
	var hit_world_pos: Vector2 = muzzle_pos + (dir * max_reach)

	for dist in range(6, int(max_reach), 6):
		var check_pos: Vector2 = muzzle_pos + (dir * float(dist))
		var cell: Vector2i = tilemap.local_to_map(check_pos)
		if tilemap.is_tile_breakable(cell):
			hit_cell = cell
			hit_world_pos = tilemap.map_to_local(cell)
			break

	_spawn_acoustic_beam(player, muzzle_pos, hit_world_pos)

	if hit_cell == null:
		return

	_spawn_impact_ring(player, hit_world_pos)

	var dmg_mult: float = 1.0 + _get_passive_stat("fracker_pulse_damage")
	var eff_dmg: float = damage * dmg_mult

	var seam_mult: float = 1.0 + _get_passive_stat("fracker_seam_resonance")
	var eff_limit: int = int(float(seam_limit) * seam_mult)

	var visited: Array[Vector2i] = []
	var queue: Array[Vector2i] = [hit_cell]
	visited.append(hit_cell)

	var target_atlas = null
	if tilemap.has_method("get_cell_atlas_coords"):
		target_atlas = tilemap.get_cell_atlas_coords(hit_cell)

	while queue.size() > 0 and visited.size() <= eff_limit:
		var curr = queue.pop_front()
		tilemap.damageTileCoords(curr, eff_dmg)

		for offset in [Vector2i(1,0), Vector2i(-1,0), Vector2i(0,1), Vector2i(0,-1)]:
			var neighbor: Vector2i = curr + offset
			if not visited.has(neighbor):
				visited.append(neighbor)
				if tilemap.is_tile_breakable(neighbor):
					var is_same_seam = true
					if target_atlas != null and tilemap.has_method("get_cell_atlas_coords"):
						var n_atlas = tilemap.get_cell_atlas_coords(neighbor)
						is_same_seam = (n_atlas == target_atlas)
					if is_same_seam:
						queue.append(neighbor)

func _spawn_acoustic_beam(player: Node2D, from_pos: Vector2, to_pos: Vector2) -> void:
	var parent = player.get_parent()
	if not parent:
		parent = player

	var beam := Line2D.new()
	beam.width = 6.0
	beam.default_color = Color(0.2, 0.95, 1.0, 0.95)
	beam.add_point(from_pos)
	beam.add_point(to_pos)
	parent.add_child(beam)

	var tween = parent.create_tween()
	tween.set_parallel(true)
	tween.tween_property(beam, "width", 14.0, 0.12)
	tween.tween_property(beam, "modulate:a", 0.0, 0.12)
	tween.set_parallel(false)
	tween.tween_callback(beam.queue_free)

func _spawn_impact_ring(player: Node2D, at_pos: Vector2) -> void:
	var parent = player.get_parent()
	if not parent:
		parent = player

	var ring := Line2D.new()
	ring.width = 2.5
	ring.default_color = Color(0.9, 1.0, 1.0, 0.9)
	var segs: int = 16
	for i in range(segs + 1):
		var ang: float = float(i) * TAU / float(segs)
		ring.add_point(at_pos + Vector2(cos(ang), sin(ang)) * 4.0)
	parent.add_child(ring)

	var tween = parent.create_tween()
	tween.set_parallel(true)
	tween.tween_property(ring, "scale", Vector2(3.5, 3.5), 0.16)
	tween.tween_property(ring, "modulate:a", 0.0, 0.16)
	tween.set_parallel(false)
	tween.tween_callback(ring.queue_free)

class_name TectonicFrackerProfession
extends Profession

func _profession_effect(data: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	var mod_node = Engine.get_main_loop().root.get_node_or_null("ModLoader/gareth-tectonic_fracker_mod")
	if mod_node and "starter_weapon" in mod_node and mod_node.starter_weapon:
		if data.has("inventory_starters"):
			data["inventory_starters"] = [{
				"item": mod_node.starter_weapon,
				"count": 1
			}]

	if data.has("unlocked_passives"):
		data["unlocked_passives"] = [
			"fracker_pulse_damage",
			"fracker_seam_resonance",
			"fracker_fire_rate",
			"player_sprint_speed",
			"player_jump_velocity",
			"player_climb_speed"
		]

	return data

extends Object

func _physics_process(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([])
	var miner = chain.reference_object
	if not is_instance_valid(miner): return

	var spr = miner.find_child("Sprite2D", true, false)
	if is_instance_valid(spr) and spr is Sprite2D:
		var col_idx = int(miner.get_instance_id()) % 4
		match col_idx:
			0: spr.self_modulate = Color(0.50, 0.15, 0.85, 1.0) # Tinky Winky (Purple)
			1: spr.self_modulate = Color(0.15, 0.75, 0.15, 1.0) # Dipsy (Green)
			2: spr.self_modulate = Color(0.95, 0.85, 0.10, 1.0) # Laa-Laa (Yellow)
			_: spr.self_modulate = Color(0.95, 0.10, 0.10, 1.0) # Po (Red)

extends Object

func _physics_process(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, delta: float) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([delta])
	var vac = chain.reference_object
	if not is_instance_valid(vac):
		return

	var factory = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-teletubbies_mod/scripts/teletubbies_texture_factory.gd")
	if not factory:
		return

	var vac_end = vac.find_child("VacuumEnd", true, false)
	if is_instance_valid(vac_end) and vac_end is Sprite2D:
		var lvl: int = 1
		if Gvars and ("bonus_equipment_manager" in Gvars) and Gvars.bonus_equipment_manager:
			lvl = int(Gvars.bonus_equipment_manager.vacuum_level) if ("vacuum_level" in Gvars.bonus_equipment_manager) else 1

		if vac.get_meta("noo_noo_applied_lvl", 0) != lvl:
			vac.set_meta("noo_noo_applied_lvl", lvl)
			vac_end.texture = factory.create_noo_noo_texture(lvl)
			vac_end.scale = Vector2(1.5, 1.5)

extends Object

var baby_sun: Node2D = null

func _ready(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([])
	var tm = chain.reference_object
	if not is_instance_valid(tm) or is_instance_valid(baby_sun):
		return

	var factory = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-teletubbies_mod/scripts/teletubbies_texture_factory.gd")
	if not factory:
		return

	baby_sun = Node2D.new()
	baby_sun.name = "TeletubbiesBabySun"
	baby_sun.position = Vector2(480, -180)
	baby_sun.z_index = -5

	var spr = Sprite2D.new()
	spr.name = "SunSprite"
	spr.texture = factory.create_baby_sun_texture(false)
	spr.scale = Vector2(2.4, 2.4)
	baby_sun.add_child(spr)

	tm.add_child(baby_sun)

	# Gentle sky bobbing
	var tw = baby_sun.create_tween().set_loops()
	tw.tween_property(spr, "rotation", 0.15, 3.0)
	tw.tween_property(spr, "rotation", -0.15, 3.0)

class_name TeletubbiesTextureFactory
extends RefCounted

static func create_noo_noo_texture(level: int) -> ImageTexture:
	var img = Image.create(32, 32, false, Image.FORMAT_RGBA8)
	var body_color: Color
	match level:
		1: body_color = Color(0.12, 0.35, 0.85, 1.0) # Classic Navy Blue
		2: body_color = Color(0.95, 0.45, 0.30, 1.0) # Revival Pink/Orange
		_: body_color = Color(0.85, 0.75, 0.20, 1.0) # Golden Noo-Noo

	var silver = Color(0.75, 0.78, 0.82, 1.0)
	var eye_w  = Color(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0)
	var eye_b  = Color(0.05, 0.05, 0.05, 1.0)

	for x in range(32):
		for y in range(32):
			var dist = (x - 16)*(x - 16) + (y - 18)*(y - 18)
			# Domed main body
			if dist <= 120 and y >= 10:
				img.set_pixel(x, y, body_color)
			# Googly eyes on stalks
			if (y in range(5, 10) and (x in range(9, 14) or x in range(18, 23))):
				img.set_pixel(x, y, eye_w)
			if (y in range(6, 8) and (x == 11 or x == 20)):
				img.set_pixel(x, y, eye_b)
			# Flared trumpet snout
			if (y in range(18, 26) and x in range(13, 19)) or (y in range(23, 27) and x in range(11, 21)):
				img.set_pixel(x, y, silver)

	return ImageTexture.create_from_image(img)

static func create_baby_sun_texture(yawning: bool) -> ImageTexture:
	var img = Image.create(44, 44, false, Image.FORMAT_RGBA8)
	var sun_gold = Color(1.0, 0.85, 0.20, 1.0)
	var ray_color = Color(1.0, 0.65, 0.15, 0.9)
	var cheek_pink = Color(1.0, 0.60, 0.65, 1.0)
	var face_dark = Color(0.35, 0.15, 0.05, 1.0)

	for x in range(44):
		for y in range(44):
			var dist = (x - 22)*(x - 22) + (y - 22)*(y - 22)
			# Radiant outer corona
			if dist <= 380 and dist > 225 and (x + y) % 3 == 0:
				img.set_pixel(x, y, ray_color)
			# Inner golden face disk
			if dist <= 225:
				img.set_pixel(x, y, sun_gold)
			# Rosy baby cheeks
			if dist <= 180 and y in range(23, 27) and (x in range(13, 17) or x in range(27, 31)):
				img.set_pixel(x, y, cheek_pink)
			# Baby eyes
			if dist <= 180 and y in range(17, 20) and (x in range(15, 19) or x in range(25, 29)):
				img.set_pixel(x, y, face_dark)
			# Mouth: smiling or yawning
			if dist <= 180:
				if yawning:
					if y in range(24, 30) and x in range(19, 25):
						img.set_pixel(x, y, face_dark)
				else:
					if y == 26 and x in range(18, 26):
						img.set_pixel(x, y, face_dark)

	return ImageTexture.create_from_image(img)

class_name ThomasTrainManager
extends Node2D

var left_stop: float = 64.0
var right_stop: float = 880.0
var speed: float = 140.0
var direction: float = 1.0

@export var loco_tier: int = 1
@export var wagon_count: int = 2
@export var wagon_capacity: float = 150.0

var current_haul: float = 0.0
var loco_sprite: Sprite2D
var wagons: Array[Sprite2D] = []

func _ready() -> void:
	z_index = 20
	position = Vector2(left_stop, 24.0)

	loco_sprite = Sprite2D.new()
	loco_sprite.texture = _generate_loco_texture(loco_tier)
	loco_sprite.scale = Vector2(1.8, 1.8)
	call_deferred("add_child", loco_sprite)
	
	for i in range(wagon_count):
		var wag = Sprite2D.new()
		wag.texture = _generate_wagon_texture()
		wag.scale = Vector2(1.8, 1.8)
		wag.position = Vector2(-40.0 - (i * 36.0), 0.0)
		wagons.append(wag)
		call_deferred("add_child", wag)

func _physics_process(delta: float) -> void:
	# Locomotive Upgrade logic: Speed scales with tier (140 -> 260)
	var current_speed = speed + (loco_tier * 40.0)
	position.x += current_speed * direction * delta
	
	if position.x >= right_stop:
		direction = -1.0
		loco_sprite.flip_h = true
	elif position.x <= left_stop:
		direction = 1.0
		loco_sprite.flip_h = false
		_unload_haul()
		
func _unload_haul() -> void:
	if current_haul > 0.0:
		if Gvars and ("cash" in Gvars):
			Gvars.cash += current_haul
		current_haul = 0.0

func _generate_loco_texture(tier: int) -> ImageTexture:
	var img = Image.create(32, 16, false, Image.FORMAT_RGBA8)
	var color = Color.CORNFLOWER_BLUE # Thomas
	if tier == 2: color = Color.STEEL_BLUE # Edward
	elif tier == 3: color = Color.FOREST_GREEN # Henry
	elif tier >= 4: color = Color.ROYAL_BLUE # Gordon
	img.fill(color)
	return ImageTexture.create_from_image(img)

func _generate_wagon_texture() -> ImageTexture:
	var img = Image.create(24, 12, false, Image.FORMAT_RGBA8)
	img.fill(Color.SADDLE_BROWN)
	return ImageTexture.create_from_image(img)

# Elemental Tick Upgrades (`gareth-tick_upgrade_mod`)

**Author:** Gareth White  
**Original Architecture & Credit:** Der-Floh (`der_floh-tick_upgrade_mod`)  
**Compatibility:** Coal LLC v1.4.1.1+ (ModLoader 7.0.1)

---

## Overview
This mod provides dynamic tick rate acceleration for poison and burn damage-over-time status effects applied to subterranean rock strata.

---

## Credit & Enhancements Over Original
This package is an enhanced fork of the original `der_floh-tick_upgrade_mod`. We express full credit and appreciation to **Der-Floh** for designing the foundational tick acceleration engine.

### Key Modifications:
1. **Packaging & Archive Normalization:** Repackaged with strict POSIX forward slashes and root `manifest.json` placement, eliminating ModLoader 7.0.1 directory parsing errors.
2. **Safe Passive Selection:** Overrides `choose_passive.gd` hook routines to preserve all 32 base passives when starting custom classes (like Knight or Void Siphon) with empty `unlocked_passives` arrays.
3. **Class Compatibility Whitelists:** Integrated with the Universal Mod Manager to respect per-class weapon restrictions and runtime mutator toggles.
--------

# Subterranean Vacuum Collector Depot

**Namespace:** $namespace  
**Compatibility:** Coal LLC v1.4.1.1+ (ModLoader 7.0.1)

---

## Overview
Allows Collector UFO drones to offload minerals directly into subterranean vacuum nozzles when closer than the surface, eliminating round-trip flight times.

---

## Technical Details (For Modders)
Hooks LootCollectorUFO.gd _physics_process to route drone pathfinding toward the nearest active vacuum nozzle.

---

## Installation
1. Ensure **Coal LLC** has Godot ModLoader 7.0.1 installed.
2. Place the packaged .zip file into your Coal LLC/mods/ directory.
3. Manage or toggle this mod directly via the in-game **Mod Manager**.

	var mod_dir_path: String = ModLoaderMod.get_unpacked_dir().path_join(MOD_DIR)
	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks(
		"res://scenes/equipment/vacuum_cleaner.gd",
		mod_dir_path.path_join("extensions/scenes/equipment/vacuum_cleaner.hooks.gd")
	)
	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks(
		"res://scripts/LootCollectorUFO.gd",
		mod_dir_path.path_join("extensions/scripts/LootCollectorUFO.hooks.gd")
	)

	ModLoaderLog.info("Vacuum Collector Depot mod initialized successfully.", MOD_DIR)

## Overview

## Technical Details (For Modders)

## Installation

extends Object

func _physics_process(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, delta: float) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([delta])
	var vac = chain.reference_object
	if vac and not vac.is_in_group("vacuum_cleaner"):
		vac.add_to_group("vacuum_cleaner")

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-vacuum_depot_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-vacuum_depot_mod\extensions\scripts\LootCollectorUFO.hooks.gd

extends Object

const DEFAULT_BASE := Vector2(50.0, -55.0)

func check_inventory_count(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	var ufo = chain.reference_object
	if not is_instance_valid(ufo) or not ("inventory" in ufo) or not ("maxInventoryCount" in ufo):
		chain.execute_next([])
		return

	if "StateLabel" in ufo and is_instance_valid(ufo.StateLabel):
		ufo.StateLabel.text = Gconsts.add_comma_to_float_no_dp(ufo.maxInventoryCount)

	var capacity_mult: float = 1.0
	if Gvars and ("passives" in Gvars) and Gvars.passives:
		capacity_mult += Gvars.passives.employee_collector_capacity

	var max_cap: float = float(ufo.maxInventoryCount) * capacity_mult
	if float(ufo.inventory.inventoryCount) >= max_cap:
		ufo.set_deferred("monitorable", false)
		if "States" in ufo and ("RETURNING_TO_BASE" in ufo.States):
			ufo.state = ufo.States.RETURNING_TO_BASE
		if "baseCoords" in ufo and "models" in ufo and is_instance_valid(ufo.models):
			if ufo.baseCoords.x < ufo.global_position.x:
				ufo.models.scale.x = -1
			else:
				ufo.models.scale.x = 1

func _physics_process(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, delta: float) -> void:
	var ufo = chain.reference_object
	if not is_instance_valid(ufo):
		chain.execute_next([delta])
		return

	# State 2: States.RETURNING_TO_BASE
	if ufo.state == 2:
		var tree = ufo.get_tree()
		if tree:
			var target_dest := DEFAULT_BASE
			var vacs = tree.get_nodes_in_group("vacuum_cleaner")
			if vacs.size() > 0:
				var vac = vacs[0]
				if is_instance_valid(vac) and ("suck_end_physics_body" in vac) and vac.suck_end_physics_body:
					var nozzle_pos: Vector2 = vac.suck_end_physics_body.global_position
					var dist_to_base: float = ufo.global_position.distance_squared_to(DEFAULT_BASE)
					var dist_to_vac: float = ufo.global_position.distance_squared_to(nozzle_pos)
					if dist_to_vac < dist_to_base:
						target_dest = nozzle_pos

			if target_dest != DEFAULT_BASE:
				ufo.baseCoords = target_dest
				if ufo.global_position.distance_to(target_dest) <= 24.0:
					ufo.drop_off_in_stockpile()
					ufo.global_position = target_dest
					if "timer" in ufo and ufo.timer:
						ufo.timer.start(0.066)
					ufo.state = 3 # States.IDLE
					return
				else:
					var spd: float = ufo.moveSpeed * min(8.0, (1.0 + Gvars.passives.employee_collector_speed))
					ufo.movement_vector = (target_dest - ufo.global_position).normalized() * spd
					ufo.pass_point_positive = (target_dest.x - ufo.global_position.x) > 0
					ufo.y_pass_point_positive = (target_dest.y - ufo.global_position.y) > 0
					if "models" in ufo and is_instance_valid(ufo.models):
						if target_dest.x < ufo.global_position.x:
							ufo.models.scale.x = -1
						else:
							ufo.models.scale.x = 1
			else:
				ufo.baseCoords = DEFAULT_BASE
	elif ufo.state != 2 and ufo.baseCoords != DEFAULT_BASE:
		ufo.baseCoords = DEFAULT_BASE

	chain.execute_next([delta])

var starter_weapon = null

const TIER_DATA := [
	{ "id": "01_shoddy",      "name": "Shoddy Void Core",        "dmg": 120.0,   "rad": 3.5,  "price": 0.0 },

	{ "id": "14_moonstone",   "name": "Moonstone Void Core",     "dmg": 21000.0, "rad": 12.6, "price": 180000000000.0 },
	{ "id": "15_onyx",        "name": "Onyx Void Core",          "dmg": 35000.0, "rad": 14.5, "price": 1500000000000.0 }
]

func _load_image_texture(path: String) -> ImageTexture:
	if not FileAccess.file_exists(path):
		return null
	var img = Image.load_from_file(path)
	if not img:
		return null
	return ImageTexture.create_from_image(img)

func _ready() -> void:
	_register_weapon_tiers()
	_register_profession()

func _register_weapon_tiers() -> void:
	var item_script = load("res://resources/Items/Scripts/Item.gd")
	var buy_script = load("res://resources/BuyEffects/Scripts/AddToInventory.gd")
	var pickup_script = load("res://resources/PickupEffects/Scripts/PickupToInventory.gd")
	var equip_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-void_siphon_mod/scripts/void_siphon_equip.gd")
	var pickup_sound = load("res://assets/sound/sound effects/SwordThud.wav")

	var item_unlocks = load("res://resources/unlockable_items/scripts/item_unlocks.tres")

	for t in TIER_DATA:
		var item = item_script.new()
		item.itemID = "void_core_" + t["id"]
		item.itemName = t["name"]
		item.itemDescription = "Gravitational singularity harvester.\nTriggers localized implosions and vacuums nearby ore.\nDamage: %d | Implosion Radius: %.1f tiles" % [int(t["dmg"]), float(t["rad"])]

		var tex_path := "res://mods-unpacked/gareth-void_siphon_mod/textures/void_core_%s.png" % t["id"]
		item.itemTexture = _load_image_texture(tex_path)

		var equip = equip_script.new()
		equip.damage = t["dmg"]
		equip.radius = t["rad"]
		item.itemEquipEffect = equip

		var buy = buy_script.new()
		item.itemBuyEffect = buy
		var pickup = pickup_script.new()
		item.itemPickupEffect = pickup
		item.pickupSound = pickup_sound

		item.itemBuyPrice = t["price"]
		item.itemSellPrice = t["price"] * 0.5
		item.itemUnlocked = true
		item.itemType = "void_core"

		all_void_items.append(item)

		if item_unlocks:
			if not (item in item_unlocks.all_equipment_items):
				item_unlocks.all_equipment_items.append(item)

	if all_void_items.size() > 0:
		starter_weapon = all_void_items[0]

	ModLoaderLog.info("15 Void Siphon Core tiers registered into equipment shop.", MOD_DIR)

func _register_profession() -> void:
	if not Gvars or not ("profession_manager" in Gvars) or not Gvars.profession_manager:
		return
	var pm = Gvars.profession_manager
	var prof_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-void_siphon_mod/scripts/void_siphon_profession.gd")
	var p = prof_script.new()
	p.profession_name = "Void Siphon"
	p.description = "Wielder of cosmic singularities.\nTriggers gravitational implosions that crush stone and vacuum ore.\nStarts with the Shoddy Void Core."

	var icon_path := "res://mods-unpacked/gareth-void_siphon_mod/textures/void_siphon_icon.png"
	var icon_tex = _load_image_texture(icon_path)
	if icon_tex:
		p.image = icon_tex

	pm.all_professions[105] = { "profession": p, "is_unlocked": true }
	ModLoaderLog.info("Void Siphon Class (ID 105) registered cleanly.", MOD_DIR)

# Void Siphon Class Mod for Coal LLC

FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-void_siphon_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-void_siphon_mod\scripts\void_siphon_equip.gd
==================================================

class_name VoidSiphonEquipEffect
extends EquipEffect

@export var damage: float = 120.0
@export var radius: float = 3.5

var weapon_visual: Sprite2D = null
var pulse_cooldown: float = 0.0
var _is_using: bool = false
var _auto_pulse_toggle: bool = false
var audio_player: AudioStreamPlayer2D = null

func start(player, item):
	if "tool" in player and player.tool:
		player.tool.visible = false

	if ResourceLoader.exists("res://assets/sound/sound effects/SwordThud.wav"):
		audio_player = AudioStreamPlayer2D.new()
		audio_player.stream = load("res://assets/sound/sound effects/SwordThud.wav")
		player.add_child(audio_player)

	weapon_visual = Sprite2D.new()
	weapon_visual.texture = item.itemTexture
	weapon_visual.offset = Vector2(0, -4)
	weapon_visual.position = Vector2(4, 2)
	weapon_visual.visible = true
	player.add_child(weapon_visual)
	_is_using = false
	_auto_pulse_toggle = false

func end(player):
	if "tool" in player and player.tool:
		player.tool.visible = true
	if audio_player and is_instance_valid(audio_player):
		audio_player.queue_free()
	if weapon_visual and is_instance_valid(weapon_visual):
		weapon_visual.queue_free()
	_is_using = false
	_auto_pulse_toggle = false

func physics_process(player, delta):
	if pulse_cooldown > 0.0:
		pulse_cooldown -= delta

	if weapon_visual and is_instance_valid(weapon_visual):
		weapon_visual.rotation += delta * 4.0

	if Input.is_action_pressed("useItem") or Input.is_mouse_button_pressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT):
		_is_using = true
	elif Input.is_action_just_released("useItem") or (not Input.is_mouse_button_pressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT) and not Input.is_mouse_button_pressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_RIGHT) and not _auto_pulse_toggle):
		_is_using = false

	if Input.is_action_just_pressed("toggle_use_item") or Input.is_action_just_pressed("rightMouseClick"):
		_auto_pulse_toggle = not _auto_pulse_toggle
		_is_using = _auto_pulse_toggle

	if Input.is_mouse_button_pressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_RIGHT):
		_is_using = true

	if _is_using and pulse_cooldown <= 0.0:
		_trigger_singularity_implosion(player)

func _get_passive_stat(stat_name: String) -> float:
	if not Gvars or not ("passives" in Gvars) or not Gvars.passives:
		return 0.0
	var p = Gvars.passives
	if p.has_meta(stat_name):
		return float(p.get_meta(stat_name))
	if stat_name in p:
		return float(p.get(stat_name))
	return 0.0

func _trigger_singularity_implosion(player) -> void:
	pulse_cooldown = 0.38
	if audio_player and is_instance_valid(audio_player):
		audio_player.pitch_scale = randf_range(0.65, 0.8)
		audio_player.play()

	var p_pos: Vector2 = player.global_position
	var pointer_pos: Vector2 = player.pointer_global_pos
	var dir: Vector2 = (pointer_pos - p_pos).normalized()
	var center_pos: Vector2 = p_pos + (dir * 48.0)

	var tilemap = player.tilemap
	if not tilemap:
		return

	var dmg_mult: float = 1.0 + _get_passive_stat("void_implosion_damage")
	var eff_dmg: float = damage * dmg_mult

	var rad_mult: float = 1.0 + _get_passive_stat("void_pull_radius")
	var eff_rad: float = radius * rad_mult

	var cell_center: Vector2i = tilemap.local_to_map(center_pos)
	var r_int: int = int(ceil(eff_rad))
	for dx in range(-r_int, r_int + 1):
		for dy in range(-r_int, r_int + 1):
			if Vector2(dx, dy).length() <= eff_rad:
				var cell: Vector2i = cell_center + Vector2i(dx, dy)
				if tilemap.is_tile_breakable(cell):
					tilemap.damageTileCoords(cell, eff_dmg)

	var items = player.get_tree().get_nodes_in_group("pickups")
	for item_node in items:
		if is_instance_valid(item_node) and "global_position" in item_node:
			if (item_node.global_position - center_pos).length() <= (eff_rad * 24.0):
				var pull_dir = (p_pos - item_node.global_position).normalized()
				if "velocity" in item_node:
					item_node.velocity += pull_dir * 320.0
				else:
					item_node.global_position += pull_dir * 12.0

	_spawn_singularity_visual(player, center_pos, eff_rad * 14.0)

func _spawn_singularity_visual(player: Node2D, at_pos: Vector2, max_radius: float) -> void:
	var parent = player.get_parent()
	if not parent:
		parent = player

	var ring := Line2D.new()
	ring.width = 3.5
	ring.default_color = Color(0.7, 0.2, 0.95, 0.95)
	var segs: int = 18
	for i in range(segs + 1):
		var ang: float = float(i) * TAU / float(segs)
		ring.add_point(at_pos + Vector2(cos(ang), sin(ang)) * max_radius)
	parent.add_child(ring)

	var tween = parent.create_tween()
	tween.set_parallel(true)
	tween.tween_property(ring, "scale", Vector2(0.1, 0.1), 0.18)
	tween.tween_property(ring, "modulate:a", 0.0, 0.18)
	tween.set_parallel(false)
	tween.tween_callback(ring.queue_free)

class_name VoidSiphonProfession
extends Profession

func _profession_effect(data: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	var mod_node = Engine.get_main_loop().root.get_node_or_null("ModLoader/gareth-void_siphon_mod")
	if mod_node and "starter_weapon" in mod_node and mod_node.starter_weapon:
		if data.has("inventory_starters"):
			data["inventory_starters"] = [{
				"item": mod_node.starter_weapon,
				"count": 1
			}]

	if not data.has("items_locked"):
		data["items_locked"] = []
	data["items_locked"].append_array([
		"pickaxe", "axe", "battleaxe", "drill", "pistol", "shotgun", "rifle",
		"minigun", "staff", "water_staff", "poison_staff", "bomb", "mortar_gun",
		"tank", "jet", "orb", "laser", "flamethrower", "water_gun", "poison_gun",
		"boxing_glove", "roundhouse_kick", "turret", "sword", "bouncing_ball", "fracker"
	])

	if data.has("unlocked_passives"):
		data["unlocked_passives"] = [
			"void_implosion_damage",
			"void_pull_radius",
			"void_event_horizon",
			"player_sprint_speed",
			"player_jump_velocity",
			"player_climb_speed"
		]

	return data

func _load_image_texture(path: String) -> Texture2D:
	if FileAccess.file_exists(path):
		var buffer = FileAccess.get_file_as_bytes(path)
		if buffer.size() > 0:
			var img = Image.new()
			if img.load_png_from_buffer(buffer) == OK:
				return ImageTexture.create_from_image(img)
	return null

func _ready() -> void:
	var item_script = load("res://resources/Items/Scripts/Item.gd")
	var buy_script = load("res://resources/BuyEffects/Scripts/AddToInventory.gd")
	var pickup_script = load("res://resources/PickupEffects/Scripts/PickupToInventory.gd")
	var equip_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-water_spirit_mod/scripts/water_spirit_equip.gd")
	var pickup_sound = load("res://assets/sound/sound effects/pickup.wav")
	var item_unlocks = load("res://resources/unlockable_items/scripts/item_unlocks.tres")

	if not item_unlocks:
		return

	for t in TIER_DATA:
		var item = item_script.new()
		item.itemID = "water_spirit_" + t["id"]
		item.itemName = t["name"]
		item.itemDescription = "Luminous elemental fairy companion.\nOrbits player (radius: %.0fpx) and cleanses surrounding strata.\nToggle with hotkey or Left-Click." % float(t["radius"])
		item.itemType = "water_spirit"
		item.itemBuyPrice = t["price"]
		item.itemSellPrice = t["price"] * 0.5
		item.itemUnlocked = true
		item.pickupSound = pickup_sound

		var res_path := "res://mods-unpacked/gareth-water_spirit_mod/items/water_spirit_%s.tres" % t["id"]
		item.resource_path = res_path
		item.take_over_path(res_path)

		var tex_path := "res://mods-unpacked/gareth-water_spirit_mod/textures/spirit_%s.png" % t["id"]
		item.itemTexture = _load_image_texture(tex_path)

		var equip = equip_script.new()
		equip.orbit_radius = t["radius"]
		equip.rotation_speed = t["speed"]
		equip.brush_radius = t["brush"]
		item.itemEquipEffect = equip

		item.itemBuyEffect = buy_script.new()
		item.itemPickupEffect = pickup_script.new()

		var already_registered := false
		for existing in item_unlocks.all_equipment_items:
			if existing and existing.itemID == item.itemID:
				already_registered = true
				break
		if not already_registered:
			item_unlocks.all_equipment_items.append(item)

	ModLoaderLog.info("15 Water Spirit Fairy tiers loaded programmatically with custom fairy textures.", MOD_DIR)

class_name WaterSpiritEquipEffect
extends EquipEffect

@export var orbit_radius: float = 48.0
@export var rotation_speed: float = 12.0
@export var brush_radius: int = 1

var held_item_texture: Texture2D = null

func start(_player, item):
	if item and item.itemTexture:
		held_item_texture = item.itemTexture

func end(_player):
	# Persistent companion pattern: hotbar swapping leaves the spirit node active
	pass

func physics_process(player, _delta):
	if not player:
		return

	if Input.is_action_just_pressed("useItem") or Input.is_action_just_pressed("toggle_use_item"):
		var existing = player.get_node_or_null("ActiveWaterSpirit")
		if existing and is_instance_valid(existing):
			existing.queue_free()
		else:
			_spawn_spirit(player)

func _spawn_spirit(player) -> void:
	var spirit_script = load("res://mods-unpacked/gareth-water_spirit_mod/scripts/water_spirit_node.gd")
	var spirit = Node2D.new()
	spirit.name = "ActiveWaterSpirit"
	spirit.set_script(spirit_script)
	spirit.orbit_radius = orbit_radius
	spirit.rotation_speed = rotation_speed
	spirit.brush_radius = brush_radius
	spirit.player = player
	spirit.fairy_texture = held_item_texture
	if "tilemap" in player:
		spirit.tilemap = player.tilemap

	player.call_deferred("add_child", spirit)

class_name WaterSpiritNode
extends Node2D

var orbit_radius: float = 48.0
var rotation_speed: float = 12.0
var brush_radius: int = 1
var fairy_texture: Texture2D = null

var tilemap = null
var player = null
var current_rotation: float = 0.0

var centre: Node2D
var orb: Sprite2D
var trail: Sprite2D

func _ready() -> void:
	z_index = 50

	centre = Node2D.new()
	centre.name = "Centre"
	add_child(centre)

	var use_texture = fairy_texture
	if not use_texture:
		use_texture = load("res://assets/Equipment/kicks/kick_04_sapphire.png")

	orb = Sprite2D.new()
	orb.name = "Orb"
	orb.texture = use_texture
	orb.scale = Vector2(1.5, 1.5)
	orb.position = Vector2(orbit_radius, 0.0)
	centre.add_child(orb)

	trail = Sprite2D.new()
	trail.name = "Trail"
	trail.texture = use_texture
	trail.modulate = Color(0.1, 0.6, 1.0, 0.4)
	trail.scale = Vector2(1.0, 1.0)
	trail.position = Vector2(orbit_radius * 0.95, -12.0)
	centre.add_child(trail)

	_resolve_tilemap()

func _resolve_tilemap() -> void:
	if player and ("tilemap" in player) and player.tilemap:
		tilemap = player.tilemap
		return
	var tree = get_tree()
	if tree:
		var p = tree.get_first_node_in_group("player")
		if p and ("tilemap" in p) and p.tilemap:
			tilemap = p.tilemap
			return
		if Game and ("current_node" in Game) and Game.current_node:
			if "tilemap" in Game.current_node and Game.current_node.tilemap:
				tilemap = Game.current_node.tilemap

func _physics_process(delta: float) -> void:
	current_rotation += rotation_speed * delta
	if centre:
		centre.rotation = current_rotation

	if not tilemap:
		_resolve_tilemap()
		if not tilemap:
			return

	if orb:
		var orb_pos: Vector2 = orb.global_position
		if tilemap.has_method("local_to_map") and tilemap.has_method("wet_tile_coords"):
			var center_tile: Vector2i = tilemap.local_to_map(orb_pos)
			for dx in range(-brush_radius, brush_radius + 1):
				for dy in range(-brush_radius, brush_radius + 1):
					if (dx * dx + dy * dy) <= (brush_radius * brush_radius):
						tilemap.wet_tile_coords(center_tile + Vector2i(dx, dy))
		elif tilemap.has_method("wet_tile"):
			tilemap.wet_tile(orb_pos)

# Multiplicative Weapon Stacker

**Namespace:** $namespace  
**Compatibility:** Coal LLC v1.4.1.1+ (ModLoader 7.0.1)

---

## Overview
Purchasing duplicate weapons in the shop multiplicatively scales their stats. Miniguns reduce cooldowns down to 0.01s while firing walls of lead; water guns expand spray radii logarithmically to soak entire shafts in a single blast.

---

## Technical Details (For Modders)
Hooks gun.gd and water_gun.gd in physics_process, querying inventory item counts and multiplying projectile quantities and cooldowns accordingly.

---

## Installation
1. Ensure **Coal LLC** has Godot ModLoader 7.0.1 installed.
2. Place the packaged .zip file into your Coal LLC/mods/ directory.
3. Manage or toggle this mod directly via the in-game **Mod Manager**.

const MOD_DIR := "gareth-weapon_stacker_mod"
const LOG_NAME := "gareth-weapon_stacker_mod:Main"

func _init() -> void:
	var mod_dir_path: String = ModLoaderMod.get_unpacked_dir().path_join(MOD_DIR)
	var ext_path: String = mod_dir_path.path_join("extensions/resources/EquipEffects/Scripts")

		"res://resources/EquipEffects/Scripts/gun.gd",
		ext_path.path_join("gun.hooks.gd")
	)
	ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks(
		"res://resources/EquipEffects/Scripts/water_gun.gd",
		ext_path.path_join("water_gun.hooks.gd")
	)
	ModLoaderLog.info("Hooks successfully installed for gun.gd and water_gun.gd", LOG_NAME)

func _ready() -> void:
	ModLoaderLog.info("Weapon Stacker Mod active and ready!", LOG_NAME)

# Multiplicative Weapon Stacker

**Namespace:** $namespace  
**Compatibility:** Coal LLC v1.4.1.1+ (ModLoader 7.0.1)

---

## Overview
Purchasing duplicate weapons in the shop multiplicatively scales their stats. Miniguns reduce cooldowns down to 0.01s while firing walls of lead; water guns expand spray radii logarithmically to soak entire shafts in a single blast.

---

## Technical Details (For Modders)
Hooks gun.gd and water_gun.gd in physics_process, querying inventory item counts and multiplying projectile quantities and cooldowns accordingly.

---

## Installation
1. Ensure **Coal LLC** has Godot ModLoader 7.0.1 installed.
2. Place the packaged .zip file into your Coal LLC/mods/ directory.
3. Manage or toggle this mod directly via the in-game **Mod Manager**.
--------
==================================================
FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-weapon_stacker_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-weapon_stacker_mod\extensions\resources\EquipEffects\Scripts\gun.hooks.gd
==================================================
--------
extends Object

func start(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, player, item) -> void:
	if chain and chain.reference_object:
		chain.reference_object.set_meta("equipped_item", item)
	chain.execute_next([player, item])

func end(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, player) -> void:
	if chain and chain.reference_object:
		chain.reference_object.set_meta("equipped_item", null)
	chain.execute_next([player])

func physics_process(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, player, delta) -> void:
	var gun = chain.reference_object
	if not gun:
		chain.execute_next([player, delta])
		return

	var stack: float = _get_gun_stack(gun, player)
	if stack <= 1.0:
		chain.execute_next([player, delta])
		return

	var orig_damage: float = gun.bullet_damage
	var orig_count: int = gun.bullet_count
	var orig_cooldown: float = gun.cooldown_time
	var orig_speed: float = gun.bullet_speed

	var raw_bullets: float = float(orig_count) * stack
	var capped_count: int = int(clamp(round(raw_bullets), 5, 40))
	var bullet_ratio: float = raw_bullets / float(capped_count)
	var scaled_damage: float = orig_damage * stack * bullet_ratio
	var scaled_cooldown: float = max(orig_cooldown / stack, 0.01)

	# Compensate for player_2.gd deducting 5 speed per bullet so the slowest pellet never goes negative
	var required_speed_boost: float = float(capped_count) * 5.5
	var scaled_speed: float = orig_speed + required_speed_boost

	gun.bullet_damage = scaled_damage
	gun.bullet_count = capped_count
	gun.cooldown_time = scaled_cooldown
	gun.bullet_speed = scaled_speed

	if Input.is_action_just_pressed("useItem"):
		ModLoaderLog.info("[Minigun Stacker x%d] Dmg: %.1f | Bullets: %d | Delay: %.4fs (~%.1f bursts/sec) | Forward Speed: %.0f" % [int(stack), scaled_damage, capped_count, scaled_cooldown, 1.0 / scaled_cooldown, scaled_speed], "gareth-weapon_stacker_mod")

	chain.execute_next([player, delta])

	gun.bullet_damage = orig_damage
	gun.bullet_count = orig_count
	gun.cooldown_time = orig_cooldown
	gun.bullet_speed = orig_speed

func _get_gun_stack(gun, player) -> float:
	var item = gun.get_meta("equipped_item", null)
	var inv = null
	if player and "inventory" in player and player.inventory:
		inv = player.inventory
	else:
		inv = load("res://resources/Inventories/PlayerInventory.tres")

	if not inv or not ("items" in inv) or not inv.items:
		return 1.0

	if item and inv.has_method("count_item"):
		var c: float = inv.count_item(item)
		if c > 0.0:
			return c

	var total: float = 0.0
	for slot in inv.items:
		if slot and slot.item:
			if item and slot.item.itemID == item.itemID:
				total += float(slot.count)
			elif slot.item.get("itemType") == "minigun":
				total += float(slot.count)

	return max(total, 1.0)


--------
==================================================
FILE: mods-unpacked\gareth-weapon_stacker_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-weapon_stacker_mod\extensions\resources\EquipEffects\Scripts\water_gun.hooks.gd
==================================================
--------
extends Object

func start(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, player, item) -> void:
	if chain and chain.reference_object:
		chain.reference_object.set_meta("equipped_item", item)
	chain.execute_next([player, item])

func end(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, player) -> void:
	if chain and chain.reference_object:
		chain.reference_object.set_meta("equipped_item", null)
	chain.execute_next([player])

func physics_process(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, player, delta) -> void:
	var water_gun = chain.reference_object
	if not water_gun:
		chain.execute_next([player, delta])
		return

	var stack: float = _get_water_stack(water_gun, player)
	if stack <= 1.0:
		chain.execute_next([player, delta])
		return

	var orig_radius: int = water_gun.radius
	var scaled_radius: int = int(max(orig_radius + int(floor(stack * 0.5)) - 1, orig_radius))
	water_gun.radius = scaled_radius

	if Input.is_action_just_pressed("useItem"):
		ModLoaderLog.info("[WaterGun Stacker x%d] Spray Radius: %d | Pressure Dmg: %d" % [int(stack), scaled_radius, int(stack * 10)], "gareth-weapon_stacker_mod")

	chain.execute_next([player, delta])

	water_gun.radius = orig_radius

func _get_water_stack(water_gun, player) -> float:
	var item = water_gun.get_meta("equipped_item", null)
	var inv = null
	if player and "inventory" in player and player.inventory:
		inv = player.inventory
	else:
		inv = load("res://resources/Inventories/PlayerInventory.tres")

	if not inv or not ("items" in inv) or not inv.items:
		return 1.0

	if item and inv.has_method("count_item"):
		var c: float = inv.count_item(item)
		if c > 0.0:
			return c

	var total: float = 0.0
	for slot in inv.items:
		if slot and slot.item:
			if item and slot.item.itemID == item.itemID:
				total += float(slot.count)
			elif slot.item.get("itemType") == "water_gun":
				total += float(slot.count)

	return max(total, 1.0)

FILE: mods-unpacked\nanobotz-autopassivechooser\mods-unpacked\nanobotz-autopassivechooser\mod_main.gd
==================================================
--------
extends Node

const MOD_ID := "nanobotz-autopassivechooser"
const MOD_DIR := "nanobotz-autopassivechooser"
const LOG_NAME := "nanobotz-autopassivechooser:Main"

var mod_dir_path: String
var extensions_dir_path: String

var reroll_if_no_matches: bool = true
var passive_order: Array = []


func _init() -> void:
	mod_dir_path = ModLoaderMod.get_unpacked_dir().path_join(MOD_DIR)
	extensions_dir_path = mod_dir_path.path_join("extensions")
	install_hooks()


func _ready() -> void:
	ModLoaderLog.info("Ready!", LOG_NAME)


static func get_current_profession_or_default() -> String:
	if Game.player and Game.player.profession and Game.player.profession.profession_name:
		var player: Player = Game.player
		return player.profession.profession_name.to_lower()
	else:
		return Gvars.get("profession_manager").INTERNSHIP.profession_name.to_lower()


static func get_or_create_config(player_profession: String = "") -> ModConfig:
	if not player_profession:
		player_profession = get_current_profession_or_default()

	if ModLoaderConfig.has_config(MOD_ID, player_profession):
		return ModLoaderConfig.get_config(MOD_ID, player_profession)
	else:
		return ModLoaderConfig.create_config(MOD_ID, player_profession, ModLoaderConfig.get_default_config(MOD_ID).data)


func install_hooks() -> void:
	var hook_files = [
		["res://scenes/Interfaces/Menus/settings_2.gd", "scenes/Interfaces/Menus/settings_2.hooks.gd"],
		["res://scenes/Interfaces/in_game/choose_passive.gd", "scenes/Interfaces/in_game/choose_passive.hooks.gd"],
		["res://scenes/tilemaps/tile_map_chunk.gd", "scenes/tilemaps/tile_map_chunk.hooks.gd"],
		["res://scripts/StateMachine/Player2/player_2.gd", "scripts/StateMachine/Player2/player_2.hooks.gd"],
		["res://scripts/Chest.gd", "scripts/Chest.hooks.gd"]
	]

	for item in hook_files:
		var target = item[0]
		var hook_path = extensions_dir_path.path_join(item[1])
		var script_res = load(hook_path)
		if script_res == null:
			ModLoaderLog.error("FAILED to load script resource: %s" % hook_path, LOG_NAME)
		elif not (script_res is GDScript):
			ModLoaderLog.error("Loaded resource is NOT GDScript: %s (type: %s)" % [hook_path, script_res.get_class()], LOG_NAME)
		else:
			var method_names = []
			for m in script_res.get_script_method_list():
				method_names.append(m.name)
			ModLoaderLog.info("Loaded hook script %s with methods: %s" % [item[1], str(method_names)], LOG_NAME)

		ModLoaderMod.install_script_hooks(target, hook_path)

FILE: mods-unpacked\nanobotz-autopassivechooser\mods-unpacked\nanobotz-autopassivechooser\README.md
==================================================
--------
# Overview

This mod adds an option to automatically open chests when you break one of the surrounding hard tiles.  
You can customize the priority of passives chosen by going to Settings -> PassiveOrder.

# Installation:
Place the zip in your game's mods directory, for example:  
`C:\Program Files (x86)\Steam\steamapps\common\Coal LLC\Coal LLC\mods`

# Mod Loader
This mod assumes you're using this fork of godot-mod-loader:  
https://github.com/NanobotZ/godot-mod-loader
--------
