FILE: mods-unpacked\nanobotz-autopassivechooser\mods-unpacked\nanobotz-autopassivechooser\extensions\scenes\Interfaces\in_game\choose_passive.hooks.gd
==================================================
--------
extends Object

var NanobotzAutoPassiveChooserMod = load("res://mods-unpacked/nanobotz-autopassivechooser/mod_main.gd")


func _process(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, delta: float) -> void:
	chain.execute_next([delta])

	var config = NanobotzAutoPassiveChooserMod.get_or_create_config()
	var passive_order = config.data.get("passive_order", []) if (config and "data" in config and config.data is Dictionary) else []
	var reroll_if_no_matches = config.data.reroll_if_no_matches

	var find_passive_idx := func (choose_between: Array) -> int:
		for passive in passive_order:
			# Respect the checkbox: never auto-pick disabled passives
			if not passive.get("enabled", false):
				continue

			for i in range(0, 3):
				var eff: String = choose_between[i]
				if eff == passive.effect:
					# Safe lookup across vanilla passives and mod custom_passives
					var cur_val: float = 0.0
					if Gvars and ("passives" in Gvars) and Gvars.passives and (eff in Gvars.passives):
						cur_val = float(Gvars.passives.get(eff)) * 100.0
					elif Gvars and ("custom_passives" in Gvars) and (Gvars.custom_passives is Dictionary) and Gvars.custom_passives.has(eff):
						cur_val = float(Gvars.custom_passives[eff]) * 100.0

					var cap: float = float(passive.get("upTo", 0.0))
					if cap <= 0.0 or cur_val < cap:
						return i
		return -1

	var orig := chain.reference_object as ChoosePassive
	if orig.live and len(orig.choose_between) > 0:
		var idx: int = find_passive_idx.call(orig.choose_between)
		var button_text: String = ""
		match idx:
			0:
				orig._on_button_pressed.call()
				button_text = orig.button.text
			1:
				orig._on_button_2_pressed.call()
				button_text = orig.button_2.text
			2:
				orig._on_button_3_pressed.call()
				button_text = orig.button_3.text
			_:
				if reroll_if_no_matches:
					orig._on_reroll_pressed.call()
					button_text = "Cash"

		if button_text != "":
			var effect_scene = load("res://mods-unpacked/nanobotz-autopassivechooser/scenes/effect_gain.tscn")
			if effect_scene:
				var effect_text: Node = effect_scene.instantiate()
				var player: Player = Game.player
				if player:
					player.add_child(effect_text)
					effect_text.set_text(button_text)

			ModLoaderLog.info("Auto choosing option " + str(idx) + ": " + button_text, NanobotzAutoPassiveChooserMod.MOD_ID)

FILE: mods-unpacked\nanobotz-autopassivechooser\mods-unpacked\nanobotz-autopassivechooser\extensions\scenes\Interfaces\Menus\settings_2.hooks.gd
==================================================

extends Object

func _ready(chain: ModLoaderHookChain) -> void:
	await chain.execute_next([])
	var menu = chain.reference_object
	if not is_instance_valid(menu): return
	
	var passive_list = menu.find_child("passive_list", true, false)
	if not is_instance_valid(passive_list): return
	
	if not passive_list.has_node("BouncingBallDamage"):
		var template = null
		for c in passive_list.get_children():
			if c is Control: 
				template = c
				break
				
		if is_instance_valid(template):
			var keys = ["Bouncing Ball Damage", "Bouncing Ball Radius", "Bouncing Ball Fire Rate"]
			var internal_keys = ["ball_blast_damage", "ball_blast_radius", "ball_fire_rate"]
			
			for i in range(keys.size()):
				var row = template.duplicate()
				row.name = keys[i].replace(" ", "")
				row.visible = true
				var lbl = row.find_child("*Label*", true, false)
				if is_instance_valid(lbl): lbl.text = keys[i]
				row.set_meta("passive_key", internal_keys[i])
				passive_list.call_deferred("add_child", row)

FILE: mods-unpacked\nanobotz-autopassivechooser\mods-unpacked\nanobotz-autopassivechooser\extensions\scenes\tilemaps\tile_map_chunk.hooks.gd
==================================================
--------
extends Object


func destroy_tile(chain: ModLoaderHookChain, idx: int, tile_properties: Dictionary):
    var chunk := chain.reference_object as TileMapChunk
    #var chunk = chain.reference_object
    var type := chunk.tiles[idx]
    # var type = chunk.tiles[idx]
    var is_hard_tile := type in [
        TileMapChunk.Tiles.HARD_CLAY,
        TileMapChunk.Tiles.HARD_STONE,
        TileMapChunk.Tiles.HARD_ICE,
        TileMapChunk.Tiles.HARD_FIRE,
        TileMapChunk.Tiles.HARD_DARK,
        TileMapChunk.Tiles.HARD_GREEN,
        TileMapChunk.Tiles.HARD_ORANGE,
        TileMapChunk.Tiles.HARD_PURPLE,
        TileMapChunk.Tiles.HARD_DARKGREEN,
        TileMapChunk.Tiles.HARD_YELLOW,
        TileMapChunk.Tiles.HARD_DARKBROWN,
        TileMapChunk.Tiles.HARD_DARKBLUE
    ]
    
    chain.execute_next([idx, tile_properties])
    
    if not is_hard_tile:
        return

    var tile_pos := chunk.array_to_pos(idx)
    for child in chunk.get_children():
        var chest := child as Chest
        if not chest or chest.looted:
            continue
        
        for x in range(-1, 2):
            for y in range(-1, 2):
                if x == 0 and y == 0:
                    continue

                var chest_pos := Vector2i(tile_pos.x + x, tile_pos.y + y)
                var chest_pos_local := chunk.map_to_local(chest_pos)
                if chest.position == chest_pos_local:
                    chest.auto_loot_chest()
                    chest.opened = true
                    chest.looted = true
                    Bus.ShowInputIndicator.emit("interact", false, chest)

func pickup_item(chain: ModLoaderHookChain):
	var chest := chain.reference_object as Chest

	var config = NanobotzAutoPassiveChooserMod.get_or_create_config()
	if config.data.skip_weapon_pickups and chest.loot.itemType not in ["gem", "passive_upgrade"]:
		var effect_text: Node = load("res://mods-unpacked/nanobotz-autopassivechooser/scenes/effect_gain.tscn").instantiate()
		var player: Player = Game.player
		player.add_child(effect_text)
		effect_text.set_text("Skipped " + chest.loot.itemName)
		return

	chain.execute_next()

FILE: mods-unpacked\nanobotz-autopassivechooser\mods-unpacked\nanobotz-autopassivechooser\scenes\effect_gain.gd
==================================================

class_name EffectGain
extends Node2D


@onready var effect_text: Label = %EffectText

@export var duration: float = 3
@export var y_movement: float = 40

var time: float = 0
var y_per_second: float = 0
var opacity_per_second: float = 0


func _ready() -> void:
	effect_text.visible = false
	y_per_second = y_movement / duration
	opacity_per_second = 1 / duration

func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	if not effect_text.visible:
		return

	effect_text.position = Vector2(effect_text.position.x, effect_text.position.y - y_per_second * delta)    
	effect_text.self_modulate.a -= opacity_per_second * delta
	time += delta

	if time > duration:
		queue_free()


func set_text(text: String) -> void:
	effect_text.text = text
	effect_text.visible = true

FILE: mods-unpacked\nanobotz-autopassivechooser\mods-unpacked\nanobotz-autopassivechooser\scenes\passive_order.gd
==================================================
--------
class_name PassiveOrder
extends Control

var NanobotzAutoPassiveChooserMod = load("res://mods-unpacked/nanobotz-autopassivechooser/mod_main.gd")

@onready var profession_option: OptionButton = %ProfessionOption
@onready var skip_weapons_check: CheckBox = %SkipWeaponsCheck
@onready var reroll_check: CheckBox = %RerollCheck
@onready var grid: GridContainer = %Grid
@onready var template: Control = %Template

var player_profession: String
var template_children_count: int


func _ready() -> void:
	prepare_profession()
	prepare_grid_and_reroll()


func prepare_profession() -> void:
	player_profession = NanobotzAutoPassiveChooserMod.get_current_profession_or_default()

	if player_profession:
		var profession_index: int = 0
		for profession_idx in Gvars.get("profession_manager").get("all_professions"):
			var profession = Gvars.get("profession_manager").get("all_professions")[profession_idx]
			var profession_name = profession.profession.profession_name
			profession_option.add_item(profession_name)
			if profession_name.to_lower() == player_profession:
				profession_index = profession_option.item_count - 1
		
		profession_option.select(profession_index)
		profession_option.item_selected.connect(_on_profession_changed.bind())


func _on_profession_changed(idx) -> void:
	player_profession = profession_option.get_item_text(idx).to_lower()
	clear_grid()
	prepare_grid_and_reroll()


func clear_grid() -> void:
	for child in grid.get_children():
		grid.remove_child(child)


func get_possible_passives_for_professions(profession_name: String) -> Array:
	var passives: Array = []
	for profession_idx in Gvars.get("profession_manager").get("all_professions"):
		var profession = Gvars.get("profession_manager").get("all_professions")[profession_idx]
		if profession_name.to_lower() == profession.profession.profession_name.to_lower():
			var data = {"unlocked_passives": []}
			data = profession.profession._profession_effect(data)
			passives = data["unlocked_passives"].duplicate()
			break

	if passives.is_empty():
		passives = ChoosePassive.ALL_PASSIVES.keys().duplicate()

	# Ensure elemental & tick rate bonuses are always configurable
	var extras: Array = ["fire_damage", "wet_damage", "poison_damage", "elemental_tick_rate"]
	for ep in extras:
		if not passives.has(ep):
			passives.append(ep)

	return passives


func prepare_grid_and_reroll() -> void:
	template_children_count = grid.columns

	var config = NanobotzAutoPassiveChooserMod.get_or_create_config(player_profession)

	# Skip weapon pickups
	skip_weapons_check.button_pressed = config.data.skip_weapon_pickups

	# Reroll
	reroll_check.button_pressed = config.data.reroll_if_no_matches

	# Order
	if not config.data.has("passive_order"):
		config.data["passive_order"] = []
	var passive_order = config.data["passive_order"]
	var possible_passives := get_possible_passives_for_professions(player_profession)

	var order_index := {}
	for i in passive_order.size():
		order_index[passive_order[i].effect] = i

	possible_passives.sort_custom(func(a, b):
		var a_in := order_index.has(a)
		var b_in := order_index.has(b)

		if a_in and b_in:
			return order_index[a] < order_index[b]
		elif a_in:
			return true
		elif b_in:
			return false
		else:
			return a < b
	)

	for effect_name in possible_passives:
		var passive_settings := {}
		for passive in passive_order:
			if passive.effect == effect_name:
				passive_settings = passive

		var passive_enabled = passive_settings.enabled if passive_settings else false
		var passive_up_to = passive_settings.upTo if passive_settings else 0

		var template_controls := template.get_children()
		for control in template_controls:
			var new_control := control.duplicate()

			if new_control.name == "Enabled":
				new_control.button_pressed = passive_enabled
			elif new_control.name == "EffectName":
				new_control.text = effect_name.replace("_", " ").capitalize()
			elif new_control.name == "UpTo":
				if passive_up_to > 0:
					new_control.text = str(int(passive_up_to))
				new_control.text_changed.connect(_on_up_to_text_changed.bind(new_control))
			elif new_control.name == "ButtonUp":
				new_control.pressed.connect(_on_button_up_pressed.bind(effect_name))
			elif new_control.name == "ButtonDown":
				new_control.pressed.connect(_on_button_down_pressed.bind(effect_name))

			new_control.name += "$" + effect_name
			grid.add_child(new_control)


func _on_button_up_pressed(effect_name: String) -> void:
	move_controls(effect_name, true)


func _on_button_down_pressed(effect_name: String) -> void:
	move_controls(effect_name, false)


func move_controls(effect_name: String, up: bool) -> void:
	var child_offset := -template_children_count if up else template_children_count * 2
	var control_offset := 0 if up else -1
	var found_first := false

	var control_idx: int = 0
	for control in grid.get_children():
		if control.name.split("$")[1] == effect_name:
			found_first = true

			var new_pos := control_idx + child_offset + control_offset
			if new_pos < 0:
				continue

			grid.move_child(control, new_pos)
			if not up:
				control_idx -= 1
		elif found_first:
			break
			
		control_idx += 1


# Removes non-digits
func _on_up_to_text_changed(text: String, control: Control) -> void:
	if control is not LineEdit:
		return

	var te := control as LineEdit
	var old_text := text
	var caret := te.caret_column

	var new_text := ""
	var removed_before := 0

	for i in old_text.length():
		if old_text[i].is_valid_int():
			new_text += old_text[i]
		elif i < caret:
			removed_before += 1

	if new_text != old_text:
		te.text = new_text
		te.caret_column = max(caret - removed_before, 0)


func _on_confirm_pressed() -> void:
	var config = NanobotzAutoPassiveChooserMod.get_or_create_config(player_profession)

	# Skip weapon pickups
	config.data.skip_weapon_pickups = skip_weapons_check.button_pressed

	# Reroll
	config.data.reroll_if_no_matches = reroll_check.button_pressed

	# Order
	if not config.data.has("passive_order"):
		config.data["passive_order"] = []
	var passive_order = config.data["passive_order"]

	passive_order.clear()
	var prev_effect := ""
	var passive := {}
	for control in grid.get_children():
		var splits = control.name.split("$")

		if splits[1] != prev_effect:
			if not prev_effect.is_empty():
				if passive.enabled:
					passive_order.append(passive)
				passive = {}

			passive.effect = splits[1]
			prev_effect = splits[1]
				
		if splits[0] == "Enabled":
			passive.enabled = control.button_pressed
		if splits[0] == "UpTo":
			passive.upTo = int(control.text) if control.text else 0

	if passive.enabled:
		passive_order.append(passive)

	ModLoaderConfig.update_config(config)

	# reload
	clear_grid()
	prepare_grid_and_reroll()

	# queue_free()


func _on_cancel_pressed() -> void:
	# queue_free()
	pass



--------
==================================================
FILE: tests\test_runner.ps1
==================================================
--------
param (
	[string]$RepoPath = "C:\Dev\coal-llc-mods"
)

Write-Host "=================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host ">>> COAL LLC CI/CD: Automated Regression & Sanity Verification" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "=================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan

$errors = 0
$warnings = 0

# Test 1: ModRegistry Contract
$regFile = "$RepoPath\mods-unpacked\gareth-mod_manager_mod\mods-unpacked\gareth-mod_manager_mod\scripts\mod_registry.gd"
if (Test-Path $regFile) {
	$regText = Get-Content $regFile -Raw
	if (-not $regText.Contains("func is_mod_active_for_run")) {
		Write-Host "  [CRITICAL FAIL] mod_registry.gd is missing is_mod_active_for_run!" -ForegroundColor Red
		$errors++
	} else {
		Write-Host "  [PASS] mod_registry.gd satisfies is_mod_active_for_run interface" -ForegroundColor Green
	}
}

# Test 2: Manifest Schemas
$manifests = Get-ChildItem -Path "$RepoPath\mods-unpacked" -Recurse -Filter "manifest.json"
foreach ($m in $manifests) {
	try {
		$j = Get-Content $m.FullName -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json
		if (-not $j.name -or -not $j.version_number) {
			Write-Host "  [FAIL] Incomplete manifest at $($m.FullName)" -ForegroundColor Red
			$errors++
		}
	} catch {
		Write-Host "  [FAIL] JSON parse error: $($m.FullName)" -ForegroundColor Red
		$errors++
	}
}

# Test 3: Hook Signatures & Execute Next
$hookScripts = Get-ChildItem -Path "$RepoPath\mods-unpacked" -Recurse -Filter "*.hooks.gd"
foreach ($h in $hookScripts) {
	$ht = Get-Content $h.FullName -Raw
	if ($ht.Contains("chain: ModLoaderHookChain") -and -not $ht.Contains("chain.execute_next")) {
		Write-Host "  [WARN] $($h.Name) has hook chain without chain.execute_next" -ForegroundColor Yellow
		$warnings++
	}
}

Write-Host "=================================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
if ($errors -eq 0) {
	Write-Host ">>> ALL REGRESSION CHECKS PASSED (Warnings: $warnings, Errors: 0)" -ForegroundColor Green
	exit 0
} else {
	Write-Host ">>> CI/CD VERIFICATION FAILED (Errors: $errors)" -ForegroundColor Red
	exit 1
}
